# Insert-Only versus Insert-Delete in Dynamic Query Evaluation

动态查询评估中的仅插入与插入删除模式对比

MAHMOUD ABO KHAMIS, RelationalAI, United States

马哈茂德·阿布·哈米斯，RelationalAI，美国

AHMET KARA, OTH Regensburg, Germany

艾哈迈德·卡拉，OTH雷根斯堡大学，德国

DAN OLTEANU, University of Zurich, Switzerland

丹·奥尔泰亚努，苏黎世大学，瑞士

DAN SUCIU, University of Washington, United States

丹·苏丘，华盛顿大学，美国

We study the dynamic query evaluation problem: Given a full conjunctive query $Q$ and a sequence of updates to the input database, we construct a data structure that supports constant-delay enumeration of the tuples in the query output after each update.

我们研究动态查询评估问题：给定一个完整合取查询$Q$和对输入数据库的更新序列，构建一个支持在每次更新后以恒定延迟枚举查询输出元组的数据结构。

We show that a sequence of $N$ insert-only updates to an initially empty database can be executed in total time $O\left( {N}^{\mathrm{w}\left( Q\right) }\right)$ ,where $\mathrm{w}\left( Q\right)$ is the fractional hypertree width of $Q$ . This matches the complexity of the static query evaluation problem for $Q$ and a database of size $N$ . One corollary is that the amortized time per single-tuple insert is constant for $\alpha$ -acyclic full conjunctive queries.

我们证明，对初始空数据库进行$N$次仅插入更新的总执行时间为$O\left( {N}^{\mathrm{w}\left( Q\right) }\right)$，其中$\mathrm{w}\left( Q\right)$是$Q$的分数超树宽度。这与$Q$在规模为$N$的数据库上静态查询评估的复杂度一致。由此推论，对于$\alpha$非循环完整合取查询，单次元组插入的摊销时间是常数。

In contrast,we show that a sequence of $N$ inserts and deletes can be executed in total time $\widetilde{O}\left( {N}^{\mathrm{w}\left( \widehat{Q}\right) }\right)$ ,where $\widehat{Q}$ is obtained from $Q$ by extending every relational atom with extra variables that represent the "lifespans" of tuples in the database. We show that this reduction is optimal in the sense that the static evaluation runtime of $\widehat{Q}$ provides a lower bound on the total update time for the output of $Q$ . Our approach achieves amortized optimal update times for the hierarchical and Loomis-Whitney join queries.

相比之下，$N$次插入和删除操作的总执行时间为$\widetilde{O}\left( {N}^{\mathrm{w}\left( \widehat{Q}\right) }\right)$，其中$\widehat{Q}$是通过为每个关系原子添加表示元组"生命周期"的额外变量从$Q$扩展而来。我们证明该归约是最优的，因为$\widehat{Q}$的静态评估运行时间构成了$Q$输出总更新时间的下界。该方法为层次化查询和Loomis-Whitney连接查询实现了摊销最优更新时间。

Additional Key Words and Phrases: incremental view maintenance; optimality; intersection joins

其他关键词与短语：增量视图维护；最优性；交集连接

## 1 INTRODUCTION

## 1 引言

Answering queries under updates to the database, also called dynamic query evaluation, is a fundamental problem in data management, with recent work addressing it from both systems and theoretical perspectives. There have been recent efforts on building systems for dynamic query evaluation, such as DBToaster [27], DynYannakakis [17, 18], F-IVM [25, 36, 37], and CROWN [43]. By allowing tuples to carry payloads with elements from rings [26], such systems can maintain complex analytics over database queries, such as linear algebra computation [35], collection programming [28], and machine learning models [25]. There has also been work on dynamic computation for intersection joins [41] and for expressive languages such as Datalog [31], Differential Datalog [1], and DBSP [10]. The descriptive complexity of various recursive Datalog queries under updates, such as reachability, has also been investigated [12, 39]. Dynamic query evaluation has also been utilized for complex event recognition [14, 15].

在数据库更新时回答查询（即动态查询评估）是数据管理的核心问题，近期研究从系统和理论角度进行了探索。已出现多个动态查询评估系统，如DBToaster[27]、DynYannakakis[17,18]、F-IVM[25,36,37]和CROWN[43]。通过允许元组携带环元素的有效载荷[26]，这些系统可维护数据库查询上的复杂分析，如线性代数计算[35]、集合编程[28]和机器学习模型[25]。还有研究涉及交集连接[41]及Datalog[31]、差分Datalog[1]、DBSP[10]等表达语言的动态计算。更新时各类递归Datalog查询（如可达性）的描述复杂度也得到研究[12,39]。动态查询评估还应用于复杂事件识别[14,15]。

While this problem attracted continuous interest in academia and industry over the past decades, it is only relatively recently that the first results on the fine-grained complexity and in particular on the optimality of this problem have emerged. These efforts aim to mirror the breakthrough made by the introduction of worst-case optimal join algorithms $\left\lbrack  {7,{33},{42}}\right\rbrack$ . Beyond notable yet limited explorations,understanding the optimality of maintenance for the entire language of conjunctive queries remains open. Prime examples of progress towards the optimality of query maintenance are the characterizations of queries that admit (worst-case or amortized) constant time per single-tuple update (insert or delete): the $q$ -hierarchical queries $\left\lbrack  {8,{17},{25},{43}}\right\rbrack$ ,queries that become $q$ -hierarchical in the presence of free access patterns [23] or by rewriting under functional dependencies [25], or queries on update sequences whose enclosureness is bounded by a constant [44]. The ${\delta }_{1}$ -hierarchical queries [22-24] and the triangle queries [20,21] admit optimal, albeit non-constant, update time conditioned on the Online Matrix-Vector Multiplication (OMv) conjecture [16].

尽管该问题过去几十年持续吸引学界和工业界关注，但直到近年才出现关于细粒度复杂度（尤其是问题最优性）的首批成果。这些努力旨在复现最坏情况最优连接算法$\left\lbrack  {7,{33},{42}}\right\rbrack$带来的突破。除个别有限探索外，理解合取查询全语言维护的最优性仍是开放问题。查询维护最优性研究的典型进展包括：支持单次元组更新（插入/删除）(最坏或摊销)常数时间的查询特征描述——$q$层次化查询$\left\lbrack  {8,{17},{25},{43}}\right\rbrack$、在自由访问模式[23]或函数依赖重写[25]下转为$q$层次化的查询、或包围度受常数限制的更新序列查询[44]。[22-24]的${\delta }_{1}$层次化查询和[20,21]的三角查询在在线矩阵向量乘法(OMv)猜想[16]条件下可实现最优（虽非常数）更新时间。

In this paper, we introduce a new approach to incremental view maintenance for full conjunctive queries (or queries for short). Our approach complements prior work in four ways.

本文提出了一种针对完全合取查询（简称查询）的增量视图维护新方法。我们的研究从四个方面对现有工作进行了补充。

First,we give reductions from the dynamic query evaluation problem for a query $Q$ to the static query evaluation problem of a derived query $\widehat{Q}$ (called the multivariate extension of $Q$ ),and vice versa. Our reductions link the complexities of the two problems and allow to transfer both algorithms and lower bounds from one problem to the other. Specifically, we give a reduction from dynamic query evaluation to a wide class of algorithms for static query evaluation, namely algorithms that meet the fractional hypertree width, thus allowing us to use those algorithms for dynamic query evaluation. Moreover, we give a reduction from static query evaluation to any algorithm for dynamic query evaluation, thus allowing us to use lower bounds on static query evaluation to infer lower bounds on dynamic query evaluation. Both reductions use a translation of the time dimension in the dynamic problem into a spatial dimension in the static problem, so that the maintenance under a stream of updates corresponds to taking the intersection of intervals representing the "lifespans" of tuples in the update stream.

首先，我们将查询$Q$的动态求值问题归约为派生查询$\widehat{Q}$（称为$Q$的多变量扩展）的静态求值问题，反之亦然。这种归约建立了两个问题复杂度的关联，使得算法设计与下界证明可以在两类问题间相互转化。具体而言，我们实现了动态查询求值到静态查询求值算法大类（满足分数超树宽度的算法）的归约，从而能将这类静态算法应用于动态场景。同时，我们也建立了静态查询求值到任意动态求值算法的归约，使得静态查询的下界结果可推导出动态查询的下界。两种归约都通过将动态问题中的时间维度转化为静态问题中的空间维度来实现，使得更新流中的维护操作对应于对元组"生命周期"时间区间的交集运算。

Second, we devise new dynamic query evaluation algorithms that use our reductions. We call them MVIVM (short for MultiVariate IVM). Their single-tuple update times are amortized. For any $\alpha$ -acyclic query in the insert-only setting, MVIVM take amortized constant single-tuple update time. This recovers a prior result [43], when restricted from free-connex to full conjunctive queries. For Loomis-Whitney queries in the insert-delete setting, MVIVM take amortized $\widetilde{O}\left( {\left| \mathcal{D}\right| }^{1/2}\right)$ update time,where $\left| \mathcal{D}\right|$ is the database size at update time; this is optimal up to a polylogarithmic factor in the database size. This recovers prior work on the triangle query, which is the Loomis-Whitney query with three variables [21]. For hierarchical queries in the insert-delete setting, MVIVM take amortized constant single-tuple update time; this is weaker than prior work [8], which showed that worst-case constant single-tuple update time can be achieved for $q$ -hierarchical queries.

其次，我们基于上述归约设计了新型动态查询求值算法MVIVM（多变量增量视图维护的缩写）。该算法采用分摊的单元组更新时间。在仅插入场景中，对于任何$\alpha$-非循环查询，MVIVM都能实现分摊常数时间的单元组更新，这复现了文献[43]将自由连接查询限定为完全合取查询时的结果。在支持插入删除的场景中，对于Loomis-Whitney查询，MVIVM实现$\widetilde{O}\left( {\left| \mathcal{D}\right| }^{1/2}\right)$分摊更新时间（$\left| \mathcal{D}\right|$为更新时的数据库规模），该结果在数据库规模的多对数因子内达到最优，复现了针对三元变量Loomis-Whitney查询（即三角形查询）的已有研究[21]。对于层次化查询，在插入删除场景中MVIVM实现分摊常数时间的单元组更新，该结果弱于文献[8]针对$q$-层次化查询提出的最坏情况常数时间更新方案。

Third, MVIVM support (worst-case, not amortized) constant delay enumeration of the tuples in the query output (full enumeration) or in the change to the query output (delta enumeration) after each update. In particular, MVIVM map an input stream of updates to an output stream of updates, so they are closed under updates. In practice, the change to the output after an update is often much smaller than the full output, making delta enumeration more efficient than full enumeration.

第三，MVIVM支持（最坏情况、非分摊）常数延迟的查询结果元组枚举（完全枚举）或更新后结果变化量枚举（增量枚举）。特别地，MVIVM将输入更新流映射为输出更新流，形成闭合的更新系统。实践中，单次更新导致的输出变化通常远小于完整输出，使得增量枚举比完全枚举更高效。

Fourth, we pinpoint the complexity gap between the insert-only and the insert-delete settings. In the insert-only setting, we show that one can readily use a worst-case optimal join algorithm like GenericJoin [33] and LeapFrogTrieJoin [42] to execute a stream of inserts one insert at a time in the same overall time as if the entire stream was executed as one bulk update (which corresponds to static evaluation). This implies an upper bound on the amortized update time for the dynamic evaluation of $Q$ in the insert-only setting. In contrast,in the insert-delete setting,a stream of both inserts and deletes can be executed one update at a time in the same overall time as the static evaluation of a query $\widehat{Q}$ ,which is obtained by extending every relational atom in $Q$ with extra variables representing the "lifespans" of tuples. This query $\widehat{Q}$ may have a higher complexity than $Q$ ,thus leading to an upper bound on the amortized update time for $Q$ in the insert-delete setting that may be higher than in the insert-only setting. Nevertheless, our reduction from the maintenance of $Q$ in the insert-delete setting to the static evaluation problem for $\widehat{Q}$ is optimal: Lower bounds on the latter problem imply lower bounds on the former. This shows that maintenance in the insert-delete setting can be more expensive than in the insert-only setting.

第四，我们精确刻画了仅插入与支持插入删除场景的复杂度差异。在仅插入场景中，研究表明可直接使用GenericJoin[33]或LeapFrogTrieJoin[42]等最坏情况最优连接算法，以与批量更新（对应静态求值）相同的总时间逐条处理插入流，这为$Q$查询在仅插入场景中的动态求值提供了更新时间的上界。相比之下，在支持插入删除的场景中，包含增删操作的更新流可通过扩展$Q$中每个关系原子（添加表示元组"生命周期"的额外变量）得到查询$\widehat{Q}$，以与静态求值相同的总时间逐条处理。由于$\widehat{Q}$可能比$Q$具有更高复杂度，导致支持插入删除场景的更新时间上界可能高于仅插入场景。但我们的归约方法已达到最优：$\widehat{Q}$静态求值的下界即对应$Q$动态维护的下界，这证明支持删除操作的维护代价可能更高。

## Motivating Examples

## 动机示例

We exemplify our results for the following three join queries:

我们通过以下三个连接查询来例证我们的研究成果：

$$
{Q}_{3p}\left( {A,B,C,D}\right)  = R\left( {A,B}\right)  \land  S\left( {B,C}\right)  \land  T\left( {C,D}\right) 
$$

$$
{Q}_{\bigtriangleup }\left( {A,B,C}\right)  = R\left( {A,B}\right)  \land  S\left( {B,C}\right)  \land  T\left( {A,C}\right)  \tag{1}
$$

$$
{Q}_{4}\left( {A,B,C,D}\right)  = R\left( {B,C,D}\right)  \land  S\left( {A,C,D}\right)  \land  T\left( {A,B,D}\right)  \land  U\left( {A,B,C}\right) 
$$

We would like to perform single-tuple updates to each query, while ensuring constant-delay enumeration of the tuples in the query output after each update. These queries are not $q$ -hierarchical,hence they cannot admit worst-case constant time per single-tuple update [8]: The OMv conjecture implies that there is no algorithm that takes $O\left( {\left| \mathcal{D}\right| }^{1/2 - \gamma }\right)$ time for single-tuple updates,for any $\gamma  > 0$ ,to any of these queries. In fact,for a database $\mathcal{D}$ ,systems like DBToaster [27], DynYannakakis [17], F-IVM [36], and CROWN [43] need at least worst-case $O\left( \left| \mathcal{D}\right| \right)$ time per single-tuple update to each of these queries. For the triangle query ${Q}_{\bigtriangleup },{\mathrm{{IVM}}}^{\epsilon }$ takes $O\left( {\left| \mathcal{D}\right| }^{1/2}\right)$ amortized time for single-tuple updates [21]. In the insert-only setting,CROWN maintains ${Q}_{3p}$ in amortized constant time per insert. However,it cannot handle non-acyclic queries like ${Q}_{\Delta }$ and ${Q}_{4}$ (with update time better than recomputation from scratch). Appendix A explains how each prior approach maintains ${Q}_{3p}$ and ${Q}_{\bigtriangleup }$ in the insert-delete setting,and compares their update times to our approach, MVIVM.

我们希望对这些查询执行单元组更新，同时确保每次更新后能实现查询输出结果的恒定延迟枚举。这些查询不符合$q$分层特性，因此无法保证每次单元组更新的最坏情况恒定时间复杂度[8]：根据OMv猜想，对于任何$\gamma  > 0$，不存在能在$O\left( {\left| \mathcal{D}\right| }^{1/2 - \gamma }\right)$时间内完成这些查询单元组更新的算法。实际上，对于数据库$\mathcal{D}$，诸如DBToaster[27]、DynYannakakis[17]、F-IVM[36]和CROWN[43]等系统，对这些查询的单元组更新至少需要最坏情况下$O\left( \left| \mathcal{D}\right| \right)$时间。对于三角形查询${Q}_{\bigtriangleup },{\mathrm{{IVM}}}^{\epsilon }$，单次更新需要$O\left( {\left| \mathcal{D}\right| }^{1/2}\right)$摊还时间[21]。在仅插入场景中，CROWN能以每次插入的摊还恒定时间维护${Q}_{3p}$。但无法处理${Q}_{\Delta }$和${Q}_{4}$这类非环状查询（其更新时间优于完全重新计算）。附录A阐述了各现有方法在插入-删除场景中如何维护${Q}_{3p}$和${Q}_{\bigtriangleup }$，并将其更新时间与我们的MVIVM方法进行对比。

We show that,for ${Q}_{3p}$ ,MVIVM performs single-tuple updates: in amortized constant time in the insert-only setting; and in amortized $\widetilde{O}\left( {\left| \mathcal{D}\right| }^{1/2}\right)$ time in the insert-delete setting $(\widetilde{O}$ notation hides a polylog $\left( \left| \mathcal{D}\right| \right)$ factor). Both times are optimal, the latter is conditioned on the OMv conjecture. The amortized constant time per insert matches that of CROWN [43] and does not contradict the non-constant lower bound for non-hierarchical queries without self-joins [8]: that lower bound proof relies on a reduction from OMv that requires both inserts and deletes. Achieving this amortized constant time, while also allowing constant-delay enumeration after each insert, is non-trivial. The lazy maintenance approach, which only updates the input data, is not sufficient to achieve this result. This is because, while it does indeed take constant insert time, it still requires the computation of the query output before the enumeration of the first output tuple. This computation takes linear time using a factorized approach [25] and needs to be repeated for every enumeration request.

我们证明，对于${Q}_{3p}$，MVIVM执行单元组更新：在仅插入场景中具有摊还恒定时间；在插入-删除场景中具有摊还$\widetilde{O}\left( {\left| \mathcal{D}\right| }^{1/2}\right)$时间（$(\widetilde{O}$表示法隐含了多对数$\left( \left| \mathcal{D}\right| \right)$因子）。这两种时间复杂度都是最优的，后者基于OMv猜想成立。每次插入的摊还恒定时间与CROWN[43]一致，且不违背无自连接的非分层查询的非恒定时间下界[8]：该下界证明依赖于需要插入和删除操作的OMv归约。实现这种摊还恒定时间，同时保证每次插入后能进行恒定延迟枚举，具有显著技术难度。仅更新输入数据的惰性维护方法不足以达成该目标，因为虽然其插入时间确实恒定，但仍需在枚举首个输出元组前计算查询结果——采用因子化方法[25]该计算需要线性时间，且每次枚举请求都需重复此过程。

MVIVM takes $O\left( {\left| \mathcal{D}\right| }^{1/2}\right)$ and $O\left( {\left| \mathcal{D}\right| }^{1/3}\right)$ amortized single-tuple insert time for the triangle query ${Q}_{\Delta }$ and,respectively,the Loomis-Whitney-4 query ${Q}_{4}$ . Given a sequence of $\left| \mathcal{D}\right|$ inserts,the total times of $O\left( {\left| \mathcal{D}\right| }^{3/2}\right)$ and $O\left( {\left| \mathcal{D}\right| }^{4/3}\right)$ , respectively, match the worst-case optimal times for the static evaluation of these two queries [33]. In the insert-delete setting,single-tuple updates take $\widetilde{O}\left( {\left| \mathcal{D}\right| }^{1/2}\right)$ amortized time for both queries,and this is optimal based on the OMv conjecture. Figure 4 in Appendix A shows the update times of MVIVM and prior work for further queries.

MVIVM对三角形查询${Q}_{\Delta }$和Loomis-Whitney-4查询${Q}_{4}$分别需要$O\left( {\left| \mathcal{D}\right| }^{1/2}\right)$和$O\left( {\left| \mathcal{D}\right| }^{1/3}\right)$摊还单次插入时间。给定$\left| \mathcal{D}\right|$次插入序列，$O\left( {\left| \mathcal{D}\right| }^{3/2}\right)$和$O\left( {\left| \mathcal{D}\right| }^{4/3}\right)$的总时间分别匹配这两个查询静态评估的最坏情况最优时间[33]。在插入-删除场景中，两个查询的单次更新均需$\widetilde{O}\left( {\left| \mathcal{D}\right| }^{1/2}\right)$摊还时间，根据OMv猜想该时间复杂度是最优的。附录A图4展示了MVIVM与现有方法对更多查询的更新时间对比。

## 2 PRELIMINARIES

## 2 基础知识

In this section, we review some preliminaries, most of which are standard in database theory.

本节回顾部分基础知识，其中多数是数据库理论中的标准内容。

We use $\mathbb{N}$ to denote the set of natural numbers including 0 . For $n \in  \mathbb{N}$ ,we define $\left\lbrack  n\right\rbrack  \overset{\text{ def }}{ = }\{ 1,2,\ldots ,n\}$ . In case $n = 0$ , we have $\left\lbrack  n\right\rbrack   = \varnothing$ .

我们用$\mathbb{N}$表示包含0的自然数集。对于$n \in  \mathbb{N}$，定义$\left\lbrack  n\right\rbrack  \overset{\text{ def }}{ = }\{ 1,2,\ldots ,n\}$。当$n = 0$时，可得$\left\lbrack  n\right\rbrack   = \varnothing$。

Data Model. Following standard terminology, a relation is a finite set of tuples and a database is a finite set of relations [2]. The size $\left| R\right|$ of a relation $R$ is the number of its tuples. The size $\left| \mathcal{D}\right|$ of a database $\mathcal{D}$ is given by the sum of the sizes of its relations.

数据模型。依照标准术语，关系(relation)是元组的有限集合，数据库(database)是关系的有限集合[2]。关系$R$的大小$\left| R\right|$即其元组数量。数据库$\mathcal{D}$的大小$\left| \mathcal{D}\right|$为其所有关系大小的总和。

Queries. We consider natural join queries or full conjunctive queries. We refer to them as queries for short. A query has the form

查询。我们研究自然连接查询(natural join queries)或完全合取查询(full conjunctive queries)，简称为查询。查询表达式形如

$$
Q\left( \mathbf{X}\right)  = {R}_{1}\left( {\mathbf{X}}_{1}\right)  \land  \cdots  \land  {R}_{k}\left( {\mathbf{X}}_{k}\right) , \tag{2}
$$

where each ${R}_{i}$ is a relation symbol,each ${X}_{i}$ is a tuple of variables,and $X = \mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack  }}{X}_{i}$ . We refer to ${X}_{i}$ as the schema of ${R}_{i}$ and treat schemas and sets of variables interchangeably,assuming a fixed ordering of variables. We call each ${R}_{i}\left( {X}_{i}\right)$ an atom of $Q$ . We denote by at $\left( Q\right)  = \left\{  {{R}_{i}\left( {X}_{i}\right)  \mid  i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack  }\right\}$ the set of all atoms of $Q$ and by at(X)the set of atoms ${R}_{i}\left( {X}_{i}\right)$ with $X \in  {X}_{i}$ . The set of all variables of $Q$ is denoted by $\operatorname{vars}\left( Q\right)  = \mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack  }}{X}_{i}$ . Since the set $X$ is always equal to $\operatorname{vars}\left( Q\right)$ for all queries we consider in the paper,we drop $X$ from the query notation and abbreviate $Q\left( X\right)$ with $Q$ in Eq. (2). We say that $Q$ is without self-joins if every relation symbol appears in at most one atom.

其中每个${R}_{i}$是关系符号，每个${X}_{i}$是变量元组，且$X = \mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack  }}{X}_{i}$。我们将${X}_{i}$称为${R}_{i}$的模式(schema)，并假设变量有固定顺序，因此模式与变量集可互换使用。每个${R}_{i}\left( {X}_{i}\right)$称为$Q$的原子(atoms)。用at$\left( Q\right)  = \left\{  {{R}_{i}\left( {X}_{i}\right)  \mid  i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack  }\right\}$表示$Q$的所有原子集合，at(X)表示满足$X \in  {X}_{i}$的原子${R}_{i}\left( {X}_{i}\right)$集合。$Q$的全部变量集合记作$\operatorname{vars}\left( Q\right)  = \mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack  }}{X}_{i}$。由于本文讨论的所有查询中集合$X$恒等于$\operatorname{vars}\left( Q\right)$，故在查询表示中省略$X$，并将式(2)中的$Q\left( X\right)$简写为$Q$。若每个关系符号至多出现在一个原子中，则称$Q$不含自连接(self-joins)。

The domain of a variable $X$ is denoted by $\operatorname{Dom}\left( X\right)$ . A value tuple $t$ over a schema $X = \left( {{X}_{1},\ldots ,{X}_{n}}\right)$ is an element from $\operatorname{Dom}\left( X\right) \overset{\text{ def }}{ = }\operatorname{Dom}\left( {X}_{1}\right)  \times  \cdots  \times  \operatorname{Dom}\left( {X}_{n}\right)$ . We denote by $t\left( X\right)$ the $X$ -value of $t$ .

变量$X$的定义域记作$\operatorname{Dom}\left( X\right)$。模式$X = \left( {{X}_{1},\ldots ,{X}_{n}}\right)$上的值元组(value tuple)$t$是$\operatorname{Dom}\left( X\right) \overset{\text{ def }}{ = }\operatorname{Dom}\left( {X}_{1}\right)  \times  \cdots  \times  \operatorname{Dom}\left( {X}_{n}\right)$的元素。用$t\left( X\right)$表示$t$的$X$值。

A tuple $t$ over schema $X$ is in the result of $Q$ if there are tuples ${t}_{i} \in  {R}_{i}$ for $i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack$ such that for all $X \in  \operatorname{vars}\left( Q\right)$ , the set $\left\{  {{t}_{i}\left( X\right)  \mid  i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack   \land  X \in  {X}_{i}}\right\}$ consists of the single value $t\left( X\right)$ . Given a query $Q$ and a database $\mathcal{D}$ ,we denote by $Q\left( \mathcal{D}\right)$ the result of $Q$ over $\mathcal{D}$ .

当存在$i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack$对应的元组${t}_{i} \in  {R}_{i}$，使得对所有$X \in  \operatorname{vars}\left( Q\right)$，集合$\left\{  {{t}_{i}\left( X\right)  \mid  i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack   \land  X \in  {X}_{i}}\right\}$仅包含单一值$t\left( X\right)$时，模式$X$上的元组$t$属于查询$Q$的结果。给定查询$Q$和数据库$\mathcal{D}$，用$Q\left( \mathcal{D}\right)$表示$Q$在$\mathcal{D}$上的执行结果。

A union of queries is of the form $Q = \mathop{\bigvee }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  n\right\rbrack  }}{Q}_{i}$ where each ${Q}_{i}$ ,called a component of $Q$ ,is a query over the same set of variables as $Q$ . The result of $Q$ is the union of the results of its components.

查询的联合采用$Q = \mathop{\bigvee }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  n\right\rbrack  }}{Q}_{i}$的形式，其中每个${Q}_{i}$（称为$Q$的组成部分）是与$Q$具有相同变量集的查询。$Q$的结果是其各组成部分结果的并集。

Query Classes. A query is ( $\alpha$ -)acyclic if we can construct a tree,called join tree,such that the nodes of the tree are the atoms of the query (coverage) and for each variable, it holds: if the variable appears in two atoms, then it appears in all atoms on the path connecting the two atoms (connectivity) [9].

查询类别。若能为查询构建一棵称为连接树的树结构，满足：树节点对应查询中的原子谓词（覆盖性），且对每个变量而言，若该变量出现在两个原子谓词中，则它必须出现在连接这两个原子谓词路径上的所有原子谓词中（连通性）[9]，则该查询是（$\alpha$-）无环的。

Hierarchical queries are a sub-class of acyclic queries. A query is called hierarchical if for any two variables $X$ and $Y$ ,it holds at $\left( X\right)  \subseteq  \operatorname{at}\left( Y\right)$ ,at $\left( Y\right)  \subseteq  \operatorname{at}\left( X\right)$ ,or at $\left( X\right)  \cap  \operatorname{at}\left( Y\right)  = \varnothing \left\lbrack  {40}\right\rbrack$ .

层次化查询是无环查询的子类。当任意两个变量$X$和$Y$满足$\left( X\right)  \subseteq  \operatorname{at}\left( Y\right)$、$\left( Y\right)  \subseteq  \operatorname{at}\left( X\right)$或$\left( X\right)  \cap  \operatorname{at}\left( Y\right)  = \varnothing \left\lbrack  {40}\right\rbrack$条件时，该查询称为层次化查询。

Loomis-Whitney queries generalize the triangle query from a clique of degree 3 to higher degrees [29]. A Loomis-Whitney query of degree $k \geq  3$ has $k$ variables ${X}_{1},\ldots ,{X}_{k}$ and has,for each subset $Y \subset  \left\{  {{X}_{1},\ldots ,{X}_{k}}\right\}$ of size $k - 1$ ,one distinct atom with schema $Y$ .

Loomis-Whitney查询将三角形查询从3度团推广到更高维度[29]。一个$k \geq  3$度的Loomis-Whitney查询包含$k$个变量${X}_{1},\ldots ,{X}_{k}$，且对于每个大小为$k - 1$的子集$Y \subset  \left\{  {{X}_{1},\ldots ,{X}_{k}}\right\}$，都有一个模式为$Y$的独立原子谓词。

A union of queries is acyclic (hierarchical, Loomis-Whitney) if each of its components is acyclic (hierarchical, Loomis-Whitney). Example B. 1 showcases the query classes mentioned above.

当查询联合中的每个组成部分都是无环（层次化、Loomis-Whitney型）查询时，该联合查询即为无环（层次化、Loomis-Whitney型）查询。示例B.1展示了上述查询类别。

Single-tuple Updates and Deltas. We denote an insert of a tuple $t$ into a relation $R$ by $+ R\left( t\right)$ and a delete of $t$ from $R$ by $- R\left( t\right)$ . The insert $+ R\left( t\right)$ inserts $t$ into $R$ if $t$ is not contained in $R$ ,and the delete $- R\left( t\right)$ deletes $t$ from $R$ if it is contained in $R$ . Each insert or delete has a timestamp $\tau$ ,which is a natural number that indicates the specific time at which the insert or delete occurs. An update ${\delta }_{\tau }R$ to $R$ at time $\tau$ is the set of all inserts and deletes to $R$ at time $\tau$ . An update ${\delta }_{\tau }R$ is applied to $R$ by first applying all the deletes in ${\delta }_{\tau }R$ and then applying all the inserts. We use $R \oplus  {\delta }_{\tau }R$ to denote the result of applying an update ${\delta }_{\tau }R$ to a relation $R$ . We use ${R}^{\left( \tau \right) }$ to refer to the specific version of a relation $R$ at timestamp $\tau$ . In particular, ${R}^{\left( \tau \right) }$ is the result of applying ${\delta }_{\tau }R$ to the previous version ${R}^{\left( \tau  - 1\right) }$ ,i.e. ${R}^{\left( \tau \right) } = {R}^{\left( \tau  - 1\right) } \uplus  {\delta }_{\tau }R$ . An update ${\delta }_{\tau }\mathcal{D}$ to a database $\mathcal{D}$ is the set of all inserts and deletes to the relations in $\mathcal{D}$ at time $\tau$ . Similarly,we use ${\mathcal{D}}^{\left( \tau \right) }$ to refer to the specific version of $\mathcal{D}$ at time $\tau$ ,i.e., ${\mathcal{D}}^{\left( \tau \right) } = {\mathcal{D}}^{\left( \tau  - 1\right) } \uplus  {\delta }_{\tau }\mathcal{D}$ . If ${\delta }_{\tau }\mathcal{D}$ consists of a single insert or delete,i.e., $\left| {{\delta }_{\tau }\mathcal{D}}\right|  = 1$ ,it is called a single-tuple update. In particular,if it consists of a single insert (or delete),we call it a single-tuple insert (or single-tuple delete). Given a query $Q$ over a database $\mathcal{D}$ ,we use ${\delta }_{\tau }Q\left( \mathcal{D}\right)$ to denote the change in $Q\left( \mathcal{D}\right)$ at time $\tau$ . Specifically, ${\delta }_{\tau }Q\left( \mathcal{D}\right)$ is the difference between $Q\left( {\mathcal{D}}^{\left( \tau \right) }\right)$ and $Q\left( {\mathcal{D}}^{\left( \tau  - 1\right) }\right)$ :

单元素组更新与增量。我们将向关系$R$中插入元组$t$的操作记为$+ R\left( t\right)$，从$R$中删除$t$的操作记为$- R\left( t\right)$。当$t$不在$R$中时，插入操作$+ R\left( t\right)$会将$t$添加到$R$；当$t$存在于$R$中时，删除操作$- R\left( t\right)$会将其移除。每个插入或删除操作都带有时间戳$\tau$，这个自然数表示操作发生的具体时间。在时刻$\tau$对$R$的更新${\delta }_{\tau }R$是该时刻所有对$R$的插入和删除操作的集合。应用更新${\delta }_{\tau }R$时，先执行所有删除操作再执行插入操作。我们用$R \oplus  {\delta }_{\tau }R$表示对关系$R$应用更新${\delta }_{\tau }R$后的结果，用${R}^{\left( \tau \right) }$指代时间戳$\tau$时关系$R$的特定版本。特别地，${R}^{\left( \tau \right) }$是通过对前一版本${R}^{\left( \tau  - 1\right) }$应用${\delta }_{\tau }R$得到的结果，即${R}^{\left( \tau \right) } = {R}^{\left( \tau  - 1\right) } \uplus  {\delta }_{\tau }R$。对数据库$\mathcal{D}$的更新${\delta }_{\tau }\mathcal{D}$是该时刻对所有关系的插入和删除操作集合。类似地，${\mathcal{D}}^{\left( \tau \right) }$表示$\mathcal{D}$在时刻$\tau$的特定版本，即${\mathcal{D}}^{\left( \tau \right) } = {\mathcal{D}}^{\left( \tau  - 1\right) } \uplus  {\delta }_{\tau }\mathcal{D}$。若${\delta }_{\tau }\mathcal{D}$仅包含单个插入或删除操作（即$\left| {{\delta }_{\tau }\mathcal{D}}\right|  = 1$），则称为单元素组更新。特别地，仅含单个插入（或删除）时称为单元素组插入（或删除）。对于数据库$\mathcal{D}$上的查询$Q$，${\delta }_{\tau }Q\left( \mathcal{D}\right)$表示时刻$\tau$时$Q\left( \mathcal{D}\right)$的变化量，具体是$Q\left( {\mathcal{D}}^{\left( \tau \right) }\right)$与$Q\left( {\mathcal{D}}^{\left( \tau  - 1\right) }\right)$的差值：

$$
{\delta }_{\tau }Q\left( \mathcal{D}\right) \overset{\text{ def }}{ = }\left\{  {+Q\left( \mathbf{t}\right)  \mid  \mathbf{t} \in  Q\left( {\mathcal{D}}^{\left( \tau \right) }\right) \text{ and }\mathbf{t} \notin  Q\left( {\mathcal{D}}^{\left( \tau  - 1\right) }\right) }\right\}  
$$

$$
 \cup  \left\{  {-Q\left( \mathbf{t}\right)  \mid  \mathbf{t} \in  Q\left( {\mathcal{D}}^{\left( \tau  - 1\right) }\right) \text{ and }\mathbf{t} \notin  Q\left( {\mathcal{D}}^{\left( \tau \right) }\right) }\right\}   \tag{3}
$$

When the time $\tau$ is clear from the context,we drop the annotation ( $\tau$ ) and write $R,{\delta R},\mathcal{D},\delta \mathcal{D},Q\left( \mathcal{D}\right)$ ,and ${\delta Q}\left( \mathcal{D}\right)$ . In the insert-only setting, since all deltas are positive, we ignore the signs and see a single-tuple update as just a tuple, ${\delta R}$ as just a relation,and so on.

当上下文明确时间$\tau$时，我们省略时间标注($\tau$)而简写为$R,{\delta R},\mathcal{D},\delta \mathcal{D},Q\left( \mathcal{D}\right)$和${\delta Q}\left( \mathcal{D}\right)$。在仅插入场景中，由于所有增量均为正值，我们忽略符号并将单元素组更新视作元组${\delta R}$，关系也作类似简化处理。

Constant-Delay Enumeration. Given a query $Q$ and a database $\mathcal{D}$ ,an enumeration procedure for $Q\left( \mathcal{D}\right)$ (or ${\delta }_{\tau }Q\left( \mathcal{D}\right)$ ) outputs the elements in $Q\left( \mathcal{D}\right)$ (or ${\delta }_{\tau }Q\left( \mathcal{D}\right)$ ) one by one in some order and without repetition. The enumeration delay of the procedure is the maximum of three times: the time between the start of the enumeration process and the output of the first element, the time between outputting any two consecutive elements, and the time between outputting the last element and the end of enumeration [13]. The enumeration delay is constant if it does not depend on $\left| \mathcal{D}\right|$ . Width Measures. Consider the following linear program for a query $Q$ and a database $\mathcal{D}$ :

恒定延迟枚举。给定查询$Q$和数据库$\mathcal{D}$，针对$Q\left( \mathcal{D}\right)$（或${\delta }_{\tau }Q\left( \mathcal{D}\right)$）的枚举过程会按某种顺序无重复地逐个输出$Q\left( \mathcal{D}\right)$（或${\delta }_{\tau }Q\left( \mathcal{D}\right)$）中的元素。该过程的枚举延迟是以下三个时间中的最大值：枚举过程开始到输出首个元素之间的时间、输出任意两个连续元素之间的时间、以及输出最后一个元素到枚举结束之间的时间[13]。若该延迟不依赖于$\left| \mathcal{D}\right|$，则称其为恒定延迟。宽度度量。考虑以下针对查询$Q$和数据库$\mathcal{D}$的线性规划：

$$
\min \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{R\left( X\right)  \in  \operatorname{at}\left( Q\right) }}\log \left( \left| R\right| \right)  \cdot  {\lambda }_{R\left( X\right) }
$$

$$
\text{s.t.}\;\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{R\left( \mathbf{X}\right)  \in  \operatorname{at}\left( Q\right) \text{ s.t. }Y \in  \mathbf{X}}}{\lambda }_{R\left( \mathbf{X}\right) } \geq  1
$$

for all $Y \in  \operatorname{vars}\left( Q\right)$ and

对所有$Y \in  \operatorname{vars}\left( Q\right)$且

$$
{\lambda }_{R\left( X\right) } \in  \left\lbrack  {0,1}\right\rbrack  
$$

for all $R\left( \mathbf{X}\right)  \in  \operatorname{at}\left( Q\right)$

对所有$R\left( \mathbf{X}\right)  \in  \operatorname{at}\left( Q\right)$

Every feasible solution ${\left( {\lambda }_{R\left( X\right) }\right) }_{R\left( X\right)  \in  \operatorname{at}\left( Q\right) }$ to the above program is called a fractional edge cover of $Q$ with respect to $\mathcal{D}$ . The optimal objective value of the program is called the fractional edge cover number ${\rho }^{ * }\left( {Q,\mathcal{D}}\right)$ of $Q$ with respect to $\mathcal{D}$ . The fractional edge cover number gives an upper bound on the size of $Q\left( \mathcal{D}\right)  : \left| {Q\left( \mathcal{D}\right) }\right|  \leq  {2}^{{\rho }^{ * }\left( {Q,\mathcal{D}}\right) }$ [7]. This bound is known as the ${AGM}$ -bound and denoted as $\operatorname{AGM}\left( {Q,D}\right)$ . We denote by ${\rho }^{ * }\left( Q\right)$ the optimal objective value of the linear program that results from the above program by replacing the objective function with $\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{R\left( X\right)  \in  \operatorname{at}\left( Q\right) }}{\lambda }_{R\left( X\right) }$ .

上述规划的任何可行解${\left( {\lambda }_{R\left( X\right) }\right) }_{R\left( X\right)  \in  \operatorname{at}\left( Q\right) }$称为$\mathcal{D}$关于$Q$的分数边覆盖。该规划的最优目标值称为$\mathcal{D}$关于$Q$的分数边覆盖数${\rho }^{ * }\left( {Q,\mathcal{D}}\right)$。该分数边覆盖数给出了$Q\left( \mathcal{D}\right)  : \left| {Q\left( \mathcal{D}\right) }\right|  \leq  {2}^{{\rho }^{ * }\left( {Q,\mathcal{D}}\right) }$大小的上界[7]，此界被称为${AGM}$界并记作$\operatorname{AGM}\left( {Q,D}\right)$。我们将上述规划目标函数替换为$\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{R\left( X\right)  \in  \operatorname{at}\left( Q\right) }}{\lambda }_{R\left( X\right) }$后，所得线性规划的最优目标值记作${\rho }^{ * }\left( Q\right)$。

Definition 2.1 (Tree Decomposition). A tree decomposition of a query $Q$ is a pair $\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)$ ,where $\mathcal{T}$ is a tree with vertices $V\left( \mathcal{T}\right)$ and $\chi  : V\left( \mathcal{T}\right)  \rightarrow  {2}^{\operatorname{vars}\left( Q\right) }$ maps each node $t$ of the tree $\mathcal{T}$ to a subset $\chi \left( t\right)$ of variables of $Q$ such that the following properties hold:

定义2.1（树分解）。查询$Q$的树分解是个二元组$\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)$，其中$\mathcal{T}$是顶点集为$V\left( \mathcal{T}\right)$的树，$\chi  : V\left( \mathcal{T}\right)  \rightarrow  {2}^{\operatorname{vars}\left( Q\right) }$将树$\mathcal{T}$的每个节点$t$映射到$Q$变量的子集$\chi \left( t\right)$，且满足以下性质：

(1) for every atom $R\left( X\right)  \in  \operatorname{at}\left( Q\right)$ ,the schema $X$ is a subset of $\chi \left( t\right)$ for some $t \in  V\left( \mathcal{T}\right)$ ,and

(1) 对每个原子$R\left( X\right)  \in  \operatorname{at}\left( Q\right)$，其模式$X$是某个$t \in  V\left( \mathcal{T}\right)$对应的$\chi \left( t\right)$的子集；且

(2) for every variable $X \in  \operatorname{vars}\left( Q\right)$ ,the set $\{ t \mid  X \in  \chi \left( t\right) \}$ is a non-empty connected subtree of $\mathcal{T}$ . The sets $\chi \left( t\right)$ are called the bags of the tree decomposition.

(2) 对每个变量$X \in  \operatorname{vars}\left( Q\right)$，集合$\{ t \mid  X \in  \chi \left( t\right) \}$构成$\mathcal{T}$的非空连通子树。集合$\chi \left( t\right)$称为树分解的包。

We use $\operatorname{TD}\left( Q\right)$ to denote the set of tree decompositions of a query $Q$ .

我们用$\operatorname{TD}\left( Q\right)$表示查询$Q$的所有树分解集合。

Definition 2.2 (The restriction ${Q}_{Y}$ of a query $Q$ ). For a query $Q$ and a subset $Y \subseteq  \operatorname{vars}\left( Q\right)$ ,we define the restriction of $Q$ to $Y$ ,denoted by ${Q}_{Y}$ ,to be the query that results from $Q$ by restricting the schema of each atom to those variables that appear in $Y$ . Formally:

定义2.2（查询$Q$的限制${Q}_{Y}$）。对于查询$Q$和子集$Y \subseteq  \operatorname{vars}\left( Q\right)$，我们将$Q$对$Y$的限制（记作${Q}_{Y}$）定义为：通过将每个原子的模式限制为仅包含$Y$中出现的变量，从$Q$得到的查询。形式化表述如下：

$$
{R}_{\mathbf{Y}}\left( {\mathbf{X} \cap  \mathbf{Y}}\right) \overset{\text{ def }}{ = }{\pi }_{\mathbf{X} \cap  \mathbf{Y}}R\left( \mathbf{X}\right) ,\;\text{ for all }R\left( \mathbf{X}\right)  \in  \operatorname{at}\left( Q\right) 
$$

$$
{Q}_{Y}\left( Y\right) \overset{\text{ def }}{ = }\mathop{\bigwedge }\limits_{{R\left( X\right)  \in  \operatorname{at}\left( Q\right) }}{R}_{Y}\left( {X \cap  Y}\right)  \tag{4}
$$

Definition 2.3 (Fractional Hypertree Width). Given a query $Q$ and a tree decomposition $\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)$ of $Q$ ,the fractional hypertree width of $\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)$ and of $Q$ are defined respectively as follows:

定义2.3（分数超树宽度）。给定查询$Q$及其树分解$\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)$，$\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)$和$Q$的分数超树宽度分别定义如下：

$$
\mathrm{w}\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right) \overset{\text{ def }}{ = }\mathop{\max }\limits_{{t \in  V\left( \mathcal{T}\right) }}{\rho }^{ * }\left( {Q}_{\chi \left( t\right) }\right) ,\;\mathrm{w}\left( Q\right) \overset{\text{ def }}{ = }\mathop{\min }\limits_{{\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)  \in  \operatorname{TD}\left( Q\right) }}\mathrm{w}\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)  \tag{5}
$$

We call a tree decomposition $\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)$ of $Q$ optimal if $\mathrm{w}\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)  = \mathrm{w}\left( Q\right)$ . The fractional hypertree width of a union $Q =$ $\mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  n\right\rbrack  }}{Q}_{i}$ of join queries is $\mathrm{w}\left( Q\right) \overset{\text{ def }}{ = }\mathop{\max }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  n\right\rbrack  }}\mathrm{w}\left( {Q}_{i}\right)$ .

当$\mathrm{w}\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)  = \mathrm{w}\left( Q\right)$时，我们称$Q$的树分解$\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)$为最优。连接查询的并集$Q =$$\mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  n\right\rbrack  }}{Q}_{i}$的分数超树宽度为$\mathrm{w}\left( Q\right) \overset{\text{ def }}{ = }\mathop{\max }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  n\right\rbrack  }}\mathrm{w}\left( {Q}_{i}\right)$。

If a query is a union of join queries, its AGM-bound (or fractional hypertree width) is the maximum AGM-bound (or fractional hypertree width) over its components.

若某查询是连接查询的并集，则其AGM界（或分数超树宽度）等于各组成部分AGM界（或分数超树宽度）的最大值。

Computational Model. We consider the RAM model of computation where schemas and data values are stored in registers of logarithmic size and operations on them can be done in constant time. We assume that each materialized relation with schema $X$ is implemented by a data structure of size $O\left( \left| R\right| \right)$ that can: (1) look up,insert,and delete tuples in constant time,and (2) enumerate the tuples in $R$ with constant delay. Given $Y \subseteq  X$ and $t \in  \operatorname{Dom}\left( Y\right)$ ,the data structure can: (1) check $t \in  {\pi }_{Y}R$ in constant time; and (2) enumerate the tuples in ${\sigma }_{Y = t}R$ with constant delay.

计算模型。我们采用RAM计算模型，其中模式与数据值存储在具有对数尺寸的寄存器中，相关操作可在常数时间内完成。假设每个具现化关系$X$通过尺寸为$O\left( \left| R\right| \right)$的数据结构实现，该结构能够：(1)在常数时间内查找、插入和删除元组；(2)以恒定延迟枚举$R$中的元组。给定$Y \subseteq  X$和$t \in  \operatorname{Dom}\left( Y\right)$，该数据结构可以：(1)在常数时间内验证$t \in  {\pi }_{Y}R$；(2)以恒定延迟枚举${\sigma }_{Y = t}R$中的元组。

## 3 PROBLEM SETTING

## 3 问题设定

We consider natural join queries, or equivalently, full conjunctive queries and refer to them as queries. We investigate the incremental view maintenance (IVM) of a query $Q$ under single-tuple updates to an initially empty database $\mathcal{D}$ . A single-tuple update is an insert or a delete of a tuple into a relation in $\mathcal{D}$ . We consider four variants of the IVM problem depending on the update and enumeration mode. With regard to updates, we distinguish between the insert-only setting, where we only allow single-tuple inserts, and the insert-delete setting, where we allow both single-tuple inserts and single-tuple deletes. With regard to enumeration, we distinguish between the full enumeration and the delta enumeration, where, after each update, the full query result and respectively the change to the query result at the current time can be enumerated with constant delay. The four IVM variants are parameterized by a query $Q$ and take as input a database $\mathcal{D}$ ,whose relations are initially empty,and a stream ${\delta }_{1}\mathcal{D},{\delta }_{2}\mathcal{D},\ldots ,{\delta }_{N}\mathcal{D}$ of $N$ single-tuple updates to $\mathcal{D}$ . Neither $N$ nor the updates are known in advance. The updates arrive one by one. Let ${\mathcal{D}}^{\left( 0\right) }\overset{\text{ def }}{ = }\varnothing ,{\mathcal{D}}^{\left( 1\right) },\ldots ,{\mathcal{D}}^{\left( N\right) }$ be the sequence of database versions,where for each $\tau  \in  \left\lbrack  N\right\rbrack$ ,the database version ${\mathcal{D}}^{\left( \tau \right) }$ results from applying the update ${\delta }_{\tau }\mathcal{D}$ to the previous version ${\mathcal{D}}^{\left( \tau  - 1\right) }$ .

我们研究自然连接查询（即完全合取查询），并将其统称为查询。针对初始为空数据库$\mathcal{D}$的单元组更新场景，我们探讨查询$Q$的增量视图维护（IVM）问题。单元组更新指对$\mathcal{D}$中关系进行元组插入或删除操作。根据更新和枚举模式的不同，我们考虑IVM问题的四种变体：在更新方面，区分仅允许插入的纯插入模式与允许插入删除的混合模式；在枚举方面，区分完整结果枚举与增量枚举——前者要求每次更新后能以恒定延迟枚举完整查询结果，后者则枚举当前时刻的查询结果变化量。这四种IVM变体以查询$Q$为参数，输入初始为空数据库$\mathcal{D}$及$N$个$\mathcal{D}$的单元组更新流${\delta }_{1}\mathcal{D},{\delta }_{2}\mathcal{D},\ldots ,{\delta }_{N}\mathcal{D}$，其中$N$和更新内容均未知。更新按序到达时，形成数据库版本序列${\mathcal{D}}^{\left( 0\right) }\overset{\text{ def }}{ = }\varnothing ,{\mathcal{D}}^{\left( 1\right) },\ldots ,{\mathcal{D}}^{\left( N\right) }$，其中每个版本${\mathcal{D}}^{\left( \tau \right) }$由前驱版本${\mathcal{D}}^{\left( \tau  - 1\right) }$应用更新${\delta }_{\tau }\mathcal{D}$生成。

<!-- Media -->

The task of the first IVM variant is to support constant-delay enumeration of the full query result $Q\left( {\mathcal{D}}^{\left( \tau \right) }\right)$ after each update: Problem: $\;{\mathrm{{IVM}}}^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$ Parameter: Query $Q$ Given: An initially empty database $\mathcal{D}$ and a stream ${\delta }_{1}\mathcal{D},\ldots ,{\delta }_{N}\mathcal{D}$ of $N$ single-tuple updates to $\mathcal{D}$ where $N$ is not known in advance. Task: Support constant-delay enumeration of $Q\left( {\mathcal{D}}^{\left( \tau \right) }\right)$ after each update ${\delta }_{\tau }\mathcal{D}$

第一种IVM变体的任务是在每次更新后支持完整查询结果$Q\left( {\mathcal{D}}^{\left( \tau \right) }\right)$的恒定延迟枚举：问题：$\;{\mathrm{{IVM}}}^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$参数：查询$Q$给定：初始为空数据库$\mathcal{D}$及未知数量$N$的单元组更新流${\delta }_{1}\mathcal{D},\ldots ,{\delta }_{N}\mathcal{D}$任务：支持每次更新${\delta }_{\tau }\mathcal{D}$后对$Q\left( {\mathcal{D}}^{\left( \tau \right) }\right)$进行恒定延迟枚举

The task of the second IVM variant is to support constant-delay enumeration of the change to the query result after each update, given by Eq. (3):

第二种IVM变体的任务是根据公式(3)，在每次更新后支持查询结果变化量的恒定延迟枚举：

<table><tbody><tr><td>问题：</td><td>${\mathrm{{IVM}}}_{\delta }^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$</td></tr><tr><td>参数：</td><td>查询 $Q$</td></tr><tr><td>已知条件：</td><td>初始为空的数据库$\mathcal{D}$及一个更新流${\delta }_{1}\mathcal{D},\ldots ,{\delta }_{N}\mathcal{D}$，该流包含$N$次对$\mathcal{D}$的单元组更新，其中$N$为未知量。</td></tr><tr><td>任务：</td><td>支持在每次更新${\delta }_{\tau }\mathcal{D}$后对${\delta }_{\tau }Q\left( \mathcal{D}\right)$进行恒定延迟枚举</td></tr></tbody></table>

---

The other two variants,denoted as ${\mathrm{{IVM}}}^{ + }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$ and ${\mathrm{{IVM}}}_{\delta }^{ + }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$ ,are identical to ${\mathrm{{IVM}}}^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$ and ${\mathrm{{IVM}}}_{\delta }^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$ ,respectively, except that the updates ${\delta }_{\tau }\mathcal{D}$ are restricted to be single-tuple inserts (no deletes are allowed). In all four variants,we are interested in the update time, i.e., the time to process a single-tuple update.

另外两种变体，标记为${\mathrm{{IVM}}}^{ + }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$和${\mathrm{{IVM}}}_{\delta }^{ + }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$，分别与${\mathrm{{IVM}}}^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$和${\mathrm{{IVM}}}_{\delta }^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$相同，唯一的区别在于更新操作${\delta }_{\tau }\mathcal{D}$被限制为单行插入（不允许删除操作）。在这四种变体中，我们重点关注更新时间，即处理单行更新所需的时间。

Example 3.1. Consider the triangle query ${Q}_{\bigtriangleup }$ from Eq. (1). Suppose we have the update stream of length 8 given in the second column of Table 1. The third and fourth column show the full result and the delta result, respectively, after each update. We will use this example as a running example throughout the paper.

示例3.1 考虑公式(1)中的三角形查询${Q}_{\bigtriangleup }$。假设我们拥有表1第二列给出的长度为8的更新流。第三列和第四列分别展示了每次更新后的完整结果集与增量结果集。本文将以此为例贯穿全文进行说明。

We obtain lower bounds on the update time for our IVM problems by reductions from the following static query evaluation problem:

我们通过将以下静态查询评估问题归约，获得了IVM问题更新时间的下界：

---

	Problem: $\;\operatorname{Eval}\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$

	问题：$\;\operatorname{Eval}\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$

		Parameter: Query $Q$

		参数：查询$Q$

Given: Database $\mathcal{D}$

给定：数据库$\mathcal{D}$

Task: Compute $Q\left( \mathcal{D}\right)$

任务：计算$Q\left( \mathcal{D}\right)$

---

<!-- Media -->

For this problem,we are interested in the time to compute $Q\left( \mathcal{D}\right)$ .

针对该问题，我们关注计算$Q\left( \mathcal{D}\right)$所需的时间。

Definition 3.2 (w(Q) and ω(Q)). Given a query $Q$ ,let w(Q) denote the fractional hypertree width of $Q$ (see Section 2). Let $\omega \left( Q\right)$ denote the smallest exponent $\kappa$ such that $\operatorname{Eval}\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$ has an algorithm with runtime $O\left( {{\left| \mathcal{D}\right| }^{\kappa  + o\left( 1\right) } + \left| {Q\left( \mathcal{D}\right) }\right| }\right)$ on any input database $\mathcal{D}$ . Given a union of queries $Q = \mathop{\bigvee }\limits_{i}{Q}_{i}$ ,we define $\mathrm{w}\left( Q\right)$ and $\omega \left( Q\right)$ to be the maximum of $\mathrm{w}\left( {Q}_{i}\right)$ and $\omega \left( {Q}_{i}\right)$ ,respectively,over all $i$ .

定义3.2（w(Q)与ω(Q)）。给定查询$Q$，令w(Q)表示$Q$的分数超树宽度（参见第2节）。令$\omega \left( Q\right)$表示最小指数$\kappa$，使得$\operatorname{Eval}\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$在任何输入数据库$\mathcal{D}$上具有$O\left( {{\left| \mathcal{D}\right| }^{\kappa  + o\left( 1\right) } + \left| {Q\left( \mathcal{D}\right) }\right| }\right)$运行时间的算法。对于查询并集$Q = \mathop{\bigvee }\limits_{i}{Q}_{i}$，我们将$\mathrm{w}\left( Q\right)$和$\omega \left( Q\right)$分别定义为所有$i$中$\mathrm{w}\left( {Q}_{i}\right)$与$\omega \left( {Q}_{i}\right)$的最大值。

The function $\omega \left( Q\right)$ is not known in general. However,we know that $\omega \left( Q\right)  \leq  w\left( Q\right)$ ; see e.g. [4]. More strongly, $\omega \left( Q\right)$ is upper bounded by the submodular width of $Q{\left\lbrack  5,6,{30}\right\rbrack  }^{2}$ . For acyclic queries,we have $\omega \left( Q\right)  = 1$ [45]. For the triangle query, $\omega \left( {Q}_{\Delta }\right)  \geq  \frac{4}{3}$ modulo the $3\mathrm{{SUM}}$ conjecture [38]. In this paper,we prove lower bounds on the IVM problems in terms of the function $\omega \left( Q\right)$ . These lower bounds are not conditioned on a fine-grained complexity conjecture,like the OMv conjecture [16] stated in Appendix B. Instead,they apply no matter what the value of $\omega \left( Q\right)$ turns out to be. In that sense, they can be thought of as unconditional. However, they are relatively straightforward to prove and are mostly meant to justify the matching upper bounds.

函数$\omega \left( Q\right)$的通用表达式尚未明确，但已知$\omega \left( Q\right)  \leq  w\left( Q\right)$（参见文献[4]）。更严格地说，$\omega \left( Q\right)$的上界受$Q{\left\lbrack  5,6,{30}\right\rbrack  }^{2}$的子模宽度限制。对于无环查询，我们有$\omega \left( Q\right)  = 1$[45]。对于三角形查询，$\omega \left( {Q}_{\Delta }\right)  \geq  \frac{4}{3}$取决于$3\mathrm{{SUM}}$猜想[38]。本文中，我们根据函数$\omega \left( Q\right)$证明了IVM问题的下界。这些下界不受细粒度复杂性猜想（如附录B所述的OMv猜想[16]）约束，无论$\omega \left( Q\right)$的具体值如何都成立。在这个意义上，它们可被视为无条件结果。不过这些证明相对直接，主要用于佐证匹配上界的合理性。

---

<!-- Footnote -->

${}^{1}$ The function $\omega \left( Q\right)$ is defined analogously to the constant $\omega$ which is the best exponent for matrix multiplication.

${}^{1}$ 函数$\omega \left( Q\right)$的定义类似于矩阵乘法最优指数常数$\omega$。

${}^{2}\left\lbrack  {5,6}\right\rbrack$ provide a query evaluation algorithm with runtime $O\left( {{\left| \mathcal{D}\right| }^{\operatorname{subw}\left( Q\right) } \cdot  \operatorname{polylog}\left| \mathcal{D}\right|  + \left| {Q\left( \mathcal{D}\right) }\right| }\right)  = O\left( {{\left| \mathcal{D}\right| }^{\operatorname{subw}\left( Q\right)  + o\left( 1\right) } + \left| {Q\left( \mathcal{D}\right) }\right| }\right)$ ,thanks to the extra $+ o\left( 1\right)$ in the exponent. $\operatorname{subw}\left( Q\right)$ denotes the submodular width of $Q$ .

${}^{2}\left\lbrack  {5,6}\right\rbrack$借助指数中额外的$+ o\left( 1\right)$因子，提供了具有$O\left( {{\left| \mathcal{D}\right| }^{\operatorname{subw}\left( Q\right) } \cdot  \operatorname{polylog}\left| \mathcal{D}\right|  + \left| {Q\left( \mathcal{D}\right) }\right| }\right)  = O\left( {{\left| \mathcal{D}\right| }^{\operatorname{subw}\left( Q\right)  + o\left( 1\right) } + \left| {Q\left( \mathcal{D}\right) }\right| }\right)$运行时间的查询评估算法。$\operatorname{subw}\left( Q\right)$表示$Q$的子模宽度。

<!-- Footnote -->

---

Complexity Measures. For all problems introduced above, we consider the query to be fixed. Moreover, we say that the amortized update time for the $\tau$ -th single-tuple update is $f\left( {N,\left| {\mathcal{D}}^{\left( \tau \right) }\right| }\right)$ for some function $f$ ,if the total time to process all $N$ updates in the stream is upper bounded by $\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{\tau  \in  \left\lbrack  N\right\rbrack  }}f\left( {N,\left| {\mathcal{D}}^{\left( \tau \right) }\right| }\right)$ . In the insert-only setting,the database size $\left| {\mathcal{D}}^{\left( \tau \right) }\right|$ at time $\tau$ is always equal to $\tau$ . Hence,for ${\mathrm{{IVM}}}^{ + }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$ and ${\mathrm{{IVM}}}_{\delta }^{ + }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$ ,we ignore the database size and only measure the update time as a function of the length $N$ of the update stream. In the insert-delete setting,the database size at time $\tau$ is upper bounded by $\tau$ and could be much smaller than $\tau$ . As a result,for ${\mathrm{{IVM}}}^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$ and ${\mathrm{{IVM}}}_{\delta }^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$ ,it is more natural to ignore $N$ and only measure the update time as a function of $\left| {\mathcal{D}}^{\left( \tau \right) }\right|$ . For the four IVM problems,the constant enumeration delay is not allowed to be amortized, regardless of whether the update time is amortized or not. For the problem Eval,we measure the computation time as a function of the size of the input database $\mathcal{D}$ . We use the $O$ -notation to state worst-case bounds and $\widetilde{O}$ -notation to hide a polylogarithmic factor in $N,\left| {\mathcal{D}}^{\left( \tau \right) }\right|$ ,or $\left| \mathcal{D}\right|$ .

复杂度度量。针对上述所有问题，我们假定查询是固定的。此外，若处理流中$N$次更新的总时间不超过$\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{\tau  \in  \left\lbrack  N\right\rbrack  }}f\left( {N,\left| {\mathcal{D}}^{\left( \tau \right) }\right| }\right)$，则称第$\tau$次单元组更新的摊还时间为$f\left( {N,\left| {\mathcal{D}}^{\left( \tau \right) }\right| }\right)$（其中$f$为某函数）。在纯插入场景中，时刻$\tau$的数据库规模$\left| {\mathcal{D}}^{\left( \tau \right) }\right|$恒等于$\tau$。因此对于${\mathrm{{IVM}}}^{ + }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$和${\mathrm{{IVM}}}_{\delta }^{ + }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$，我们忽略数据库规模，仅将更新时间作为更新流长度$N$的函数进行度量。在插入-删除场景中，时刻$\tau$的数据库规模上限为$\tau$且可能远小于该值。故对于${\mathrm{{IVM}}}^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$和${\mathrm{{IVM}}}_{\delta }^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$，忽略$N$而将更新时间作为$\left| {\mathcal{D}}^{\left( \tau \right) }\right|$的函数更为合理。对于四个IVM问题，无论更新时间是否摊还，恒定枚举延迟都不允许被摊还。对于Eval问题，我们将计算时间作为输入数据库$\mathcal{D}$规模的函数进行度量。使用$O$符号表示最坏情况界，$\widetilde{O}$符号用于隐藏$N,\left| {\mathcal{D}}^{\left( \tau \right) }\right|$或$\left| \mathcal{D}\right|$中的多对数因子。

Multivariate Extensions. For the purpose of stating our results, we introduce the following central concept, which gives our approach its name, MultiVariate IVM (MVIVM for short).

多元扩展。为阐述研究结果，我们引入以下核心概念，该方法因此得名多元IVM（简称MVIVM）。

Definition 3.3 (Multivariate extension $\widehat{Q}$ of a query $Q$ ). Consider a query $Q\left( \mathbf{X}\right)  = {R}_{1}\left( {\mathbf{X}}_{1}\right)  \land  \ldots  \land  {R}_{k}\left( {\mathbf{X}}_{k}\right)$ where $\mathbf{X} =$ $\mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack  }}{X}_{i}$ ,and fresh variables ${Z}_{1},\ldots ,{Z}_{k}$ that do not occur in $Q$ . (In this paper,we drop head variables $\mathbf{X}$ and write $Q$ instead of $Q\left( \mathbf{X}\right)$ for brevity.) Let ${\sum }_{k}$ be the set of permutations of the set $\left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack$ . For any permutation $\mathbf{\sigma } = \left( {{\sigma }_{1},\ldots ,{\sigma }_{k}}\right)  \in  {\sum }_{k}$ , we denote by ${\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }$ the query that results from $Q$ by extending the schema of each atom ${R}_{{\sigma }_{i}}\left( {X}_{{\sigma }_{i}}\right)$ with the variables ${Z}_{1},\ldots ,{Z}_{i}$ :

定义3.3（查询$Q$的多元扩展$\widehat{Q}$）。考虑查询$Q\left( \mathbf{X}\right)  = {R}_{1}\left( {\mathbf{X}}_{1}\right)  \land  \ldots  \land  {R}_{k}\left( {\mathbf{X}}_{k}\right)$，其中$\mathbf{X} =$$\mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack  }}{X}_{i}$，以及未出现在$Q$中的新变量${Z}_{1},\ldots ,{Z}_{k}$。（本文为简洁省略头部变量$\mathbf{X}$，直接写作$Q$而非$Q\left( \mathbf{X}\right)$。）令${\sum }_{k}$表示集合$\left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack$的排列组合。对于任意排列$\mathbf{\sigma } = \left( {{\sigma }_{1},\ldots ,{\sigma }_{k}}\right)  \in  {\sum }_{k}$，通过为每个原子${R}_{{\sigma }_{i}}\left( {X}_{{\sigma }_{i}}\right)$的模式扩展变量${Z}_{1},\ldots ,{Z}_{i}$，从$Q$派生出的查询记为${\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }$：

$$
{\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma } = {\widehat{R}}_{{\sigma }_{1}}\left( {{Z}_{1},{\mathbf{X}}_{{\sigma }_{1}}}\right)  \land  {\widehat{R}}_{{\sigma }_{2}}\left( {{Z}_{1},{Z}_{2},{\mathbf{X}}_{{\sigma }_{2}}}\right)  \land  \cdots  \land  {\widehat{R}}_{{\sigma }_{k}}\left( {{Z}_{1},\ldots ,{Z}_{k},{\mathbf{X}}_{{\sigma }_{k}}}\right)  \tag{6}
$$

The multivariate extension $\widehat{Q}$ of $Q$ is the union of ${\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }$ over all permutations $\sigma  \in  {\sum }_{k}$ :

多元扩展$\widehat{Q}$是$Q$在所有排列$\sigma  \in  {\sum }_{k}$上的${\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }$并集：

$$
\widehat{Q} = \mathop{\bigvee }\limits_{{\sigma  \in  {\sum }_{k}}}{\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma } \tag{7}
$$

We call each ${\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }$ a component of $\widehat{Q}$ .

我们将每个${\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }$称为$\widehat{Q}$的一个分量。

Example 3.4. The multivariate extension ${\widehat{Q}}_{\Delta }$ of ${Q}_{\Delta }$ from Eq. (1) consists of 6 components ${\widehat{Q}}_{123},{\widehat{Q}}_{132},{\widehat{Q}}_{213},{\widehat{Q}}_{231},{\widehat{Q}}_{312}$ and ${\widehat{Q}}_{321}$ . The first component, ${\widehat{Q}}_{123}$ ,orders the input relations as(R,S,T)and applies Eq. (6) by adding $\left\{  {Z}_{1}\right\}$ to $R$ , $\left\{  {{Z}_{1},{Z}_{2}}\right\}$ to $S$ ,and $\left\{  {{Z}_{1},{Z}_{2},{Z}_{3}}\right\}$ to $T$ ,thus resulting in $\widehat{R}\left( {{Z}_{1},A,B}\right) ,\widehat{S}\left( {{Z}_{1},{Z}_{2},B,C}\right)$ ,and $\widehat{T}\left( {{Z}_{1},{Z}_{2},{Z}_{3},A,C}\right)$ respectively. Below, we add the subscript 123 to each relation to indicate the component for later convenience:

示例3.4 方程(1)中${Q}_{\Delta }$的多元扩展${\widehat{Q}}_{\Delta }$包含6个分量${\widehat{Q}}_{123},{\widehat{Q}}_{132},{\widehat{Q}}_{213},{\widehat{Q}}_{231},{\widehat{Q}}_{312}$和${\widehat{Q}}_{321}$。第一个分量${\widehat{Q}}_{123}$将输入关系排序为(R,S,T)，并通过向$R$添加$\left\{  {Z}_{1}\right\}$、向$S$添加$\left\{  {{Z}_{1},{Z}_{2}}\right\}$、向$T$添加$\left\{  {{Z}_{1},{Z}_{2},{Z}_{3}}\right\}$来应用方程(6)，从而分别得到$\widehat{R}\left( {{Z}_{1},A,B}\right) ,\widehat{S}\left( {{Z}_{1},{Z}_{2},B,C}\right)$和$\widehat{T}\left( {{Z}_{1},{Z}_{2},{Z}_{3},A,C}\right)$。下文为便于后续引用，我们在每个关系后添加下标123以标识分量：

$$
{\widehat{Q}}_{123} = {\widehat{R}}_{123}\left( {{Z}_{1},A,B}\right)  \land  {\widehat{S}}_{123}\left( {{Z}_{1},{Z}_{2},B,C}\right)  \land  {\widehat{T}}_{123}\left( {{Z}_{1},{Z}_{2},{Z}_{3},A,C}\right)  \tag{8}
$$

The other components are given in Eq. (39).

其余分量见方程(39)。

## 4 OVERVIEW OF MAIN RESULTS

## 4 主要结果概述

In this section, we overview our main results and discuss their implications.

本节将概述我们的主要研究成果并讨论其意义。

### 4.1 Insert-Only Setting

### 4.1 只插入场景

In the insert-only setting, we show that off-the-shelf worst-case optimal join algorithms, e.g., LeapFrogTrieJoin [42], can be used to achieve the best known amortized update time for the dynamic query evaluation of arbitrary join queries, so including the cyclic queries, while supporting constant delay for full/delta enumeration. Specifically, we give an upper bound on the single-tuple update time in terms of the fractional hypertree width $\mathrm{w}\left( Q\right)$ of $Q$ :

在只插入场景中，我们证明现成的最坏情况最优连接算法（如LeapFrogTrieJoin[42]）可用于实现任意连接查询动态求值的最佳已知分摊更新时间，包括循环查询，同时支持完整/增量枚举的常数延迟。具体而言，我们根据$Q$的分数超树宽度$\mathrm{w}\left( Q\right)$给出单次元组更新的时间上界：

THEOREM 4.1. For any query $Q$ ,both ${\mathrm{{IVM}}}^{ + }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$ and ${\mathrm{{IVM}}}_{\delta }^{ + }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$ can be solved with $O\left( {N}^{\mathrm{w}\left( Q\right)  - 1}\right)$ amortized update time and non-amortized constant enumeration delay,where $N$ is the number of single-tuple inserts.

定理4.1 对于任何查询$Q$，${\mathrm{{IVM}}}^{ + }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$和${\mathrm{{IVM}}}_{\delta }^{ + }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$都能以$O\left( {N}^{\mathrm{w}\left( Q\right)  - 1}\right)$的分摊更新时间和非分摊常数枚举延迟求解，其中$N$表示单次元组插入次数。

The upper bound in Theorem 4.1 is met by our algorithm outlined in Sec. 5. It uses worst-case optimal join algorithms and tree decompositions. Amortization is necessary as some inserts may be costly, while many others are necessarily relatively cheaper, so the average insert time matches the cost of computing a factorized representation of the query result divided by the number of inserts.

第5节所述算法达到了定理4.1中的上界。该算法采用最坏情况最优连接算法和树分解技术。由于某些插入操作可能代价高昂，而其他许多插入必然相对廉价，因此需要分摊处理，使得平均插入时间等于查询结果的因子化表示计算成本除以插入次数。

Since every acyclic query has a fractional hypertree width of one, Theorem 4.1 implies that every acyclic query can be maintained with amortized constant time per insert:

由于所有无环查询的分数超树宽度均为1，定理4.1表明每个无环查询都能以每次插入的分摊常数时间维护：

Corollary 4.2 (THEOREM 4.1). For any acyclic query $Q,{IV}{M}^{ + }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$ and ${IV}{M}_{\delta }^{ + }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$ can be solved with $O\left( 1\right)$ amortized update time and non-amortized constant enumeration delay.

推论4.2（定理4.1） 对于任何无环查询$Q,{IV}{M}^{ + }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$，${IV}{M}_{\delta }^{ + }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$可通过$O\left( 1\right)$的分摊更新时间和非分摊常数枚举延迟求解。

Corollary 4.2 recovers the amortized constant update time for acyclic joins from prior work [43]. It also shows that the insert-only setting can be computationally cheaper than the insert-delete setting investigated in prior work [8]: In the insert-delete setting,the non-hierarchical acyclic queries cannot admit $O\left( {\left| \mathcal{D}\right| }^{1/2 - \gamma }\right)$ update time (while keeping the enumeration delay constant) for any database $\mathcal{D}$ and $\gamma  > 0$ [8],conditioned on the OMv conjecture.

推论4.2重现了先前研究[43]中无环连接的摊销常数更新时间。同时表明，仅插入场景的计算成本可能低于先前研究[8]探讨的插入-删除场景：在插入-删除场景下，根据OMv猜想，非层次化无环查询无法对任何数据库$\mathcal{D}$和$\gamma  > 0$实现$O\left( {\left| \mathcal{D}\right| }^{1/2 - \gamma }\right)$更新时间（同时保持枚举延迟恒定）。

We accompany the upper bound from Theorem 4.1 with a lower bound on the insertion time in terms of the lower bound $\omega \left( Q\right)$ on the static evaluation of $Q$ (Definition 3.2). Unlike prior lower bounds $\left\lbrack  {8,{20}}\right\rbrack$ ,this lower bound is not conditioned on a fine-grained complexity conjecture, such as the OMv conjecture [16]. However, its proof is straightforward by viewing static evaluation as a stream of inserts. It is meant to show that Theorem 4.1 is optimal up to the gap between $\mathrm{w}\left( Q\right)$ and $\omega \left( Q\right) .{}^{3}$ It also applies to both amortized and non-amortized update time and enumeration delay. ${}^{4}$

我们在定理4.1的上界基础上，结合$Q$静态评估下界$\omega \left( Q\right)$（定义3.2），提出了插入时间的下界。与先前依赖细粒度复杂性猜想（如OMv猜想[16]）的下界$\left\lbrack  {8,{20}}\right\rbrack$不同，该下界通过将静态评估视为插入流即可直接证明。其旨在表明定理4.1在$\mathrm{w}\left( Q\right)$与$\omega \left( Q\right) .{}^{3}$之间的差距范围内已达到最优。该结论同时适用于摊销/非摊销更新时间及枚举延迟。${}^{4}$

Proposition 4.3. For any query $Q$ and any constant $\gamma  > 0$ ,neither ${\mathrm{{IVM}}}^{ + }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$ nor ${\mathrm{{IVM}}}_{\delta }^{ + }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$ can be solved with $\widetilde{O}\left( {N}^{\omega \left( Q\right)  - 1 - \gamma }\right)$ (amortized) update time and (non-amortized) constant enumeration delay.

命题4.3 对于任意查询$Q$和常数$\gamma  > 0$，既无法以$\widetilde{O}\left( {N}^{\omega \left( Q\right)  - 1 - \gamma }\right)$（摊销）更新时间和（非摊销）常数枚举延迟实现${\mathrm{{IVM}}}^{ + }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$，也无法实现${\mathrm{{IVM}}}_{\delta }^{ + }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$。

### 4.2 Insert-Delete Setting

### 4.2 插入-删除场景

In the insert-delete setting, our approach can maintain arbitrary join queries, and in particular any cyclic join query, with update times that can be asymptotically lower than recomputation. In particular, we give an upper bound on the update time for a query $Q$ in terms of the fractional hypertree width of the multivariate extension $\widehat{Q}$ of $Q$ (Definition 3.3):

在插入-删除场景中，我们的方法能维护任意连接查询（特别是循环连接查询），其更新时间渐近低于重新计算成本。具体而言，我们通过查询$Q$的多变量扩展$\widehat{Q}$（定义3.3）的分数超树宽度，给出了更新时间上界：

Theorem 4.4. For any query $Q$ ,both ${\operatorname{IVM}}^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$ and ${\operatorname{IVM}}_{\delta }^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$ can be solved with $\widetilde{O}\left( {\left| {\mathcal{D}}^{\left( \tau \right) }\right| }^{\mathrm{w}\left( \widehat{Q}\right)  - 1}\right)$ amortized update time and non-amortized constant enumeration delay,where $\widehat{Q}$ is the multivariate extension of $Q$ ,and $\left| {\mathcal{D}}^{\left( \tau \right) }\right|$ is the current database size at update time $\tau$ .

定理4.4 对于任意查询$Q$，当$\widehat{Q}$为$Q$的多变量扩展、$\left| {\mathcal{D}}^{\left( \tau \right) }\right|$为更新时刻$\tau$的当前数据库大小时，${\operatorname{IVM}}^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$和${\operatorname{IVM}}_{\delta }^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$均可通过$\widetilde{O}\left( {\left| {\mathcal{D}}^{\left( \tau \right) }\right| }^{\mathrm{w}\left( \widehat{Q}\right)  - 1}\right)$摊销更新时间和非摊销常数枚举延迟实现。

---

<!-- Footnote -->

${}^{3}$ More broadly,our lower bounds are meant to introduce a new framework to assess the optimality of algorithms in database theory,in terms of the function $\omega \left( Q\right)$ .

${}^{3}$更广泛地说，我们的下界旨在引入新框架，通过函数$\omega \left( Q\right)$评估数据库理论中算法的最优性。

${}^{4}$ Note that a lower bound on the amortized time also implies the same lower bound on the worst-case time. This is because if an amortized algorithm does not exist, then a worst-case algorithm does not exist either.

${}^{4}$需注意，摊销时间下界同样意味着最坏情况时间的相同下界。因为若不存在摊销算法，则最坏情况算法亦不存在。

<!-- Footnote -->

---

Sec. 7.1 overviews our algorithm that meets the upper bound in Theorem 4.4. It involves intersection joins (Sec. 6). The following statements shed light on the relationship between a query $Q$ and its multivariate extension $\widehat{Q}$ (their proofs can be found in Appendix F):

第7.1节概述了我们实现定理4.4上界的算法，其涉及交集连接（第6节）。以下陈述揭示了查询$Q$与其多变量扩展$\widehat{Q}$之间的关系（证明见附录F）：

- $Q$ is hierarchical if and only if its multivariate extension $\widehat{Q}$ is acyclic,or equivalently $\mathrm{w}\left( \widehat{Q}\right)  = 1$ .

- 当且仅当多变量扩展$\widehat{Q}$为无环（等价于$\mathrm{w}\left( \widehat{Q}\right)  = 1$）时，$Q$具有层次性。

- If $Q$ is non-hierarchical,then $\mathrm{w}\left( \widehat{Q}\right)  \geq  \frac{3}{2}$ .

- 若$Q$非层次化，则$\mathrm{w}\left( \widehat{Q}\right)  \geq  \frac{3}{2}$。

- If $Q$ is Loomis-Whitney of any degree,then $\mathrm{w}\left( \widehat{Q}\right)  = \frac{3}{2}$ .

- 若$Q$为任意阶数的Loomis-Whitney形式，则$\mathrm{w}\left( \widehat{Q}\right)  = \frac{3}{2}$。

- For any query $Q$ ,we have $\mathrm{w}\left( Q\right)  \leq  \mathrm{w}\left( \widehat{Q}\right)  \leq  \mathrm{w}\left( Q\right)  + 1$ .

- 对于任意查询$Q$，我们有$\mathrm{w}\left( Q\right)  \leq  \mathrm{w}\left( \widehat{Q}\right)  \leq  \mathrm{w}\left( Q\right)  + 1$。

Immediate corollaries of these statements are that, in the insert-delete setting, our approach needs: (1) amortized $\widetilde{O}\left( 1\right)$ update time for hierarchical queries; and (2) amortized $\widetilde{O}\left( {\left| \mathcal{D}\right| }^{1/2}\right)$ update time for the triangle query,which is the Loomis-Whitney query of degree 3 . These update times mirror those given in prior work on $q$ -hierarchical queries [8] and the full triangle query [21], albeit our setting is more restricted: it considers full conjunctive queries and initially empty databases and the update times are amortized and have a polylog factor.

这些命题的直接推论表明，在插入-删除场景下，我们的方法需要：(1) 层次化查询的$\widetilde{O}\left( 1\right)$摊还更新时间；(2) 三角查询（即3阶Loomis-Whitney查询）的$\widetilde{O}\left( {\left| \mathcal{D}\right| }^{1/2}\right)$摊还更新时间。这些更新时间与先前关于$q$-层次化查询[8]和完整三角查询[21]的研究结果一致，尽管我们的设定限制更多：仅考虑完全连接查询和初始空数据库，且更新时间为含有多对数因子的摊还时间。

A close analysis of the lower bound proof for non-hierarchical queries in prior work [8] reveals that for any nonhierarchical query $Q$ and any $\gamma  > 0$ ,there is no algorithm that solves ${\mathrm{{IVM}}}^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$ or ${\mathrm{{IVM}}}_{\delta }^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$ with amortized $\widetilde{O}\left( {N}^{1/2 - \gamma }\right)$ update time, unless the OMv-conjecture fails (Proposition F.5 in Appendix F). Following Theorem 4.4 and the fact that $\mathrm{w}\left( \widehat{Q}\right)  = \frac{3}{2}$ for any Loomis-Whitney query $Q$ ,we conclude that both ${\mathrm{{IVM}}}^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$ and ${\mathrm{{IVM}}}_{\delta }^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$ can be solved with $\widetilde{O}\left( {\left| \mathcal{D}\right| }^{1/2}\right)$ amortized update time,which is optimal,unless the OMv conjecture fails.

深入分析先前研究[8]中非层次化查询的下界证明可知：对于任意非层次化查询$Q$和任意$\gamma  > 0$，若OMv猜想成立，则不存在能以$\widetilde{O}\left( {N}^{1/2 - \gamma }\right)$摊还更新时间求解${\mathrm{{IVM}}}^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$或${\mathrm{{IVM}}}_{\delta }^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$的算法（附录F命题F.5）。根据定理4.4及$\mathrm{w}\left( \widehat{Q}\right)  = \frac{3}{2}$对任意Loomis-Whitney查询$Q$成立的事实，我们得出结论：除非OMv猜想不成立，否则${\mathrm{{IVM}}}^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$和${\mathrm{{IVM}}}_{\delta }^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$均可通过最优的$\widetilde{O}\left( {\left| \mathcal{D}\right| }^{1/2}\right)$摊还更新时间求解。

First-order IVM, i.e., delta queries, and even higher-order IVM, i.e., delta queries with materialized views, cannot achieve the update time of our approach. It was already discussed in prior work that for the triangle join query, both IVM approaches need linear update time per single-tuple update [20,21]. See Appendix A. The IVM ${}^{\epsilon }$ approach resorts to a heavy/light partitioning argument that is tailored to the triangle query and does not generalize to other cyclic queries $\left\lbrack  {{20},{21}}\right\rbrack$ . Instead,our approach solves this problem more systematically and for any query $Q$ by translating the temporal dimension (the tuple lifespan as defined by its insert and possible delete) into spatial attributes (the multivariate encoding of the tuple lifespan),taking a tree decomposition of the multivariate extension $\widehat{Q}$ of $Q$ ,and by materializing and maintaining the bags of this tree decomposition.

一阶增量视图维护（即delta查询）乃至高阶增量视图维护（即带物化视图的delta查询）均无法达到本方法的更新时间。先前研究已指出，对于三角连接查询，两种增量视图维护方法都需要单次元组更新的线性更新时间[20,21]。参见附录A。增量视图维护${}^{\epsilon }$方法采用专为三角查询设计的轻重分区论证，无法推广至其他循环查询$\left\lbrack  {{20},{21}}\right\rbrack$。相比之下，我们的方法通过将时间维度（由插入和可能删除定义的元组生存期）转化为空间属性（元组生存期的多元编码），对$Q$的多元扩展$\widehat{Q}$进行树分解，并物化维护该树分解的各包，从而更系统化地解决该问题。

We complement the upper bound in Theorem 4.4 with a lower bound in terms of the lower bound $\omega \left( {\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }\right)$ for the static evaluation of any component ${\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }$ of $\widehat{Q}$ . It is not conditioned of a complexity conjecture. However,it only applies to the IVM ${}_{\delta }^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$ problem. In particular,it is meant to show that our upper bound from Theorem 4.4 is tight for ${\mathrm{{IVM}}}_{\delta }^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$ up to the gap between $\mathrm{w}\left( \widehat{Q}\right)$ and $\omega \left( {\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }\right)$ . It also applies to both amortized and non-amortized update time and enumeration delay. Sec. 7.2 gives the high-level idea:

我们通过静态评估任意组件${\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }$的下界$\omega \left( {\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }\right)$，为定理4.4中的上界补充了一个下界。该结论不依赖于复杂性猜想，但仅适用于增量视图维护(IVM)${}_{\delta }^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$问题。特别地，它旨在证明定理4.4中的上界对于${\mathrm{{IVM}}}_{\delta }^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$是紧致的，其间隙仅存在于$\mathrm{w}\left( \widehat{Q}\right)$与$\omega \left( {\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }\right)$之间。该结论同时适用于摊销与非摊销的更新时间及枚举延迟。第7.2节给出了高层思路：

THEOREM 4.5. Let $Q$ be a query and ${\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }$ any component of its multivariate extension. For any constant $\gamma  > 0,{\operatorname{IVM}}_{\delta }^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$ cannot be solved with (amortized) update time $\widetilde{O}\left( {\left| {\mathcal{D}}^{\left( \tau \right) }\right| }^{\omega \left( {\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }\right)  - 1 - \gamma }\right)$ and (non-amortized) constant enumeration delay.

定理4.5. 设$Q$为查询，${\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }$为其多元扩展的任意组件。对于任意常数$\gamma  > 0,{\operatorname{IVM}}_{\delta }^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$，无法在(摊销)更新时间$\widetilde{O}\left( {\left| {\mathcal{D}}^{\left( \tau \right) }\right| }^{\omega \left( {\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }\right)  - 1 - \gamma }\right)$与(非摊销)常数枚举延迟下求解。

## 5 IVM: INSERT-ONLY SETTING

## 5 IVM：仅插入场景

In this section,we give an overview of our algorithm for ${\mathrm{{IVM}}}^{ + }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$ that meets the upper bound of Theorem 4.1. We leave the details and proofs to Appendix C. We start with the following lemma (Recall notation from Sec. 2):

本节概述满足定理4.1上界的${\mathrm{{IVM}}}^{ + }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$算法，细节与证明见附录C。首先给出以下引理（参见第2节符号约定）：

LEMMA 5.1. Given a query $Q$ ,an initially empty database ${\mathcal{D}}^{\left( 0\right) }$ ,and a stream of $N$ single-tuple inserts,we can compute the new output tuples ${\delta }_{\tau }Q\left( D\right)$ after every insert ${\delta }_{\tau }\mathcal{D}$ ,where the total computation time over all inserts is $O\left( {N + \operatorname{AGM}\left( {Q,{\mathcal{D}}^{\left( N\right) }}\right) }\right)$ .

引理5.1. 给定查询$Q$、初始空数据库${\mathcal{D}}^{\left( 0\right) }$及$N$次单元组插入流，可在每次插入${\delta }_{\tau }\mathcal{D}$后计算新输出元组${\delta }_{\tau }Q\left( D\right)$，所有插入操作的总计算时间为$O\left( {N + \operatorname{AGM}\left( {Q,{\mathcal{D}}^{\left( N\right) }}\right) }\right)$。

The total computation time above is the same as the AGM bound [7] of $Q$ over the final database ${\mathcal{D}}^{\left( N\right) }$ . In particular, even in the static setting where ${\mathcal{D}}^{\left( N\right) }$ is given upfront,the output size $\left| {Q\left( {\mathcal{D}}^{\left( N\right) }\right) }\right|$ can be as large as $\operatorname{AGM}\left( {Q,{\mathcal{D}}^{\left( N\right) }}\right)$ in the worst-case. Moreover,worst-case optimal join algorithms cannot beat this runtime $\left\lbrack  {{32},{33},{42}}\right\rbrack$ . The above lemma is proved based on the query decomposition lemma [32,34],which says the following. Let $Q$ be a query and let $Y \subseteq  \operatorname{vars}\left( Q\right)$ . Then,the AGM bound of $Q$ can be decomposed into a sum of AGM bounds of "residual" queries: one query $Q \bowtie  \mathbf{y}$ for each tuple $\mathbf{y}$ over the variables $Y$ . Appendix C gives a proof of the query decomposition lemma and an example.

上述总计算时间等同于最终数据库${\mathcal{D}}^{\left( N\right) }$上$Q$的AGM界[7]。特别地，即使在预先给定${\mathcal{D}}^{\left( N\right) }$的静态场景中，最坏情况下输出规模$\left| {Q\left( {\mathcal{D}}^{\left( N\right) }\right) }\right|$可能达到$\operatorname{AGM}\left( {Q,{\mathcal{D}}^{\left( N\right) }}\right)$。且最坏情况最优连接算法无法突破该运行时$\left\lbrack  {{32},{33},{42}}\right\rbrack$。该引理基于查询分解引理[32,34]得证，该引理表明：设$Q$为查询，$Y \subseteq  \operatorname{vars}\left( Q\right)$，则$Q$的AGM界可分解为若干"残差"查询AGM界之和：对变量$Y$上的每个元组$\mathbf{y}$，对应一个查询$Q \bowtie  \mathbf{y}$。附录C提供了查询分解引理的证明及示例。

LEMMA 5.2 (Query Decomposition LemMA [32,34]). Given a query $Q$ and a subset $Y \subseteq  \operatorname{vars}\left( Q\right)$ ,let ${\left( {\lambda }_{R\left( X\right) }\right) }_{R\left( X\right)  \in  \operatorname{at}\left( Q\right) }$ be a fractional edge cover of $Q$ . Then,the following inequality holds:

引理5.2（查询分解引理[32,34]）。给定查询$Q$及其子集$Y \subseteq  \operatorname{vars}\left( Q\right)$，令${\left( {\lambda }_{R\left( X\right) }\right) }_{R\left( X\right)  \in  \operatorname{at}\left( Q\right) }$为$Q$的分数边覆盖，则下列不等式成立：

(9)
