In the above, $\mathbf{y} \in  \operatorname{Dom}\left( Y\right)$ indicates that the tuple $\mathbf{y}$ has schema $\mathbf{Y}$ . Moreover, $R\left( \mathbf{X}\right)  \ltimes  \mathbf{y}$ denotes the semijoin of the atom $R\left( \mathbf{X}\right)$ with the tuple $\mathbf{y}$ .

上式中，$\mathbf{y} \in  \operatorname{Dom}\left( Y\right)$表示元组$\mathbf{y}$具有模式$\mathbf{Y}$。此外，$R\left( \mathbf{X}\right)  \ltimes  \mathbf{y}$表示原子$R\left( \mathbf{X}\right)$与元组$\mathbf{y}$的半连接运算。

Example 5.3 (for Lemma 5.1). Consider the triangle query ${Q}_{\Delta }$ from Eq. (1). Lemma 5.1 says that given a stream of $N$ single-tuple inserts into $R,S$ ,and $T$ ,we can compute the new output tuples after every insert in a total time of $O\left( {N}^{3/2}\right)$ . To achieve this,we need to maintain two indices for $R\left( {A,B}\right)$ : one index that sorts $R$ first by $A$ and then by $B$ ,while the other sorts $R$ first by $B$ and then by $A$ . For every insert to $R$ ,we update the two indices simultaneously. Similarly,we maintain two indices for each of $S$ and $T$ .

示例5.3（针对引理5.1）。考虑式(1)中的三角形查询${Q}_{\Delta }$。引理5.1指出，当向$R,S$、$T$持续插入$N$个单元素组时，我们可在$O\left( {N}^{3/2}\right)$总时间内计算每次插入后的新输出元组。为此需要为$R\left( {A,B}\right)$维护两个索引：一个索引按$A$主序、$B$次序排列$R$，另一个则按$B$主序、$A$次序排列。每次向$R$插入时同步更新双索引。$S$和$T$也需各自维护双索引。

Following notation from Sec. 2,let ${R}^{\left( 0\right) }\overset{\text{ def }}{ = }\varnothing ,{R}^{\left( 1\right) },\ldots ,{R}^{\left( N\right) }$ be the sequence of versions of $R$ after each insert,and the same goes for $S$ and $T$ . Suppose that the $\tau$ -th insert in the stream is inserting a tuple(a,b)into $R\left( {A,B}\right)$ . The new output tuples that are added due to this insert correspond to the output of the following query: (Below, we drop the database instance $\mathcal{D}$ from the notation ${\delta }_{\tau }{Q}_{ \bigtriangleup  }\left( \mathcal{D}\right)$ since $\mathcal{D}$ is clear from the context.)

沿用第2节的符号约定，设${R}^{\left( 0\right) }\overset{\text{ def }}{ = }\varnothing ,{R}^{\left( 1\right) },\ldots ,{R}^{\left( N\right) }$为$R$在每次插入后的版本序列，$S$和$T$同理。假设数据流中第$\tau$次插入是将元组(a,b)加入$R\left( {A,B}\right)$，此次插入产生的新输出元组对应以下查询结果：（下文将省略符号${\delta }_{\tau }{Q}_{ \bigtriangleup  }\left( \mathcal{D}\right)$中的数据库实例$\mathcal{D}$，因上下文已明确。）

$$
{\delta }_{\tau }{Q}_{\Delta }\left( {A,B,C}\right) \overset{\text{ def }}{ = }{\sigma }_{B = b}{S}^{\left( \tau \right) }\left( {B,C}\right)  \land  {\sigma }_{A = a}{T}^{\left( \tau \right) }\left( {A,C}\right) 
$$

The output size of this query is upper bounded by:

该查询的输出规模上界为：

$$
\min \left( {\left| {{\sigma }_{B = b}{S}^{\left( \tau \right) }\left( {B,C}\right) }\right| ,\left| {{\sigma }_{A = a}{T}^{\left( \tau \right) }\left( {A,C}\right) }\right| }\right)  \leq  \sqrt{\left| {{\sigma }_{B = b}{S}^{\left( \tau \right) }\left( {B,C}\right) }\right|  \cdot  \left| {{\sigma }_{A = a}{T}^{\left( \tau \right) }\left( {A,C}\right) }\right| } \tag{10}
$$

Moreover,the above query can be computed in time within $O\left( 1\right)$ factor from the quantity in Eq. (10). To achieve this time,we need to use the index for $S\left( {B,C}\right)$ that is indexed by $B$ first,and we also need the index for $T\left( {A,C}\right)$ that is indexed by $A$ first. Because ${S}^{\left( \tau \right) } \subseteq  {S}^{\left( N\right) }$ and ${T}^{\left( \tau \right) } \subseteq  {T}^{\left( N\right) }$ ,the quantity in Eq. (10) is bounded by:

此外，该查询可在式(10)结果乘以$O\left( 1\right)$系数的时间复杂度内完成计算。为此需要使用$B$为主键的$S\left( {B,C}\right)$索引，以及$A$为主键的$T\left( {A,C}\right)$索引。由于${S}^{\left( \tau \right) } \subseteq  {S}^{\left( N\right) }$和${T}^{\left( \tau \right) } \subseteq  {T}^{\left( N\right) }$的约束，式(10)结果存在上界：

$$
\sqrt{\left| {{\sigma }_{B = b}{S}^{\left( N\right) }\left( {B,C}\right) }\right|  \cdot  \left| {{\sigma }_{A = a}{T}^{\left( N\right) }\left( {A,C}\right) }\right| } \tag{11}
$$

The query decomposition lemma (specifically Eq. (30) in Example C.1) says that the sum of the quantity in Eq. (11) over all $\left( {a,b}\right)  \in  {R}^{\left( N\right) }$ is bounded by $\sqrt{\left| {R}^{\left( N\right) }\right|  \cdot  \left| {S}^{\left( N\right) }\right|  \cdot  \left| {T}^{\left( N\right) }\right| } \leq  {N}^{3/2}$ . Hence,all inserts into $R$ take time $O\left( {N}^{3/2}\right)$ .

查询分解引理（具体参见示例C.1中式(30)）表明：式(11)结果对所有$\left( {a,b}\right)  \in  {R}^{\left( N\right) }$求和的上界为$\sqrt{\left| {R}^{\left( N\right) }\right|  \cdot  \left| {S}^{\left( N\right) }\right|  \cdot  \left| {T}^{\left( N\right) }\right| } \leq  {N}^{3/2}$。因此，所有向$R$的插入操作耗时$O\left( {N}^{3/2}\right)$。

Now suppose that the $\tau$ -th insert in the stream is inserting a tuple(b,c)into $S\left( {B,C}\right)$ . To handle this insert,we compute the query:

现假设数据流中的第$\tau$次插入操作是将元组(b,c)插入到$S\left( {B,C}\right)$中。为处理此插入，我们需计算以下查询：

$$
{\delta }_{\tau }{Q}_{\Delta }\left( {A,B,C}\right) \overset{\text{ def }}{ = }{\sigma }_{B = b}{R}^{\left( \tau \right) }\left( {A,B}\right)  \land  {\sigma }_{C = c}{T}^{\left( \tau \right) }\left( {A,C}\right) 
$$

To compute this query in the desired time,we need to use the index for $T\left( {A,C}\right)$ that is indexed by $C$ first. This is why we need to maintain two indices for $T\left( {A,C}\right)$ . The same goes for $R$ and $S$ .

要在预期时间内完成该查询计算，需优先使用$T\left( {A,C}\right)$上基于$C$构建的索引。这正是我们需要为$T\left( {A,C}\right)$维护两个索引的原因。$R$与$S$同理。

Instead of the AGM-bound, the upper bound in Theorem 4.1 is given in terms of the fractional hypertree width of $Q$ . To achieve this bound,we take an optimal tree decomposition of $Q$ and maintain a materialized relation for every bag in the tree decomposition using Lemma 5.1. In order to support constant-delay enumeration of the output, we "calibrate" the bags by semijoin reducing adjacent bags with one another. The following example illustrates this idea.

不同于AGM界，定理4.1中的上界采用$Q$的分数超树宽度表示。为实现该界限，我们选取$Q$的最优树分解，并利用引理5.1为分解中的每个袋维护物化关系。为支持输出的恒定延迟枚举，我们通过半连接归约相邻袋的方式进行"校准"。下例将阐释该思想。

Example 5.4 (for Theorem 4.1,IVM ${}^{ + }$ ). Suppose we want to solve ${\mathrm{{IVM}}}^{ + }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$ for the following query $Q$ consisting of two adjacent triangles: One triangle over $\{ A,B,C\}$ and the other over $\{ B,C,D\}$ .

例5.4（对应定理4.1的IVM${}^{ + }$）。假设我们需要求解由两个相邻三角形组成的查询${\mathrm{{IVM}}}^{ + }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$：一个三角形基于$\{ A,B,C\}$，另一个基于$\{ B,C,D\}$。

$$
Q\left( {A,B,C,D}\right)  = R\left( {A,B}\right)  \land  S\left( {B,C}\right)  \land  T\left( {A,C}\right)  \land  U\left( {B,D}\right)  \land  V\left( {C,D}\right)  \tag{12}
$$

The above query $Q$ has a fractional hypertree width of $\frac{3}{2}$ . In particular,one optimal tree decomposition of $Q$ consists of two bags: One child bag ${B}_{1} = \{ A,B,C\}$ and another root bag ${B}_{2} = \{ B,C,D\}$ . Theorem 4.1 says that given a stream of $N$ single-tuple inserts, $Q$ can be updated in a total time of $O\left( {N}^{3/2}\right)$ where we can do constant-delay enumeration of the output after every insert. To achieve this, we maintain the following query plan:

上述查询$Q$的分数超树宽度为$\frac{3}{2}$。其最优树分解包含两个袋：子袋${B}_{1} = \{ A,B,C\}$和根袋${B}_{2} = \{ B,C,D\}$。定理4.1指出，给定$N$次单元组插入流，可在$O\left( {N}^{3/2}\right)$总时间内更新$Q$，且每次插入后都能实现输出的恒定延迟枚举。为此我们维护以下查询计划：

$$
{Q}_{1}\left( {A,B,C}\right)  = R\left( {A,B}\right)  \land  S\left( {B,C}\right)  \land  T\left( {A,C}\right) 
$$

$$
{P}_{1}\left( {B,C}\right)  = {Q}_{1}\left( {A,B,C}\right) 
$$

$$
{Q}_{2}^{\prime }\left( {B,C,D}\right)  = {P}_{1}\left( {B,C}\right)  \land  S\left( {B,C}\right)  \land  U\left( {B,D}\right)  \land  V\left( {C,D}\right) 
$$

By Lemma 5.1, ${Q}_{1}$ above can be updated in a total time of $O\left( {N}^{3/2}\right)$ ,as shown in Example 5.3. Also, ${P}_{1}$ can be updated in the same time because it is just a projection of ${Q}_{1}$ . Regardless of the size of ${P}_{1}$ ,the query ${Q}_{2}^{\prime }$ can be updated in a total time of $O\left( {N}^{3/2}\right)$ because its AGM bound is upper bounded by the input relations $S,U$ and $V$ . To do constant-delay enumeration of $Q\left( {A,B,C,D}\right)$ ,we enumerate(b,c,d)from ${Q}_{2}^{\prime }$ ,and for every(b,c),we enumerate the corresponding $A$ -values from ${Q}_{1}$ . Note that at least one $A$ -value must exist because ${Q}_{2}^{\prime }$ includes ${P}_{1}$ ,which is the projection of ${Q}_{1}$ .

根据引理5.1，上例中的${Q}_{1}$可在$O\left( {N}^{3/2}\right)$总时间内更新（如例5.3所示）。${P}_{1}$作为${Q}_{1}$的投影也可在相同时间内更新。无论${P}_{1}$规模如何，查询${Q}_{2}^{\prime }$都可在$O\left( {N}^{3/2}\right)$总时间内更新，因其AGM界受输入关系$S,U$和$V$约束。为实现$Q\left( {A,B,C,D}\right)$的恒定延迟枚举，我们从${Q}_{2}^{\prime }$枚举(b,c,d)，并对每个(b,c)从${Q}_{1}$枚举对应$A$值。注意至少存在一个$A$值，因${Q}_{2}^{\prime }$包含${Q}_{1}$的投影${P}_{1}$。

In the above example,we only calibrate bottom-up from the leaf ${Q}_{1}$ to the root ${Q}_{2}^{\prime }$ . However,this is not sufficient for ${\mathrm{{IVM}}}_{\delta }^{ + }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$ . Instead,we also need to calibrate top-down,as the following example demonstrates.

上例中我们仅从叶节点${Q}_{1}$到根节点${Q}_{2}^{\prime }$自底向上校准。但对${\mathrm{{IVM}}}_{\delta }^{ + }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$而言这并不充分，如下例所示还需进行自顶向下校准。

Example 5.5 (for Theorem 4.1,IV ${\mathrm{M}}_{\delta }^{ + }$ ). Consider again the query (12) from Example 5.4. Suppose now that we want to extend our solution from Example 5.4 to the IVM ${}_{\delta }^{ + }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$ problem. In particular,suppose we have an insert of a tuple(a,b) into $R$ above. In order to enumerate the new output tuples corresponding to(a,b),we have to start our enumeration from ${Q}_{1}$ as the root. For that purpose, ${Q}_{1}$ also needs to be calibrated with ${Q}_{2}^{\prime }$ . To that end,in addition to ${Q}_{1},{P}_{1}$ and ${Q}_{2}^{\prime }$ that are defined in Example 5.4, we also need to maintain the following relations:

例5.5（针对定理4.1的IV部分${\mathrm{M}}_{\delta }^{ + }$）。再次考虑例5.4中的查询(12)。假设现在我们需要将例5.4的解决方案扩展到IVM${}_{\delta }^{ + }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$问题。具体而言，假设我们向$R$插入元组(a,b)。为了枚举与(a,b)对应的新输出元组，我们必须从${Q}_{1}$作为根节点开始枚举。为此，${Q}_{1}$还需要与${Q}_{2}^{\prime }$进行校准。因此，除了例5.4中定义的${Q}_{1},{P}_{1}$和${Q}_{2}^{\prime }$外，我们还需维护以下关系：

$$
{P}_{1}^{\prime \prime }\left( {B,C}\right)  = {Q}_{2}^{\prime }\left( {B,C,D}\right) 
$$

$$
{Q}_{1}^{\prime \prime }\left( {A,B,C}\right)  = {Q}_{1}\left( {A,B,C}\right)  \land  {P}_{1}^{\prime \prime }\left( {B,C}\right) 
$$

The AGM bound of ${Q}_{1}^{\prime \prime }$ is still ${N}^{3/2}$ ,and so is the AGM bound of ${P}_{1}^{\prime \prime }$ . Hence,we can maintain all inserts into them in a total time of $O\left( {N}^{3/2}\right)$ . Moreover,note that by definition, ${Q}_{1}^{\prime \prime }$ must be the projection of $Q\left( {A,B,C,D}\right)$ onto $\{ A,B,C\}$ .

${Q}_{1}^{\prime \prime }$的AGM边界仍是${N}^{3/2}$，${P}_{1}^{\prime \prime }$的AGM边界亦然。因此，我们可以在$O\left( {N}^{3/2}\right)$总时间内维护所有插入操作。此外请注意，根据定义，${Q}_{1}^{\prime \prime }$必须是$Q\left( {A,B,C,D}\right)$在$\{ A,B,C\}$上的投影。

Now,suppose we have an insert of a tuple $t$ into either one of the relations $S,U$ or $V$ . Then,we can enumerate the new output tuples that are added due to this insert by starting from ${Q}_{2}^{\prime }$ ,just like we did in Example 5.4. However,if we have inserts into $R$ or $T$ ,then we have to start our enumeration from ${Q}_{1}^{\prime \prime }$ . For each output tuple(a,b,c)of ${Q}_{1}^{\prime \prime }$ that joins with $t$ ,we enumerate the corresponding $D$ -values from ${Q}_{2}^{\prime }$ . There must be at least one $D$ -value because ${Q}_{1}^{\prime \prime }$ is the projection of the output $Q$ onto $\{ A,B,C\}$ .

现在假设我们向关系$S,U$或$V$插入元组$t$。那么可以像例5.4那样，从${Q}_{2}^{\prime }$开始枚举因此次插入新增的输出元组。但如果向$R$或$T$插入数据，则必须从${Q}_{1}^{\prime \prime }$开始枚举。对于${Q}_{1}^{\prime \prime }$中与$t$连接的每个输出元组(a,b,c)，我们从${Q}_{2}^{\prime }$枚举对应的$D$值。由于${Q}_{1}^{\prime \prime }$是输出$Q$在$\{ A,B,C\}$上的投影，至少存在一个$D$值。

## 6 TECHNICAL TOOLS: INTERSECTION JOINS

## 6 技术工具：交集连接

Before we explain our upper and lower bounds for IVM in the insert-delete setting in Sec. 7, we review in this section some necessary background on intersection joins in the static setting.

在第七节阐述插入-删除场景下IVM的上下界之前，本节先回顾静态场景中交集连接的必要背景知识。

Definition 6.1 (Intersection queries [3]). An intersection query is a (full conjunctive) query that contains interval variables, which are variables whose domains are discrete intervals over the natural numbers. For instance, the discrete interval $\left\lbrack  {3,7}\right\rbrack$ consists of the natural numbers from 3 to 7 . For better distinction,we denote an interval variable by $\left\lbrack  X\right\rbrack$ and call the variables that are not interval variables point variables. A value of an interval variable $\left\lbrack  X\right\rbrack$ is denoted by $\left\lbrack  x\right\rbrack$ ,which is an interval. Two or more intervals join if they intersect. In particular,the semantics of an intersection query $Q = {R}_{1}\left( {X}_{1}\right)  \land  \cdots  \land  {R}_{k}\left( {X}_{k}\right)$ is defined as follows. A tuple $t$ over the schema vars(Q)is in the result of $Q$ if there are tuples ${t}_{i} \in  {R}_{i}$ for $i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack$ such that for all $X \in  \operatorname{vars}\left( Q\right)$ ,it holds:

定义6.1（交集查询[3]）。交集查询是包含区间变量的（完全合取）查询，这些变量的定义域是自然数上的离散区间。例如离散区间$\left\lbrack  {3,7}\right\rbrack$包含3至7的自然数。为区分起见，我们用$\left\lbrack  X\right\rbrack$表示区间变量，非区间变量则称为点变量。区间变量$\left\lbrack  X\right\rbrack$的值记作$\left\lbrack  x\right\rbrack$，即一个区间。两个及以上区间相交时产生连接。具体而言，交集查询$Q = {R}_{1}\left( {X}_{1}\right)  \land  \cdots  \land  {R}_{k}\left( {X}_{k}\right)$的语义定义如下：模式vars(Q)上的元组$t$属于$Q$的结果集，当且仅当存在$i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack$的元组${t}_{i} \in  {R}_{i}$使得对所有$X \in  \operatorname{vars}\left( Q\right)$满足：

- if $X$ is a point variable,then the set of points $\left\{  {{t}_{i}\left( X\right)  \mid  i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack   \land  X \in  {X}_{i}}\right\}$ consists of a single value,which is the point $t\left( X\right)$ ;

- 若$X$为点变量，则点集$\left\{  {{t}_{i}\left( X\right)  \mid  i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack   \land  X \in  {X}_{i}}\right\}$仅包含单一值，即点$t\left( X\right)$；

- if $\left\lbrack  X\right\rbrack$ is an interval variable,then the intervals in the set $\left\{  {{t}_{i}\left( \left\lbrack  X\right\rbrack  \right)  \mid  i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack   \land  \left\lbrack  X\right\rbrack   \in  {X}_{i}}\right\}$ have a non-empty intersection,which is the interval $\mathbf{t}\left( \left\lbrack  X\right\rbrack  \right)$ .

- 若$\left\lbrack  X\right\rbrack$为区间变量，则集合$\left\{  {{t}_{i}\left( \left\lbrack  X\right\rbrack  \right)  \mid  i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack   \land  \left\lbrack  X\right\rbrack   \in  {X}_{i}}\right\}$中的区间存在非空交集，该交集即区间$\mathbf{t}\left( \left\lbrack  X\right\rbrack  \right)$。

In this paper, we are only interested in a special class of intersection queries, defined below:

本文仅关注一类特殊的交集查询，其定义如下：

Definition 6.2 (The time extension $\bar{Q}$ of a query $Q$ ). Let $Q = {R}_{1}\left( {X}_{1}\right)  \land  \cdots  \land  {R}_{k}\left( {X}_{k}\right)$ be a query where $\operatorname{vars}\left( Q\right)$ are all point variables,and let $\left\lbrack  Z\right\rbrack$ be a new interval variable. The time extension $\bar{Q}$ of $Q$ is an intersection query that results from $Q$ by adding $\left\lbrack  Z\right\rbrack$ to the schema of each atom:

定义6.2（查询$Q$的时间扩展$\bar{Q}$）。设$Q = {R}_{1}\left( {X}_{1}\right)  \land  \cdots  \land  {R}_{k}\left( {X}_{k}\right)$为所有变量均为点变量的查询，$\left\lbrack  Z\right\rbrack$为新增区间变量。查询$Q$的时间扩展$\bar{Q}$是通过向每个原子模式添加$\left\lbrack  Z\right\rbrack$而得到的交集查询：

$$
\bar{Q} = {\bar{R}}_{1}\left( {\left\lbrack  Z\right\rbrack  ,{\mathbf{X}}_{1}}\right)  \land  \cdots  \land  {\bar{R}}_{k}\left( {\left\lbrack  Z\right\rbrack  ,{\mathbf{X}}_{k}}\right)  \tag{13}
$$

A query $\bar{Q}$ is called a time extension if it is the time extension of some query $Q$ .

若某查询$\bar{Q}$是另一查询$Q$的时间扩展，则称其为时间扩展查询。

We call $\bar{Q}$ the "time extension" of $Q$ because in Sec. 7.1,we use the interval $\left\lbrack  Z\right\rbrack$ in $\bar{Q}$ to represent the "lifespan" of tuples in $Q$ .

我们将$\bar{Q}$称为$Q$的"时间扩展"，因为在第7.1节中，使用$\bar{Q}$中的区间$\left\lbrack  Z\right\rbrack$来表示$Q$中元组的"生命周期"。

Example 6.3. Consider this intersection query,which is the time extension of ${Q}_{\bigtriangleup }$ from Eq. (1):

示例6.3. 考虑以下交集查询，它是公式(1)中${Q}_{\bigtriangleup }$的时间扩展：

$$
{\bar{Q}}_{ \bigtriangleup  }\left( {\left\lbrack  Z\right\rbrack  ,A,B,C}\right)  = \bar{R}\left( {\left\lbrack  Z\right\rbrack  ,A,B}\right)  \land  \bar{S}\left( {\left\lbrack  Z\right\rbrack  ,B,C}\right)  \land  \bar{T}\left( {\left\lbrack  Z\right\rbrack  ,A,C}\right)  \tag{14}
$$

In the above, $\left\lbrack  Z\right\rbrack$ is an interval variable while $A,B,C$ are point variables. Consider the database instance $\overline{\mathcal{D}}$ depicted in Figure 1. The output of ${\bar{Q}}_{ \vartriangle  }$ on this instance is depicted in Figure 1d.

上式中，$\left\lbrack  Z\right\rbrack$为区间变量而$A,B,C$为点变量。考虑图1所示的数据库实例$\overline{\mathcal{D}}$，查询${\bar{Q}}_{ \vartriangle  }$在该实例上的输出如图1d所示。

Let $\bar{Q}$ be the time extension of a query $Q$ . Prior work reduces the evaluation of $\bar{Q}$ to the evaluation of $\widehat{Q}$ ,where $\widehat{Q}$ is the multivariate extension of $Q$ (Eq. (7)),whose variables are all point variables [3]. Moreover,it is shown in [3] that this reduction is optimal (up to a $\widetilde{O}\left( 1\right)$ factor). In particular,the query $\bar{Q}$ is exactly as hard as the hardest component ${\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }$ in $\widehat{Q}$ . This is shown by a backward reduction from (the static evaluation of) each component ${\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }$ to $\bar{Q}$ . We summarize both reductions below and defer the details to Appendix D and [3]. We rely on both reductions to prove upper and lower bounds for IVM in the insert-delete setting (Sec. 4.2).

设$\bar{Q}$为查询$Q$的时间扩展。现有研究将$\bar{Q}$的求值归约为$\widehat{Q}$的求值，其中$\widehat{Q}$是$Q$的多变量扩展（公式(7)），其变量均为点变量[3]。文献[3]证明该归约具有最优性（至多相差$\widetilde{O}\left( 1\right)$倍）。特别地，查询$\bar{Q}$的复杂度与$\widehat{Q}$中最难的分量${\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }$相当，这通过将每个分量${\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }$（静态求值）反向归约至$\bar{Q}$得以证明。我们将在附录D和文献[3]中详述这两种归约方法，并基于它们在第4.2节中推导出插入-删除场景下增量视图维护的上下界。

### 6.1 Reduction from $\bar{Q}$ to $\widehat{Q}$

### 6.1 从$\bar{Q}$到$\widehat{Q}$的归约

Suppose we want to evaluate $\bar{Q}$ on a given database instance $\overline{\mathcal{D}}$ . We construct a segment tree, ${\mathcal{T}}_{N}$ ,over the interval variable $\left\lbrack  Z\right\rbrack$ ,where the parameter $N$ is roughly the maximum number of different $Z$ -values. A segment tree ${\mathcal{T}}_{N}$ is a balanced binary tree with $N$ leaves. Each node corresponds to an interval. The root corresponds to the interval $\left\lbrack  {1,N}\right\rbrack$ , its left and right child correspond to the intervals $\left\lbrack  {1,N/2}\right\rbrack$ and $\left\lbrack  {N/2 + 1,N}\right\rbrack$ respectively,and so on. Each node can be represented by a bitstring of length at most ${\log }_{2}N$ . Figure 2 depicts the segment tree ${\mathcal{T}}_{8}$ . Each interval $\left\lbrack  z\right\rbrack$ can be broken down into at most $O\left( {\log N}\right)$ nodes in ${\mathcal{T}}_{N}$ . We refer to these nodes as the canonical partition of $\left\lbrack  z\right\rbrack$ ,and denote them by ${\mathrm{{CP}}}_{N}\left( \left\lbrack  z\right\rbrack  \right)$ . For example,the canonical partitions of $\left\lbrack  {2,8}\right\rbrack$ and $\left\lbrack  {2,5}\right\rbrack$ in the segment tree ${\mathcal{T}}_{8}$ are:

假设我们需要在给定数据库实例$\overline{\mathcal{D}}$上评估$\bar{Q}$。我们构建一个线段树${\mathcal{T}}_{N}$，其基于区间变量$\left\lbrack  Z\right\rbrack$，其中参数$N$大致表示不同$Z$值的最大数量。线段树${\mathcal{T}}_{N}$是一棵具有$N$个叶节点的平衡二叉树，每个节点对应一个区间——根节点对应区间$\left\lbrack  {1,N}\right\rbrack$，其左右子节点分别对应区间$\left\lbrack  {1,N/2}\right\rbrack$和$\left\lbrack  {N/2 + 1,N}\right\rbrack$，以此类推。每个节点可用长度不超过${\log }_{2}N$的比特串表示。图2展示了线段树${\mathcal{T}}_{8}$的结构。任意区间$\left\lbrack  z\right\rbrack$最多可分解为${\mathcal{T}}_{N}$中的$O\left( {\log N}\right)$个节点，这些节点称为$\left\lbrack  z\right\rbrack$的标准划分，记作${\mathrm{{CP}}}_{N}\left( \left\lbrack  z\right\rbrack  \right)$。例如，线段树${\mathcal{T}}_{8}$中$\left\lbrack  {2,8}\right\rbrack$和$\left\lbrack  {2,5}\right\rbrack$的标准划分如下：

$$
{\mathrm{{CP}}}_{8}\left( \left\lbrack  {2,8}\right\rbrack  \right)  = \{ {001},{01},1\} ,\;{\mathrm{{CP}}}_{8}\left( \left\lbrack  {2,5}\right\rbrack  \right)  = \{ {001},{01},{100}\}  \tag{15}
$$

The canonical partition of the intersection of some intervals is roughly the intersection of their canonical partitions (Lemma D.1). Two (or more) nodes in the segment tree correspond to overlapping intervals if and only if one node is an ancestor of the other, which can only happen if one of the two corresponding bitstrings is a prefix of the other (Lemma D.1). For example, the nodes labeled by 1 and 100 in Figure 2 correspond to two overlapping intervals, namely $\left\lbrack  {5,8}\right\rbrack$ and $\left\lbrack  {5,5}\right\rbrack$ ,because the string 1 is a prefix of 100 . Therefore,testing intersections can be reduced to testing whether some bitstrings form a chain of prefixes. Armed with this idea,we convert $\overline{\mathcal{D}}$ into a database instance $\widehat{\mathcal{D}}$ for $\widehat{Q}$ ,called the canonical partition of $\overline{\mathcal{D}}$ and denoted by ${\mathrm{{CP}}}_{N}\left( \overline{\mathcal{D}}\right)$ . There is a mapping between the outputs of $\bar{Q}\left( \overline{\mathcal{D}}\right)$ and $\widehat{Q}\left( \widehat{\mathcal{D}}\right)$ , as the following example shows. See Theorem D. 5 for more details.

若干区间交集的标准划分大致等于它们标准划分的交集（引理D.1）。当且仅当一个节点是另一个节点的祖先时（即两个对应比特串存在前缀关系时），线段树中两个（或多个）节点对应的区间才会重叠（引理D.1）。例如图2中标签为1和100的节点对应重叠区间$\left\lbrack  {5,8}\right\rbrack$和$\left\lbrack  {5,5}\right\rbrack$，因为字符串1是100的前缀。因此，交集测试可转化为检测比特串是否形成前缀链。基于此，我们将$\overline{\mathcal{D}}$转换为$\widehat{Q}$的数据库实例$\widehat{\mathcal{D}}$，称为$\overline{\mathcal{D}}$的标准划分并记作${\mathrm{{CP}}}_{N}\left( \overline{\mathcal{D}}\right)$。如以下示例所示，$\bar{Q}\left( \overline{\mathcal{D}}\right)$与$\widehat{Q}\left( \widehat{\mathcal{D}}\right)$的输出存在映射关系，详见定理D.5。

Example 6.4. Suppose we want to reduce ${\bar{Q}}_{ \bigtriangleup  }$ from Eq. (14) to ${\widehat{Q}}_{ \bigtriangleup  }$ from Example 3.4. In particular,we are given the database instance $\overline{\mathcal{D}}$ from Figure 1 and want to compute the corresponding instance $\widehat{\mathcal{D}}$ for ${\widehat{Q}}_{ \bigtriangleup  }$ . We show how to construct ${\widehat{R}}_{123},{\widehat{S}}_{123},{\widehat{T}}_{123}$ for ${\widehat{Q}}_{123}$ in Eq. (8). The remaining ${\widehat{Q}}_{132},\ldots ,{\widehat{Q}}_{321}$ are similar. The query ${\widehat{Q}}_{123}$ is meant to test whether $\bar{R},\bar{S},\bar{T}$ contain three intervals $\left\lbrack  {z}^{R}\right\rbrack  ,\left\lbrack  {z}^{S}\right\rbrack  ,\left\lbrack  {z}^{T}\right\rbrack$ whose canonical partitions contain bitstrings ${z}^{R},{z}^{S},{z}^{T}$ where ${z}^{R}$ is a prefix of ${z}^{S}$ which is a prefix of ${z}^{T}$ . This can only happen if there are bitstrings ${z}_{2},{z}_{3}$ where ${z}^{S} = {z}^{R} \circ  {z}_{2}$ and ${z}^{T} = {z}^{S} \circ  {z}_{3}$ ,where $\circ$ denotes string concatenation. To that end,we define:

例6.4. 假设我们需要将式(14)中的${\bar{Q}}_{ \bigtriangleup  }$约简为例3.4中的${\widehat{Q}}_{ \bigtriangleup  }$。具体而言，给定图1中的数据库实例$\overline{\mathcal{D}}$，需计算${\widehat{Q}}_{ \bigtriangleup  }$对应的实例$\widehat{\mathcal{D}}$。我们将展示如何为式(8)中的${\widehat{Q}}_{123}$构建${\widehat{R}}_{123},{\widehat{S}}_{123},{\widehat{T}}_{123}$，其余${\widehat{Q}}_{132},\ldots ,{\widehat{Q}}_{321}$同理。查询${\widehat{Q}}_{123}$用于检测$\bar{R},\bar{S},\bar{T}$是否包含三个区间$\left\lbrack  {z}^{R}\right\rbrack  ,\left\lbrack  {z}^{S}\right\rbrack  ,\left\lbrack  {z}^{T}\right\rbrack$，其规范分区包含位串${z}^{R},{z}^{S},{z}^{T}$，其中${z}^{R}$是${z}^{S}$的前缀，而${z}^{S}$又是${z}^{T}$的前缀。这种情况仅当存在位串${z}_{2},{z}_{3}$满足${z}^{S} = {z}^{R} \circ  {z}_{2}$和${z}^{T} = {z}^{S} \circ  {z}_{3}$时成立，此处$\circ$表示字符串连接。为此我们定义：

$$
{\widehat{R}}_{123}\overset{\text{ def }}{ = }\left\{  {\left( {{z}_{1},a,b}\right) \mid \exists \left\lbrack  z\right\rbrack   : \left( {\left\lbrack  z\right\rbrack  ,a,b}\right)  \in  \bar{R} \land  {z}_{1} \in  {\mathrm{{CP}}}_{N}\left( \left\lbrack  z\right\rbrack  \right) }\right\}   \tag{16}
$$

$$
{\widehat{S}}_{123}\overset{\text{ def }}{ = }\left\{  {\left( {{z}_{1},{z}_{2},b,c}\right) \mid \exists \left\lbrack  z\right\rbrack   : \left( {\left\lbrack  z\right\rbrack  ,b,c}\right)  \in  \bar{S} \land  \left( {{z}_{1} \circ  {z}_{2}}\right)  \in  {\mathrm{{CP}}}_{N}\left( \left\lbrack  z\right\rbrack  \right) }\right\}  
$$

$$
{\widehat{T}}_{123}\overset{\text{ def }}{ = }\left\{  {\left( {{z}_{1},{z}_{2},{z}_{3},a,c}\right) \mid \exists z : \left( {\left\lbrack  z\right\rbrack  ,a,c}\right)  \in  \bar{T} \land  \left( {{z}_{1} \circ  {z}_{2} \circ  {z}_{3}}\right)  \in  {\mathrm{{CP}}}_{N}\left( \left\lbrack  z\right\rbrack  \right) }\right\}  
$$

Note that $\left| {\widehat{T}}_{123}\right|  = O\left( {\left| \bar{T}\right|  \cdot  \operatorname{polylog}\left( N\right) }\right)$ and can be constructed in time $O\left( {\left| \bar{T}\right|  \cdot  \operatorname{polylog}\left( N\right) }\right)$ ,because the height of the segment tree is $O\left( {\log N}\right)$ . The same goes for ${\widehat{R}}_{123}$ and ${\widehat{S}}_{123}$ . Let $\varepsilon$ denote the empty string. Applying the above to $\overline{\mathcal{D}}$ from Figure $1,{\widehat{R}}_{123},{\widehat{S}}_{123},{\widehat{T}}_{123}$ respectively contain the tuples: $\left( {\varepsilon ,{a}_{1},{b}_{1}}\right) ,\left( {\varepsilon ,{01},{b}_{1},{c}_{1}}\right) ,\left( {\varepsilon ,{01},\varepsilon ,{a}_{1},{c}_{1}}\right)$ ,which join together producing the output tuple $\left( {\varepsilon ,{01},\varepsilon ,{a}_{1},{b}_{1},{c}_{1}}\right)$ of ${\widehat{Q}}_{123}$ . This is a witness to the output tuple $\left( {\left\lbrack  {3,5}\right\rbrack  ,{a}_{1},{b}_{1},{c}_{1}}\right)$ of ${\bar{Q}}_{ \bigtriangleup  }$ . The full answer to ${\bar{Q}}_{ \bigtriangleup  }$ ,given by Figure 1d,can be retrieved from the union of the answers to the six queries ${\widehat{Q}}_{132},\ldots ,{\widehat{Q}}_{321}$ . See Example D. 2 for more details.

注意$\left| {\widehat{T}}_{123}\right|  = O\left( {\left| \bar{T}\right|  \cdot  \operatorname{polylog}\left( N\right) }\right)$可在$O\left( {\left| \bar{T}\right|  \cdot  \operatorname{polylog}\left( N\right) }\right)$时间内构建完成，因为线段树的高度为$O\left( {\log N}\right)$。${\widehat{R}}_{123}$和${\widehat{S}}_{123}$同理。设$\varepsilon$表示空字符串。将上述方法应用于图$1,{\widehat{R}}_{123},{\widehat{S}}_{123},{\widehat{T}}_{123}$中的$\overline{\mathcal{D}}$，可分别得到包含以下元组：$\left( {\varepsilon ,{a}_{1},{b}_{1}}\right) ,\left( {\varepsilon ,{01},{b}_{1},{c}_{1}}\right) ,\left( {\varepsilon ,{01},\varepsilon ,{a}_{1},{c}_{1}}\right)$，这些元组连接后生成${\widehat{Q}}_{123}$的输出元组$\left( {\varepsilon ,{01},\varepsilon ,{a}_{1},{b}_{1},{c}_{1}}\right)$。这是${\bar{Q}}_{ \bigtriangleup  }$输出元组$\left( {\left\lbrack  {3,5}\right\rbrack  ,{a}_{1},{b}_{1},{c}_{1}}\right)$的见证。由图1d给出的${\bar{Q}}_{ \bigtriangleup  }$完整答案，可通过合并六个查询${\widehat{Q}}_{132},\ldots ,{\widehat{Q}}_{321}$的答案获得。详见例D.2。

### 6.2 Reduction from $\widehat{Q}$ to $\bar{Q}$

### 6.2 从$\widehat{Q}$到$\bar{Q}$的约简

We now summarize the backward reduction from the static evaluation of each component ${\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }$ of $\widehat{Q}$ back to $\bar{Q}$ ,based on [3]. We will utilize this reduction later to establish our lower bound in Sec. 7.2.

基于文献[3]，我们现在总结从各组件${\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }$静态评估到$\bar{Q}$的后向归约过程。后续将在第7.2节利用该归约建立下界。

Example 6.5. Continuing with Example 6.3,let us consider the query ${\widehat{Q}}_{123}$ from (8). Let ${\widehat{\mathcal{D}}}_{123}^{\prime }$ be an arbitrary database instance for ${\widehat{\mathcal{Q}}}_{123}$ . (Unlike Example 6.3,here we cannot make any assumption about how ${\widehat{\mathcal{D}}}_{123}^{\prime }$ was constructed.) We show how to use an oracle for the intersection query ${\bar{Q}}_{\Delta }$ from (14) in order to compute ${\widehat{Q}}_{123}\left( {\widehat{D}}_{123}^{\prime }\right)$ in the same time. WLOG we can assume that each value $x$ that appears in ${\widehat{\mathcal{D}}}_{123}^{\prime }$ is a bitstring of length exactly $\ell$ for some constant $\ell$ . (If a bitstring has length less than $\ell$ ,we can pad it with zeros.) We construct a segment tree ${\mathcal{T}}_{{N}^{\prime }}$ where ${N}^{\prime }\overset{\text{ def }}{ = }{2}^{3\ell }$ ,i.e. a segment tree of depth $3\ell$ . (We chose 3 in this example because it is the number of atoms in ${\widehat{Q}}_{123}$ .) Each node $v$ in the segment tree corresponds to an interval that is contained in $\left\lbrack  {N}^{\prime }\right\rbrack$ and is identified by a bitstring of length at most $3\ell$ . Given a bitstring $b$ of length at most $3\ell$ ,let ${\operatorname{seg}}_{{N}^{\prime }}\left( b\right)$ denote the corresponding interval in the segment tree ${\mathcal{T}}_{{N}^{\prime }}$ . We construct the following database instance ${\overline{\mathcal{D}}}^{\prime }$ for ${\bar{Q}}_{\bigtriangleup }$ :

示例6.5 延续示例6.3，考虑式(8)中的查询${\widehat{Q}}_{123}$。设${\widehat{\mathcal{D}}}_{123}^{\prime }$为${\widehat{\mathcal{Q}}}_{123}$的任意数据库实例（不同于示例6.3，此处不能对${\widehat{\mathcal{D}}}_{123}^{\prime }$的构建方式做任何假设）。我们将演示如何利用式(14)交集查询${\bar{Q}}_{\Delta }$的预言机，在相同时间复杂度内计算${\widehat{Q}}_{123}\left( {\widehat{D}}_{123}^{\prime }\right)$。不失一般性，可假定${\widehat{\mathcal{D}}}_{123}^{\prime }$中每个值$x$都是长度为常数$\ell$的比特串（若长度不足$\ell$则补零）。构建深度为$3\ell$的线段树${\mathcal{T}}_{{N}^{\prime }}$（本例选择3因其对应${\widehat{Q}}_{123}$中的原子数），每个节点$v$对应$\left\lbrack  {N}^{\prime }\right\rbrack$内区间并通过至多$3\ell$长度的比特串标识。给定长度不超过$3\ell$的比特串$b$，用${\operatorname{seg}}_{{N}^{\prime }}\left( b\right)$表示线段树${\mathcal{T}}_{{N}^{\prime }}$中对应区间。最终为${\bar{Q}}_{\bigtriangleup }$构建如下数据库实例${\overline{\mathcal{D}}}^{\prime }$：

$$
{\bar{R}}^{\prime } = \left\{  {\left( {{\operatorname{seg}}_{{N}^{\prime }}\left( {z}_{1}\right) ,a,b}\right)  \mid  \left( {{z}_{1},a,b}\right)  \in  {\widehat{R}}_{123}^{\prime }}\right\}  
$$

$$
{\bar{S}}^{\prime } = \left\{  {\left( {{\operatorname{seg}}_{{N}^{\prime }}\left( {{z}_{1} \circ  {z}_{2}}\right) ,b,c}\right)  \mid  \left( {{z}_{1},{z}_{2},b,c}\right)  \in  {\widehat{S}}_{123}^{\prime }}\right\}  
$$

$$
{\bar{T}}^{\prime } = \left\{  {\left( {{\operatorname{seg}}_{{N}^{\prime }}\left( {{z}_{1} \circ  {z}_{2} \circ  {z}_{3}}\right) ,a,c}\right)  \mid  \left( {{z}_{1},{z}_{2},{z}_{3},a,c}\right)  \in  {\widehat{T}}_{123}^{\prime }}\right\}  
$$

Following [3],we can show that there is a one-to-one mapping between the output tuples of ${\overline{\mathcal{Q}}}_{ \vartriangle  }\left( {\overline{\mathcal{D}}}^{\prime }\right)$ and the output tuples of ${\widehat{Q}}_{123}\left( {\widehat{\mathcal{D}}}_{123}^{\prime }\right)$ . This follows from the observation that the three intervals ${\operatorname{seg}}_{{N}^{\prime }}\left( {z}_{1}^{R}\right) ,{\operatorname{seg}}_{{N}^{\prime }}\left( {{z}_{1}^{S} \circ  {z}_{2}^{S}}\right)$ , ${\operatorname{seg}}_{{N}^{\prime }}\left( {z}_{1}^{T}\right.$ - $\left. {{z}_{2}^{T} \circ  {z}_{3}^{T}}\right)$ overlap if and only if ${z}_{1}^{R} = {z}_{1}^{S} = {z}_{1}^{T}$ and ${z}_{2}^{S} = {z}_{2}^{T}$ . (Recall that the bitstrings ${z}_{1}^{R},{z}_{1}^{S},\ldots$ all have the same length $\ell$ .) Moreover note that ${\bar{R}}^{\prime },{\bar{S}}^{\prime },{\bar{T}}^{\prime }$ have the same sizes as ${\widehat{R}}_{123}^{\prime },{\widehat{S}}_{123}^{\prime },{\widehat{T}}_{123}^{\prime }$ respectively and they can be constructed in linear time.

根据文献[3]可证，${\overline{\mathcal{Q}}}_{ \vartriangle  }\left( {\overline{\mathcal{D}}}^{\prime }\right)$与${\widehat{Q}}_{123}\left( {\widehat{\mathcal{D}}}_{123}^{\prime }\right)$的输出元组存在一一映射关系。这是因为当且仅当${z}_{1}^{R} = {z}_{1}^{S} = {z}_{1}^{T}$且${z}_{2}^{S} = {z}_{2}^{T}$时，三个区间${\operatorname{seg}}_{{N}^{\prime }}\left( {z}_{1}^{R}\right) ,{\operatorname{seg}}_{{N}^{\prime }}\left( {{z}_{1}^{S} \circ  {z}_{2}^{S}}\right)$、${\operatorname{seg}}_{{N}^{\prime }}\left( {z}_{1}^{T}\right.$-$\left. {{z}_{2}^{T} \circ  {z}_{3}^{T}}\right)$才会重叠（注意所有比特串${z}_{1}^{R},{z}_{1}^{S},\ldots$长度均为$\ell$）。此外需注意${\bar{R}}^{\prime },{\bar{S}}^{\prime },{\bar{T}}^{\prime }$与${\widehat{R}}_{123}^{\prime },{\widehat{S}}_{123}^{\prime },{\widehat{T}}_{123}^{\prime }$大小分别相同，且均可线性时间内构建。

## 7 IVM: INSERT-DELETE SETTING

## 7 增量视图维护：插入-删除场景

In this section, we give a brief overview of how we obtain our upper and lower bounds for IVM in the insert-delete setting from Theorem 4.4 and 4.5, respectively. Details are deferred to Appendix E.

本节简要概述如何分别基于定理4.4和4.5，在插入-删除场景下获得IVM的上界与下界。具体细节详见附录E。

### 7.1 Upper bound for ${\mathrm{{IVM}}}^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$

### 7.1 ${\mathrm{{IVM}}}^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$的上界证明

In order to prove Theorem 4.4, we start by describing an algorithm that meets a weaker version of the upper bound in Theorem 4.4,where the current database size $\left| {\mathcal{D}}^{\left( \tau \right) }\right|$ is replaced by the number of single-tuple updates $N$ : (Note that $\left| {\mathcal{D}}^{\left( \tau \right) }\right|  \leq  N$ and could be unboundedly smaller.)

为证明定理4.4，我们首先描述一个满足较弱上界条件的算法，其中当前数据库规模$\left| {\mathcal{D}}^{\left( \tau \right) }\right|$被替换为单次元组更新次数$N$：（注意$\left| {\mathcal{D}}^{\left( \tau \right) }\right|  \leq  N$可能无限小）

LEMMA 7.1. For any query $Q$ ,both ${\mathrm{{IVM}}}^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$ and ${\mathrm{{IVM}}}_{\delta }^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$ can be solved with $\widetilde{O}\left( {N}^{\mathrm{w}\left( \widehat{Q}\right)  - 1}\right)$ amortized update time and non-amortized constant enumeration delay,where $\widehat{Q}$ is the multivariate extension of $Q$ and $N$ is the number of single-tuple updates.

引理7.1. 对于任意查询$Q$，${\mathrm{{IVM}}}^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$和${\mathrm{{IVM}}}_{\delta }^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$均可通过$\widetilde{O}\left( {N}^{\mathrm{w}\left( \widehat{Q}\right)  - 1}\right)$摊还更新时间和非摊还常数枚举延迟求解，其中$\widehat{Q}$是$Q$的多变量扩展，$N$表示单次元组更新次数。

In particular, we can show that we can use the algorithm from Lemma 7.1 as a black box in order to meet the stronger upper bound in Theorem 4.4. The main reason why the algorithm from Lemma 7.1 does not immediately meet the upper bound from Theorem 4.4 is that $N$ can grow much larger than the database size $\left| \mathcal{D}\right|$ ,especially in the scenario where the update stream contains many deletes. However, whenever that happens, we can "reset" the algorithm from Lemma 7.1 by restarting from scratch and inserting all the tuples in the current database $\mathcal{D}$ as a stream of $\left| \mathcal{D}\right|$ inserts. If this is done carefully,then we can ensure that the total number of updates since the last reset is not significantly larger than $\left| \mathcal{D}\right|$ . Appendix E. 3 gives the details.

特别地，我们可以将引理7.1的算法作为黑盒使用以满足定理4.4的更强上界。该算法不能直接满足定理4.4上界的主要原因是$N$可能远大于数据库规模$\left| \mathcal{D}\right|$，尤其在更新流包含大量删除操作时。此时可通过重启算法实现"重置"：将当前数据库$\mathcal{D}$的所有元组作为$\left| \mathcal{D}\right|$次插入流重新处理。若操作得当，可确保上次重置后的总更新次数不超过$\left| \mathcal{D}\right|$量级。详见附录E.3。

We now focus on demonstrating our algorithm that proves Lemma 7.1 for IVM ${}^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$ . Algorithm 2 in Appendix E. 1 gives the general algorithm. Appendix E. 2 shows how to extend it to ${\operatorname{IVM}}_{\delta }^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$ .

现重点演示证明引理7.1中IVM${}^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$的算法。附录E.1的算法2给出通用框架，附录E.2阐述其扩展至${\operatorname{IVM}}_{\delta }^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$的方法。

Example 7.2 (for Lemma 7.1). Suppose we want to solve the problem ${\mathrm{{IVM}}}^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  {Q}_{\Delta }\right\rbrack$ for ${Q}_{\Delta }$ in Eq. (1),where we have a stream of $N$ single-tuple inserts/deletes into $R,S$ and $T$ . For simplicity,assume that $N$ is known in advance. (If $N$ is not known in advance,we can initially assume $N$ to be a constant and keep doubling $N$ every time we exceed it. See Remark E. 1 for details.) We introduce a new interval variable $\left\lbrack  Z\right\rbrack$ and use it to represent time. Specifically, $\left\lbrack  Z\right\rbrack$ will represent the lifespan of every tuple in the database,in the spirit of temporal databases [19]. By adding $\left\lbrack  Z\right\rbrack$ to every atom,we obtain the time extension ${\bar{Q}}_{\bigtriangleup }$ of ${Q}_{\bigtriangleup }$ in Eq. (14). Suppose that the $\tau$ -th update is an insert of a tuple(a,b)into $R$ ,i.e. $+ R\left( {a,b}\right)$ . Then,we apply the insert $+ \bar{R}\left( {\left\lbrack  {\tau ,\infty }\right\rbrack  ,a,b}\right)$ into the time extension,indicating that the tuple(a,b)lives in $R$ from time $\tau$ on (since we don’t know yet its future deletion time). Now suppose that the ${\tau }^{\prime }$ -th update (for some ${\tau }^{\prime } > \tau$ ) is a delete of the same tuple(a,b)in $R$ . Then,we replace the tuple $\left( {\left\lbrack  {\tau ,\infty }\right\rbrack  ,a,b}\right)$ in $\bar{R}$ with $\left( {\left\lbrack  {\tau ,{\tau }^{\prime }}\right\rbrack  ,a,b}\right)$ . We can extract the result of ${Q}_{\bigtriangleup }$ at any time $\tau$ by selecting output tuples $\left( {\left\lbrack  z\right\rbrack  ,a,b,c}\right)$ of ${\bar{Q}}_{\bigtriangleup }$ where the interval $\left\lbrack  z\right\rbrack$ contains the current time $\tau$ . Therefore,if we can efficiently maintain ${\bar{Q}}_{\bigtriangleup }$ ,we can efficiently maintain ${Q}_{\bigtriangleup }$ . Table 2 shows the same stream of 8 updates into ${Q}_{\bigtriangleup }$ as Table 1 but adds the corresponding updates to ${\bar{Q}}_{\bigtriangleup }$ . The final database $\overline{\mathcal{D}}$ after all updates have taken place is the same as the one shown in Figure 1.

例7.2（针对引理7.1）。假设我们需要求解方程(1)中关于${Q}_{\Delta }$的问题${\mathrm{{IVM}}}^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  {Q}_{\Delta }\right\rbrack$，其中存在持续向$R,S$和$T$进行$N$次单元组插入/删除的数据流。为简化起见，假定$N$预先已知（若$N$未知，可先假设其为常数并在每次超出时倍增，详见备注E.1）。我们引入新的区间变量$\left\lbrack  Z\right\rbrack$来表示时间——具体而言，该变量将遵循时态数据库[19]的理念，表征数据库中每个元组的生存周期。通过为每个原子添加$\left\lbrack  Z\right\rbrack$，我们得到了方程(14)中${Q}_{\bigtriangleup }$的时间扩展${\bar{Q}}_{\bigtriangleup }$。假设第$\tau$次更新是向$R$插入元组(a,b)（即$+ R\left( {a,b}\right)$），则我们对该时间扩展执行插入操作$+ \bar{R}\left( {\left\lbrack  {\tau ,\infty }\right\rbrack  ,a,b}\right)$，表明元组(a,b)自时刻$\tau$起存在于$R$中（因其删除时间尚未可知）。若第${\tau }^{\prime }$次更新（满足${\tau }^{\prime } > \tau$）要删除$R$中的相同元组(a,b)，则将$\bar{R}$中的元组$\left( {\left\lbrack  {\tau ,\infty }\right\rbrack  ,a,b}\right)$替换为$\left( {\left\lbrack  {\tau ,{\tau }^{\prime }}\right\rbrack  ,a,b}\right)$。通过筛选${\bar{Q}}_{\bigtriangleup }$中区间$\left\lbrack  z\right\rbrack$包含当前时刻$\tau$的输出元组$\left( {\left\lbrack  z\right\rbrack  ,a,b,c}\right)$，可随时获取${Q}_{\bigtriangleup }$的结果。因此，若能高效维护${\bar{Q}}_{\bigtriangleup }$，就能高效维护${Q}_{\bigtriangleup }$。表2展示了与表1相同的8次更新流作用于${Q}_{\bigtriangleup }$的过程，并补充了对应${\bar{Q}}_{\bigtriangleup }$的更新操作。所有更新完成后的最终数据库$\overline{\mathcal{D}}$与图1所示一致。

To maintain ${\widehat{Q}}_{\bigtriangleup }$ ,we use the six components ${\widehat{Q}}_{123},\ldots ,{\widehat{Q}}_{321}$ of the multivariate extension ${\widehat{Q}}_{\bigtriangleup }$ of ${Q}_{\bigtriangleup }$ from Example 3.4. In particular,we construct and maintain the relations ${\widehat{R}}_{123},{\widehat{S}}_{123}$ and ${\widehat{T}}_{123}$ from Eq. (16) and forth. We use them along with Theorem 4.1 to maintain ${\widehat{Q}}_{123}$ (and the same goes for ${\widehat{Q}}_{132},\ldots$ ). For example,suppose that the number of updates $N$ is 8,and consider the insert $+ S\left( {{b}_{1},{c}_{1}}\right)$ at time 2 in Table 2. This insert corresponds to inserting the following tuples into ${\widehat{S}}_{123}$ : (Recall Eq. (15). Below, ${z}_{1}$ and ${z}_{2}$ are bitstrings and ${z}_{1} \circ  {z}_{2}$ is their concatenation.)

为维持${\widehat{Q}}_{\bigtriangleup }$，我们采用示例3.4中${Q}_{\bigtriangleup }$多元扩展${\widehat{Q}}_{\bigtriangleup }$的六个分量${\widehat{Q}}_{123},\ldots ,{\widehat{Q}}_{321}$。具体而言，我们构建并维护方程(16)及后续内容中的关系${\widehat{R}}_{123},{\widehat{S}}_{123}$与${\widehat{T}}_{123}$。结合定理4.1，我们得以维持${\widehat{Q}}_{123}$（同理适用于${\widehat{Q}}_{132},\ldots$）。例如，假设更新次数$N$为8次，现考虑表2中时间点2的插入操作$+ S\left( {{b}_{1},{c}_{1}}\right)$。该插入对应于向${\widehat{S}}_{123}$添加如下元组：（参见方程(15)。下述${z}_{1}$与${z}_{2}$为比特串，${z}_{1} \circ  {z}_{2}$表示其拼接结果。）

$$
 + \left\{  {\left( {{z}_{1},{z}_{2},{b}_{1},{c}_{1}}\right)  \mid  \left( {{z}_{1} \circ  {z}_{2}}\right)  \in  {\mathrm{{CP}}}_{8}\left( \left\lbrack  {2,8}\right\rbrack  \right) }\right\}  
$$

The corresponding delete $- S\left( {{b}_{1},{c}_{1}}\right)$ at time 5 corresponds to deleting and inserting the following two sets of tuples from/to ${\widehat{S}}_{123}$ :

在时间点5对应的删除操作$- S\left( {{b}_{1},{c}_{1}}\right)$涉及从${\widehat{S}}_{123}$中删除及插入以下两组元组：

$$
 - \left\{  {\left( {{z}_{1},{z}_{2},{b}_{1},{c}_{1}}\right)  \mid  \left( {{z}_{1} \circ  {z}_{2}}\right)  \in  {\mathrm{{CP}}}_{8}\left( \left\lbrack  {2,8}\right\rbrack  \right) }\right\}  \; + \left\{  {\left( {{z}_{1},{z}_{2},{b}_{1},{c}_{1}}\right)  \mid  \left( {{z}_{1} \circ  {z}_{2}}\right)  \in  {\mathrm{{CP}}}_{8}\left( \left\lbrack  {2,5}\right\rbrack  \right) }\right\}  
$$

Out of the box, Theorem 4.1 is only limited to inserts. However, we can show that these pairs of inserts/deletes have a special structure that allows us to maintain the guarantees of Theorem 4.1. In particular, at time 5 , when we truncate a tuple $t$ from $\left\lbrack  {2,8}\right\rbrack$ to $\left\lbrack  {2,5}\right\rbrack$ ,even after truncation, $t$ still joins with the same set of tuples that it used to join with before the truncation. This is because the only intervals that overlap with $\left\lbrack  {2,8}\right\rbrack$ but not with $\left\lbrack  {2,5}\right\rbrack$ are the ones that start after 5. But these intervals are in the future, hence they don't exist yet in the database (see Proposition E.2). We use this idea to amortize the cost of truncations over the inserts.

默认情况下，定理4.1仅适用于插入操作。但我们可以证明这些成对的插入/删除具有特殊结构，能维持定理4.1的保证。具体而言，在时间点5将元组<b0></b1>从$\left\lbrack  {2,8}\right\rbrack$截断至$\left\lbrack  {2,5}\right\rbrack$后，$t$仍能与截断前相同的元组集合进行连接。这是因为与$\left\lbrack  {2,8}\right\rbrack$重叠但不与$\left\lbrack  {2,5}\right\rbrack$重叠的时间区间仅始于5之后，而这些未来区间尚未存在于数据库中（参见命题E.2）。我们利用此特性将截断操作的成本分摊至插入操作。

For ${\mathrm{{IVM}}}^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  {Q}_{\Delta }\right\rbrack$ ,our target is to do constant-delay enumeration of the full output ${Q}_{\Delta }$ at time $\tau$ . To that end,we enumerate tuples $\left( {\left\lbrack  z\right\rbrack  ,a,b,c}\right)$ of ${\bar{Q}}_{\Delta }$ where the interval $\left\lbrack  z\right\rbrack$ contains the current time $\tau$ . And to enumerate those,we enumerate tuples $\left( {{z}_{1},{z}_{2},{z}_{3},a,b,c}\right)$ from ${\widehat{Q}}_{123},\ldots ,{\widehat{Q}}_{321}$ where ${z}_{1} \circ  {z}_{2} \circ  {z}_{3}$ corresponds to an interval in ${\mathrm{{CP}}}_{N}\left( \left\lbrack  z\right\rbrack  \right)$ that contains $\tau$ . This is a selection condition over $\left( {{z}_{1},{z}_{2},{z}_{3}}\right)$ . By construction,the tree decomposition of any component,say ${\widehat{Q}}_{123}$ ,must contain a bag $W$ that contains the variables $\left\{  {{z}_{1},{z}_{2},{z}_{3}}\right\}$ . This is because,by Definition 3.3, ${\widehat{Q}}_{123}$ contains an atom ${\widehat{T}}_{123}$ that contains these variables. We designate $W$ as the root of tree decomposition of ${\widehat{Q}}_{123}$ and start our constant-delay enumeration from $W$ . The same goes for ${\widehat{Q}}_{132},\ldots ,{\widehat{Q}}_{321}$ . Moreover,the enumeration outputs of ${\widehat{Q}}_{123},\ldots ,{\widehat{Q}}_{321}$ are disjoint in this case. See the proof of Lemma E.3.

对于${\mathrm{{IVM}}}^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  {Q}_{\Delta }\right\rbrack$，我们的目标是在时间$\tau$实现全输出${Q}_{\Delta }$的恒定延迟枚举。为此，我们枚举${\bar{Q}}_{\Delta }$中满足区间$\left\lbrack  z\right\rbrack$包含当前时间$\tau$的元组$\left( {\left\lbrack  z\right\rbrack  ,a,b,c}\right)$。具体实现时，从${\widehat{Q}}_{123},\ldots ,{\widehat{Q}}_{321}$枚举满足${z}_{1} \circ  {z}_{2} \circ  {z}_{3}$对应${\mathrm{{CP}}}_{N}\left( \left\lbrack  z\right\rbrack  \right)$中包含$\tau$区间的元组$\left( {{z}_{1},{z}_{2},{z}_{3},a,b,c}\right)$——这本质上是针对$\left( {{z}_{1},{z}_{2},{z}_{3}}\right)$的选择条件。根据构造原理，任何组件（例如${\widehat{Q}}_{123}$）的树分解必须包含含变量$\left\{  {{z}_{1},{z}_{2},{z}_{3}}\right\}$的包$W$，因为根据定义3.3，${\widehat{Q}}_{123}$包含含有这些变量的原子${\widehat{T}}_{123}$。我们将$W$指定为${\widehat{Q}}_{123}$树分解的根节点，并从此开始恒定延迟枚举。${\widehat{Q}}_{132},\ldots ,{\widehat{Q}}_{321}$同理。此外，本例中${\widehat{Q}}_{123},\ldots ,{\widehat{Q}}_{321}$的枚举输出互不相交。详见引理E.3的证明。

For ${\mathrm{{IVM}}}_{\delta }^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  {Q}_{\Delta }\right\rbrack$ ,our target is to enumerate the change to the output at time $\tau$ . To that end,we enumerate tuples $\left( {\left\lbrack  z\right\rbrack  ,a,b,c}\right)$ of ${\bar{Q}}_{\Delta }$ where the interval $\left\lbrack  z\right\rbrack$ has $\tau$ as an endpoint. Appendix E. 2 explains the technical challenges and how to address them.

对于${\mathrm{{IVM}}}_{\delta }^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  {Q}_{\Delta }\right\rbrack$，我们的目标是枚举时刻$\tau$输出的变化量。为此，我们枚举${\bar{Q}}_{\Delta }$的元组$\left( {\left\lbrack  z\right\rbrack  ,a,b,c}\right)$，其中区间$\left\lbrack  z\right\rbrack$以$\tau$为端点。附录E.2阐述了技术难点及解决方案。

### 7.2 Lower bound for ${\mathrm{{IVM}}}_{\delta }^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$

### 7.2 ${\mathrm{{IVM}}}_{\delta }^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$的下界证明

In order to prove the lower bound from Theorem 4.5,we prove a stronger lower bound where $\left| {\mathcal{D}}^{\left( \tau \right) }\right|$ is replaced by the number of single-tuple updates $N$ . The following example demonstrates our lower bound for ${\operatorname{IVM}}_{\delta }^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$ ,which is based on a reduction from the static evaluation of a component ${\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }$ of the multivariate extension of $Q$ to ${\operatorname{IVM}}_{\delta }^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$ . Algorithm 3 in Appendix E. 4 details the reduction.

为证明定理4.5中的下界，我们建立了一个更强的下界模型：将$\left| {\mathcal{D}}^{\left( \tau \right) }\right|$替换为单次元组更新次数$N$。以下示例通过将$Q$多元扩展中组件${\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }$的静态求值归约为${\operatorname{IVM}}_{\delta }^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$，展示了我们针对${\operatorname{IVM}}_{\delta }^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$的下界证明。附录E.4的算法3详述了该归约过程。

Example 7.3 (for Theorem 4.5). Consider ${Q}_{\Delta }$ from Eq. (1) and ${\widehat{Q}}_{123}$ from Eq. (8). Suppose that there exists $\gamma  > 0$ such that ${\operatorname{IVM}}_{\delta }^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  {Q}_{\Delta }\right\rbrack$ can be solved with amortized update time $\widetilde{O}\left( {\left| {\mathcal{D}}^{\left( \tau \right) }\right| }^{\kappa }\right)$ where $\left| {\mathcal{D}}^{\left( \tau \right) }\right|$ is the database size, $\kappa  \doteq$ $\omega \left( {\widehat{Q}}_{123}\right)  - 1 - \gamma$ ,and $\omega$ is given by Definition 3.2. (Note that $\widetilde{O}\left( {\left| {\mathcal{D}}^{\left( \tau \right) }\right| }^{\kappa }\right)  = \widetilde{O}\left( {N}^{\kappa }\right)$ since $\left| {\mathcal{D}}^{\left( \tau \right) }\right|  \leq  N$ .) We show that this implies that $\operatorname{Eval}\left\lbrack  {\widehat{Q}}_{123}\right\rbrack$ can be solved in time $\widetilde{O}\left( {{\left| {\widehat{\mathcal{D}}}_{123}\right| }^{\omega \left( {\widehat{Q}}_{123}\right)  - \gamma } + \left| {{\widehat{Q}}_{123}\left( {\widehat{\mathcal{D}}}_{123}\right) }\right| }\right)$ on any database instance ${\widehat{\mathcal{D}}}_{123}$ , thus contradicting the definition of $\omega \left( {\widehat{Q}}_{123}\right)$ .

示例7.3（对应定理4.5）。设${Q}_{\Delta }$来自式(1)，${\widehat{Q}}_{123}$来自式(8)。假设存在$\gamma  > 0$使得${\operatorname{IVM}}_{\delta }^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  {Q}_{\Delta }\right\rbrack$能以摊销更新时间<b4></b5>求解（其中$\left| {\mathcal{D}}^{\left( \tau \right) }\right|$表示数据库规模，$\kappa  \doteq$$\omega \left( {\widehat{Q}}_{123}\right)  - 1 - \gamma$，且$\omega$由定义3.2给出。注：因$\left| {\mathcal{D}}^{\left( \tau \right) }\right|  \leq  N$故$\widetilde{O}\left( {\left| {\mathcal{D}}^{\left( \tau \right) }\right| }^{\kappa }\right)  = \widetilde{O}\left( {N}^{\kappa }\right)$）。我们将证明这意味着可在$\widetilde{O}\left( {{\left| {\widehat{\mathcal{D}}}_{123}\right| }^{\omega \left( {\widehat{Q}}_{123}\right)  - \gamma } + \left| {{\widehat{Q}}_{123}\left( {\widehat{\mathcal{D}}}_{123}\right) }\right| }\right)$时间内求解任意数据库实例${\widehat{\mathcal{D}}}_{123}$上的$\operatorname{Eval}\left\lbrack  {\widehat{Q}}_{123}\right\rbrack$，这与$\omega \left( {\widehat{Q}}_{123}\right)$的定义相矛盾。

Let us take an arbitrary database instance ${\widehat{\mathcal{D}}}_{123} = \left( {{\widehat{R}}_{123},{\widehat{S}}_{123},{\widehat{T}}_{123}}\right)$ and evaluate ${\widehat{\mathcal{Q}}}_{123}$ over ${\widehat{\mathcal{D}}}_{123}$ using the oracle that solves ${\mathrm{{IVM}}}_{\delta }^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  {Q}_{\Delta }\right\rbrack$ . Example 6.5 shows how to reduce $\operatorname{Eval}\left\lbrack  {\widehat{Q}}_{123}\right\rbrack$ over ${\widehat{\mathcal{D}}}_{123}$ to the static evaluation of ${\bar{Q}}_{\Delta }$ from Eq. (14) over a database instance $\overline{\mathcal{D}} = \left( {\bar{R},\bar{S},\bar{T}}\right)$ that satisfies: $\left| \overline{\mathcal{D}}\right|  = \left| {\widehat{\mathcal{D}}}_{123}\right|$ and $\left| {{\bar{Q}}_{\Delta }\left( \overline{\mathcal{D}}\right) }\right|  = \left| {{\widehat{Q}}_{123}\left( {\widehat{\mathcal{D}}}_{123}\right) }\right|$ . Moreover, $\overline{\mathcal{D}}$ can be constructed in linear time (Theorem D.8). Next,we reduce the evaluation of ${\bar{Q}}_{\Delta }$ over $\overline{\mathcal{D}}$ into ${\operatorname{IVM}}_{\delta }^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  {Q}_{\Delta }\right\rbrack$ .

让我们选取一个任意的数据库实例${\widehat{\mathcal{D}}}_{123} = \left( {{\widehat{R}}_{123},{\widehat{S}}_{123},{\widehat{T}}_{123}}\right)$，并利用解决${\mathrm{{IVM}}}_{\delta }^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  {Q}_{\Delta }\right\rbrack$的预言机在${\widehat{\mathcal{D}}}_{123}$上评估${\widehat{\mathcal{Q}}}_{123}$。示例6.5展示了如何将${\widehat{\mathcal{D}}}_{123}$上的$\operatorname{Eval}\left\lbrack  {\widehat{Q}}_{123}\right\rbrack$规约到对满足$\left| \overline{\mathcal{D}}\right|  = \left| {\widehat{\mathcal{D}}}_{123}\right|$和$\left| {{\bar{Q}}_{\Delta }\left( \overline{\mathcal{D}}\right) }\right|  = \left| {{\widehat{Q}}_{123}\left( {\widehat{\mathcal{D}}}_{123}\right) }\right|$条件的数据库实例$\overline{\mathcal{D}} = \left( {\bar{R},\bar{S},\bar{T}}\right)$上，基于方程(14)的${\bar{Q}}_{\Delta }$静态评估。此外，$\overline{\mathcal{D}}$可在线性时间内构建完成（定理D.8）。接着，我们将$\overline{\mathcal{D}}$上的${\bar{Q}}_{\Delta }$评估进一步规约为${\operatorname{IVM}}_{\delta }^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  {Q}_{\Delta }\right\rbrack$。

We interpret the interval $\left\lbrack  Z\right\rbrack$ of a tuple in ${\bar{Q}}_{ \bigtriangleup  }$ as the "lifespan" of a corresponding tuple in ${Q}_{ \bigtriangleup  }$ ,similar to Section 7.1. In particular,we initialize empty relations $R,S,T$ to be used as input for ${Q}_{\Delta }$ ,which will be maintained under a sequence of updates for the ${\mathrm{{IVM}}}_{\delta }^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  {Q}_{\Delta }\right\rbrack$ problem. We also sort the tuples $\left( {\left\lbrack  {\alpha ,\beta }\right\rbrack  ,a,b}\right)$ of $\bar{R}$ based on the end points $\alpha$ and $\beta$ of their $\left\lbrack  Z\right\rbrack$ -intervals. Each tuple $\left( {\left\lbrack  {\alpha ,\beta }\right\rbrack  ,a,b}\right)$ appears twice in the order: once paired with its beginning $\alpha$ and another with its end $\beta$ . Similarly,we add the tuples $\left( {\left\lbrack  {\alpha ,\beta }\right\rbrack  ,b,c}\right)$ of $\bar{S}$ and $\left( {\left\lbrack  {\alpha ,\beta }\right\rbrack  ,a,c}\right)$ of $\bar{T}$ to the same sorted list,where each tuple appears twice. Now,we go through the list in order. If the next tuple $\left( {\left\lbrack  {\alpha ,\beta }\right\rbrack  ,a,b}\right)$ is paired with its beginning $\alpha$ , then we insert the tuple(a,b)into $R$ . However,if the next tuple $\left( {\left\lbrack  {\alpha ,\beta }\right\rbrack  ,a,b}\right)$ is paired with its end $\beta$ ,then we delete (a,b)from $R$ . The same goes for $S$ and $T$ . The number of updates, $N$ ,is $2\left| \mathcal{D}\right|$ ,and the total time needed to process them is $\widetilde{O}\left( {\left| \overline{\mathcal{D}}\right| }^{\kappa  + 1}\right)$ .

我们将${\bar{Q}}_{ \bigtriangleup  }$中元组的区间$\left\lbrack  Z\right\rbrack$解释为${Q}_{ \bigtriangleup  }$中对应元组的"生命周期"，类似于第7.1节所述。具体而言，我们初始化空关系$R,S,T$作为${Q}_{\Delta }$的输入，这些关系将在${\mathrm{{IVM}}}_{\delta }^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  {Q}_{\Delta }\right\rbrack$问题的更新序列下维护。我们还根据$\bar{R}$中元组$\left( {\left\lbrack  {\alpha ,\beta }\right\rbrack  ,a,b}\right)$的$\left\lbrack  Z\right\rbrack$区间端点$\alpha$和$\beta$对其进行排序。每个元组$\left( {\left\lbrack  {\alpha ,\beta }\right\rbrack  ,a,b}\right)$在排序中出现两次：一次与其起始点$\alpha$配对，另一次与其终止点$\beta$配对。同样地，我们将$\bar{S}$的元组$\left( {\left\lbrack  {\alpha ,\beta }\right\rbrack  ,b,c}\right)$和$\bar{T}$的元组$\left( {\left\lbrack  {\alpha ,\beta }\right\rbrack  ,a,c}\right)$加入同一排序列表，其中每个元组也出现两次。接着按顺序遍历列表。若下一个元组$\left( {\left\lbrack  {\alpha ,\beta }\right\rbrack  ,a,b}\right)$与其起始点$\alpha$配对，则将元组(a,b)插入$R$；若与终止点$\beta$配对，则从$R$中删除(a,b)。对$S$和$T$也采取相同操作。更新次数$N$为$2\left| \mathcal{D}\right|$，处理这些更新所需总时间为$\widetilde{O}\left( {\left| \overline{\mathcal{D}}\right| }^{\kappa  + 1}\right)$。

To compute the output of ${\bar{Q}}_{\Delta }$ ,we do the following. After every insert or delete in ${Q}_{\Delta }$ ,we use the oracle for ${\mathrm{{IVM}}}_{\delta }^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  {Q}_{\Delta }\right\rbrack$ to enumerate the change in the output of ${Q}_{\Delta }$ . Whenever the oracle reports an insert of an output tuple (a,b,c)at time $\tau$ and the delete of the same tuple at a later time ${\tau }^{\prime } > \tau$ ,then we know that $\left( {\left\lbrack  {\tau ,{\tau }^{\prime }}\right\rbrack  ,a,b,c}\right)$ is an output tuple of ${\bar{Q}}_{\bigtriangleup }$ . Therefore,the overall time needed to compute the output of ${\bar{Q}}_{\bigtriangleup }$ (including the total update time from before) is $\widetilde{O}\left( {{\left| \overline{\mathcal{D}}\right| }^{\kappa  + 1} + \left| {{\bar{Q}}_{\Delta }\left( \overline{\mathcal{D}}\right) }\right| }\right)  = \widetilde{O}\left( {{\left| {\widehat{\mathcal{D}}}_{123}\right| }^{\kappa  + 1} + \left| {{\widehat{Q}}_{123}\left( {\widehat{\mathcal{D}}}_{123}\right) }\right| }\right)$ . This contradicts the definition of $\omega \left( {\widehat{Q}}_{123}\right)$ .

为计算${\bar{Q}}_{\Delta }$的输出，我们执行以下操作：每次在${Q}_{\Delta }$中插入或删除后，使用${\mathrm{{IVM}}}_{\delta }^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  {Q}_{\Delta }\right\rbrack$的预言机来枚举${Q}_{\Delta }$输出的变化。当预言机报告在时间$\tau$插入输出元组(a,b,c)并在较晚时间${\tau }^{\prime } > \tau$删除同一元组时，即可判定$\left( {\left\lbrack  {\tau ,{\tau }^{\prime }}\right\rbrack  ,a,b,c}\right)$是${\bar{Q}}_{\bigtriangleup }$的输出元组。因此，计算${\bar{Q}}_{\bigtriangleup }$输出所需的总时间（含此前所有更新时间）为$\widetilde{O}\left( {{\left| \overline{\mathcal{D}}\right| }^{\kappa  + 1} + \left| {{\bar{Q}}_{\Delta }\left( \overline{\mathcal{D}}\right) }\right| }\right)  = \widetilde{O}\left( {{\left| {\widehat{\mathcal{D}}}_{123}\right| }^{\kappa  + 1} + \left| {{\widehat{Q}}_{123}\left( {\widehat{\mathcal{D}}}_{123}\right) }\right| }\right)$，这与$\omega \left( {\widehat{Q}}_{123}\right)$的定义相矛盾。

Appendix E. 4 highlights the challenges in extending the above lower bound from IVM ${}_{\delta }^{ \pm  }$ to IVM ${}^{ \pm  }$ ,which remains an open problem.

附录E.4重点阐述了将上述下界从IVM${}_{\delta }^{ \pm  }$扩展到IVM${}^{ \pm  }$所面临的挑战，这仍是一个未解难题。

## 8 CONCLUSION

## 8 结论

This paper puts forward two-way reductions between the dynamic and static query evaluation problems that allow us to transfer a wide class of algorithms and lower bounds between the two problems. For the dynamic problem, the paper characterizes the complexity gap between the insert-only and the insert-delete settings. The proposed algorithms recover best known amortized update times and produce new ones. The matching lower bounds justify why our upper bounds are natural. The lower bound function $\omega \left( Q\right)$ that we define can be used as a general tool to assess the optimality of some algorithms in database theory.

本文提出了动态与静态查询评估问题间的双向归约方法，使得两类问题中的广泛算法及下界可以相互转化。针对动态问题，本文刻画了仅插入与插入-删除场景间的复杂度差异。所提算法不仅复现了已知最佳摊销更新时间，还产生了新的结果。匹配的下界证明我们的上界具有自然性。定义的下界函数$\omega \left( Q\right)$可作为评估数据库理论中某些算法最优性的通用工具。

The assumptions that the queries have all variables free and the input database be initially empty can be lifted without difficulty. To support arbitrary conjunctive queries, our algorithms need to consider free-connex tree decompositions, which ensure constant-delay enumeration of the tuples in the query result. To support non-empty initial databases, we can use a preprocessing phase to construct our data structure, where all initial tuples get the same starting timestamp. The complexities stated in the paper need to be changed to account for the size of the initial database as part of the size of the update sequence.

查询变量全自由且初始数据库为空的假设可轻松解除。为支持任意连接查询，算法需考虑自由连接树分解以保证查询结果元组的常数延迟枚举。对于非空初始数据库，可通过预处理阶段构建数据结构，所有初始元组赋予相同起始时间戳。论文所述复杂度需调整为将初始数据库规模计入更新序列规模。

Although the proposed dynamic algorithms MVIVM are designed to maintain base relations and the query output as sets, they can be extended to maintain bags. As in DBToaster [27] and F-IVM [25], MVIVM can be extended to maintain the multiplicity of each tuple in a base relation or view and enumerate each tuple in the query output together with its multiplicity.

尽管动态算法MVIVM设计用于维护基关系与查询输出集合，但可扩展至维护包(bag)结构。类似DBToaster[27]和F-IVM[25]，MVIVM能跟踪基关系或视图中每个元组的多重度，并在枚举查询输出时附带其多重度。

## A COMPARISON TO PRIOR IVM APPROACHES

## A 与既有IVM方法的比较

<!-- Media -->

<!-- figureText: ${Q}_{2p}^{\prime }$ ${Q}_{2p}^{\prime \prime }$ ${Q}_{3p}$ ${Q}_{ * }$ ID $A$ TBD ${Q}_{\square }$ ${Q}_{\Delta }$ ${Q}_{\Delta }^{\prime }$ B ${Q}_{3u}$ ${Q}_{4u}$ ② $B$ -->

<img src="https://cdn.noedgeai.com/01962f96-6177-7227-9eb4-989c6b2c72e5_20.jpg?x=405&y=360&w=880&h=601&r=0"/>

Fig. 3. Hypergraphs of the queries analyzed in this section. Atoms are represented by hyperedges.

图3. 本节分析查询的超图表示，原子谓词以超边呈现。

<!-- Media -->

In this section, we compare our MVIVM approach with existing IVM approaches. We later focus on two specific queries,namely ${Q}_{3p}$ and ${Q}_{\Delta }$ ,and explain in detail how they are maintained by all considered approaches. We focus on the problem ${\mathrm{{IVM}}}^{ \pm  }$ ,where the task is to process single-tuple inserts and deletes and enumerate the full query result upon each enumeration request. We consider the following existing approaches:

本节将MVIVM与现有IVM方法对比，重点分析${Q}_{3p}$和${Q}_{\Delta }$两个查询，详述各方法如何维护它们。我们聚焦${\mathrm{{IVM}}}^{ \pm  }$问题场景——处理单条元组增删操作并在每次枚举请求时输出完整查询结果。现有方法包括：

- Naïve: This approach recomputes the query result from scratch after each update.

- 朴素法：每次更新后重新计算查询结果

- Delta [11]: This is a first-order IVM approach that materializes the query result and computes the delta or the change to the query result for each update. It updates the query result with the delta result.

- Delta法[11]：一阶IVM方法，物化查询结果并计算每次更新的增量变化，用增量更新结果

- F-IVM [25,37]: This is a higher-order IVM approach that speeds up the delta computation using a view tree whose structure is modelled on a variable order. Its update times are shown to be at least as good as the prior higher-order IVM approach DBToaster [27].

- F-IVM[25,37]：高阶IVM方法，通过基于变量序构建的视图树加速增量计算，其更新时间不劣于前代高阶方法DBToaster[27]

- IVM ${}^{\epsilon }\left\lbrack  {{20},{21}}\right\rbrack$ : This is an adaptive IVM methodology that uses delta computation and materialized views and also takes the degrees of data values into account. It is adaptive in the sense that it treats updates referring to values with high degree differently from those with low degree. The specific attributes whose values are partitioned with regard to their degrees and the partitioning threshold that lead to the best update time can vary depending on the query structure. So far, there is no algorithm that produces the optimal partitioning strategy for arbitrary queries. Therefore, ${\mathrm{{IVM}}}^{\epsilon }$ is not a concrete algorithm for arbitrary queries but rather a methodology. It was shown in $\left\lbrack  {{20},{21}}\right\rbrack$ how to manually tailor this methodology to the triangle query and several non-hierarchical queries to achieve the worst-case optimal update time.

- IVM${}^{\epsilon }\left\lbrack  {{20},{21}}\right\rbrack$：自适应IVM方法论，结合增量计算与物化视图，并考量数据值的度数差异。其自适应性体现在区分处理高/低度数数据值的更新。最优的属性分区策略与阈值因查询结构而异，目前尚无通用算法能自动生成任意查询的最优分区方案，故${\mathrm{{IVM}}}^{\epsilon }$是方法论而非具体算法。文献$\left\lbrack  {{20},{21}}\right\rbrack$展示了如何针对三角形查询及若干非层次化查询手动应用该方法论以实现最坏情况最优更新时间。

- CROWN (Change pROpagation Without joiNs) [43]: This is a higher-order IVM approach tailored to free-connex $\left( {\alpha \text{-}}\right)$ acyclic queries. Its maintenance approach is in line with F-IVM,when applied to free-connex acyclic queries, yet it provides a more refined complexity analysis that takes the structure of the update sequence into account. For certain update sequences, such as insert-only or first-in-first-out, the amortized update time is constant. For the purpose of comparing against the other IVM approaches, which do not consider such a fine-grained analysis, we consider the complexity results of CROWN for arbitrary update sequences.

- CROWN（无连接变更传播）[43]：这是一种针对自由连接无环查询的高阶增量视图维护方法。其维护机制与F-IVM在自由连接无环查询上的应用一致，但提供了更精细的复杂度分析，该分析考虑了更新序列的结构特性。对于特定更新序列（如纯插入或先进先出序列），其摊还更新时间可达到常数级别。为与其他未进行此类细粒度分析的IVM方法对比，我们采用CROWN在任意更新序列下的复杂度结果。

Another higher-order IVM approach is DynYannakakis [17, 18], which can maintain acyclic queries. It achieves the same asymptotic update times as F-IVM and CROWN for acyclic queries.

另一高阶IVM方法是DynYannakakis[17,18]，可维护无环查询。对于无环查询，其渐进更新时间与F-IVM和CROWN相当。

We consider the following non-hierarchical join queries (all variables are free). They are visualized in Figure 3:

我们考察以下非层次化连接查询（所有变量均为自由变量），其可视化结果见图3：

$$
{Q}_{2p}^{\prime } = R\left( {A,B,C}\right)  \land  S\left( {A,B}\right)  \land  T\left( {B,C}\right)  \tag{17}
$$

$$
{Q}_{3p} = R\left( {A,B}\right)  \land  S\left( {B,C}\right)  \land  T\left( {C,D}\right)  \tag{18}
$$

$$
{Q}_{\bigtriangleup } = R\left( {A,B}\right)  \land  S\left( {B,C}\right)  \land  T\left( {A,C}\right)  \tag{19}
$$

$$
{Q}_{\bigtriangleup }^{\prime } = R\left( {A,B}\right)  \land  S\left( {B,C}\right)  \land  T\left( {A,C}\right)  \land  U\left( C\right)  \tag{20}
$$

$$
{Q}_{ * } = R\left( {A,B,C,D}\right)  \land  S\left( {A,B}\right)  \land  T\left( {B,C}\right)  \land  U\left( {B,D}\right)  \tag{21}
$$

$$
{Q}_{3u} = R\left( {A,B,C}\right)  \land  S\left( A\right)  \land  T\left( B\right)  \land  U\left( C\right)  \tag{22}
$$

$$
{Q}_{4u} = R\left( {A,B,C,D}\right)  \land  S\left( A\right)  \land  T\left( B\right)  \land  U\left( C\right)  \land  V\left( D\right)  \tag{23}
$$

$$
{Q}_{2p}^{\prime \prime } = R\left( A\right)  \land  S\left( {A,B}\right)  \land  T\left( {B,C}\right)  \land  U\left( C\right)  \tag{24}
$$

$$
{Q}_{\square } = R\left( {A,B}\right)  \land  S\left( {B,C}\right)  \land  T\left( {C,D}\right)  \land  U\left( {A,D}\right)  \tag{25}
$$

Figure 4 summarizes the update times of all aforementioned IVM approaches, give in data complexity. The update times of Naïve,Delta,and F-IVM are worst-case and those of CROWN,IVM ${}^{\epsilon }$ ,and MVIVM are amortized. The update times of MVIVM have an extra polylog( $\left| \mathcal{D}\right|$ ) factor,which is hidden by the $\widetilde{O}$ -notation. Since CROWN is designed for free-connex acyclic queries, we skip its complexity for the acyclic ones, which are marked "NA". It is not clear how to manually tailor the IVM ${}^{\epsilon }$ approach to all queries,thus some entries are marked "Open". In terms of query complexity, MVIVM has an extra factor of $k$ !,where $k$ is the number of input relations,due to the use of multivariate extensions (Definiton 3.3).

图4汇总了前述所有IVM方法在数据复杂度下的更新时间。Naïve、Delta和F-IVM的时间为最坏情况，CROWN、IVM${}^{\epsilon }$和MVIVM为摊还时间。MVIVM更新时间含额外polylog($\left| \mathcal{D}\right|$)因子，该因子被$\widetilde{O}$记号隐藏。因CROWN专为自由连接无环查询设计，其无环查询复杂度标为"NA"。IVM${}^{\epsilon }$方法难以手动适配所有查询，故部分条目标为"Open"。在查询复杂度方面，由于采用多元扩展（定义3.3），MVIVM需额外乘以$k$!因子，其中$k$为输入关系数。

We observe that, besides the last two queries, MVIVM is on par or outperforms the existing approaches. For the last two queries,it performs only worse than ${\mathrm{{IVM}}}^{\epsilon }$ . However,in contrast to ${\mathrm{{IVM}}}^{\epsilon }$ ,MVIVM is a systematic algorithm that works for all queries,whereas ${\mathrm{{IVM}}}^{\epsilon }$ is a general methodology where a heavy-light partitioning strategy needs to be manually tailored to a specific query. Such a strategy defines (i) on which tuples of variables in each relation to partition into heavy and light, and (ii) what is the heavy-light threshold for each relation partition. There are infinitely many possible partitioning thresholds and hence partitioning strategies. It is not possible to try them all out in order to find the best one. Therefore,so far there does not exist an algorithm that produces an optimal ${\mathrm{{IVM}}}^{\epsilon }$ strategy for any query. ${}^{5}$

观察可见，除最后两个查询外，MVIVM性能持平或优于现有方法。对于最后两个查询，其表现仅逊于${\mathrm{{IVM}}}^{\epsilon }$。但与${\mathrm{{IVM}}}^{\epsilon }$不同的是，MVIVM是适用于所有查询的系统化算法，而${\mathrm{{IVM}}}^{\epsilon }$是需要手动为特定查询定制轻重分区策略的方法论。该策略需定义：(i)每个关系中按哪些变量元组进行轻重分区；(ii)各关系分区的轻重阈值。由于存在无限多可能的分区阈值与策略，无法通过穷举找到最优解。因此目前尚无能为任意查询生成最优${\mathrm{{IVM}}}^{\epsilon }$策略的算法。${}^{5}$

In the following,we illustrate how the aforementioned IVM approaches maintain the 3-path query ${Q}_{3p}$ and the triangle query ${Q}_{\bigtriangleup }$ .

下文将阐述前述IVM方法如何维护3路径查询${Q}_{3p}$和三角形查询${Q}_{\bigtriangleup }$。

Convention. To simplify the following presentation,we denote a single tuple update to some relation $R\left( {A,B}\right)$ by ${\delta R}\left( {a,b}\right)$ ,which expresses that the tuple(a,b)is inserted to or deleted from $R$ . Whenever it simplifies the explanation of an update procedure, we do not distinguish between an insert and a delete and describe the main computation steps common to both cases.

约定：为简化表述，我们用${\delta R}\left( {a,b}\right)$表示对关系$R\left( {A,B}\right)$的单条元组更新，指代元组(a,b)在$R$中的插入或删除。在解释更新流程时，如无特殊需要，将不区分插入与删除操作，仅描述两者共有的核心计算步骤。

---

<!-- Footnote -->

${}^{5}$ At a technical level,the reason why MVIVM has a worse update time for a query like ${Q}_{\square }$ than IVM ${}^{\epsilon }$ is because MVIVM is limited to the fractional hypertree width,while for ${Q}_{\square }$ ,it is known that the submodular width is strictly smaller than the fractional hypertree width [5,6,30]. MVIVM does not immediately generalize to the submodular width because the query decomposition lemma (Lemma 5.2) does not. Known algorithms that meet the submodular width $\left\lbrack  {5,6}\right\rbrack$ are limited to the static setting and do not seem to generalize to the dynamic setting. We leave this generalization as an open problem.

${}^{5}$从技术层面看，MVIVM在${Q}_{\square }$类查询上更新时间逊于IVM${}^{\epsilon }$的原因在于：MVIVM受限于分数超树宽度，而已知${Q}_{\square }$的子模宽度严格小于分数超树宽度[5,6,30]。由于查询分解引理（引理5.2）的限制，MVIVM无法直接推广至子模宽度。现有满足子模宽度$\left\lbrack  {5,6}\right\rbrack$的算法仅适用于静态场景，尚未推广至动态场景。我们将此推广列为开放问题。

图4. 不同IVM方法在数据复杂度下处理问题${\mathrm{{IVM}}}^{ \pm  }$的更新时间。Naïve、Delta和F-IVM方法的更新时间为最坏情况，而CROWN、IVM${}^{\epsilon }$和MVIVM为均摊时间。$\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}$隐藏了多对数$\left( \left| \mathcal{D}\right| \right)$因子。查询如图3所示并由公式(17)-(25)给出。MVIVM是本文提出的方法。CROWN不适用于循环查询，故部分条目标记为"NA"。IVM${}^{\epsilon }$并非具体算法而是通用方法论，由于难以手动配置所有查询，部分条目标记为"Open"。在查询复杂度中，MVIVM具有查询依赖因子$k$!，其中$k$为输入关系数量。

<!-- Media -->

### A.1 Naïve

### A.1 朴素方法

After each update, Naïve computes from scratch a data structure that allows for the constant-delay enumeration of the query result. The update time is given by the time to compute such a data structure. Using the Yannakakis algorithm [45],we can compute in $O\left( \left| \mathcal{D}\right| \right)$ time such a data structure for ${Q}_{3p}$ . For ${Q}_{ \bigtriangleup  }$ ,the best known strategy to achieve constant-delay enumeration is to compute the query result using a worst-case optimal join algorithm,which takes $O\left( {\left| \mathcal{D}\right| }^{3/2}\right)$ time [33].

每次更新后，朴素方法重新计算支持恒定延迟枚举查询结果的数据结构。更新时间即构建该结构所需时长。使用Yannakakis算法[45]，可为${Q}_{3p}$在$O\left( \left| \mathcal{D}\right| \right)$时间内构建此结构。对于${Q}_{ \bigtriangleup  }$，已知最佳策略是通过最坏情况最优连接算法计算查询结果，耗时$O\left( {\left| \mathcal{D}\right| }^{3/2}\right)$[33]。

### A.2 Delta

### A.2 增量方法

Delta maintains the materialized query result and computes for each update the corresponding delta query. It updates the query result by iterating over the tuples in the result of the delta query and adding them to or deleting them from query result (depending on whether the update is an insert or a delete).

增量方法维护物化查询结果，并为每次更新计算对应的增量查询。通过遍历增量查询结果中的元组，将其添加至或从查询结果中删除（根据更新类型为插入或删除而定）。

3-Path Query. Given a single-tuple update ${\delta R}\left( {a,b}\right)$ to relation $R$ ,the delta of ${Q}_{3p}$ is defined as:

三路径查询。给定关系$R$的单元组更新${\delta R}\left( {a,b}\right)$，${Q}_{3p}$的增量定义为：

$$
\delta {Q}_{3p}\left( {a,b,C,D}\right)  = {\delta R}\left( {a,b}\right)  \land  S\left( {b,C}\right)  \land  T\left( {C,D}\right) ,
$$

where the lower case letters $a$ and $b$ signalize that the variables $A$ and $B$ are fixed to the values $a$ and respectively $b$ . Using the Yannakakis algorithm [45], we can enumerate the the result of the delta query with constant delay after linear preprocessing time. The deltas for updates to the other relations can be computed analogously. We conclude that the overall update time is $O\left( \left| \mathcal{D}\right| \right)$ .

其中小写字母$a$和$b$表示变量$A$与$B$分别固定为$a$和$b$。通过Yannakakis算法[45]，可在线性预处理后以恒定延迟枚举增量查询结果。其他关系的更新增量可类似计算，故总更新时间为$O\left( \left| \mathcal{D}\right| \right)$。

Triangle Query. Given a single-tuple update ${\delta R}\left( {a,b}\right)$ ,the delta of ${Q}_{\bigtriangleup }$ is defined as:

三角查询。给定单元组更新${\delta R}\left( {a,b}\right)$，${Q}_{\bigtriangleup }$的增量定义为：

$$
\delta {Q}_{\bigtriangleup }\left( {a,b,C}\right)  = {\delta R}\left( {a,b}\right)  \land  S\left( {b,C}\right)  \land  T\left( {a,C}\right) 
$$

To compute this delta query,we need to intersect the list of $C$ -values paired with $b$ in $S$ and the list of $C$ -values paired with $a$ in $T$ . Since both lists can be of size $\left| \mathcal{D}\right|$ ,the time to compute the intersection is $O\left( \left| \mathcal{D}\right| \right)$ . Updates to the other relations are handled analogously. We conclude that the update time is $O\left( \left| \mathcal{D}\right| \right)$ .

计算此增量查询需交叉验证$S$中与$b$配对的$C$值列表，及$T$中与$a$配对的$C$值列表。因两列表规模可达$\left| \mathcal{D}\right|$，交集计算时间为$O\left( \left| \mathcal{D}\right| \right)$。其他关系更新同理处理，故更新时间为$O\left( \left| \mathcal{D}\right| \right)$。

### A.3 F-IVM

### A.3 F-IVM

F-IVM uses delta computation and maintains materialized views besides the query result. The set of views is usually organized as a view tree. Given an update, it computes the delta of each view affected by the update.

F-IVM采用增量计算法，除查询结果外还维护物化视图集。视图集通常组织为视图树结构，根据更新内容计算受影响视图的增量。

3-Path Query. Figure 5 (left) shows a view tree for the path query ${Q}_{3p}$ . The leaves of the view tree are the input relations. A view with a single child is obtained from its child view by projecting away a variable. A view with more that one child is obtained by joining the child views.

三路径查询。图5(左)展示路径查询${Q}_{3p}$的视图树。树叶为输入关系，单子视图通过投影消除变量获得，多子视图通过连接子视图获得。

Fig. 5. Left: View tree used by F-IVM for the 3-path query ${Q}_{3p} = R\left( {A,B}\right)  \land  S\left( {B,C}\right)  \land  T\left( {C,D}\right)$ ; middle: delta view tree under a single-tuple update to relation $R$ ; right: delta view tree under a single-tuple update to relation $S$ .

图5. 左：F-IVM用于3路径查询${Q}_{3p} = R\left( {A,B}\right)  \land  S\left( {B,C}\right)  \land  T\left( {C,D}\right)$的视图树；中：关系$R$单元组更新时的增量视图树；右：关系$S$单元组更新时的增量视图树。

<!-- Media -->

Algorithm 1 shows how to enumerate from the view tree the result of ${Q}_{3p}$ with constant delay. We use the root view ${V}_{RST}$ to retrieve the $C$ -values that appear in the result tuples and use the view ${V}_{RS}$ and the relations $R$ and $T$ to fetch the matching $B - ,A -$ ,and $D$ -values.

算法1展示了如何以恒定延迟从视图树中枚举${Q}_{3p}$的结果。我们使用根视图${V}_{RST}$检索结果元组中的$C$值，并利用视图${V}_{RS}$及关系$R$、$T$获取匹配的$B - ,A -$和$D$值。

Next,we explain how the view tree is maintained under single-tuple updates to relations $R$ and $S$ . Updates to $T$ are handled analogously. Given a single-tuple update ${\delta R}\left( {a,b}\right)$ to relation $R$ ,we need to compute the deltas of all views in the view tree from the leaf $R$ to the root. Figure 5 (middle) shows the delta view tree where the delta views are highlighted in red. We obtain the delta view $\delta {V}_{R}$ from ${\delta R}$ by projecting the tuple(a,b)onto $B$ ,which requires constant time. We compute $\delta {V}_{RS}$ by iterating over all $C$ -values paired with $b$ in $S$ ,which takes at most linear time. We obtain $\delta {V}_{RS}^{\prime }$ from $\delta {V}_{RS}$ by projecting away $b$ from all tuples in the latter view,which takes at most linear time. The root delta view $\delta {V}_{RST}$ is obtained by computing the intersection of the $C$ -values in $\delta {V}_{RS}^{\prime }$ and those in ${V}_{T}$ ,which takes at most linear time.

接下来解释视图树在关系$R$和$S$单元组更新时的维护机制。对$T$的更新处理方式类似。给定关系$R$的单元组更新${\delta R}\left( {a,b}\right)$，需计算从叶节点$R$到根节点所有视图的增量。图5（中）红色高亮显示的增量视图树中，通过将元组(a,b)投影到$B$获取增量视图$\delta {V}_{R}$（耗时恒定）。通过遍历$S$中与$b$配对的$C$值计算$\delta {V}_{RS}$（至多线性时间）。从$\delta {V}_{RS}$中消去$b$得到$\delta {V}_{RS}^{\prime }$（至多线性时间）。根增量视图$\delta {V}_{RST}$通过计算$\delta {V}_{RS}^{\prime }$与${V}_{T}$中$C$值的交集获得（至多线性时间）。

<!-- Media -->

Algorithm 1 Enumeration of the result of ${Q}_{3p}$

算法1 ${Q}_{3p}$结果的枚举

---

$\mathbf{{Input}}$

	- The view tree for ${Q}_{3p}$ - Figure 5 (left)

	- ${Q}_{3p}$的视图树 - 图5（左）

$\mathbf{{Algorithm}}$

For each $C$ -value $c \in  {V}_{RST}$

对于每个$C$值$c \in  {V}_{RST}$

	for each $B$ -value $b$ such that $\left( {b,c}\right)  \in  {V}_{RS}$

	对于满足$\left( {b,c}\right)  \in  {V}_{RS}$的每个$B$值$b$

		for each $A$ -value $a$ such that $\left( {a,b}\right)  \in  R$

		对于满足$\left( {a,b}\right)  \in  R$的每个$A$值$a$

			for each $D$ -value $d$ such that $\left( {c,d}\right)  \in  T$

			对于满足$\left( {c,d}\right)  \in  T$的每个$D$值$d$

				output(a,b,c,d)

				输出(a,b,c,d)

<!-- Media -->

Given a single-tuple update ${\delta S}\left( {b,c}\right)$ to relation $S$ ,Figure 5 (right) shows the corresponding delta view tree. The computation of the delta view $\delta {V}_{RS}$ requires a constant-time lookup of $b$ in ${V}_{R}$ . The delta view $\delta {V}_{RS}^{\prime }$ is obtained from $\delta {V}_{RS}$ by projecting the tuple(b,c)onto $C$ ,which requires constant time. The computation of the root delta view $\delta {V}_{RST}$ requires a constant-time lookup of $c$ in ${V}_{T}$ .

给定关系$S$的单元组更新${\delta S}\left( {b,c}\right)$，图5（右）展示对应的增量视图树。增量视图$\delta {V}_{RS}$需在${V}_{R}$中对$b$进行恒定时间查找。通过将元组(b,c)投影到$C$从$\delta {V}_{RS}$获得$\delta {V}_{RS}^{\prime }$（耗时恒定）。根增量视图$\delta {V}_{RST}$需在${V}_{T}$中对$c$进行恒定时间查找。

We conclude that the view tree in Figure 5 (left) can be maintained in $O\left( \left| \mathcal{D}\right| \right)$ time,which means that the update time is $O\left( \left| \mathcal{D}\right| \right)$ .

由此得出图5（左）视图树可在$O\left( \left| \mathcal{D}\right| \right)$时间内维护，即更新时间复杂度为$O\left( \left| \mathcal{D}\right| \right)$。

Triangle Query. Figure 6 (left) shows a view tree for the triangle query ${Q}_{\Delta }$ . The view tree can be used as follows to enumerate the query result with constant delay. For each(A,B)-tuple(a,b)in ${V}_{RST}$ ,we iterate over the $C$ -values paired with(a,b)in ${V}_{ST}$ . For each such $c$ ,we output the tuple(a,b,c).

三角查询。图6（左）展示三角查询${Q}_{\Delta }$的视图树。该视图树可按以下方式以恒定延迟枚举查询结果：对于${V}_{RST}$中的每个(A,B)元组(a,b)，遍历${V}_{ST}$中与(a,b)配对的$C$值，对每个$c$输出元组(a,b,c)。

Figure 6 shows the delta view trees for single-tuple updates to $S$ (middle delta view tree) and $R$ (right delta view tree). The computation of delta views is similar to the case of the 3-path query. Hence, we only give the computation times. Given an update to $S$ ,all delta views on the path from $S$ to the root can be computed in linear time. Given an update to $R$ ,the delta view $\delta {V}_{RST}$ can be computed in constant time. For updates to $T$ ,the delta views (not shown in Figure 6) require at most linear computation time.

图6展示了针对$S$（中部增量视图树）和$R$（右侧增量视图树）的单次元组更新增量视图树。增量视图的计算过程与3路径查询情况类似，因此仅提供计算耗时。当$S$发生更新时，从$S$到根节点的路径上所有增量视图均可在线性时间内完成计算。对于$R$的更新，增量视图$\delta {V}_{RST}$可在常数时间内计算得出。而$T$的更新所涉及的增量视图（图6未展示）最多需要线性计算时间。

We conclude that F-IVM maintains the triangle query with $O\left( \left| \mathcal{D}\right| \right)$ update time.

我们得出结论：F-IVM能以$O\left( \left| \mathcal{D}\right| \right)$的更新时间复杂度维护三角形查询。

## A. $4{IVM}^{\epsilon }$

Similar to F-IVM,IVM ${}^{\epsilon }$ uses delta computation and materialized views. Additionally,it takes degrees of data values into account. It partitions relations based on value degrees. Since all relations appearing in the two queries ${Q}_{3p}$ and ${Q}_{\Delta }$ are binary,we focus here on the partitioning of binary relations. The partition of a relation $R\left( {A,B}\right)$ on variable $A$ consists of a light part ${R}^{L}$ and a heavy part ${R}^{H}$ defined as follows: ${R}^{L} = \left\{  {\left( {a,b}\right)  \in  R\left| \right| {\sigma }_{A = a}R\left|  < \right| \mathcal{D}{\left| \right| }^{1/2}}\right\}$ and ${R}^{H} = R \smallsetminus  {R}^{L}$ . It directly follows from the definition of the relation parts: for any $A$ -value $a$ ,it holds $\left| {{\sigma }_{A = a}{R}^{L}}\right|  < {\left| \mathcal{D}\right| }^{1/2}$ ; $\left| {{\pi }_{A}{R}^{H}}\right|  \leq  {\left| \mathcal{D}\right| }^{1/2}$ .

与F-IVM类似，IVM${}^{\epsilon }$采用增量计算与物化视图技术，并额外考量数据值的度数特征。该系统根据值度数对关系进行划分。由于出现在${Q}_{3p}$和${Q}_{\Delta }$两个查询中的所有关系均为二元关系，本文重点讨论二元关系的划分方法。关系$R\left( {A,B}\right)$基于变量$A$的划分包含轻量部分${R}^{L}$与重量部分${R}^{H}$，其定义如下：${R}^{L} = \left\{  {\left( {a,b}\right)  \in  R\left| \right| {\sigma }_{A = a}R\left|  < \right| \mathcal{D}{\left| \right| }^{1/2}}\right\}$且${R}^{H} = R \smallsetminus  {R}^{L}$。根据关系部分的定义可直接得出：对于任意$A$值$a$，始终满足$\left| {{\sigma }_{A = a}{R}^{L}}\right|  < {\left| \mathcal{D}\right| }^{1/2}$；$\left| {{\pi }_{A}{R}^{H}}\right|  \leq  {\left| \mathcal{D}\right| }^{1/2}$。

3-Path Query. We partition relation $S$ on variable $B$ . The other relations are not partitioned. Given $s \in  \{ H,L\}$ ,we denote ${Q}_{3p}^{s} = R\left( {A,B}\right) ,{S}^{s}\left( {B,C}\right) ,T\left( {C,D}\right)$ . The query ${Q}_{3p}$ can be rewritten as the disjoint union $\mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{s \in  \{ H,L\} }}{Q}_{3p}^{s}$ . IVM ${}^{\epsilon }$ creates the two view trees visualized in Figure 7 (top row). The first tree represents ${Q}_{3p}^{L}$ and the second one represents ${Q}_{3p}^{H}$ .

3路径查询。我们对关系$S$按变量$B$进行划分，其余关系保持未划分状态。给定$s \in  \{ H,L\}$时，记作${Q}_{3p}^{s} = R\left( {A,B}\right) ,{S}^{s}\left( {B,C}\right) ,T\left( {C,D}\right)$。查询${Q}_{3p}$可重写为互斥并集$\mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{s \in  \{ H,L\} }}{Q}_{3p}^{s}$。IVM${}^{\epsilon }$创建了图7（上排）所示的两棵视图树，第一棵树表示${Q}_{3p}^{L}$，第二棵表示${Q}_{3p}^{H}$。
