Fig. 7. Top row: View trees used by ${\mathrm{{IVM}}}^{\epsilon }$ for the 3-path query ${Q}_{3p} = R\left( {A,B}\right)  \land  S\left( {B,C}\right)  \land  T\left( {C,D}\right)$ ; bottom row: delta view trees under a single-tuple update to relation $R$ (left) and relation $T$ (right).

图7. 上排：${\mathrm{{IVM}}}^{\epsilon }$用于3路径查询${Q}_{3p} = R\left( {A,B}\right)  \land  S\left( {B,C}\right)  \land  T\left( {C,D}\right)$的视图树；下排：关系$R$（左）与关系$T$（右）单次元组更新时的增量视图树。

<!-- Media -->

The result tuples of ${Q}_{3p}^{L}$ can be enumerated from its view tree in exactly the same way as from the view tree maintained by F-IVM and depicted in Figure 5. The enumeration from the view tree of ${Q}_{3p}^{H}$ follows the same principle. Upon each enumeration request for ${Q}_{3p}$ ,we can first enumerate the tuples from the view tree for ${Q}_{3p}^{L}$ and then enumerate the tuples from the view tree for ${Q}_{3p}^{H}$ . Since the two queries are disjoint,no tuple will be enumerated twice.

${Q}_{3p}^{L}$的结果元组可从其视图树中枚举，方式与F-IVM维护的视图树（如图5所示）完全相同。${Q}_{3p}^{H}$的视图树枚举遵循相同原则。每次处理${Q}_{3p}$的枚举请求时，可先枚举${Q}_{3p}^{L}$视图树的元组，再枚举${Q}_{3p}^{H}$视图树的元组。由于两个查询互不相交，不会重复枚举任何元组。

We analyze the times to maintain the two view trees under updates. Figure 7 (bottom left) visualizes the delta view tree for ${Q}_{3p}^{L}$ under a single-tuple update ${\delta R}\left( {a,b}\right)$ to $R$ . We compute the delta view $\delta {V}_{R}$ in constant time by projecting the tuple(a,b)onto $b$ . To compute the delta view $\delta {V}_{RS}$ ,we need to iterate over the $C$ -values paired with $b$ in ${S}^{L}$ . Since ${S}^{L}$ is light,there are less than ${\left| \mathcal{D}\right| }^{1/2}$ such $C$ -values. Hence,the computation time for $\delta {V}_{RS}$ is $O\left( {\left| \mathcal{D}\right| }^{1/2}\right)$ . The delta view $\delta {V}_{RS}^{\prime }$ results from $\delta {V}_{RS}$ by projecting away from each tuple the value $b$ ,which takes $O\left( {\left| \mathcal{D}\right| }^{1/2}\right)$ time. The computation of $\delta {V}_{RST}$ requires the computation of the intersection of the $C$ -values in $\delta {V}_{RS}^{\prime }$ and the $C$ -values in ${V}_{T}$ ,which takes $O\left( {\left| \mathcal{D}\right| }^{1/2}\right)$ time.

我们分析更新操作下维护两个视图树的时间消耗。图7（左下）展示了${Q}_{3p}^{L}$在单元组更新${\delta R}\left( {a,b}\right)$到$R$时的增量视图树。通过将元组(a,b)投影到$b$，可在常数时间内计算出增量视图$\delta {V}_{R}$。计算增量视图$\delta {V}_{RS}$时，需要遍历${S}^{L}$中与$b$配对的$C$值。因${S}^{L}$为轻量级，此类$C$值数量少于${\left| \mathcal{D}\right| }^{1/2}$，故$\delta {V}_{RS}$的计算时间为$O\left( {\left| \mathcal{D}\right| }^{1/2}\right)$。增量视图$\delta {V}_{RS}^{\prime }$通过从$\delta {V}_{RS}$的每个元组中移除$b$值获得，耗时$O\left( {\left| \mathcal{D}\right| }^{1/2}\right)$。$\delta {V}_{RST}$的计算需要求取$\delta {V}_{RS}^{\prime }$与${V}_{T}$中$C$值的交集，耗时$O\left( {\left| \mathcal{D}\right| }^{1/2}\right)$。

Now,we consider the delta view tree for ${Q}_{3p}^{H}$ under a single-tuple update ${\delta T}\left( {c,d}\right)$ to $T$ (bottom right delta view tree in Figure 7). The delta view $\delta {V}_{T}$ is computed by projecting the tuple(c,d)onto $c$ ,which takes constant time. To compute the delta view $\delta {V}_{ST}$ ,we iterate over the $B$ -values paired with $c$ in ${S}^{H}$ . Since ${S}^{H}$ is heavy,there are at most ${\left| \mathcal{D}\right| }^{1/2}$ such $B$ -values. Thus,the computation time for $\delta {V}_{ST}$ is $O\left( {\left| \mathcal{D}\right| }^{1/2}\right)$ . The remaining delta views are computed within the same time bounds.

现在考察${Q}_{3p}^{H}$在单元组更新${\delta T}\left( {c,d}\right)$到$T$时的增量视图树（图7右下）。通过将元组(c,d)投影到$c$，可在常数时间内计算出增量视图$\delta {V}_{T}$。计算$\delta {V}_{ST}$时需要遍历${S}^{H}$中与$c$配对的$B$值。因${S}^{H}$为重量级，此类$B$值最多有${\left| \mathcal{D}\right| }^{1/2}$个，故$\delta {V}_{ST}$的计算时间为$O\left( {\left| \mathcal{D}\right| }^{1/2}\right)$。其余增量视图的计算耗时相同量级。

Analogously,we can show that the two view trees in Figure 7 can be maintained in $O\left( {\left| \mathcal{D}\right| }^{1/2}\right)$ time under updates to the other relations. We conclude that ${\mathrm{{IVM}}}^{\epsilon }$ maintains the 3-path query with $O\left( {\left| \mathcal{D}\right| }^{1/2}\right)$ update time.

同理可证，图7中两个视图树在对其他关系进行更新时，维护时间均为$O\left( {\left| \mathcal{D}\right| }^{1/2}\right)$。由此得出结论：${\mathrm{{IVM}}}^{\epsilon }$能以$O\left( {\left| \mathcal{D}\right| }^{1/2}\right)$更新时间维护3路径查询。

Triangle Query. In case of the triangle query,we partition relation $R$ on variable $A$ ,relation $S$ on $B$ ,and relation $T$ on $C$ . Given $r,s,t \in  \{ H,L\}$ ,we denote ${Q}_{ \land  }^{rst} = {R}^{r}\left( {A,B}\right) ,{S}^{s}\left( {B,C}\right) ,{T}^{t}\left( {A,C}\right)$ . We replace the index $r$ by $*$ if the full relation $R$ participates in the query (and not only its heavy or light part). Same goes for relations $S$ and $T$ . The query ${Q}_{\Delta }$ can be rewritten as the disjoint union $\mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{r,s,t \in  \{ H,L\} }}{Q}^{rst} \cdot  {\mathrm{{IVM}}}^{\epsilon }$ creates the three view trees visualized in Figure 8. Observe that each view tree refers at its leaves to two relation parts (e.g., ${R}^{H}$ and ${S}^{L}$ in the first view tree) and a full relation (e.g., $T$ in the first view tree). Similar to the case of the 3-path query,we can show that the three view trees can be maintained under single-tuple updates in $O\left( {\left| \mathcal{D}\right| }^{1/2}\right)$ time.

三角形查询。在处理三角形查询时，我们对关系$R$按变量$A$进行分区，关系$S$按$B$分区，关系$T$按$C$分区。给定$r,s,t \in  \{ H,L\}$时，我们记作${Q}_{ \land  }^{rst} = {R}^{r}\left( {A,B}\right) ,{S}^{s}\left( {B,C}\right) ,{T}^{t}\left( {A,C}\right)$。若完整关系$R$参与查询（而非仅其重部或轻部），则将索引$r$替换为$*$。此规则同样适用于关系$S$和$T$。查询${Q}_{\Delta }$可重写为不相交并集$\mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{r,s,t \in  \{ H,L\} }}{Q}^{rst} \cdot  {\mathrm{{IVM}}}^{\epsilon }$，由此生成图8所示的三棵视图树。注意每棵视图树的叶子节点指向两个关系分区（如第一棵视图树中的${R}^{H}$和${S}^{L}$）和一个完整关系（如第一棵视图树中的$T$）。与3路径查询情况类似，可证明这三棵视图树能在$O\left( {\left| \mathcal{D}\right| }^{1/2}\right)$时间内支持单元组更新维护。

Fig. 8. View trees used by ${\mathrm{{IVM}}}^{\epsilon }$ for the triangle query ${Q}_{ \bigtriangleup  }\left( {A,B,C}\right)  = R\left( {A,B}\right)  \land  S\left( {B,C}\right)  \land  T\left( {A,C}\right)$ . Note that $T$ is partitioned into ${T}^{L}$ and ${T}^{H}$ based on $C$ ,while $R$ and $S$ are partitioned based on $A$ and $B$ ,respectively.

图8. ${\mathrm{{IVM}}}^{\epsilon }$用于三角形查询${Q}_{ \bigtriangleup  }\left( {A,B,C}\right)  = R\left( {A,B}\right)  \land  S\left( {B,C}\right)  \land  T\left( {A,C}\right)$的视图树。注意$T$基于$C$被划分为${T}^{L}$和${T}^{H}$，而$R$和$S$分别基于$A$和$B$进行划分。

<!-- Media -->

Rebalancing and Amortization. Updates can change the degrees of values, which means that a light value can become heavy and vice-versa. The original ${\mathrm{{IVM}}}^{\epsilon }$ algorithm moves tuples between the heavy and the light part of a relation whenever the degrees of the values in the tuples pass certain thresholds [21]. This leads to additional time cost that can be amortized over update sequences such that the amortized update time remains $O\left( {\left| \mathcal{D}\right| }^{1/2}\right)$ .

再平衡与摊销。更新操作可能改变值的度，导致轻值变为重值，反之亦然。原始${\mathrm{{IVM}}}^{\epsilon }$算法在元组中值的度超过特定阈值时[21]，会在关系的重部与轻部之间移动元组。这会带来额外时间成本，但可通过更新序列摊销，使得摊销后的更新时间保持为$O\left( {\left| \mathcal{D}\right| }^{1/2}\right)$。

### A.5 CROWN

### A.5 皇冠算法

CROWN is a higher order IVM approach. It uses view trees where each view is either a projection of another view or a semi-join between a view and other views. Since CROWN is tailored to free-connex acyclic queries, we illustrate it for the 3-path query ${Q}_{3p}$ only.

CROWN是一种高阶增量视图维护方法。它使用的视图树中，每个视图要么是其他视图的投影，要么是视图与其他视图的半连接。由于CROWN专为自由连接无环查询设计，本文仅以3路径查询${Q}_{3p}$为例说明。

3-Path Query. The view trees constructed by CROWN follow the so-called generalized join trees which, unlike classical join trees, can contain nodes that consist of variables that are a strict subset of the schema of a relation appearing in the query. Figure 9 shows a possible join tree for ${Q}_{3p}$ . The view tree corresponding to this generalized join tree

3路径查询。CROWN构建的视图树遵循广义连接树结构，与经典连接树不同，其节点可由严格小于查询中出现的关系模式的变量子集构成。图9展示了${Q}_{3p}$可能的连接树，其对应的视图树

Fig. 9. A generalized join tree for the 3-path query ${Q}_{3p} = R\left( {A,B}\right) ,S\left( {B,C}\right) ,T\left( {C,D}\right)$ .

图9. 3路径查询${Q}_{3p} = R\left( {A,B}\right) ,S\left( {B,C}\right) ,T\left( {C,D}\right)$的广义连接树。

<!-- Media -->

is the same as the one used by F-IVM (Figure 5 left). The views ${V}_{R},{V}_{RS}^{\prime }$ ,and ${V}_{T}$ are called projection views,since they are obtained by a projection on their child views. The views ${V}_{RS}$ and ${V}_{RST}$ are called semi-join views,since they are computed by semi-joining one child view with other child views. The enumeration of result tuples is exactly as explained in Section A.3.

与F-IVM所用结构相同（图5左）。视图${V}_{R},{V}_{RS}^{\prime }$和${V}_{T}$称为投影视图，因其通过对子视图投影获得；视图${V}_{RS}$和${V}_{RST}$称为半连接视图，通过将子视图与其他子视图半连接计算得到。结果元组的枚举方式与A.3节所述完全一致。

CROWN uses special indices that can speed up delta computation by constant factors. We explain these indices for the view tree in Figure 5 (left). For each value $b$ in the projection view ${V}_{R}$ ,it maintains a counter count ${t}_{R}$ that stores the number of tuples in $R$ with $B$ -value $b$ . There are similar counters count ${}_{R{S}^{\prime }}$ and ${\operatorname{count}}_{T}$ for the other projection views ${V}_{R{S}^{\prime }}$ and ${V}_{T}$ . Additionally,there is a counter coun ${t}_{RS}$ that counts for each(B,C)-value(b,c)in $S$ ,the number of child views that contain matching tuples. More precisely:

CROWN采用特殊索引结构，可将增量计算速度提升常数倍。我们以图5（左）的视图树为例说明这些索引：对于投影视图${V}_{R}$中的每个值$b$，系统维护计数器count${t}_{R}$来记录$R$中具有$B$值$b$的元组数量。投影视图${V}_{R{S}^{\prime }}$和${V}_{T}$同样设有计数器count${}_{R{S}^{\prime }}$与count${\operatorname{count}}_{T}$。此外，计数器coun${t}_{RS}$会统计$S$中每个(B,C)值组合(b,c)对应的子视图匹配元组数量。具体而言：

$$
{\operatorname{count}}_{RS}\left\lbrack  {b,c}\right\rbrack   = \left\{  \begin{array}{ll} 2 & \text{ if }b \in  {V}_{R}\text{ and }\left( {b,c}\right)  \in  S \\  0 & \text{ if }b \notin  {V}_{R}\text{ and }\left( {b,c}\right)  \notin  S \\  1 & \text{ otherwise } \end{array}\right. 
$$

Crown maintains also a counter count ${}_{RST}$ that counts for each $C$ -value $c \in  {V}_{RS}^{\prime } \cup  {V}_{T}$ ,the number of child views that contain $c$ .

Crown还维护计数器count${}_{RST}$，用于统计每个$C$值$c \in  {V}_{RS}^{\prime } \cup  {V}_{T}$在子视图中出现$c$的次数。

We explain how the view tree is maintained under an insert of a tuple(a,b)to relation $R$ . Other types of updates are handled analogously. If $b$ is included in ${V}_{R}$ ,we increase count ${}_{R}\left\lbrack  b\right\rbrack$ by 1 . Otherwise,we add $b$ to ${V}_{R}$ and set count ${}_{R}\left\lbrack  b\right\rbrack   =$ 1. Then,we update ${V}_{RS}$ as follows. For each tuple $\left( {b,{c}^{\prime }}\right)$ in $S$ ,we increase count ${}_{RS}\left\lbrack  {b,{c}^{\prime }}\right\rbrack$ by 1 and check whether ${\operatorname{count}}_{RS}\left\lbrack  {b,{c}^{\prime }}\right\rbrack   = 2$ . If yes,we add the tuple $\left( {b,{c}^{\prime }}\right)$ to ${V}_{RS}$ . To propagate the change further up to the root,we use the counters count ${}_{R{S}^{\prime }}$ and ${\operatorname{count}}_{RST}$ in a similar manner. The overall update time is $O\left( \left| \mathcal{D}\right| \right)$ for arbitrary update sequences.

我们阐述向关系$R$插入元组(a,b)时视图树的维护机制（其他更新操作同理）。若$b$已存在于${V}_{R}$，则将count${}_{R}\left\lbrack  b\right\rbrack$递增1；否则将$b$加入${V}_{R}$并初始化count${}_{R}\left\lbrack  b\right\rbrack   =$为1。接着按以下方式更新${V}_{RS}$：对$S$中的每个元组$\left( {b,{c}^{\prime }}\right)$，先递增count${}_{RS}\left\lbrack  {b,{c}^{\prime }}\right\rbrack$，再检测是否满足${\operatorname{count}}_{RS}\left\lbrack  {b,{c}^{\prime }}\right\rbrack   = 2$条件。若满足，则将$\left( {b,{c}^{\prime }}\right)$加入${V}_{RS}$。通过类似方式操作计数器count${}_{R{S}^{\prime }}$和count${\operatorname{count}}_{RST}$，可将变更向上传递至根节点。该算法对任意更新序列的时间复杂度为$O\left( \left| \mathcal{D}\right| \right)$。

### A.6 MVIVM

### A.6 MVIVM

Finally, we highlight some of the key insights of our approach, MVIVM, that enables it to maintain the triangle query ${Q}_{\Delta }$ in amortized time $\widetilde{O}\left( {\left| \mathcal{D}\right| }^{1/2}\right)$ . Example 7.2 presents the high-level idea while Appendix E. 1 explains the technical subtleties that arise. At a high-level,our approach maintains ${Q}_{\Delta }$ by constructing and maintaining the six multivariate extension components ${\widehat{\mathcal{Q}}}_{123},\ldots ,{\widehat{\mathcal{Q}}}_{321}$ given by Eq. (8) and (39). Each of these component queries is maintained using Theorem 4.1 along with a variety of technical lemmas described in Appendix E.1. It can be mechanically verified that each of these components has a fractional hypertree width of $\frac{3}{2}$ ,leading to our amortized update time of $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}\left( {\left| \mathcal{D}\right| }^{1/2}\right)$ .

最后重点说明MVIVM方法实现三角查询${Q}_{\Delta }$均摊时间复杂度$\widetilde{O}\left( {\left| \mathcal{D}\right| }^{1/2}\right)$的关键设计：示例7.2展示核心思想，附录E.1详述技术细节。该方法通过构建并维护公式(8)和(39)给出的六个多元扩展组件${\widehat{\mathcal{Q}}}_{123},\ldots ,{\widehat{\mathcal{Q}}}_{321}$来实现${Q}_{\Delta }$的维护。各组件查询的维护结合定理4.1及附录E.1的技术引理，经机械验证可知每个组件的分数超树宽度为$\frac{3}{2}$，最终实现$\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}\left( {\left| \mathcal{D}\right| }^{1/2}\right)$的均摊更新时间。

Let’s take the first component ${\widehat{Q}}_{123}$ as an example:

以首个组件${\widehat{Q}}_{123}$为例：

$$
{\widehat{Q}}_{123} = {\widehat{R}}_{123}\left( {{Z}_{1},A,B}\right)  \land  {\widehat{S}}_{123}\left( {{Z}_{1},{Z}_{2},B,C}\right)  \land  {\widehat{T}}_{123}\left( {{Z}_{1},{Z}_{2},{Z}_{3},A,C}\right) 
$$

This component has an optimal tree decomposition, depicted in Figure 10, that consists of two bags:

该组件具有如图10所示的最优树分解结构，包含两个节点：

- a root bag containing $\left\{  {{Z}_{1},{Z}_{2},A,B,C}\right\}$ (without ${Z}_{3}$ ),and

- 根节点含$\left\{  {{Z}_{1},{Z}_{2},A,B,C}\right\}$（不含${Z}_{3}$）

- a child bag containing $\left\{  {{Z}_{1},{Z}_{2},{Z}_{3},A,C}\right\}$ (without $B$ ).

- 子节点含$\left\{  {{Z}_{1},{Z}_{2},{Z}_{3},A,C}\right\}$（不含$B$）

<!-- figureText: ${Z}_{1},{Z}_{2},A,B,C$ ${Z}_{1},{Z}_{2},{Z}_{3},A,C$ -->

Fig. 10. An optimal tree decomposition for ${\widehat{Q}}_{123}$ from Eq. (8).

图10. 公式(8)中${\widehat{Q}}_{123}$的最优树分解结构

<!-- Media -->

Recall that ${Z}_{1},{Z}_{2}$ ,and ${Z}_{3}$ are temporal attributes representing the lifespans of tuples in $R,S$ ,and $T$ . In particular,the lifespan of a tuple in $T\left( {A,C}\right)$ is represented by the concatenation of ${Z}_{1} \circ  {Z}_{2} \circ  {Z}_{3}$ ; see Example 6.4. The above tree decomposition corresponds to a query plan where we are projecting ${Z}_{3}$ out of the child bag before joining with the root bag. Projecting ${Z}_{3}$ out of the child bag corresponds to "expanding" the lifespans of tuples of $T\left( {A,C}\right)$ from the lifespan ${Z}_{1} \circ  {Z}_{2} \circ  {Z}_{3}$ to the longer lifespan ${Z}_{1} \circ  {Z}_{2}$ . What this means algorithmically is that we are lazily processing deletes from $T$ in order to avoid doing excessive work for each delete,especially in the case where the same tuple from $T$ is inserted and deleted many times. In particular,a sequence of repeated insert-delete of the same tuple of $T$ corresponds to lifespans whose encodings may share the same prefix ${Z}_{1} \circ  {Z}_{2}$ . Hence,we don’t need to propagate changes from the child bag to the root bag for every such insert-delete because the lifespan ${Z}_{1} \circ  {Z}_{2}$ remains in the child bag throughout all these insert-deletes. Without this lazy processing,each such repeated insert/delete into $T$ would have required linear update time,thus we would have failed to meet our target update time of $\widetilde{O}\left( {\left| \mathcal{D}\right| }^{1/2}\right)$ . This implicit lazy processing is just one example of sophisticated behavior that comes automatically out of our approach.

回顾${Z}_{1},{Z}_{2}$和${Z}_{3}$是表示$R,S$与$T$中元组生命周期的时态属性。特别地，$T\left( {A,C}\right)$中元组的生命周期由${Z}_{1} \circ  {Z}_{2} \circ  {Z}_{3}$的串联表示（参见示例6.4）。上述树分解对应一个查询计划：在与根包进行连接前，我们先从子包中投影移除${Z}_{3}$。从子包投影移除${Z}_{3}$相当于将$T\left( {A,C}\right)$元组的生命周期从${Z}_{1} \circ  {Z}_{2} \circ  {Z}_{3}$"扩展"至更长的${Z}_{1} \circ  {Z}_{2}$。算法层面的含义是：我们延迟处理$T$的删除操作，以避免为每次删除执行冗余计算——尤其当$T$中同一元组被反复插入删除时。具体而言，$T$中同一元组的连续插入-删除序列对应的生命周期编码可能共享相同前缀${Z}_{1} \circ  {Z}_{2}$。因此我们无需为每次此类操作将变更从子包传播至根包，因为${Z}_{1} \circ  {Z}_{2}$的生命周期在这些操作期间始终存在于子包中。若无此延迟处理机制，$T$每次重复插入/删除都将导致线性级更新时间，从而无法达成$\widetilde{O}\left( {\left| \mathcal{D}\right| }^{1/2}\right)$的目标更新时间。这种隐式延迟处理是我们方法自动实现的复杂行为范例之一。

## B MISSING DETAILS IN PRELIMINARIES

## B 预备知识中缺失的细节

Query Classes. We give below some examples of acyclic, hierarchical, and Loomis-Whitney queries, where the latter two are defined in Section 2. Recall that for a query $Q$ and a variable $X \in  \operatorname{vars}\left( Q\right)$ ,we use at(X)to denote the set of atoms of $Q$ that contain $X$ in their schema.

查询类别。下文给出无环查询、层次化查询及Loomis-Whitney查询的示例（后两类定义见第2节）。对于查询$Q$和变量$X \in  \operatorname{vars}\left( Q\right)$，我们用at(X)表示$Q$中包含$X$的模式原子的集合。

Example B. 1 (Query Classes). The query ${Q}_{\mathrm{h}} = R\left( {A,B}\right)  \land  S\left( {A,C}\right)$ is hierarchical. We have at $\left( A\right)  = \{ R\left( {A,B}\right) ,S\left( {A,C}\right) \}$ , $\operatorname{at}\left( B\right)  = \{ R\left( {A,B}\right) \}$ ,and at $\left( C\right)  = \{ S\left( {A,C}\right) \}$ . It holds at $\left( B\right)  \subseteq  \operatorname{at}\left( A\right)$ ,at $\left( C\right)  \subseteq  \operatorname{at}\left( A\right)$ ,and at $\left( B\right)  \cap  \operatorname{at}\left( C\right)  = \varnothing$ . The query ${Q}_{\text{nh }} = R\left( A\right)  \land  S\left( {A,B}\right)  \land  T\left( B\right)$ is acyclic but not hierarchical. Indeed,we have at $\left( A\right)  = \{ R\left( A\right) ,S\left( {A,B}\right) \}$ and at $\left( B\right)  = \{ T\left( B\right)$ , $S\left( {A,B}\right) \}$ and it does not hold at $\left( A\right)  \subseteq  \operatorname{at}\left( B\right)$ nor at $\left( B\right)  \subseteq  \operatorname{at}\left( A\right)$ nor at $\left( A\right)  \cap  \operatorname{at}\left( B\right)  = \varnothing$ . A possible join tree for ${Q}_{nh}$ has $R\left( {A,B}\right)$ as root and the other two atoms as the children of the root. The triangle query (or Loomis-Whitney query of degree 3) is of the form ${Q}_{\bigtriangleup } = R\left( {A,B}\right)  \land  S\left( {B,C}\right)  \land  T\left( {A,C}\right)$ . A Loomis-Whitney query of degree 4 is of the form ${Q}_{\mathrm{{lw}}4} = R\left( {A,B,C}\right)  \land  S\left( {B,C,D}\right)  \land  T\left( {C,D,A}\right)  \land  U\left( {D,A,B}\right) .$

示例B.1（查询类）。查询${Q}_{\mathrm{h}} = R\left( {A,B}\right)  \land  S\left( {A,C}\right)$具有层次性。在$\left( A\right)  = \{ R\left( {A,B}\right) ,S\left( {A,C}\right) \}$处、$\operatorname{at}\left( B\right)  = \{ R\left( {A,B}\right) \}$处以及$\left( C\right)  = \{ S\left( {A,C}\right) \}$处均成立。该查询在$\left( B\right)  \subseteq  \operatorname{at}\left( A\right)$处、$\left( C\right)  \subseteq  \operatorname{at}\left( A\right)$处和$\left( B\right)  \cap  \operatorname{at}\left( C\right)  = \varnothing$处均满足条件。而查询${Q}_{\text{nh }} = R\left( A\right)  \land  S\left( {A,B}\right)  \land  T\left( B\right)$虽是无环的，却不具备层次性。具体而言，在$\left( A\right)  = \{ R\left( A\right) ,S\left( {A,B}\right) \}$和$\left( B\right)  = \{ T\left( B\right)$处存在$S\left( {A,B}\right) \}$，但在$\left( A\right)  \subseteq  \operatorname{at}\left( B\right)$、$\left( B\right)  \subseteq  \operatorname{at}\left( A\right)$及$\left( A\right)  \cap  \operatorname{at}\left( B\right)  = \varnothing$处均不成立。对于${Q}_{nh}$，一种可能的连接树是以$R\left( {A,B}\right)$为根节点，其余两个原子作为根的子节点。三角形查询（或称3阶Loomis-Whitney查询）的形式为${Q}_{\bigtriangleup } = R\left( {A,B}\right)  \land  S\left( {B,C}\right)  \land  T\left( {A,C}\right)$，而4阶Loomis-Whitney查询则表现为${Q}_{\mathrm{{lw}}4} = R\left( {A,B,C}\right)  \land  S\left( {B,C,D}\right)  \land  T\left( {C,D,A}\right)  \land  U\left( {D,A,B}\right) .$

Variable Elimination Orders. We review the equivalence between tree decompositions and variable elimination orders, which is a folklore concept in graph and database theory; see e.g. [4] and references thereof. Given a query $Q$ ,a variable elimination order $\mu$ for $Q$ is an order of the variables of $Q$ that we use to eliminate them one by one. In order to eliminate a variable $X$ ,we take all atoms $R\left( \mathbf{X}\right)$ where $X \in  \mathbf{X}$ and replace them with a new atom ${R}_{X}^{\mu }\left( {{U}_{X}^{\mu }-\{ X\} }\right)$ where ${U}_{X}^{\mu }$ is the union of schemas $X$ of all these atoms $R\left( X\right)$ that contain $X$ . It is long known that for a fixed variable elimination order $\mu$ ,the sets ${U}_{X}^{\mu }$ are the bags of some tree decomposition of $Q$ . Moreover,one of these tree decompositions that are obtained from variable elimination orders is optimal, i.e. has a minimum fractional hypertree width. Therefore, the fractional hypertree width can be equivalently defined as follows. Let ${\sum }_{\text{vars }\left( Q\right) }$ denote the set of all permutations of the variables of $Q$ ,and let $\mathbf{\mu } \in  {\sum }_{\operatorname{vars}\left( Q\right) }$ be a variable elimination order for $Q$ :

变量消元顺序。我们回顾树分解与变量消元顺序之间的等价性——这是图论与数据库理论中的经典概念，具体可参阅文献[4]及其参考文献。给定查询$Q$时，其变量消元顺序$\mu$是指对$Q$中变量进行逐个消元的排列次序。消元变量$X$时，需将所有包含$X \in  \mathbf{X}$的原子$R\left( \mathbf{X}\right)$替换为新的原子${R}_{X}^{\mu }\left( {{U}_{X}^{\mu }-\{ X\} }\right)$，其中${U}_{X}^{\mu }$是这些原子$R\left( X\right)$的模式并集。学界早已证明：固定变量消元顺序$\mu$时，集合${U}_{X}^{\mu }$构成$Q$某个树分解的包。更进一步，由变量消元顺序导出的树分解中存在最优解（即具有最小分数超树宽度）。因此分数超树宽度可等价定义为：令${\sum }_{\text{vars }\left( Q\right) }$表示$Q$变量所有排列组合，$\mathbf{\mu } \in  {\sum }_{\operatorname{vars}\left( Q\right) }$为$Q$的变量消元顺序：

$$
\mathrm{w}\left( \mathbf{\mu }\right) \overset{\text{ def }}{ = }\mathop{\max }\limits_{{X \in  \mathbf{\mu }}}{\rho }^{ * }\left( {Q}_{{\mathbf{U}}_{X}^{\mathbf{\mu }}}\right) 
$$

$$
\mathrm{w}\left( Q\right) \overset{\text{ def }}{ = }\mathop{\min }\limits_{{\mathbf{\mu } \in  {\sum }_{\operatorname{vars}\left( Q\right) }}}\mathrm{w}\left( \mathbf{\mu }\right)  \tag{26}
$$

In particular,it is known that (26) and (5) are equivalent; see for example [4] and references thereof. We call $\mathrm{w}\left( \mathbf{\mu }\right)$ the fractional hypertree width of the variable elimination order $\mathbf{\mu }$ . Moreover,we call $\mathbf{\mu }$ optimal if $\mathrm{w}\left( \mathbf{\mu }\right)  = \mathrm{w}\left( Q\right)$ .

特别地，已知公式(26)与(5)等价（参见文献[4]）。我们将$\mathrm{w}\left( \mathbf{\mu }\right)$称为变量消元顺序$\mathbf{\mu }$的分数超树宽度。当$\mathrm{w}\left( \mathbf{\mu }\right)  = \mathrm{w}\left( Q\right)$时，称$\mathbf{\mu }$为最优消元顺序。

Online Matrix-Vector Multiplication (OMv) Conjecture. In order to state the OMv conjecture, we first state the OMv problem. Given a Boolean matrix $M$ and $n$ column vectors ${v}_{1},\ldots ,{v}_{n}$ that arrive one-by-one,the task is to output the vector $M{v}_{r}$ after seeing ${v}_{r}$ for each $r \in  \left\lbrack  n\right\rbrack$ :

在线矩阵向量乘法(OMv)猜想。阐述OMv猜想前，先定义OMv问题：给定布尔矩阵$M$及按序到达的$n$个列向量${v}_{1},\ldots ,{v}_{n}$，需在每次接收到${v}_{r}$后输出向量$M{v}_{r}$：

Problem: OMv

问题：OMv

Given: $\;n \times  n$ Boolean matrix $M$ and a stream of ${nn} \times  1$ Boolean column vectors ${v}_{1},\ldots ,{v}_{n}$

输入：$\;n \times  n$布尔矩阵$M$及${nn} \times  1$个布尔列向量流${v}_{1},\ldots ,{v}_{n}$

Task: Compute $M{v}_{r}$ after seeing each ${v}_{r}$

任务：每次接收${v}_{r}$后计算$M{v}_{r}$

CONJECTURE B.2 (OMv [16]). For any $\gamma  > 0$ ,there is no algorithm that solves the OMv problem in $O\left( {n}^{3 - \gamma }\right)$ time.

猜想B.2(OMv[16])。对于任意$\gamma  > 0$，不存在能在$O\left( {n}^{3 - \gamma }\right)$时间内解决OMv问题的算法。

Online Vector-Matrix-Vector Multiplication (OuMv) Conjecture. The OuMv problem is defined as follows. Given a Boolean matrix $M$ and $n$ pairs of column vectors $\left( {{u}_{1},{v}_{1}}\right) ,\ldots ,\left( {{u}_{n},{v}_{n}}\right)$ that arrive one-by-one,the task is to output the product ${\mathbf{u}}_{r}^{\top }\mathbf{M}{\mathbf{v}}_{r}$ after seeing $\left( {{\mathbf{u}}_{r},{\mathbf{v}}_{r}}\right)$ for each $r \in  \left\lbrack  n\right\rbrack$ :

在线向量-矩阵-向量乘法(OuMv)猜想。OuMv问题定义如下：给定布尔矩阵$M$及按序到达的$n$对列向量$\left( {{u}_{1},{v}_{1}}\right) ,\ldots ,\left( {{u}_{n},{v}_{n}}\right)$，需在每次接收到$\left( {{\mathbf{u}}_{r},{\mathbf{v}}_{r}}\right)$后输出乘积${\mathbf{u}}_{r}^{\top }\mathbf{M}{\mathbf{v}}_{r}$：

Problem: OuMv

问题：OuMv

Given: $\;n \times  n$ Boolean matrix $\mathbf{M}$ and a stream of $n$ pairs of $n \times  1$ Boolean column vectors

输入：$\;n \times  n$布尔矩阵$\mathbf{M}$及$n$对$n \times  1$布尔列向量流

$\left( {{\mathbf{u}}_{1},{\mathbf{v}}_{1}}\right) ,\ldots ,\left( {{\mathbf{u}}_{n},{\mathbf{v}}_{n}}\right)$

Task: Compute ${\mathbf{u}}_{r}^{\top }\mathbf{M}{\mathbf{v}}_{r}$ after seeing each $\left( {{\mathbf{u}}_{r},{\mathbf{v}}_{r}}\right)$

任务：在观察到每个$\left( {{\mathbf{u}}_{r},{\mathbf{v}}_{r}}\right)$后计算${\mathbf{u}}_{r}^{\top }\mathbf{M}{\mathbf{v}}_{r}$

CONJECTURE B.3 (OUMv [16]). For any $\gamma  > 0$ ,there is no algorithm that solves the OuMv problem in $O\left( {n}^{3 - \gamma }\right)$ time.

猜想B.3（OUMv [16]）。对于任意$\gamma  > 0$，不存在能在$O\left( {n}^{3 - \gamma }\right)$时间内解决OuMv问题的算法。

It is known that the OMv conjecture implies the OuMv conjecture [16].

已知OMv猜想蕴含OuMv猜想[16]。

## C MISSING DETAILS IN SECTION 5

## C 第五章缺失细节

In this section, we explain in detail our results on IVM in the insert-only setting, where no deletes are allowed. The main goal is to prove Theorem 4.1 (as well as Proposition 4.3). The results in this section also serve as building blocks for our results on the insert-delete setting in Section E.

本节详细阐述仅插入场景下的增量视图维护(IVM)研究成果，该场景禁止删除操作。主要目标是证明定理4.1（及命题4.3）。本节结果同时构成附录E中插入-删除场景研究的理论基础。

### C.1 Preliminaries: The Query Decomposition Lemma

### C.1 预备知识：查询分解引理

We prove the query decomposition lemma (Lemma 5.2) and illustrate it by an example. This lemma is the core of the proof of Lemma 5.1, which in turn is a key ingredient in the proof of Theorem 4.1. Recall notation from Section B.

我们将证明查询分解引理（引理5.2）并通过示例说明。该引理是引理5.1证明的核心，而后者又是定理4.1证明的关键要素。相关符号定义参见附录B。

The query decomposition lemma basically says the following. Let $Q$ be a query and let $Y \subseteq  \operatorname{vars}\left( Q\right)$ . Then,the AGM bound of $Q$ can be decomposed into a sum of AGM bounds of "residual" queries: one query $Q \bowtie  \mathbf{y}$ for each tuple $\mathbf{y}$ over the variables $\mathbf{Y}$ .

查询分解引理的核心内容是：设$Q$为查询，$Y \subseteq  \operatorname{vars}\left( Q\right)$为变量子集，则$Q$的AGM界可分解为若干"残差"查询AGM界之和——对变量集$\mathbf{Y}$上的每个元组$\mathbf{y}$，对应生成一个残差查询$Q \bowtie  \mathbf{y}$。

LEMMA 5.2 (Query Decomposition Lemma [32,34]). Given a query $Q$ and a subset $Y \subseteq  \operatorname{vars}\left( Q\right)$ ,let ${\left( {\lambda }_{R\left( X\right) }\right) }_{R\left( X\right)  \in  \operatorname{at}\left( Q\right) }$ be a fractional edge cover of $Q$ . Then,the following inequality holds:

引理5.2（查询分解引理[32,34]）。给定查询$Q$及其子集$Y \subseteq  \operatorname{vars}\left( Q\right)$，设${\left( {\lambda }_{R\left( X\right) }\right) }_{R\left( X\right)  \in  \operatorname{at}\left( Q\right) }$为$Q$的分数边覆盖，则下列不等式成立：

(27)

In the above, $\mathbf{y} \in  \operatorname{Dom}\left( Y\right)$ indicates that the tuple $\mathbf{y}$ has schema $\mathbf{Y}$ . Moreover, $R\left( \mathbf{X}\right)  \ltimes  \mathbf{y}$ denotes the semijoin of the atom $R\left( \mathbf{X}\right)$ with the tuple $\mathbf{y}$ .

上式中，$\mathbf{y} \in  \operatorname{Dom}\left( Y\right)$表示元组$\mathbf{y}$的模式为$\mathbf{Y}$；$R\left( \mathbf{X}\right)  \ltimes  \mathbf{y}$表示原子$R\left( \mathbf{X}\right)$与元组$\mathbf{y}$的半连接结果。

Proof of Lemma 5.2. In order to prove inequality (27),we prove the following special case where $Y$ consists of a single variable $Y$ :

引理5.2证明。为证明不等式(27)，先证明$Y$为单变量时的特例情形：

$$
\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{y \in  \operatorname{Dom}\left( Y\right) }}\mathop{\prod }\limits_{{R\left( \mathbf{X}\right)  \in  \operatorname{at}\left( Q\right) }}{\left| R\left( \mathbf{X}\right)  \ltimes  y\right| }^{{\lambda }_{R\left( \mathbf{X}\right) }} \leq  \mathop{\prod }\limits_{{R\left( \mathbf{X}\right)  \in  \operatorname{at}\left( Q\right) }}{\left| R\right| }^{{\lambda }_{R\left( \mathbf{X}\right) }} \tag{28}
$$

Inequality (27) follows by repeatedly applying (28) to each variable in $Y$ . To that end,note that for each "residual" query, ${Q}_{y}\overset{\text{ def }}{ = }Q \bowtie  y$ ,the following is a valid fractional edge cover,where ${R}_{y}\left( X\right) \overset{\text{ def }}{ = }R\left( X\right)  \bowtie  y$ :

通过对$Y$中每个变量重复应用式(28)可推得式(27)。需注意对于每个残差查询${Q}_{y}\overset{\text{ def }}{ = }Q \bowtie  y$，当${R}_{y}\left( X\right) \overset{\text{ def }}{ = }R\left( X\right)  \bowtie  y$时下列分数边覆盖成立：

$$
{\left( {\lambda }_{{R}_{y}}\left( X\right) \right) }_{{R}_{y}\left( X\right)  \in  \operatorname{at}\left( {Q}_{y}\right) }
$$

Now, we prove (28):

现在证明式(28)：

$$

$$
\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{y \in  \operatorname{Dom}\left( Y\right) }}\mathop{\prod }\limits_{\substack{{R\left( X\right)  \in  \operatorname{at}\left( Q\right) } \\  {Y \in  X} }}{\left| {\sigma }_{Y = y}R\left( X\right) \right| }^{{\lambda }_{R\left( X\right) }}\mathop{\prod }\limits_{\substack{{R\left( X\right)  \in  \operatorname{at}\left( Q\right) } \\  {Y \notin  X} }}{\left| R\right| }^{{\lambda }_{R\left( X\right) }} = 
$$

$$
\mathop{\prod }\limits_{\substack{{R\left( X\right)  \in  \operatorname{at}\left( Q\right) } \\  {Y \notin  X} }}{\left| R\right| }^{{\lambda }_{R\left( X\right) }}\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{y \in  \operatorname{Dom}\left( Y\right) }}\mathop{\prod }\limits_{\substack{{R\left( X\right)  \in  \operatorname{at}\left( Q\right) } \\  {Y \in  X} }}{\left| {\sigma }_{Y = y}R\left( X\right) \right| }^{{\lambda }_{R\left( X\right) }} \leq   \tag{29}

\mathop{\prod }\limits_{\substack{{R\left( X\right)  \in  \operatorname{at}\left( Q\right) } \\  {Y \notin  X} }}{\left| R\right| }^{{\lambda }_{R\left( X\right) }}\mathop{\prod }\limits_{\substack{{R\left( X\right)  \in  \operatorname{at}\left( Q\right) } \\  {Y \in  X} }}{\left( \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{y \in  \operatorname{Dom}\left( Y\right) }}\left| {\sigma }_{Y = y}R\left( X\right) \right| \right) }^{{\lambda }_{R\left( X\right) }} =

\mathop{\prod }\limits_{\substack{{R\left( \mathbf{X}\right)  \in  \operatorname{at}\left( Q\right) } \\  {Y \notin  \mathbf{X}} }}{\left| R\right| }^{{\lambda }_{R\left( \mathbf{X}\right) }}\mathop{\prod }\limits_{\substack{{R\left( \mathbf{X}\right)  \in  \operatorname{at}\left( Q\right) } \\  {Y \in  \mathbf{X}} }}{\left| R\right| }^{{\lambda }_{R\left( \mathbf{X}\right) }} = 
$$

Inequality (29) above follows from Hölder’s inequality,which applies specifically because the sum of ${\lambda }_{R\left( X\right) }$ over all atoms $R\left( \mathbf{X}\right)$ that contain $Y$ is at least 1 .

式(29)的成立依据是Hölder不等式，其适用条件为：所有包含$Y$的原子$R\left( \mathbf{X}\right)$对应${\lambda }_{R\left( X\right) }$值之和至少为1。

Example C.1 (for Lemma 5.2). Consider the triangle query:

示例C.1（说明引理5.2）。考虑三角形查询：

$$
{Q}_{ \bigtriangleup  }\left( {A,B,C}\right)  = R\left( {A,B}\right)  \land  S\left( {B,C}\right)  \land  T\left( {A,C}\right)

$$
\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{\left( {a,b}\right)  \in  R}}\sqrt{\left| {{\sigma }_{B = b}S\left( {B,C}\right) }\right|  \cdot  \left| {{\sigma }_{A = a}T\left( {A,C}\right) }\right| } \leq  \sqrt{\left| R\right|  \cdot  \left| S\right|  \cdot  \left| T\right| } \tag{30}
$$

This inequality can be proved as follows:

$$

$$
\sqrt{\left| S\right| } \cdot  \mathop{\sum }\limits_{a}\sqrt{\left| {{\sigma }_{A = a}R\left( {A,B}\right) }\right|  \cdot  \left| {{\sigma }_{A = a}T\left( {A,C}\right) }\right| } = 
$$

\mathop{\sum }\limits_{a}\sqrt{\left| {{\sigma }_{A = a}R\left( {A,B}\right) }\right|  \cdot  \left| {{\sigma }_{A = a}T\left( {A,C}\right) }\right|  \cdot  \left| S\right| } = 
$$

$$
\mathop{\sum }\limits_{a}\sqrt{\left| {\sigma }_{A = a}T\left( A,C\right) \right| }\sqrt{\left( {\mathop{\sum }\limits_{b}\left| {{\sigma }_{A = a,B = b}R\left( {A,B}\right) }\right| }\right) \left( {\mathop{\sum }\limits_{b}\left| {{\sigma }_{B = b}S\left( {B,C}\right) }\right| }\right) } \geq  
$$

$$
\mathop{\sum }\limits_{a}\sqrt{\left| {\sigma }_{A = a}T\left( A,C\right) \right| }\mathop{\sum }\limits_{b}\sqrt{\left| {{\sigma }_{A = a,B = b}R\left( {A,B}\right) }\right|  \cdot  \left| {{\sigma }_{B = b}S\left( {B,C}\right) }\right| } =

The two inequalities above follow from the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality, which is a special case of Hölder's inequality.

上述两个不等式源自柯西-施瓦茨不等式（Cauchy-Schwarz inequality），这是赫尔德不等式（Hölder's inequality）的一个特例。

### C.2 Proof of Lemma 5.1

### C.2 引理5.1的证明

Theorem 4.1 gives an upper bound on the amortized update time for IVM in the insert-only setting in terms of the fractional hypertree width of the query $Q$ . Before we show how to achieve this bound in Sections C. 3 and C.4,we prove in this section a weaker bound that is based on the AGM-bound of $Q$ . In particular,we prove Lemma 5.1,which says the following. Given a query $Q$ (whose input relations are initially empty) and a stream of $N$ single-tuple inserts into $Q$ ,we can update $Q$ after every insert and report the new output tuples ${\delta Q}$ in a total time for all updates/reports together that does not exceed the AGM bound of $Q$ . Note that here we are referring to the final AGM bound of $Q$ after all inserts have been performed.

定理4.1给出了仅插入场景下IVM摊销更新时间的上界，该上界以查询$Q$的分数超树宽度表示。在C.3和C.4节展示如何达成该上界前，本节将基于$Q$的AGM界证明一个较弱的上界。具体而言，我们将证明引理5.1，其内容如下：给定初始关系为空的查询$Q$及对$Q$的$N$次单元组插入流，我们能在总时间不超过$Q$的AGM界的前提下，在每次插入后更新$Q$并报告新输出元组${\delta Q}$。此处AGM界指所有插入完成后的最终AGM界。

Lemma 5.1. Given a query $Q$ ,an initially empty database ${\mathcal{D}}^{\left( 0\right) }$ ,and a stream of $N$ single-tuple inserts,we can compute the new output tuples, ${\delta }_{\tau }Q\left( D\right)$ ,after every insert ${\delta }_{\tau }\mathcal{D}$ where the total time over all inserts combined is $O(N +$ $\operatorname{AGM}\left( {Q,{\mathcal{D}}^{\left( N\right) }}\right) )$ .

引理5.1. 给定查询$Q$、初始空数据库${\mathcal{D}}^{\left( 0\right) }$及$N$次单元组插入流，我们可在每次插入${\delta }_{\tau }\mathcal{D}$后计算新输出元组${\delta }_{\tau }Q\left( D\right)$，且所有插入操作的总时间为$O(N +$$\operatorname{AGM}\left( {Q,{\mathcal{D}}^{\left( N\right) }}\right) )$。

The proof of Lemma 5.1 relies on the query decomposition lemma (Lemma 5.2 [32, 34]). Example 5.3 demonstrates Lemma 5.1 over the triangle query ${Q}_{\Delta }$ . Lemma 5.1 considers only the ${\mathrm{{IVM}}}_{\delta }^{ + }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$ problem,but the proof can be straightforwardly extended to ${\mathrm{{IVM}}}^{ + }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$ .

引理5.1的证明依赖于查询分解引理（引理5.2 [32,34]）。示例5.3通过三角形查询${Q}_{\Delta }$演示了该引理。虽然引理5.1仅针对${\mathrm{{IVM}}}_{\delta }^{ + }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$问题，但其证明可直接推广至${\mathrm{{IVM}}}^{ + }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$场景。

Proof of Lemma 5.1. For a given relation $R$ in $Q$ ,we use ${R}^{\left( 0\right) }\overset{\text{ def }}{ = }\varnothing ,{R}^{\left( 1\right) },\ldots ,{R}^{\left( N\right) }$ to denote the sequence of versions of $R$ after each insert. Because we only have inserts here,we have $0 = \left| {R}^{\left( 0\right) }\right|  \leq  \left| {R}^{\left( 1\right) }\right|  \leq  \ldots  \leq  \left| {R}^{\left( N\right) }\right|  \leq  N$ . Moreover,for each $\tau  \in  \left\lbrack  N\right\rbrack$ ,let ${\delta }_{\tau }R\overset{\text{ def }}{ = }{R}^{\left( \tau \right) } \smallsetminus  {R}^{\left( \tau  - 1\right) }$ . Note that $\left| {{\delta }_{\tau }R}\right|$ is either 1 or 0 depending on whether the $\tau$ -th insert was into the relation $R$ or into another relation of $Q$ . Let ${\left( {\lambda }_{R\left( X\right) }\right) }_{R\left( X\right)  \in  \operatorname{at}\left( Q\right) }$ be a fractional edge cover for $Q$ . We will show how to perform all $N$ inserts and report ${\delta }_{\tau }Q$ after every insert in a total time of $O\left( \theta \right)$ (over all inserts/reports combined) where

引理5.1的证明. 对于查询$Q$中的给定关系$R$，用${R}^{\left( 0\right) }\overset{\text{ def }}{ = }\varnothing ,{R}^{\left( 1\right) },\ldots ,{R}^{\left( N\right) }$表示每次插入后$R$的版本序列。由于仅涉及插入操作，故有$0 = \left| {R}^{\left( 0\right) }\right|  \leq  \left| {R}^{\left( 1\right) }\right|  \leq  \ldots  \leq  \left| {R}^{\left( N\right) }\right|  \leq  N$。此外，对每个$\tau  \in  \left\lbrack  N\right\rbrack$，设${\delta }_{\tau }R\overset{\text{ def }}{ = }{R}^{\left( \tau \right) } \smallsetminus  {R}^{\left( \tau  - 1\right) }$。注意$\left| {{\delta }_{\tau }R}\right|$取值为1或0，取决于第$\tau$次插入是否作用于关系$R$。设${\left( {\lambda }_{R\left( X\right) }\right) }_{R\left( X\right)  \in  \operatorname{at}\left( Q\right) }$为$Q$的分数边覆盖，我们将展示如何在总时间$O\left( \theta \right)$内（含所有插入/报告操作）完成$N$次插入，并在每次插入后报告${\delta }_{\tau }Q$，其中

$$
\theta \overset{\text{ def }}{ = }\mathop{\prod }\limits_{{R\left( X\right)  \in  \operatorname{at}\left( Q\right) }}{\left| {R}^{\left( N\right) }\right| }^{{\lambda }_{R\left( X\right) }}
$$

In particular,for a fixed atom $S\left( Y\right)  \in  \operatorname{at}\left( Q\right)$ ,we will show how to perform all inserts into $S$ in a total time of $O\left( \theta \right)$ . For any $\tau  \in  \left\lbrack  N\right\rbrack$ where ${\delta }_{\tau }S \neq  \varnothing$ ,the insertion of ${\delta }_{\tau }S$ into $S$ can be performed in time:

特别地，对于一个固定原子$S\left( Y\right)  \in  \operatorname{at}\left( Q\right)$，我们将展示如何在总时间$O\left( \theta \right)$内完成所有向$S$的插入操作。对于任意满足${\delta }_{\tau }S \neq  \varnothing$的$\tau  \in  \left\lbrack  N\right\rbrack$，将${\delta }_{\tau }S$插入$S$可在以下时间内完成：

$$
\mathop{\prod }\limits_{{R\left( \mathbf{X}\right)  \in  \operatorname{at}\left( Q\right) }}{\left| {R}^{\left( \tau \right) }\left( \mathbf{X}\right)  \ltimes  {\delta }_{\tau }S\left( \mathbf{Y}\right) \right| }^{{\lambda }_{R\left( \mathbf{X}\right) }} \tag{31}
$$

This can be done by solving the query

这可以通过求解查询来实现

$$
{\delta }_{\tau }Q\overset{\text{ def }}{ = }\mathop{\bigwedge }\limits_{{R\left( \mathbf{X}\right)  \in  \operatorname{at}\left( Q\right) }}{R}^{\left( \tau \right) }\left( \mathbf{X}\right)  \ltimes  {\delta }_{\tau }S\left( \mathbf{Y}\right) 
$$

In particular,(31) is the AGM bound of ${\delta }_{\tau }Q$ . We can meet this bound if we solve ${\delta }_{\tau }Q$ using a worst-case optimal join algorithm $\left\lbrack  {{32},{33},{42}}\right\rbrack$ . For that purpose,each relation $R$ needs to be indexed with a trie [42] using an appropriate variable ordering for this query. ${}^{6}$ The total runtime of all inserts into $S$ is therefore bounded by: ${}^{7}$

特别地，(31)式是${\delta }_{\tau }Q$的AGM界。若使用最坏情况最优连接算法$\left\lbrack  {{32},{33},{42}}\right\rbrack$求解${\delta }_{\tau }Q$，即可达到该界限。为此，每个关系$R$需采用适合该查询的变量顺序建立字典树索引[42]。${}^{6}$因此，所有向$S$插入操作的总运行时间上界为：${}^{7}$

$$
\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{\tau  \in  \left\lbrack  N\right\rbrack  }}\mathop{\prod }\limits_{{R\left( X\right)  \in  \operatorname{at}\left( Q\right) }}{\left| {R}^{\left( \tau \right) }\left( X\right)  \ltimes  {\delta }_{\tau }S\left( Y\right) \right| }^{{\lambda }_{R\left( X\right) }} \leq  \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{\tau  \in  \left\lbrack  N\right\rbrack  }}\mathop{\prod }\limits_{{R\left( X\right)  \in  \operatorname{at}\left( Q\right) }}{\left| {R}^{\left( N\right) }\left( X\right)  \ltimes  {\delta }_{\tau }S\left( Y\right) \right| }^{{\lambda }_{R\left( X\right) }}
$$

$$
 \leq  \mathop{\prod }\limits_{{R\left( X\right)  \in  \operatorname{at}\left( Q\right) }}{\left| {R}^{\left( N\right) }\right| }^{{\lambda }_{R\left( X\right) }} = \theta 
$$

The first inequality above holds because ${R}^{\left( \tau \right) } \subseteq  {R}^{\left( N\right) }$ ,while the second inequality follows from (27). Moreover,note that (31) is an upper bound on the output size of ${\delta }_{\tau }Q$ (because it is the AGM-bound). From the above,we have $\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{\tau  \in  \left\lbrack  N\right\rbrack  }}\left| {{\delta }_{\tau }Q}\right|  \leq  \theta$

第一个不等式成立是因为${R}^{\left( \tau \right) } \subseteq  {R}^{\left( N\right) }$，而第二个不等式源自(27)式。此外需注意，(31)式是${\delta }_{\tau }Q$输出大小的上界（因其为AGM界）。由上述可得$\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{\tau  \in  \left\lbrack  N\right\rbrack  }}\left| {{\delta }_{\tau }Q}\right|  \leq  \theta$

### C.3 Upper bound for ${\mathrm{{IVM}}}^{ + }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$

### C.3 ${\mathrm{{IVM}}}^{ + }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$的上界

In this section,we prove Theorem 4.1 for ${\mathrm{{IVM}}}^{ + }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$ ,which basically says the following: Given a query $Q$ whose fractional hypertree width is $\mathrm{w}\left( Q\right)$ and a stream of $N$ single-tuple inserts (where all input relations are initially empty), we can maintain some data structure for $Q$ and update it after every insert where the total update time over all inserts combined is $O\left( {N}^{\mathrm{w}\left( Q\right) }\right)$ . Moreover,we can use this data structure at any time to do constant-delay enumeration of the output of $Q$ . In particular,fix an optimal tree decomposition of $Q$ ,i.e. one whose width is $\mathrm{w}\left( Q\right)$ . The data structure that we use consists of a collection of intermediate relations: One for each bag of the chosen tree decomposition. Moreover, every bag will be "calibrated" with its children bags in the tree decomposition. We use Lemma 5.1 to update every bag within a total time of $O\left( {N}^{\mathrm{w}\left( Q\right) }\right)$ . Moreover,because every bag is calibrated with its children bags,we can always do constant-delay enumeration starting from the root bag. Example 5.4 demonstrates the idea.

本节我们将针对${\mathrm{{IVM}}}^{ + }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$证明定理4.1，其核心内容是：给定一个分数超树宽度为$\mathrm{w}\left( Q\right)$的查询$Q$，以及$N$次单元组插入流（所有输入关系初始为空），我们可以为$Q$维护一个数据结构，在每次插入后更新它，且所有插入操作的总更新时间不超过$O\left( {N}^{\mathrm{w}\left( Q\right) }\right)$。此外，可随时利用该数据结构以恒定延迟枚举$Q$的输出结果。具体而言，固定$Q$的最优树分解（即宽度为$\mathrm{w}\left( Q\right)$的分解），所用数据结构由若干中间关系组成：所选树分解的每个袋对应一个关系。每个袋还会与树分解中的子袋进行"校准"。我们使用引理5.1在总时间$O\left( {N}^{\mathrm{w}\left( Q\right) }\right)$内更新每个袋。由于每个袋都与子袋校准，因此总能从根袋开始进行恒定延迟枚举。示例5.4演示了这一思想。

---

${}^{6}$ We might need to maintain multiple tries (with different variable orderings) for the same relation $R\left( X\right)$ in order to handle inserts into different atoms $S\left( Y\right)$ .

${}^{6}$ 我们可能需要为同一关系$R\left( X\right)$维护多个尝试树（采用不同变量顺序）$S\left( Y\right)$，以处理向不同原子插入数据的情况。

${}^{7}$ In order to avoid incurring an extra factor of $\log N$ in the runtime,we can switch from tries to hash tables [42].

${}^{7}$ 为避免在运行时产生$\log N$的额外开销因子，可从尝试树切换为哈希表[42]。
