THEOREM 4.1. For any query $Q$ ,both ${\mathrm{{IVM}}}^{ + }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$ and ${\mathrm{{IVM}}}_{\delta }^{ + }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$ can be solved with $O\left( {N}^{\mathrm{w}\left( Q\right)  - 1}\right)$ amortized update time, where $N$ is the number of single-tuple inserts.

定理4.1. 对于任意查询$Q$，${\mathrm{{IVM}}}^{ + }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$和${\mathrm{{IVM}}}_{\delta }^{ + }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$均可通过$O\left( {N}^{\mathrm{w}\left( Q\right)  - 1}\right)$的摊还更新时间求解，其中$N$表示单元组插入次数。

Proof of Theorem 4.1. The case of ${\operatorname{IVM}}^{ + }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$ : Let $\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)$ be a tree decomposition of $Q$ whose fractional hypertree width is $\mathrm{w}\left( Q\right)$ . (Recall notation from Section B.) Fix an orientation of $\mathcal{T}$ starting from some root node $r \in  V\left( \mathcal{T}\right)$ . Given a node $t \in  V\left( \mathcal{T}\right)$ ,we will use parent(t),children(t),ancestors(t)and decendants(t)to refer to the parent,children, ancestors and descendants of $t$ in $\mathcal{T}$ ,respectively,under the fixed orientation of $\mathcal{T}$ . For every node $t \in  V\left( \mathcal{T}\right)$ ,let ${X}_{t}\overset{\text{ def }}{ = }\chi \left( t\right)$ be the variables of $t$ and ${Q}_{t}\overset{\text{ def }}{ = }{Q}_{{X}_{t}}$ be the restriction of $Q$ to ${X}_{t}$ ,given by Definition 2.2. We call ${Q}_{t}$ a bag query. Let $\mathbf{X}\overset{\text{ def }}{ = }\operatorname{vars}\left( Q\right)$ . By definition,the query $Q$ is equivalent to the join of the outputs of all bag queries:

定理4.1证明。对于${\operatorname{IVM}}^{ + }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$的情形：设$\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)$为$Q$的树分解，其分数超树宽度为$\mathrm{w}\left( Q\right)$（参见附录B的符号约定）。固定从根节点$r \in  V\left( \mathcal{T}\right)$开始的$\mathcal{T}$方向。给定节点$t \in  V\left( \mathcal{T}\right)$时，将分别用parent(t)、children(t)、ancestors(t)和decendants(t)表示$\mathcal{T}$中$t$在固定方向下的父节点、子节点、祖先节点及后代节点。对每个节点$t \in  V\left( \mathcal{T}\right)$，令${X}_{t}\overset{\text{ def }}{ = }\chi \left( t\right)$为$t$的变量集，${Q}_{t}\overset{\text{ def }}{ = }{Q}_{{X}_{t}}$为根据定义2.2得到的$Q$在${X}_{t}$上的限制，称${Q}_{t}$为袋查询。设$\mathbf{X}\overset{\text{ def }}{ = }\operatorname{vars}\left( Q\right)$。根据定义，查询$Q$等价于所有袋查询输出的连接：

$$
Q\left( \mathbf{X}\right)  = \mathop{\bigwedge }\limits_{{t \in  V\left( \mathcal{T}\right) }}{Q}_{t}\left( {\mathbf{X}}_{t}\right) 
$$

Moreover,for every node $t \in  V\left( \mathcal{T}\right)$ ,we have ${\rho }^{ * }\left( {Q}_{t}\right)  \leq  \mathrm{w}\left( Q\right)$ . For each node $t$ ,let ${Y}_{t}\overset{\text{ def }}{ = }{X}_{t} \cap  {X}_{p}$ where $p$ is the parent of $t$ . (If $t$ is the root,then ${X}_{p}$ is considered to be empty,hence ${Y}_{t}$ is empty.) We construct a query plan for $Q$ by defining a collection of intermediate relations ${Q}_{t}^{\prime },{P}_{t}^{\prime }$ for every node $t \in  V\left( \mathcal{T}\right)$ . In particular, ${Q}_{t}^{\prime },{P}_{t}^{\prime }$ are defined inductively starting from the leaves of $\mathcal{T}$ all the way to the root $r$ :

此外，对每个节点$t \in  V\left( \mathcal{T}\right)$都有${\rho }^{ * }\left( {Q}_{t}\right)  \leq  \mathrm{w}\left( Q\right)$。对于节点$t$，设${Y}_{t}\overset{\text{ def }}{ = }{X}_{t} \cap  {X}_{p}$（其中$p$是$t$的父节点；若$t$为根节点，则视${X}_{p}$为空集，故${Y}_{t}$为空）。我们通过为每个节点$t \in  V\left( \mathcal{T}\right)$定义中间关系集合${Q}_{t}^{\prime },{P}_{t}^{\prime }$来构建$Q$的查询计划。具体而言，${Q}_{t}^{\prime },{P}_{t}^{\prime }$从$\mathcal{T}$的叶节点开始向根节点$r$方向归纳定义：

$$
{Q}_{t}^{\prime }\left( {X}_{t}\right) \overset{\text{ def }}{ = }{Q}_{t}\left( {X}_{t}\right)  \land  \mathop{\bigwedge }\limits_{{c \in  \operatorname{children}\left( t\right) }}{P}_{c}^{\prime }\left( {Y}_{c}\right)  \tag{32}
$$

$$
{P}_{t}^{\prime }\left( {Y}_{t}\right) \overset{\text{ def }}{ = }{\pi }_{{Y}_{t}}{Q}_{t}^{\prime }\left( {X}_{t}\right)  \tag{33}
$$

For any node $t \in  V\left( \mathcal{T}\right)$ ,we have:

对于任意节点$t \in  V\left( \mathcal{T}\right)$，满足：

$$
{Q}_{t}^{\prime }\left( {\mathbf{X}}_{t}\right)  = {\pi }_{{\mathbf{X}}_{t}}\mathop{\bigwedge }\limits_{{d \in  \operatorname{decendants}\left( t\right) \cup \{ t\} }}{Q}_{d}\left( {\mathbf{X}}_{d}\right)  \tag{34}
$$

The proof is by induction starting from the leaves of $\mathcal{T}$ to the root. It is similar to the bottom-up phase of the Yannakakis algorithm [45].

该证明采用从$\mathcal{T}$叶节点到根节点的归纳法，类似于Yannakakis算法[45]的自底向上阶段。

The AGM-bound of each bag query ${Q}_{t}$ is at most ${N}^{\mathrm{w}\left( Q\right) }$ . Since adding a new relation to a bag does not increase the AGM bound of the bag,the AGM-bound of each ${Q}_{t}^{\prime }$ is also at most ${N}^{\mathrm{w}\left( Q\right) }$ . Hence,the output size of each ${Q}_{t}^{\prime }$ (and by extension ${P}_{t}^{\prime }$ ) is at most ${N}^{\mathrm{w}\left( Q\right) }$ . Each query ${Q}_{t}^{\prime }$ can have two types of inserts: Inserts coming from (the input relations of) ${Q}_{t}$ and inserts coming from ${P}_{c}^{\prime }$ for the children $c$ of $t$ . By Lemma 5.1,all updates to ${Q}_{t}^{\prime }$ combined take time $O\left( {N}^{\mathrm{w}\left( Q\right) }\right)$ . Moreover,the projection ${P}_{t}^{\prime }$ of ${Q}_{t}^{\prime }$ can also be updated in the same time.

每个包查询${Q}_{t}$的AGM上界(AGM-bound)至多为${N}^{\mathrm{w}\left( Q\right) }$。由于向包中添加新关系不会增加其AGM上界，因此每个${Q}_{t}^{\prime }$的AGM上界同样至多为${N}^{\mathrm{w}\left( Q\right) }$。因此，每个${Q}_{t}^{\prime }$（进而${P}_{t}^{\prime }$）的输出大小上限为${N}^{\mathrm{w}\left( Q\right) }$。每个查询${Q}_{t}^{\prime }$可能接收两类插入操作：来自${Q}_{t}$（输入关系）的插入，以及来自$t$的子节点$c$的${P}_{c}^{\prime }$插入。根据引理5.1，对${Q}_{t}^{\prime }$的所有更新操作总耗时为$O\left( {N}^{\mathrm{w}\left( Q\right) }\right)$。此外，${Q}_{t}^{\prime }$的投影${P}_{t}^{\prime }$也可在相同时间内完成更新。

In order to do constant-delay enumeration of the output of $Q\left( X\right)$ ,we start from the root bag $r$ and enumerate the output of ${Q}_{r}^{\prime }$ . By (34), ${Q}_{r}^{\prime }\left( {X}_{r}\right)  = {\pi }_{{X}_{r}}Q\left( X\right)$ . For every tuple ${x}_{r}$ in the output of ${Q}_{r}^{\prime }$ ,we enumerate the corresponding tuples ${x}_{c}$ in the output of ${Q}_{c}^{\prime }$ for every child $c$ of $r$ . (Note that for every child $c$ ,there must be at least one tuple ${x}_{c}$ that joins with ${x}_{r}$ ,thanks to (34).) We inductively continue this process until we reach the leaves of $\mathcal{T}$ . In particular,for every node $t \in  V\left( \mathcal{T}\right)$ and every tuple ${x}_{t}$ that we enumerate from ${Q}_{t}^{\prime }$ ,we enumerate the corresponding tuples ${x}_{c}$ from ${Q}_{c}^{\prime }$ for every child $c$ of $t$ ,and we rely on (34) to ensure that there is at least one tuple ${x}_{c}$ for every child $c$ of $t$ .

为实现$Q\left( X\right)$输出结果的常数延迟枚举，我们从根包$r$出发，枚举${Q}_{r}^{\prime }$的输出。根据式(34)，${Q}_{r}^{\prime }\left( {X}_{r}\right)  = {\pi }_{{X}_{r}}Q\left( X\right)$。对于${Q}_{r}^{\prime }$输出中的每个元组${x}_{r}$，我们为$r$的每个子节点$c$枚举${Q}_{c}^{\prime }$输出中对应的元组${x}_{c}$（注意：由于式(34)保证，每个子节点$c$至少存在一个能与${x}_{r}$连接的元组${x}_{c}$）。我们递归延续此过程直至抵达$\mathcal{T}$的叶节点。具体而言，对于每个节点$t \in  V\left( \mathcal{T}\right)$及从${Q}_{t}^{\prime }$枚举的每个元组${x}_{t}$，我们为$t$的每个子节点$c$枚举${Q}_{c}^{\prime }$中对应的元组${x}_{c}$，并依赖式(34)确保$t$的每个子节点$c$至少存在一个匹配元组${x}_{c}$。

### C.4 Upper bound for ${\mathrm{{IVM}}}_{\delta }^{ + }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$

### C.4 ${\mathrm{{IVM}}}_{\delta }^{ + }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$的上界

In this section,we prove Theorem 4.1 for the ${\mathrm{{IVM}}}_{\delta }^{ + }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$ problem. In particular,we explain how to modify the algorithm and proof from Section C.3,which was originally meant for the ${\mathrm{{IVM}}}^{ + }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$ problem. Just like we did in Section C.3, here also we will construct an optimal tree decomposition of $Q$ that minimizes the fractional hypertree width,and we will maintain materialized relations for every bag of the tree decomposition. However, suppose that we have an insert of a tuple $t$ into a relation $R$ and we want to enumerate the new output tuples ${\delta Q}$ that are added due to this particular insert. We can no longer start our constant-delay enumeration from the root of the tree decomposition, as we did in Section C.3. Instead,we have to find the bag $B$ that contains $R$ ,select $t$ ,and start our enumeration from $B$ ,as if $B$ were the root of the tree decomposition. In order to be able to enumerate the output of $Q$ starting from any bag $B$ of the tree decomposition,all bags need to be fully calibrated with each other. In particular,in Section C.3,we only calibrated bags in a bottom-up fashion where each bag was calibrated with its children bags. But here, we have to do full calibration consisting of two phases: One bottom-up phase where we calibrate each bag with its children, followed by a top-down phase where we calibrate each bag with its parent. These two calibrations are similar to the two phases of the Yannakakis algorithm [45].

本节将针对${\mathrm{{IVM}}}_{\delta }^{ + }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$问题证明定理4.1。具体而言，我们将阐述如何修改C.3节中原先针对${\mathrm{{IVM}}}^{ + }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$问题设计的算法与证明。如同C.3节的操作，这里我们也将构建$Q$的最优树分解以最小化分数超树宽度，并为树分解的每个包维护物化关系。但当向关系$R$插入元组$t$时，若需枚举因此次插入新增的输出元组${\delta Q}$，则不能像C.3节那样从树分解的根节点开始恒定延迟枚举。相反，必须找到包含$R$的包$B$，选择$t$后从$B$开始枚举——视该包为树分解的根节点。为实现从树分解任意包$B$起始枚举$Q$输出的能力，所有包之间需完全校准。特别地，C.3节仅采用自底向上校准（每个包与其子包校准），而此处需进行包含两个阶段的完全校准：先是自底向上校准各包与其子包，随后自顶向下校准各包与其父包。这两个校准阶段类似Yannakakis算法[45]的双阶段流程。

THEOREM 4.1. For any query $Q$ ,both ${\mathrm{{IVM}}}^{ + }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$ and ${\mathrm{{IVM}}}_{\delta }^{ + }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$ can be solved with $O\left( {N}^{\mathrm{w}\left( Q\right)  - 1}\right)$ amortized update time, where $N$ is the number of single-tuple inserts.

定理4.1 对于任意查询$Q$，${\mathrm{{IVM}}}^{ + }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$与${\mathrm{{IVM}}}_{\delta }^{ + }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$均可通过$O\left( {N}^{\mathrm{w}\left( Q\right)  - 1}\right)$摊销更新时间求解，其中$N$为单次元组插入次数。

Example 5.5 illustrates the proof idea.

示例5.5展示了该证明思路。

Proof of THEOREM 4.1. The case of ${\mathrm{{IVM}}}_{\delta }^{ + }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$ : We will use the same notation as in the proof of Theorem 4.1 for ${\mathrm{{IVM}}}^{ + }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$ in Section C.3. In particular,here also we will fix an optimal tree decomposition $\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)$ of $Q$ and we will fix an orientation of $\mathcal{T}$ starting from some root node $r \in  V\left( \mathcal{T}\right)$ . In addition to ${Q}_{t}^{\prime }$ and ${P}_{t}^{\prime }$ given by (32) and (33),we will maintain two additional materialized relations ${Q}_{t}^{\prime \prime }$ and ${P}_{t}^{\prime \prime }$ for every node $t \in  V\left( \mathcal{T}\right)$ . However,this time,we will define them inductively starting from the root $r$ all the way to the leaves of $\mathcal{T}$ : (Recall that for the root $r$ ,we have ${Y}_{r} = \varnothing$ .)

定理4.1证明 ${\mathrm{{IVM}}}_{\delta }^{ + }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$情形：我们将沿用C.3节定理4.1证明中关于${\mathrm{{IVM}}}^{ + }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$的符号体系。特别地，此处同样固定$Q$的最优树分解$\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)$，并固定从根节点$r \in  V\left( \mathcal{T}\right)$起始的$\mathcal{T}$方向。除(32)(33)式给出的${Q}_{t}^{\prime }$与${P}_{t}^{\prime }$外，还将为每个节点$t \in  V\left( \mathcal{T}\right)$维护两个额外物化关系${Q}_{t}^{\prime \prime }$和${P}_{t}^{\prime \prime }$。但本次采用从根节点$r$到$\mathcal{T}$叶节点的归纳定义方式（注意根节点$r$满足${Y}_{r} = \varnothing$）。

$$
{Q}_{t}^{\prime \prime }\left( {X}_{t}\right) \overset{\text{ def }}{ = }{Q}_{t}^{\prime }\left( {X}_{t}\right)  \land  {P}_{t}^{\prime \prime }\left( {Y}_{t}\right) 
$$

$$
{P}_{t}^{\prime \prime }\left( {Y}_{t}\right) \overset{\text{ def }}{ = }\left\{  \begin{array}{ll} \text{ true } & \text{ if }t = r \\  {\pi }_{{Y}_{t}}{Q}_{p}^{\prime \prime }\left( {X}_{p}\right) , & \text{ where }p = \text{ parent }\left( t\right) \;\text{ otherwise } \end{array}\right. 
$$

By induction,we can now prove that for any node $t$ ,

通过归纳法可证：对于任意节点$t$

$$
{Q}_{t}^{\prime \prime }\left( {\mathbf{X}}_{t}\right)  = \pi {X}_{t}Q\left( \mathbf{X}\right)  \tag{35}
$$

(Compare the above to (34).) The induction now starts from the root $r$ and goes all the way to the leaves of $\mathcal{T}$ . It is similar to the top-down phase of the Yannakakis algorithm [45]. The AGM bound for each ${Q}_{t}^{\prime \prime }$ is still upper bounded by ${N}^{\mathrm{w}\left( Q\right) }$ . Hence,they can be maintained in a total time of $O\left( {N}^{\mathrm{w}\left( Q\right) }\right)$ . The same goes for ${P}_{t}^{\prime \prime }$ .

（对比上式与(34)）归纳过程从根节点$r$开始直至$\mathcal{T}$的叶节点，类似于Yannakakis算法[45]的自顶向下阶段。每个${Q}_{t}^{\prime \prime }$的AGM界仍以${N}^{\mathrm{w}\left( Q\right) }$为上界，故其总维护时间为$O\left( {N}^{\mathrm{w}\left( Q\right) }\right)$。${P}_{t}^{\prime \prime }$同理。

Now suppose we have an insert of a tuple $t$ into a relation $R\left( Z\right)$ and we want to enumerate the new output tuples ${\delta Q}$ that are added due to this insert. We find the node $t$ in $\mathcal{T}$ where $Z \subseteq  \chi \left( t\right)$ . The existence of such a node $t$ is guaranteed by the definition of a tree decomposition (Definition 2.1). Now,we re-orient the tree $\mathcal{T}$ so that $t$ becomes the root. We enumerate output tuples ${x}_{t}$ of ${Q}_{t}^{\prime \prime }$ that join with $t$ . For each such tuple ${x}_{t}$ ,we enumerate the corresponding tuples ${x}_{c}$ from ${Q}_{c}^{\prime \prime }$ for every child $c$ of $t$ . (These are the children of $t$ under the new orientation of the tree $\mathcal{T}$ ). By (35),we guarantee that for each tuple ${x}_{t}$ and each child $c$ ,there must be at least one tuple ${x}_{c}$ in ${Q}_{c}^{\prime \prime }$ that joins with ${x}_{t}$ . We continue this process inductively all the way to the leaves of $\mathcal{T}$ .

假设现在有一个元组$t$被插入到关系$R\left( Z\right)$中，我们需要枚举因此次插入而新增的输出元组${\delta Q}$。首先在$\mathcal{T}$中找到节点$t$所在位置（其中$Z \subseteq  \chi \left( t\right)$）。根据树分解的定义（定义2.1），该节点$t$必然存在。现将树$\mathcal{T}$重新定向，使$t$成为根节点。枚举${Q}_{t}^{\prime \prime }$中与$t$连接的所有输出元组${x}_{t}$。对于每个这样的元组${x}_{t}$，再针对$t$的每个子节点$c$，从${Q}_{c}^{\prime \prime }$枚举对应的元组${x}_{c}$（这些子节点是在树$\mathcal{T}$新定向下$t$的子节点）。根据式(35)保证，对于每个元组${x}_{t}$和子节点$c$，${Q}_{c}^{\prime \prime }$中至少存在一个能与${x}_{t}$连接的元组${x}_{c}$。我们通过归纳法持续此过程，直至遍历到树$\mathcal{T}$的所有叶节点。

### C.5 Lower bound for ${\mathrm{{IVM}}}^{ + }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$ and ${\mathrm{{IVM}}}_{\delta }^{ + }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$

### C.5 ${\mathrm{{IVM}}}^{ + }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$与${\mathrm{{IVM}}}_{\delta }^{ + }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$的下界证明

In this section,we prove Proposition 4.3,which gives a lower bound on the amortized updated time for both ${\mathrm{{IVM}}}^{ + }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$ and ${\operatorname{IVM}}_{\delta }^{ + }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$ in terms of a lower bound on the static evaluation of $Q$ .

本节将证明命题4.3，该命题通过$Q$静态求值下界，给出了${\mathrm{{IVM}}}^{ + }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$和${\operatorname{IVM}}_{\delta }^{ + }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$摊销更新时间的下界。

Proposition 4.3. For any query $Q$ and any constant $\gamma  > 0$ ,neither ${IV}{M}^{ + }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$ nor ${IV}{M}_{\delta }^{ + }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$ can be solved with $\widetilde{O}\left( {N}^{\omega \left( Q\right)  - 1 - \gamma }\right)$ (amortized) update time.

命题4.3. 对于任意查询$Q$和任意常数$\gamma  > 0$，无论是${IV}{M}^{ + }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$还是${IV}{M}_{\delta }^{ + }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$都无法在$\widetilde{O}\left( {N}^{\omega \left( Q\right)  - 1 - \gamma }\right)$（摊销）更新时间内求解。

Proof of Proposition 4.3. Fix a query $Q$ . We first consider ${\mathrm{{IVM}}}^{ + }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$ . Suppose,for sake of contradiction,that there is a constant $\gamma  > 0$ where ${\operatorname{IVM}}^{ + }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$ can be solved with amortized update time $\widetilde{O}\left( {N}^{\kappa }\right)$ where $\kappa \overset{\text{ def }}{ = }\omega \left( Q\right)  - 1 - \gamma$ . We will show how to use an oracle for ${\mathrm{{IVM}}}^{ + }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$ to solve $\operatorname{Eval}\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$ in time $\widetilde{O}\left( {{\left| \mathcal{D}\right| }^{\omega \left( Q\right)  - \gamma } + \left| {Q\left( \mathcal{D}\right) }\right| }\right)$ on any database instance $\mathcal{D}$ . This contradicts the definition of $\omega \left( Q\right)$ (Definition 3.2).

命题4.3的证明. 设定查询$Q$。首先考虑${\mathrm{{IVM}}}^{ + }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$。假设存在常数$\gamma  > 0$使得${\operatorname{IVM}}^{ + }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$能以$\kappa \overset{\text{ def }}{ = }\omega \left( Q\right)  - 1 - \gamma$的摊销更新时间$\widetilde{O}\left( {N}^{\kappa }\right)$求解（反证假设）。我们将展示如何利用${\mathrm{{IVM}}}^{ + }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$的预言机，在任何数据库实例$\mathcal{D}$上以$\widetilde{O}\left( {{\left| \mathcal{D}\right| }^{\omega \left( Q\right)  - \gamma } + \left| {Q\left( \mathcal{D}\right) }\right| }\right)$时间求解$\operatorname{Eval}\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$，这与$\omega \left( Q\right)$的定义（定义3.2）相矛盾。

We construct a stream of inserts of length $N = \left| \mathcal{D}\right|$ where we insert one tuple of $\mathcal{D}$ at a time. We apply these inserts one by one in a total time of $\widetilde{O}\left( {N}^{1 + \kappa }\right)$ . After the last insert,we invoke constant-delay enumeration of $Q\left( \mathcal{D}\right)$ . Thus,the overall time is $\widetilde{O}\left( {{N}^{1 + \kappa } + \left| {Q\left( \mathcal{D}\right) }\right| }\right)$ .

我们构建一个长度为$N = \left| \mathcal{D}\right|$的插入流，每次插入一个$\mathcal{D}$元组。这些插入操作在总时间$\widetilde{O}\left( {N}^{1 + \kappa }\right)$内逐一执行。最后一次插入后，我们调用$Q\left( \mathcal{D}\right)$的恒定延迟枚举。因此，总耗时为$\widetilde{O}\left( {{N}^{1 + \kappa } + \left| {Q\left( \mathcal{D}\right) }\right| }\right)$。

For ${\operatorname{IVM}}_{\delta }^{ + }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$ ,the proof is very similar except that we enumerate ${\delta Q}\left( \mathcal{D}\right)$ after every insert.

对于${\operatorname{IVM}}_{\delta }^{ + }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$，证明过程非常相似，区别仅在于每次插入后都需枚举${\delta Q}\left( \mathcal{D}\right)$。

## D MISSING DETAILS IN SECTION 6

## D 第六章节缺失细节

In this section,we review some necessary background on intersection joins in the static setting. Let $Q$ be a query, $\bar{Q}$ be its time extension,and $\widehat{Q}$ be its multivariate extension. Suppose we want to evaluate $\bar{Q}$ over a given database instance $\overline{\mathcal{D}}$ . This evaluation problem can be reduced [3] to the evaluation of $\widehat{Q}$ over some database instance, $\widehat{\mathcal{D}}$ ,that satisfies $\left| \widehat{\mathcal{D}}\right|  = \widetilde{O}\left( \left| \overline{\mathcal{D}}\right| \right)$ and can be constructed in time $\widetilde{O}\left( \left| \overline{\mathcal{D}}\right| \right)$ . Moreover,this reduction is optimal (up to a $\widetilde{O}\left( 1\right)$ factor). In particular,the query $\bar{Q}$ is exactly as hard as the hardest component ${\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }$ in $\widehat{Q}$ . This is shown [3] by a backward reduction from each component ${\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }$ to $\bar{Q}$ . We explain both reductions in detail below. Recall that both reductions are in the context of static evaluation. However later on in Section E, we will rely on both reductions in order to prove upper and lower bounds on the IVM problem in the insert-delete setting.

本节回顾静态环境下交集连接的必要背景知识。设$Q$为查询，$\bar{Q}$为其时间扩展，$\widehat{Q}$为其多元扩展。假设我们需要在给定数据库实例$\overline{\mathcal{D}}$上评估$\bar{Q}$。该评估问题可归约[3]为对满足$\left| \widehat{\mathcal{D}}\right|  = \widetilde{O}\left( \left| \overline{\mathcal{D}}\right| \right)$条件的数据库实例$\widehat{\mathcal{D}}$评估$\widehat{Q}$，且可在$\widetilde{O}\left( \left| \overline{\mathcal{D}}\right| \right)$时间内完成构造。此归约是最优的（至多相差$\widetilde{O}\left( 1\right)$因子）。特别地，查询$\bar{Q}$的难度等同于$\widehat{Q}$中最复杂组件${\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }$的难度。文献[3]通过将每个组件${\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }$反向归约至$\bar{Q}$证明了这一点。下文将详细阐述这两种归约方法。需注意这些归约均基于静态评估场景，但在后续E章节中，我们将借助这两种归约来证明插入-删除场景下IVM问题的上下界。

### D.1 Reduction from $\bar{Q}$ to $\widehat{Q}$

### D.1 从$\bar{Q}$到$\widehat{Q}$的归约

The reduction in [3] from a time extension query $\bar{Q}$ to the corresponding multivariate extension query $\widehat{Q}$ relies on constructing a segment tree for the interval variable $\left\lbrack  Z\right\rbrack$ in $\bar{Q}$ .

文献[3]中从时间扩展查询$\bar{Q}$到多元扩展查询$\widehat{Q}$的归约，依赖于为$\bar{Q}$中的区间变量$\left\lbrack  Z\right\rbrack$构建线段树。

Segment Trees. Given $N = {2}^{n}$ for some $n \in  \mathbb{N}$ ,a (discrete) segment tree ${\mathcal{T}}_{N}$ over $N$ is a complete binary tree whose nodes are discrete intervals that are subsets of $\left\lbrack  N\right\rbrack$ . A segment tree has the following properties:

线段树。给定$n \in  \mathbb{N}$的$N = {2}^{n}$，在$N$上的（离散）线段树${\mathcal{T}}_{N}$是一个完全二叉树，其节点是$\left\lbrack  N\right\rbrack$子集的离散区间。线段树具有以下特性：

- The leaves from left-to-right are the intervals $\left\lbrack  {1,1}\right\rbrack  ,\ldots ,\left\lbrack  {N,N}\right\rbrack$ consisting of single values.

- 从左至右的叶节点是单值区间$\left\lbrack  {1,1}\right\rbrack  ,\ldots ,\left\lbrack  {N,N}\right\rbrack$

- Each inner node is the union of its child nodes.

- 每个内部节点是其子节点的并集

Figure 2 depicts a segment tree ${\mathcal{T}}_{8}$ for $N = 8$ . Following [3],we identify each node of a segment tree by a bitstring: The root is identified by the empty string $\varepsilon$ ; for each inner node that is identified by a bitstring $b$ ,the left and right child nodes are identified by the bitstrings $b \circ  0$ and respectively $b \circ  1$ (where $\circ$ denotes string concatenation). We denote by $V\left( {\mathcal{T}}_{N}\right)$ the set of nodes of the segment tree ${\mathcal{T}}_{N}$ and by parent(v)the parent node of a node $v$ . Given an interval $x \subseteq  \left\lbrack  N\right\rbrack$ ,its canonical partition with respect to $N$ is defined as the set of maximal nodes $y$ of ${\mathcal{T}}_{N}$ whose corresponding

图2展示了一棵线段树${\mathcal{T}}_{8}$用于$N = 8$。根据文献[3]，我们通过比特串标识线段树的每个节点：根节点由空字符串$\varepsilon$标识；对于每个由比特串$b$标识的内部节点，其左右子节点分别由比特串$b \circ  0$和$b \circ  1$标识（其中$\circ$表示字符串连接）。我们用$V\left( {\mathcal{T}}_{N}\right)$表示线段树${\mathcal{T}}_{N}$的节点集合，用parent(v)表示节点$v$的父节点。给定区间$x \subseteq  \left\lbrack  N\right\rbrack$，其关于$N$的规范分区定义为${\mathcal{T}}_{N}$中对应

intervals are contained in $x$ ,i.e.

区间包含于$x$内的最大节点集<b12></b12>，即

$$
{\mathrm{{CP}}}_{N}\left( x\right) \overset{\text{ def }}{ = }\left\{  {y \in  V\left( {\mathcal{T}}_{N}\right)  \mid  y \subseteq  x\text{ but parent }\left( y\right)  \nsubseteq  x}\right\}  
$$

For any interval $x \subseteq  \left\lbrack  N\right\rbrack  ,{\mathrm{{CP}}}_{N}\left( x\right)$ is partition of $x$ ; see [3]. Moreover $\left| {{\mathrm{{CP}}}_{N}\left( x\right) }\right|  = O\left( {\log N}\right)$ . For example,in the segment tree ${\mathcal{T}}_{8}$ from Figure 2,we have,

对于任意区间$x \subseteq  \left\lbrack  N\right\rbrack  ,{\mathrm{{CP}}}_{N}\left( x\right)$，其规范分区是$x$的划分，详见文献[3]。此外$\left| {{\mathrm{{CP}}}_{N}\left( x\right) }\right|  = O\left( {\log N}\right)$。例如在图2所示的线段树${\mathcal{T}}_{8}$中，我们有：

$$
{\mathrm{{CP}}}_{8}\left( \left\lbrack  {1,8}\right\rbrack  \right)  = \{ \varepsilon \} 
$$

$$
{\mathrm{{CP}}}_{8}\left( \left\lbrack  {2,5}\right\rbrack  \right)  = \{ {001},{01},{100}\} 
$$

Lemma D.1 ([3]). Consider a set of discrete intervals $I = \left\{  {{x}_{1},\ldots ,{x}_{k}}\right\}$ with ${x}_{i} \subseteq  \left\lbrack  N\right\rbrack$ for some $N \in  \mathbb{N}$ for $i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack$ . It holds that $\mathop{\bigcap }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack  }}{x}_{i} \neq  \varnothing$ if and only if there is a permutation $\sigma$ of $\left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack$ and a tuple $\left( {{b}_{1},\ldots ,{b}_{k}}\right)$ of bitstrings such that

引理D.1（文献[3]）。考虑一组离散区间$I = \left\{  {{x}_{1},\ldots ,{x}_{k}}\right\}$，其中对于$i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack$存在某个$N \in  \mathbb{N}$使得${x}_{i} \subseteq  \left\lbrack  N\right\rbrack$。当且仅当存在$\left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack$的排列$\sigma$和比特串元组$\left( {{b}_{1},\ldots ,{b}_{k}}\right)$时，有$\mathop{\bigcap }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack  }}{x}_{i} \neq  \varnothing$成立：

$$
\left( {{b}_{1} \circ  \cdots  \circ  {b}_{j}}\right)  \in  {\mathrm{{CP}}}_{N}\left( {x}_{{\sigma }_{j}}\right) \text{for}j \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack  \text{.} \tag{36}
$$

Moreover,the set of bitstrings ${b}_{1} \circ  \cdots  \circ  {b}_{k}$ for all tuples $\left( {{b}_{1},\ldots ,{b}_{k}}\right)$ that satisfy (36) for some permutation $\sigma$ of $\left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack$ is exactly the canonical partition of the intersection $\mathop{\bigcap }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack  }}{x}_{i}$ :

此外，对于所有满足式(36)的元组$\left( {{b}_{1},\ldots ,{b}_{k}}\right)$（其中$\sigma$是$\left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack$的某个排列），其比特串集${b}_{1} \circ  \cdots  \circ  {b}_{k}$恰好是交集$\mathop{\bigcap }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack  }}{x}_{i}$的规范分区：

$$
{\mathrm{{CP}}}_{N}\left( {\mathop{\bigcap }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack  }}{x}_{i}}\right)  = \left\{  {\left( {{b}_{1} \circ  \ldots  \circ  {b}_{k}}\right)  \mid  \text{ there is a permutation }\sigma \text{ of }\left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack  \text{ that satisfies }\left( {36}\right) }\right\}  
$$

(Recall that $\circ$ is the concatenation operator on strings.) The above lemma is the core of the reduction. It basically says that a set of $k$ intervals $\mathcal{I}$ intersect if and only if their canonical partitions contain $k$ nodes that fall on the same root-to-leaf path in the segment tree. The nodes on this path can appear in any order, and the order is captured by the permutation $\sigma$ . For example,consider again the segment tree from Figure 2. The intervals $\left( {\left\lbrack  {1,8}\right\rbrack  ,\left\lbrack  {2,5}\right\rbrack  ,\left\lbrack  {3,7}\right\rbrack  }\right)$ overlap. Indeed,the canonical partitions of these intervals contain the nodes $\left( {\varepsilon ,{01},{01}}\right)$ respectively,and these nodes fall on the path from the root $\varepsilon$ to the leaf 010,thus witnessing the intersection. Alternatively,the intersection is also witnessed by the nodes $\left( {\varepsilon ,{100},{10}}\right)$ ,which fall on the path from the root $\varepsilon$ to the leaf 100 . These are the only two witness to the intersection,and they correspond to the two bitstrings 01 and 100 . Note that $\{ {01},{100}\}$ is indeed the canonical partition of $\left\lbrack  {3,5}\right\rbrack$ ,which is the intersection of the three intervals $\left( {\left\lbrack  {1,8}\right\rbrack  ,\left\lbrack  {2,5}\right\rbrack  ,\left\lbrack  {3,7}\right\rbrack  }\right)$ . This is basically what the second part of the lemma says.

（回顾$\circ$是字符串连接运算符。）上述引理是归约的核心，其本质表明：当且仅当$k$个区间$\mathcal{I}$的规范划分包含位于线段树同一条根到叶路径上的$k$个节点时，这些区间才会相交。路径上的节点可任意排列，其顺序由置换$\sigma$体现。例如图2线段树中，区间$\left( {\left\lbrack  {1,8}\right\rbrack  ,\left\lbrack  {2,5}\right\rbrack  ,\left\lbrack  {3,7}\right\rbrack  }\right)$存在重叠。这些区间的规范划分分别包含节点$\left( {\varepsilon ,{01},{01}}\right)$，这些节点恰好位于从根节点$\varepsilon$到叶节点010的路径上，从而证明相交性。此外，该相交性也可由位于根节点$\varepsilon$到叶节点100路径上的节点$\left( {\varepsilon ,{100},{10}}\right)$证明。这是仅有的两个相交见证路径，对应二进制串01和100。注意$\{ {01},{100}\}$确实是三个区间$\left( {\left\lbrack  {1,8}\right\rbrack  ,\left\lbrack  {2,5}\right\rbrack  ,\left\lbrack  {3,7}\right\rbrack  }\right)$交集$\left\lbrack  {3,5}\right\rbrack$的规范划分，这正是引理第二部分所阐述的内容。

Example D.2. Suppose we want to solve the query ${\bar{Q}}_{ \bigtriangleup  }$ from Example 6.3. We can start by constructing a segment tree for the interval variable $\left\lbrack  Z\right\rbrack$ and replacing each value $\left\lbrack  z\right\rbrack$ of $\left\lbrack  Z\right\rbrack$ by the components in its canonical partition ${\mathrm{{CP}}}_{N}\left( \left\lbrack  z\right\rbrack  \right)$ . We can then use Lemma D. 1 to look for intersections. In particular, let's first try to find triples of segment-tree nodes $\left( {{z}^{R},{z}^{S},{z}^{T}}\right)$ from $R,S,T$ respectively that appear on the same root-to-leaf path in the specific order $\left( {{z}^{R},{z}^{S},{z}^{T}}\right)$ . This can only happen if the bitstring ${z}^{R}$ is a prefix of ${z}^{S}$ which in turn is a prefix of ${z}^{T}$ . To test this prefix pattern,we can break each ${z}^{S}$ into a concatenation two strings ${z}^{S} = {z}_{1}^{S} \circ  {z}_{1}^{S}$ in all possible ways and similarly break ${z}^{T}$ into a concatenation of three string ${z}^{T} = {z}_{1}^{T} \circ  {z}_{2}^{T} \circ  {z}_{3}^{T}$ and then check whether ${z}^{R} = {z}_{1}^{S} = {z}_{1}^{T}$ and ${z}_{2}^{S} = {z}_{2}^{T}$ . In particular,we construct the following input relations:

示例D.2 假设需处理示例6.3中的查询${\bar{Q}}_{ \bigtriangleup  }$。可先为区间变量$\left\lbrack  Z\right\rbrack$构建线段树，并将$\left\lbrack  Z\right\rbrack$的每个取值$\left\lbrack  z\right\rbrack$替换为其规范划分${\mathrm{{CP}}}_{N}\left( \left\lbrack  z\right\rbrack  \right)$的组件。随后运用引理D.1检测相交性。具体而言，首先尝试从$R,S,T$中分别找出满足特定顺序$\left( {{z}^{R},{z}^{S},{z}^{T}}\right)$且位于同一条根到叶路径上的三个线段树节点$\left( {{z}^{R},{z}^{S},{z}^{T}}\right)$。这种情况仅当比特串${z}^{R}$是${z}^{S}$的前缀，而${z}^{S}$又是${z}^{T}$的前缀时才会发生。为验证此前缀模式，可将每个${z}^{S}$拆分为所有可能的两部分串${z}^{S} = {z}_{1}^{S} \circ  {z}_{1}^{S}$，同理将${z}^{T}$拆分为三部分串${z}^{T} = {z}_{1}^{T} \circ  {z}_{2}^{T} \circ  {z}_{3}^{T}$，再检验${z}^{R} = {z}_{1}^{S} = {z}_{1}^{T}$与${z}_{2}^{S} = {z}_{2}^{T}$是否成立。具体构造如下输入关系：

$$
{\widehat{R}}_{123} = \left\{  {\left( {{z}_{1},a,b}\right) \mid \exists \left\lbrack  z\right\rbrack   : \left( {\left\lbrack  z\right\rbrack  ,a,b}\right)  \in  \bar{R} \land  {z}_{1} \in  {\mathrm{{CP}}}_{N}\left( \left\lbrack  z\right\rbrack  \right) }\right\}   \tag{37}
$$

$$
{\widehat{S}}_{123} = \left\{  {\left( {{z}_{1},{z}_{2},b,c}\right) \mid \exists \left\lbrack  z\right\rbrack   : \left( {\left\lbrack  z\right\rbrack  ,b,c}\right)  \in  \bar{S} \land  \left( {{z}_{1} \circ  {z}_{2}}\right)  \in  {\mathrm{{CP}}}_{N}\left( \left\lbrack  z\right\rbrack  \right) }\right\}  
$$

$$
{\widehat{T}}_{123} = \left\{  {\left( {{z}_{1},{z}_{2},{z}_{3},a,c}\right) \mid \exists z : \left( {\left\lbrack  z\right\rbrack  ,a,c}\right)  \in  \bar{T} \land  \left( {{z}_{1} \circ  {z}_{2} \circ  {z}_{3}}\right)  \in  {\mathrm{{CP}}}_{N}\left( \left\lbrack  z\right\rbrack  \right) }\right\}  
$$

Then we solve the query

随后求解该查询

$$
{\widehat{Q}}_{123}\left( {{Z}_{1},{Z}_{2},{Z}_{3},A,B,C}\right)  = {\widehat{R}}_{123}\left( {{Z}_{1},A,B}\right)  \land  {\widehat{S}}_{123}\left( {{Z}_{1},{Z}_{2},B,C}\right)  \land  {\widehat{T}}_{123}\left( {{Z}_{1},{Z}_{2},{Z}_{3},A,C}\right)  \tag{38}
$$

Note that $\left| {\widehat{T}}_{123}\right|  = O\left( {\left| \bar{T}\right|  \cdot  \operatorname{polylog}\left( N\right) }\right)$ and the same goes for ${\widehat{R}}_{123}$ and ${\widehat{S}}_{123}$ . This is because the height of the segment tree is $O\left( {\log N}\right)$ . Consider now the database instance depicted in Figure 1. Note that the corresponding relation ${\widehat{R}}_{123}$ above contains (among others) the tuple $\left( {\varepsilon ,{a}_{1},{b}_{1}}\right)$ while ${\widehat{S}}_{123}$ contains the tuples $\left( {\varepsilon ,{01},{b}_{1},{c}_{1}}\right)$ and ${\widehat{T}}_{123}$ contains the tuple $\left( {\varepsilon ,{01},\varepsilon ,{a}_{1},{c}_{1}}\right)$ . Those tuples join together producing the output tuple $\left( {\varepsilon ,{01},\varepsilon ,{a}_{1},{b}_{1},{c}_{1}}\right)$ of ${\widehat{Q}}_{123}$ ,which is a witness to the output tuple $\left( {\left\lbrack  {3,5}\right\rbrack  ,{a}_{1},{b}_{1},{c}_{1}}\right)$ of ${\bar{Q}}_{ \vartriangle  }$ .

注意$\left| {\widehat{T}}_{123}\right|  = O\left( {\left| \bar{T}\right|  \cdot  \operatorname{polylog}\left( N\right) }\right)$，同理适用于${\widehat{R}}_{123}$和${\widehat{S}}_{123}$。这是因为线段树的高度为$O\left( {\log N}\right)$。现在考虑图1所示的数据库实例，需注意上述对应关系${\widehat{R}}_{123}$包含（其中包括）元组$\left( {\varepsilon ,{a}_{1},{b}_{1}}\right)$，而${\widehat{S}}_{123}$包含元组$\left( {\varepsilon ,{01},{b}_{1},{c}_{1}}\right)$，且${\widehat{T}}_{123}$包含元组$\left( {\varepsilon ,{01},\varepsilon ,{a}_{1},{c}_{1}}\right)$。这些元组连接后生成${\widehat{Q}}_{123}$的输出元组$\left( {\varepsilon ,{01},\varepsilon ,{a}_{1},{b}_{1},{c}_{1}}\right)$，该元组是${\bar{Q}}_{ \vartriangle  }$输出元组$\left( {\left\lbrack  {3,5}\right\rbrack  ,{a}_{1},{b}_{1},{c}_{1}}\right)$的见证。

The query ${\widehat{Q}}_{123}$ above only considers the specific permutation(R,S,T)of the input relations $\{ R,S,T\}$ . We have five other permutations corresponding to the following five queries:

上述查询${\widehat{Q}}_{123}$仅考虑输入关系$\{ R,S,T\}$的特定排列(R,S,T)。我们还有另外五个排列对应以下五个查询：

$$
{\widehat{Q}}_{132} = {\widehat{R}}_{132}\left( {{Z}_{1},A,B}\right)  \land  {\widehat{S}}_{132}\left( {{Z}_{1},{Z}_{2},{Z}_{3},B,C}\right)  \land  {\widehat{T}}_{132}\left( {{Z}_{1},{Z}_{2},A,C}\right) 
$$

$$
{\widehat{Q}}_{213} = {\widehat{R}}_{213}\left( {{Z}_{1},{Z}_{2},A,B}\right)  \land  {\widehat{S}}_{213}\left( {{Z}_{1},B,C}\right)  \land  {\widehat{T}}_{213}\left( {{Z}_{1},{Z}_{2},{Z}_{3},A,C}\right) 
$$

$$
{\widehat{Q}}_{231} = {\widehat{R}}_{231}\left( {{Z}_{1},{Z}_{2},{Z}_{3},A,B}\right)  \land  {\widehat{S}}_{231}\left( {{Z}_{1},B,C}\right)  \land  {\widehat{T}}_{231}\left( {{Z}_{1},{Z}_{2},A,C}\right) 
$$

$$
{\widehat{Q}}_{312} = {\widehat{R}}_{312}\left( {{Z}_{1},{Z}_{2},A,B}\right)  \land  {\widehat{S}}_{312}\left( {{Z}_{1},{Z}_{2},{Z}_{3},B,C}\right)  \land  {\widehat{T}}_{312}\left( {{Z}_{1},A,C}\right) 
$$

$$
{\widehat{Q}}_{321} = {\widehat{R}}_{321}\left( {{Z}_{1},{Z}_{2},{Z}_{3},A,B}\right)  \land  {\widehat{S}}_{321}\left( {{Z}_{1},{Z}_{2},B,C}\right)  \land  {\widehat{T}}_{321}\left( {{Z}_{1},A,C}\right)  \tag{39}
$$

Over the database instance depicted in Figure 1,note that ${\widehat{R}}_{132}$ contains the tuple $\left( {\varepsilon ,{a}_{1},{b}_{1}}\right)$ while ${\widehat{T}}_{132}$ contains $\left( {\varepsilon ,{10},{a}_{1},{c}_{1}}\right)$ and ${\widehat{S}}_{132}$ contains $\left( {\varepsilon ,{10},0,{b}_{1},{c}_{1}}\right)$ . These tuples join together producing the output tuple $\left( {\varepsilon ,{10},0,{a}_{1},{b}_{1},{c}_{1}}\right)$ of ${\widehat{Q}}_{132}$ ,which is a yet another witness to the output tuple $\left( {\left\lbrack  {3,5}\right\rbrack  ,{a}_{1},{b}_{1},{c}_{1}}\right)$ of ${\bar{Q}}_{\Delta }$ . In particular,the output tuple $\left( {\left\lbrack  {3,5}\right\rbrack  ,{a}_{1},{b}_{1},{c}_{1}}\right)$ has two different witnesses in ${\widehat{Q}}_{\Delta }$ specifically because the canonical partition of $\left\lbrack  {3,5}\right\rbrack$ consists of two nodes,namely 01 and 100. More generally,any output tuple $\left( {\left\lbrack  z\right\rbrack  ,a,b,c}\right)$ of ${\bar{Q}}_{\Delta }$ has as many witnesses in ${\widehat{Q}}_{\Delta }$ as the number of nodes in the canonical partition of $\left\lbrack  z\right\rbrack$ . (Recall that for any interval $\left\lbrack  z\right\rbrack$ ,we have $\left| {{\mathrm{{CP}}}_{N}\left( \left\lbrack  z\right\rbrack  \right) }\right|  = O\left( {\log N}\right)$ .)

在图1所示数据库实例中，注意${\widehat{R}}_{132}$包含元组$\left( {\varepsilon ,{a}_{1},{b}_{1}}\right)$，而${\widehat{T}}_{132}$包含$\left( {\varepsilon ,{10},{a}_{1},{c}_{1}}\right)$，且${\widehat{S}}_{132}$包含$\left( {\varepsilon ,{10},0,{b}_{1},{c}_{1}}\right)$。这些元组连接后生成${\widehat{Q}}_{132}$的输出元组$\left( {\varepsilon ,{10},0,{a}_{1},{b}_{1},{c}_{1}}\right)$，这是${\bar{Q}}_{\Delta }$输出元组$\left( {\left\lbrack  {3,5}\right\rbrack  ,{a}_{1},{b}_{1},{c}_{1}}\right)$的另一个见证。特别地，输出元组$\left( {\left\lbrack  {3,5}\right\rbrack  ,{a}_{1},{b}_{1},{c}_{1}}\right)$在${\widehat{Q}}_{\Delta }$中有两个不同见证，正是因为$\left\lbrack  {3,5}\right\rbrack$的规范分区由两个节点组成（即01和100）。更一般地说，${\bar{Q}}_{\Delta }$的任何输出元组$\left( {\left\lbrack  z\right\rbrack  ,a,b,c}\right)$在${\widehat{Q}}_{\Delta }$中的见证数量，等于$\left\lbrack  z\right\rbrack$规范分区中的节点数。（需注意对于任意区间$\left\lbrack  z\right\rbrack$，我们有$\left| {{\mathrm{{CP}}}_{N}\left( \left\lbrack  z\right\rbrack  \right) }\right|  = O\left( {\log N}\right)$。）

The answer to ${\bar{Q}}_{\Delta }$ can be retrieved from the union of the answers to the six queries above. The new relations ${\widehat{R}}_{123},{\widehat{S}}_{123},\ldots$ can be constructed in time $O\left( {N \cdot  \text{polylog}N}\right)$ where $N$ is the input size to ${\bar{Q}}_{\Delta }$ .

关于${\bar{Q}}_{\Delta }$的答案可从上述六个查询结果的并集中获取。新关系${\widehat{R}}_{123},{\widehat{S}}_{123},\ldots$可在$O\left( {N \cdot  \text{polylog}N}\right)$时间内构建完成，其中$N$表示${\bar{Q}}_{\Delta }$的输入规模。

Extrapolating from the above example, we introduce the following definition:

基于上述示例进行推演，我们引入以下定义：

Definition D.3 (Canonical partition of a relation ${\mathrm{{CP}}}_{N}^{\left( i\right) }\left( R\right)$ ). Let $t = \left( {\left\lbrack  z\right\rbrack  ,{x}_{1},\ldots ,{x}_{m}}\right)$ be a tuple where $\left\lbrack  z\right\rbrack$ is an interval and ${x}_{1},\ldots ,{x}_{m}$ are points. Given natural numbers $N$ and $i$ ,the canonical partition of $t$ is defined as the following set of tuples

定义D.3（关系${\mathrm{{CP}}}_{N}^{\left( i\right) }\left( R\right)$的规范划分）。设$t = \left( {\left\lbrack  z\right\rbrack  ,{x}_{1},\ldots ,{x}_{m}}\right)$为一个元组，其中$\left\lbrack  z\right\rbrack$是区间，${x}_{1},\ldots ,{x}_{m}$为点集。给定自然数$N$和$i$，$t$的规范划分定义为以下元组集合

$$
{\mathrm{{CP}}}_{N}^{\left( i\right) }\left( {\left\lbrack  z\right\rbrack  ,{x}_{1},\ldots ,{x}_{m}}\right) \overset{\text{ def }}{ = }\left\{  {\left( {{z}_{1},\ldots ,{z}_{i},{x}_{1},\ldots ,{x}_{m}}\right)  \mid  \left( {{z}_{1} \circ  \cdots  \circ  {z}_{i}}\right)  \in  {\mathrm{{CP}}}_{N}\left( \left\lbrack  z\right\rbrack  \right) }\right\}   \tag{40}
$$

We lift the definition of a canonical partition ${\mathrm{{CP}}}_{N}^{\left( i\right) }$ to relations and define ${\mathrm{{CP}}}_{N}^{\left( i\right) }\left( R\right)$ as the union of the canonical partitions of the tuples in the given relation $R$ . We also lift the definition to deltas and define ${\mathrm{{CP}}}_{N}^{\left( i\right) }\left( {\delta R}\right)$ as the corresponding delta for ${\mathrm{{CP}}}_{N}^{\left( i\right) }\left( R\right)$ .

我们将规范划分${\mathrm{{CP}}}_{N}^{\left( i\right) }$的定义推广至关系，并将${\mathrm{{CP}}}_{N}^{\left( i\right) }\left( R\right)$定义为给定关系$R$中各元组规范划分的并集。同时将该定义延伸至增量，将${\mathrm{{CP}}}_{N}^{\left( i\right) }\left( {\delta R}\right)$定义为${\mathrm{{CP}}}_{N}^{\left( i\right) }\left( R\right)$对应的增量。

Definition D.4 (Canonical partition of a database instance ${\mathrm{{CP}}}_{N}\left( \overline{\mathcal{D}}\right)$ ). Let $Q = {R}_{1}\left( {X}_{1}\right)  \land  \cdots  \land  {R}_{k}\left( {X}_{k}\right)$ be a query and $\bar{Q} = {\bar{R}}_{1}\left( {\left\lbrack  Z\right\rbrack  ,{X}_{1}}\right)  \land  \cdots  \land  {\bar{R}}_{k}\left( {\left\lbrack  Z\right\rbrack  ,{X}_{k}}\right)$ its time extension. Given a permutation $\sigma  \in  {\sum }_{k}$ ,let ${\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }$ be the corresponding component of its multivariate extension $\widehat{Q}$ :

定义D.4（数据库实例${\mathrm{{CP}}}_{N}\left( \overline{\mathcal{D}}\right)$的规范划分）。设$Q = {R}_{1}\left( {X}_{1}\right)  \land  \cdots  \land  {R}_{k}\left( {X}_{k}\right)$为查询，$\bar{Q} = {\bar{R}}_{1}\left( {\left\lbrack  Z\right\rbrack  ,{X}_{1}}\right)  \land  \cdots  \land  {\bar{R}}_{k}\left( {\left\lbrack  Z\right\rbrack  ,{X}_{k}}\right)$为其时间扩展。给定排列$\sigma  \in  {\sum }_{k}$，令${\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }$表示其多元扩展$\widehat{Q}$的对应分量：

$$
{\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma } = {\widehat{R}}_{{\sigma }_{1}}\left( {{Z}_{1},{X}_{{\sigma }_{1}}}\right)  \land  {\widehat{R}}_{{\sigma }_{2}}\left( {{Z}_{1},{Z}_{2},{X}_{{\sigma }_{2}}}\right)  \land  \cdots  \land  {\widehat{R}}_{{\sigma }_{k}}\left( {{Z}_{1},\ldots ,{Z}_{k},{X}_{{\sigma }_{k}}}\right) 
$$

Given a database instance $\overline{\mathcal{D}}$ for $\bar{Q}$ ,the canonical partition of $\overline{\mathcal{D}}$ using $\sigma$ ,denoted by ${\mathrm{{CP}}}_{N}^{\sigma }\left( \overline{\mathcal{D}}\right)$ ,is a database instance ${\widehat{\mathcal{D}}}_{\sigma }$ for ${\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }$ that is defined as:

对于$\bar{Q}$的数据库实例$\overline{\mathcal{D}}$，使用$\sigma$的规范划分（记作${\mathrm{{CP}}}_{N}^{\sigma }\left( \overline{\mathcal{D}}\right)$）是为${\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }$定义的数据库实例${\widehat{\mathcal{D}}}_{\sigma }$，其表达式为：

$$
{\widehat{R}}_{{\sigma }_{i}}\overset{\text{ def }}{ = }{\mathrm{{CP}}}_{N}^{\left( i\right) }\left( {\bar{R}}_{{\sigma }_{i}}\right) ,\;\text{ for }i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack   \tag{41}
$$

Moreover,define ${\mathrm{{CP}}}_{N}\left( \overline{\mathcal{D}}\right)$ to be a database instance for $\widehat{Q}$ which is a combination of ${\mathrm{{CP}}}_{N}^{\sigma }\left( \overline{\mathcal{D}}\right)$ for every $\sigma  \in  {\sum }_{k}$ :

此外，定义${\mathrm{{CP}}}_{N}\left( \overline{\mathcal{D}}\right)$为$\widehat{Q}$的数据库实例，该实例由每个$\sigma  \in  {\sum }_{k}$对应的${\mathrm{{CP}}}_{N}^{\sigma }\left( \overline{\mathcal{D}}\right)$组合而成：

$$
{\mathrm{{CP}}}_{N}\left( \overline{\mathcal{D}}\right) \overset{\text{ def }}{ = }{\left( {\mathrm{{CP}}}_{N}^{\sigma }\left( \overline{\mathcal{D}}\right) \right) }_{\sigma  \in  {\sum }_{k}}
$$

We lift the definition of ${\mathrm{{CP}}}_{N}^{\sigma }$ to deltas and define ${\mathrm{{CP}}}_{N}^{\sigma }\left( {\delta \overline{\mathcal{D}}}\right)$ to be the corresponding delta for ${\mathrm{{CP}}}_{N}^{\sigma }\left( \overline{\mathcal{D}}\right)$ . We define ${\mathrm{{CP}}}_{N}\left( {\delta \overline{\mathcal{D}}}\right)$ similarly.

我们将${\mathrm{{CP}}}_{N}^{\sigma }$的定义延伸至增量，将${\mathrm{{CP}}}_{N}^{\sigma }\left( {\delta \overline{\mathcal{D}}}\right)$定义为${\mathrm{{CP}}}_{N}^{\sigma }\left( \overline{\mathcal{D}}\right)$对应的增量。${\mathrm{{CP}}}_{N}\left( {\delta \overline{\mathcal{D}}}\right)$的定义同理。

(37) is a special case of (41) above. The following theorem basically says that evaluating $\bar{Q}\left( \overline{\mathcal{D}}\right)$ is equivalent to evaluating $\widehat{Q}$ on the canonical partition of $\overline{\mathcal{D}}$ .

(37)是上述(41)的特例。以下定理本质上表明：评估$\bar{Q}\left( \overline{\mathcal{D}}\right)$等价于在$\overline{\mathcal{D}}$的规范划分上评估$\widehat{Q}$。

THEOREM D. 5 (IMPLICIT IN [3]). Given a tuple $\left( {{z}_{1},\ldots ,{z}_{k},{x}_{1},\ldots ,{x}_{m}}\right)$ ,define

定理D.5（隐含于文献[3]）。给定元组$\left( {{z}_{1},\ldots ,{z}_{k},{x}_{1},\ldots ,{x}_{m}}\right)$，定义

$$
{G}_{k}\left( {{z}_{1},\ldots ,{z}_{k},{x}_{1},\ldots ,{x}_{m}}\right) \overset{\text{ def }}{ = }\left( {{z}_{1} \circ  \cdots  \circ  {z}_{k},{x}_{1},\ldots ,{x}_{m}}\right)  \tag{42}
$$

We lift the definition of ${G}_{k}$ to relations and define ${G}_{k}\left( R\right)$ to be the relation resulting from applying $G$ to every tuple in $R$ . Then,

我们将${G}_{k}$的定义提升至关系层面，并将${G}_{k}\left( R\right)$定义为对$R$中每个元组应用$G$所得到的关系。因此，

$$
{\mathrm{{CP}}}_{N}^{\left( 1\right) }\left( {\bar{Q}\left( \overline{\mathcal{D}}\right) }\right)  = {G}_{k}\left( {\widehat{Q}\left( {{\mathrm{{CP}}}_{N}\left( \overline{\mathcal{D}}\right) }\right) }\right)  \tag{43}
$$

### D.2 Reduction from $\widehat{Q}$ to $\bar{Q}$

### D.2 从$\widehat{Q}$到$\bar{Q}$的归约

In the previous Section D.1,we saw how to reduce the static evaluation of $\bar{Q}$ to $\widehat{Q}$ . We now summarize the backward reduction from the static evaluation of each component ${\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }$ of $\widehat{Q}$ back to $\bar{Q}$ ,based on [3]. Later on,we will use this backward reduction to prove lower bounds on the IVM problem in Section E.4.

在D.1节中，我们已阐述如何将$\bar{Q}$的静态评估归约为$\widehat{Q}$。现基于文献[3]，总结将$\widehat{Q}$各分量${\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }$的静态评估反向归约至$\bar{Q}$的方法。后续将在E.4节利用该反向归约证明IVM问题的下界。

Example 6.5 introduces the high-level idea of the backward reduction. Extrapolating from Example 6.5, we introduce the following concept:

示例6.5介绍了反向归约的核心思想。通过该示例的延伸，我们引入以下概念：

Definition D. 6 (Interval version of a database instance $\operatorname{IV}\left( {\widehat{\mathcal{D}}}_{\sigma }\right)$ ). Let $Q = {R}_{1}\left( {X}_{1}\right)  \land  \cdots  \land  {R}_{k}\left( {X}_{k}\right)$ be a query, $\bar{Q}$ its time extension, $\sigma$ be a permutation of $\left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack$ ,and ${\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }$ be a component of the multivariate extension $\widehat{Q}$ of $Q$ . Let ${\widehat{\mathcal{D}}}_{\sigma }$ be an arbitrary database instance for ${\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }$ . Define the interval version of ${\widehat{\mathcal{D}}}_{\sigma }$ ,denoted by $\operatorname{IV}\left( {\widehat{\mathcal{D}}}_{\sigma }\right)$ ,to be a database instance $\bar{D}$ for $\bar{Q}$ that is constructed as follows: WLOG assume each value $x$ that appears in ${\widehat{\mathcal{D}}}_{\sigma }$ is a bitstring of length exactly $\ell$ for some constant $\ell$ (pad with zeros if needed) and let $N\overset{\text{ def }}{ = }{2}^{k\ell }$ . Let ${\operatorname{seg}}_{N}\left( b\right)$ map a bitstring $b$ of length at most $k\ell$ to the corresponding interval in the segment tree ${\mathcal{T}}_{N}$ . Then $\operatorname{IV}\left( {\widehat{\mathcal{D}}}_{\sigma }\right)$ consists of the following relations for $i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack$ :

定义D.6（数据库实例$\operatorname{IV}\left( {\widehat{\mathcal{D}}}_{\sigma }\right)$的区间版本）。设$Q = {R}_{1}\left( {X}_{1}\right)  \land  \cdots  \land  {R}_{k}\left( {X}_{k}\right)$为查询，$\bar{Q}$为其时间扩展，$\sigma$是$\left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack$的排列，${\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }$为$Q$多元扩展$\widehat{Q}$的分量。设${\widehat{\mathcal{D}}}_{\sigma }$是${\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }$的任意数据库实例。定义${\widehat{\mathcal{D}}}_{\sigma }$的区间版本（记作$\operatorname{IV}\left( {\widehat{\mathcal{D}}}_{\sigma }\right)$）为按以下方式构造的$\bar{Q}$数据库实例$\bar{D}$：不失一般性，假定${\widehat{\mathcal{D}}}_{\sigma }$中每个值$x$均为长度恰好$\ell$的比特串（不足补零），并设$N\overset{\text{ def }}{ = }{2}^{k\ell }$。令${\operatorname{seg}}_{N}\left( b\right)$将长度不超过$k\ell$的比特串$b$映射至线段树${\mathcal{T}}_{N}$对应区间，则$\operatorname{IV}\left( {\widehat{\mathcal{D}}}_{\sigma }\right)$包含以下$i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack$关系：

$$
{\bar{R}}_{{\sigma }_{i}}\overset{\text{ def }}{ = }\left\{  {\left( {{\operatorname{seg}}_{N}\left( {{z}_{1} \circ  \cdots  \circ  {z}_{i}}\right) ,{x}_{1},\ldots ,{x}_{{m}_{i}}}\right)  \mid  \left( {{z}_{1},\ldots ,{z}_{i},{x}_{1},\ldots ,{x}_{{m}_{i}}}\right)  \in  {\widehat{R}}_{{\sigma }_{i}}}\right\}  
$$

We lift the definition of IV to deltas and define $\operatorname{IV}\left( {\delta {\widehat{\mathcal{D}}}_{\sigma }}\right)$ to be the corresponding delta for $\operatorname{IV}\left( {\widehat{\mathcal{D}}}_{\sigma }\right)$ .

我们将IV的定义扩展至增量，定义$\operatorname{IV}\left( {\delta {\widehat{\mathcal{D}}}_{\sigma }}\right)$为$\operatorname{IV}\left( {\widehat{\mathcal{D}}}_{\sigma }\right)$对应的增量。

Proposition D.7. For any database instance ${\widehat{\mathcal{D}}}_{\sigma }$ for ${\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }$ ,we have

命题D.7。对于${\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }$的任意数据库实例${\widehat{\mathcal{D}}}_{\sigma }$，有

$$
\left| {\mathrm{{IV}}\left( {\widehat{\mathcal{D}}}_{\sigma }\right) }\right|  = \left| {\widehat{\mathcal{D}}}_{\sigma }\right|  \tag{44}
$$

Moreover, $\operatorname{IV}\left( {\widehat{\mathcal{D}}}_{\sigma }\right)$ can be computed in linear time in $\left| {\widehat{\mathcal{D}}}_{\sigma }\right|$ .

此外，$\operatorname{IV}\left( {\widehat{\mathcal{D}}}_{\sigma }\right)$可在$\left| {\widehat{\mathcal{D}}}_{\sigma }\right|$的线性时间内计算完成。

The following theorem basically says that evaluating ${\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }$ on ${\widehat{\mathcal{D}}}_{\sigma }$ is equivalent to evaluating $\bar{Q}$ on the interval version of ${\widehat{\mathcal{D}}}_{\sigma }$ .

以下定理本质上表明，在${\widehat{\mathcal{D}}}_{\sigma }$上评估${\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }$等价于在${\widehat{\mathcal{D}}}_{\sigma }$的区间版本上评估$\bar{Q}$。

THEOREM D. 8 (IMPLICIT IN [3]). Given a tuple $\left( {{z}_{1},\ldots ,{z}_{k},{x}_{1},\ldots ,{x}_{m}}\right)$ ,define

定理 D.8（隐含于文献[3]）。给定元组$\left( {{z}_{1},\ldots ,{z}_{k},{x}_{1},\ldots ,{x}_{m}}\right)$，定义

$$
{H}_{k}\left( {\left\lbrack  z\right\rbrack  ,{x}_{1},\ldots ,{x}_{m}}\right) \overset{\text{ def }}{ = }\left\{  {\left( {{z}_{1},\ldots ,{z}_{k},{x}_{1},\ldots ,{x}_{m}}\right)  \mid  \left( {{z}_{1} \circ  \cdots  \circ  {z}_{k}}\right)  \in  {\mathrm{{CP}}}_{N}\left( \left\lbrack  z\right\rbrack  \right)  \land  \left| {z}_{1}\right|  = \cdots  = \left| {z}_{k}\right| }\right\}   \tag{45}
$$

Moreover,we lift ${H}_{k}$ to relations and define ${H}_{k}$ of a relation $R$ to be the relation resulting from applying ${H}_{k}$ to every tuple in R. Then,

此外，我们将${H}_{k}$提升至关系层面，并将关系$R$的${H}_{k}$定义为对R中每个元组应用${H}_{k}$后得到的关系。于是有：

$$
{\widehat{Q}}_{\mathbf{\sigma }}\left( {\widehat{\mathcal{D}}}_{\mathbf{\sigma }}\right)  = {H}_{k}\left( {\bar{Q}\left( {\mathrm{{IV}}\left( {\widehat{\mathcal{D}}}_{\mathbf{\sigma }}\right) }\right) }\right)  \tag{46}
$$

$$
\left| {{\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }\left( {\widehat{\mathcal{D}}}_{\sigma }\right) }\right|  = \left| {\bar{Q}\left( {\operatorname{IV}\left( {\widehat{\mathcal{D}}}_{\sigma }\right) }\right) }\right|  \tag{47}
$$

## E MISSING DETAILS FROM SECTION 7

## E 第7章节缺失细节

Theorem 4.4 gives an upper bound on the amortized update time for both the IVM ${}^{ \pm  }$ and IVM ${}_{\delta }^{ \pm  }$ problems in terms of the current database size $\left| \mathcal{D}\right|$ . In order to prove it,we first prove Lemma 7.1 which gives a weaker upper bound where $\left| \mathcal{D}\right|$ is replaced by the number of single-tuple updates $N$ . We prove Lemma 7.1 for ${\mathrm{{IVM}}}^{ \pm  }$ in Section E. 1 and for ${\mathrm{{IVM}}}_{\delta }^{ \pm  }$ in Section E.2. In Section E.3, we show how to use Lemma 7.1 as a black box in order to prove Theorem 4.4.

定理4.4针对IVM${}^{ \pm  }$和IVM${}_{\delta }^{ \pm  }$问题，根据当前数据库规模$\left| \mathcal{D}\right|$给出了摊销更新时间的上界。为证明该定理，我们首先证明引理7.1——该引理给出一个较弱的上界，其中$\left| \mathcal{D}\right|$被替换为单次元组更新次数$N$。我们在E.1节证明${\mathrm{{IVM}}}^{ \pm  }$的引理7.1，在E.2节证明${\mathrm{{IVM}}}_{\delta }^{ \pm  }$的情况。E.3节将展示如何将引理7.1作为黑盒来证明定理4.4。

In contrast to Theorem 4.4,Theorem 4.5 gives a lower bound on the amortized update time only for ${\mathrm{{IVM}}}_{\delta }^{ \pm  }$ in terms of the current database size $\left| \mathcal{D}\right|$ . We prove it in Section E.4,by proving a stronger lower bound where $\left| \mathcal{D}\right|$ is replaced by the number of single-tuple updates $N$ .

与定理4.4形成对比的是，定理4.5仅针对${\mathrm{{IVM}}}_{\delta }^{ \pm  }$问题，根据当前数据库规模$\left| \mathcal{D}\right|$给出了摊销更新时间的下界。我们通过证明更强的下界（其中$\left| \mathcal{D}\right|$替换为单次元组更新次数$N$）在E.4节完成证明。

### E.1 Upper Bound for ${\mathrm{{IVM}}}^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$

### E.1 ${\mathrm{{IVM}}}^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$的上界证明

Example 7.2 introduces the high-level idea of our algorithm for ${\mathrm{{IVM}}}^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$ . Algorithm 2 summarizes our algorithmic framework for ${\mathrm{{IVM}}}^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$ that meets Lemma 7.1. Algorithm 2 assumes that the length $N$ of the update stream is known in advance. However, the following remark explains how to remove this assumption.

示例7.2介绍了${\mathrm{{IVM}}}^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$算法的高层思想。算法2总结了满足引理7.1的${\mathrm{{IVM}}}^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$算法框架，该框架假设更新流长度$N$已知。但以下说明将解释如何消除此假设。

Remark E.1. If the number of updates $N$ is not known in advance,we can initialize $N$ to 1 and keep doubling $N$ every time we reach $N$ updates. We show in the proof of Lemma E. 3 that amortized analysis maintains the same overall runtime.

备注E.1。若更新次数$N$未知，可将$N$初始化为1，并在达到$N$次更新时进行倍增。引理E.3的证明将表明，摊销分析仍可保持总体时间复杂度不变。

For the rest of this section,we prove that Algorithm 2 meets Lemma 7.1. Fix a query $Q$ together with its time extension $\bar{Q}$ (Definition 6.2) and its multivariate extension $\widehat{Q}$ (Eq. (7)). We split the proof of Lemma 7.1 for IVM ${}^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$ into three parts. First,we reduce the maintenance of $Q$ to the maintenance of $\bar{Q}$ (Lemma E.1). Then,we reduce the latter problem to the maintenance of $\widehat{Q}$ (Lemma E.3). Finally,we show that $\widehat{Q}$ can be maintained with $\widetilde{O}\left( {N}^{\mathrm{w}\left( \widehat{Q}\right)  - 1}\right)$ amortized update time,where $\mathrm{w}\left( \widehat{Q}\right)$ is the fractional hypertree width of $\widehat{Q}$ (Lemma E.4). Lemma 7.1 for IVM ${}^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$ then follows from Lemmas E.1, E.3, and E.4.

本节剩余部分将证明算法2满足引理7.1。固定查询$Q$及其时间扩展$\bar{Q}$（定义6.2）与多元扩展$\widehat{Q}$（式7）。我们将IVM${}^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$的引理7.1证明分为三部分：首先将$Q$的维护归约到$\bar{Q}$的维护（引理E.1）；然后将后者归约到$\widehat{Q}$的维护（引理E.3）；最后证明$\widehat{Q}$能以$\widetilde{O}\left( {N}^{\mathrm{w}\left( \widehat{Q}\right)  - 1}\right)$的摊销更新时间维护，其中$\mathrm{w}\left( \widehat{Q}\right)$是$\widehat{Q}$的分数超树宽度（引理E.4）。由此通过引理E.1、E.3和E.4可得IVM${}^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$的引理7.1。

E.1.1 From the Maintenance of $Q$ to the Maintenance of $\bar{Q}$ . We reduce the maintenance of $Q$ to the maintenance of $\bar{Q}$ . Let $\mathcal{D}$ be the database instance for $Q$ and $\overline{\mathcal{D}}$ be the database instance for $\bar{Q}$ . As mentioned before,the idea is to use the interval variable $\left\lbrack  Z\right\rbrack$ in $\bar{Q}$ to describe the "lifespan" of the tuples in the database $\mathcal{D}$ for $Q$ . Given an atom $\bar{R}\left( {\left\lbrack  Z\right\rbrack  ,X}\right)$ in $\bar{Q}$ and a tuple $t$ over $\mathbf{X}$ ,we introduce the following types of updates to relation $\bar{R}$ :

E.1.1 从$Q$维护到$\bar{Q}$维护。我们将$Q$的维护归约到$\bar{Q}$的维护。设$\mathcal{D}$为$Q$的数据库实例，$\overline{\mathcal{D}}$为$\bar{Q}$的数据库实例。如前所述，核心思路是利用$\bar{Q}$中的区间变量$\left\lbrack  Z\right\rbrack$来描述$Q$数据库$\mathcal{D}$中元组的"生命周期"。给定$\bar{Q}$中的原子$\bar{R}\left( {\left\lbrack  Z\right\rbrack  ,X}\right)$和$\mathbf{X}$上的元组$t$，我们对关系$\bar{R}$引入以下更新类型：

- $+ \bar{R}\left( {\left\lbrack  {\tau ,\infty }\right\rbrack  ,t}\right)$ : Intuitively,this single-tuple insert indicates that the tuple $t$ lives in the database from time $\tau$ on.

- $+ \bar{R}\left( {\left\lbrack  {\tau ,\infty }\right\rbrack  ,t}\right)$：直观上，该单条插入操作表示元组$t$自时间$\tau$起存在于数据库中。

Algorithm 2 Algorithm of ${\mathrm{{IVM}}}^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  \mathrm{Q}\right\rbrack$ Inputs - $Q = {R}_{1}\left( {\mathbf{X}}_{1}\right)  \land  \cdots  \land  {R}_{k}\left( {\mathbf{X}}_{k}\right)$ $\vartriangleright$ Input query to ${\mathrm{{IVM}}}^{ \pm  }$ - An initially empty database $\mathcal{D}$ - Database instance for $Q$ - $N$ : The length of the update stream - See Remark E.1 Initialization - Construct the time extension $\bar{Q}$ - Definition 6.2 - Initialize $\overline{\mathcal{D}}$ to be empty - Database instance for $\bar{Q}$ - Construct the multivariate extension $\widehat{Q}$ $\vartriangleright$ Eq. (7) - Initialize $\widehat{\mathcal{D}}$ to be empty $\vartriangleright$ Database instance for $\widehat{Q}$ - For each component ${\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }$ of $\widehat{Q}$ - Initialize a tree decomposition using Theorem 4.1 Invariants - $\widehat{\mathcal{D}} = {\mathrm{{CP}}}_{N}\left( \overline{\mathcal{D}}\right)$ $\vartriangleright$ Definition D. 4 Insertion ${\delta }_{\tau }\mathcal{D} = \left\{  {+{R}_{j}\left( t\right) }\right\}$ $\vartriangleright$ The $\tau$ -th update inserts a tuple $t$ into ${R}_{j}$ - ${\delta }_{\tau }\overline{\mathcal{D}} \leftarrow  \left\{  {+{\bar{R}}_{j}\left( {\left\lbrack  {\tau ,\infty }\right\rbrack  ,\mathbf{t}}\right) }\right\}$ $\vartriangleright$ Insert $\left( {\left\lbrack  {\tau ,\infty }\right\rbrack  ,\mathbf{t}}\right)$ into ${\bar{R}}_{j}$ - $\overline{\mathcal{D}} \leftarrow  \overline{\mathcal{D}} \uplus  {\delta }_{\tau }\bar{D}$ $\vartriangleright$ Apply ${\delta }_{\tau }\overline{\mathcal{D}}$ to $\overline{\mathcal{D}}$ - ${\delta }_{\tau }\overset{⏜}{\mathcal{D}} \leftarrow  {\mathrm{{CP}}}_{N}\left( {{\delta }_{\tau }\overline{\mathcal{D}}}\right)$ $\vartriangleright$ Definition D. 4 - $\widehat{\mathcal{D}} \leftarrow  \widehat{\mathcal{D}} \uplus  {\delta }_{\tau }\widehat{\mathcal{D}}$ $\vartriangleright$ Use Theorem 4.1

算法2 ${\mathrm{{IVM}}}^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  \mathrm{Q}\right\rbrack$算法 输入项 - $Q = {R}_{1}\left( {\mathbf{X}}_{1}\right)  \land  \cdots  \land  {R}_{k}\left( {\mathbf{X}}_{k}\right)$ $\vartriangleright$ 输入查询至${\mathrm{{IVM}}}^{ \pm  }$ - 初始空数据库$\mathcal{D}$ - $Q$的数据库实例 - $N$：更新流长度 - 参见备注E.1 初始化 - 构建时间扩展$\bar{Q}$ - 定义6.2 - 初始化$\overline{\mathcal{D}}$为空集 - $\bar{Q}$的数据库实例 - 构建多元扩展$\widehat{Q}$ $\vartriangleright$ 公式(7) - 初始化$\widehat{\mathcal{D}}$为空集 $\vartriangleright$ $\widehat{Q}$的数据库实例 - 对$\widehat{Q}$的每个分量${\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }$ - 使用定理4.1初始化树分解 不变量 - $\widehat{\mathcal{D}} = {\mathrm{{CP}}}_{N}\left( \overline{\mathcal{D}}\right)$ $\vartriangleright$ 定义D.4 插入操作${\delta }_{\tau }\mathcal{D} = \left\{  {+{R}_{j}\left( t\right) }\right\}$ $\vartriangleright$ 第$\tau$次更新向${R}_{j}$插入元组$t$ - ${\delta }_{\tau }\overline{\mathcal{D}} \leftarrow  \left\{  {+{\bar{R}}_{j}\left( {\left\lbrack  {\tau ,\infty }\right\rbrack  ,\mathbf{t}}\right) }\right\}$ $\vartriangleright$ 将$\left( {\left\lbrack  {\tau ,\infty }\right\rbrack  ,\mathbf{t}}\right)$插入${\bar{R}}_{j}$ - $\overline{\mathcal{D}} \leftarrow  \overline{\mathcal{D}} \uplus  {\delta }_{\tau }\bar{D}$ $\vartriangleright$ 对$\overline{\mathcal{D}}$应用${\delta }_{\tau }\overline{\mathcal{D}}$ - ${\delta }_{\tau }\overset{⏜}{\mathcal{D}} \leftarrow  {\mathrm{{CP}}}_{N}\left( {{\delta }_{\tau }\overline{\mathcal{D}}}\right)$ $\vartriangleright$ 定义D.4 - $\widehat{\mathcal{D}} \leftarrow  \widehat{\mathcal{D}} \uplus  {\delta }_{\tau }\widehat{\mathcal{D}}$ $\vartriangleright$ 应用定理4.1

Deletion ${\delta }_{\tau }\mathcal{D} = \left\{  {-{R}_{j}\left( \mathbf{t}\right) }\right\}$ $\vartriangleright$ The $\tau$ -th update deletes a tuple $\mathbf{t}$ from ${R}_{j}$

删除操作${\delta }_{\tau }\mathcal{D} = \left\{  {-{R}_{j}\left( \mathbf{t}\right) }\right\}$ $\vartriangleright$ 第$\tau$次更新从${R}_{j}$删除元组$\mathbf{t}$

- Find $\left( {\left\lbrack  {{\tau }^{\prime },\infty }\right\rbrack  ,\mathbf{t}}\right)  \in  {\bar{R}}_{j}$

- 查找$\left( {\left\lbrack  {{\tau }^{\prime },\infty }\right\rbrack  ,\mathbf{t}}\right)  \in  {\bar{R}}_{j}$

- ${\delta }_{\tau }\overline{\mathcal{D}} \leftarrow  \left\{  {-{\bar{R}}_{j}\left( {\left\lbrack  {{\tau }^{\prime },\infty }\right\rbrack  ,t}\right) , + {\bar{R}}_{j}\left( {\left\lbrack  {{\tau }^{\prime },\tau }\right\rbrack  ,t}\right) }\right\}$ $\vartriangleright$ Replace $\left( {\left\lbrack  {{\tau }^{\prime },\infty }\right\rbrack  ,\mathbf{t}}\right)$ with $\left( {\left\lbrack  {{\tau }^{\prime },\tau }\right\rbrack  ,\mathbf{t}}\right)$ in ${\bar{R}}_{j}$

- ${\delta }_{\tau }\overline{\mathcal{D}} \leftarrow  \left\{  {-{\bar{R}}_{j}\left( {\left\lbrack  {{\tau }^{\prime },\infty }\right\rbrack  ,t}\right) , + {\bar{R}}_{j}\left( {\left\lbrack  {{\tau }^{\prime },\tau }\right\rbrack  ,t}\right) }\right\}$ $\vartriangleright$ 将$\left( {\left\lbrack  {{\tau }^{\prime },\infty }\right\rbrack  ,\mathbf{t}}\right)$替换为$\left( {\left\lbrack  {{\tau }^{\prime },\tau }\right\rbrack  ,\mathbf{t}}\right)$在${\bar{R}}_{j}$中

- $\overline{\mathcal{D}} \leftarrow  \overline{\mathcal{D}} \uplus  {\delta }_{\tau }\bar{D}$

- ${\delta }_{\tau }\overset{⏜}{\mathcal{D}} \leftarrow  {\mathrm{{CP}}}_{N}\left( {{\delta }_{\tau }\overline{\mathcal{D}}}\right)$

- $\widehat{\mathcal{D}} \leftarrow  \widehat{\mathcal{D}} \uplus  {\delta }_{\tau }\widehat{\mathcal{D}}$ $\vartriangleright$ Proposition E. 2 Enumeration

- $\widehat{\mathcal{D}} \leftarrow  \widehat{\mathcal{D}} \uplus  {\delta }_{\tau }\widehat{\mathcal{D}}$ $\vartriangleright$ 命题E.2 枚举

- Enumerate (using Theorem 4.1)

- 枚举（使用定理4.1）

$$
\begin{array}{l} {\pi }_{\text{vars }}\left( Q\right) \;\mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{\substack{{{z}_{1},\ldots ,{z}_{k + 1} \in  \{ 0,1{\} }^{ * }} \\  {{z}_{1} \circ  \cdots  \circ  {z}_{k + 1} = \operatorname{CP}\left( \left\lbrack  {\tau ,\tau }\right\rbrack  \right) } }}{\sigma }_{{Z}_{1} \circ  \cdots  \circ  {z}_{k + 1} = \operatorname{CP}\left( \left\lbrack  {\tau ,\tau }\right\rbrack  \right) } \\  \end{array}
$$

<!-- Media -->

- $\left( {-\bar{R}\left( {\left\lbrack  {{\tau }^{\prime },\infty }\right\rbrack  ,t}\right) , + \bar{R}\left( {\left\lbrack  {{\tau }^{\prime },\tau }\right\rbrack  ,t}\right) }\right)$ for some ${\tau }^{\prime } < \tau$ . This compound update is called truncation and replaces $\left( {\left\lbrack  {{\tau }^{\prime },\infty }\right\rbrack  ,t}\right)$ in $\bar{R}$ with $\left( {\left\lbrack  {{\tau }^{\prime },\tau }\right\rbrack  ,t}\right)$ . Intuitively,it signals that the lifespan of tuple $t$ ends at time $\tau$ .

- $\left( {-\bar{R}\left( {\left\lbrack  {{\tau }^{\prime },\infty }\right\rbrack  ,t}\right) , + \bar{R}\left( {\left\lbrack  {{\tau }^{\prime },\tau }\right\rbrack  ,t}\right) }\right)$ 对于某些${\tau }^{\prime } < \tau$。此复合更新称为截断操作，将$\bar{R}$中的$\left( {\left\lbrack  {{\tau }^{\prime },\infty }\right\rbrack  ,t}\right)$替换为$\left( {\left\lbrack  {{\tau }^{\prime },\tau }\right\rbrack  ,t}\right)$。直观而言，它表示元组$t$的生命周期在时间$\tau$终止。

We call an update stream ${\delta }_{1}\overline{\mathcal{D}},{\delta }_{2}\overline{\mathcal{D}},\ldots$ for $\overline{\mathcal{D}}$ restricted if each update ${\delta }_{\tau }\overline{\mathcal{D}}$ is an insert of the form $+ \bar{R}\left( {\left\lbrack  {\tau ,\infty }\right\rbrack  ,t}\right)$ or a truncation of the form $\left( {-\bar{R}\left( {\left\lbrack  {{\tau }^{\prime },\infty }\right\rbrack  ,t}\right) , + \bar{R}\left( {\left\lbrack  {{\tau }^{\prime },\tau }\right\rbrack  ,t}\right) }\right)$ . The restricted result of $\bar{Q}$ after update ${\delta }_{\tau }\overline{\mathcal{D}}$ is defined as all output tuples of $\bar{Q}$ whose $\left\lbrack  Z\right\rbrack$ -interval contains $\tau$ :

若每个更新${\delta }_{\tau }\overline{\mathcal{D}}$都是形式为$+ \bar{R}\left( {\left\lbrack  {\tau ,\infty }\right\rbrack  ,t}\right)$的插入或形式为$\left( {-\bar{R}\left( {\left\lbrack  {{\tau }^{\prime },\infty }\right\rbrack  ,t}\right) , + \bar{R}\left( {\left\lbrack  {{\tau }^{\prime },\tau }\right\rbrack  ,t}\right) }\right)$的截断，则称更新流${\delta }_{1}\overline{\mathcal{D}},{\delta }_{2}\overline{\mathcal{D}},\ldots$对于$\overline{\mathcal{D}}$是受限的。更新${\delta }_{\tau }\overline{\mathcal{D}}$后$\bar{Q}$的受限结果定义为：所有输出元组的$\left\lbrack  Z\right\rbrack$区间包含$\tau$的$\bar{Q}$元组：

$$
{\bar{Q}}_{\tau }\left( {\overline{\mathcal{D}}}^{\left( \tau \right) }\right) \overset{\text{ def }}{ = }{\sigma }_{\tau  \in  \left\lbrack  Z\right\rbrack  }\bar{Q}\left( {\overline{\mathcal{D}}}^{\left( \tau \right) }\right) , \tag{48}
$$

Note that by construction, we have

请注意，根据构造原理，我们得出

$$
Q\left( {\mathcal{D}}^{\left( \tau \right) }\right)  = {\pi }_{\operatorname{vars}\left( Q\right) }{\bar{Q}}_{\tau }\left( {\overline{\mathcal{D}}}^{\left( \tau \right) }\right)  \tag{49}
$$
