We introduce the maintenance problem for the intersection query $\bar{Q}$ :

现针对交集查询$\bar{Q}$提出维护问题：

---

	Problem: $\;\overline{\operatorname{IVM}}\left\lbrack  \bar{Q}\right\rbrack$

	问题：$\;\overline{\operatorname{IVM}}\left\lbrack  \bar{Q}\right\rbrack$

	Parameter: A time extension query $\bar{Q}$

	参数：时间扩展查询$\bar{Q}$

Given: Restricted update stream ${\delta }_{1}\overline{\mathcal{D}},{\delta }_{2}\overline{\mathcal{D}},\ldots$

给定：受限更新流${\delta }_{1}\overline{\mathcal{D}},{\delta }_{2}\overline{\mathcal{D}},\ldots$

Task: Ensure constant-delay enumeration of ${\bar{Q}}_{\tau }\left( {\overline{\mathcal{D}}}^{\left( \tau \right) }\right)$ after each update ${\delta }_{\tau }\overline{\mathcal{D}}$

任务：确保每次更新${\delta }_{\tau }\overline{\mathcal{D}}$后能持续延迟枚举${\bar{Q}}_{\tau }\left( {\overline{\mathcal{D}}}^{\left( \tau \right) }\right)$

---

<!-- Media -->

We make the reduction from ${\mathrm{{IVM}}}^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$ to $\overline{\mathrm{{IVM}}}\left\lbrack  \bar{Q}\right\rbrack$ precise:

我们将从${\mathrm{{IVM}}}^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$到$\overline{\mathrm{{IVM}}}\left\lbrack  \bar{Q}\right\rbrack$的归约关系精确表述如下：

LEMMA E.1. If $\overline{IVM}\left\lbrack  \bar{Q}\right\rbrack$ can be solved with (amortized) $f\left( N\right)$ update time for any update stream of length $N$ and some function $f$ ,then ${IV}{M}^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$ can be solved with (amortized) $O\left( {f\left( N\right) }\right)$ update time.

引理 E.1。若对于任意长度为$N$的更新流及某个函数$f$，$\overline{IVM}\left\lbrack  \bar{Q}\right\rbrack$能以（均摊）$f\left( N\right)$更新时间求解，则${IV}{M}^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$也能以（均摊）$O\left( {f\left( N\right) }\right)$更新时间求解。

Proof. The reduction from IVM ${}^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$ to $\overline{\mathrm{{IVM}}}\left\lbrack  \bar{Q}\right\rbrack$ works as follows. Consider an update stream ${\delta }_{1}\mathcal{D},{\delta }_{2}\mathcal{D},\ldots$ for $Q$ . For each $\tau  \geq  1$ ,we pursue the following strategy. If ${\delta }_{\tau }\mathcal{D}$ is an insert $+ R\left( t\right)$ ,we execute the insert $+ \bar{R}\left( {\left\lbrack  {\tau ,\infty }\right\rbrack  ,t}\right)$ on the database $\overline{\mathcal{D}}$ of $\bar{Q}$ . If it is a delete $- R\left( t\right)$ ,we apply the truncation $\left( {-\bar{R}\left( {\left\lbrack  {{\tau }^{\prime },\infty }\right\rbrack  ,t}\right) , + \bar{R}\left( {\left\lbrack  {{\tau }^{\prime },\tau }\right\rbrack  ,t}\right) }\right)$ . For any enumeration request to $Q$ that comes immediately after some update ${\delta }_{\tau }\mathcal{D}$ ,we trigger the enumeration procedure for $\bar{Q}$ ,which enumerates the tuples in ${\bar{Q}}_{\tau }\left( {\overline{\mathcal{D}}}^{\left( \tau \right) }\right)$ with constant delay. (Recall (49).) By construction,the tuples in ${\bar{Q}}_{\tau }\left( {\overline{\mathcal{D}}}^{\left( \tau \right) }\right)$ must be distinct with respect to their projections onto their point variables (i.e. vars(Q)). In particular,

证明。从IVM${}^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$到$\overline{\mathrm{{IVM}}}\left\lbrack  \bar{Q}\right\rbrack$的归约过程如下：考虑$Q$的更新流${\delta }_{1}\mathcal{D},{\delta }_{2}\mathcal{D},\ldots$。对于每个$\tau  \geq  1$，我们采取如下策略：若${\delta }_{\tau }\mathcal{D}$是插入操作$+ R\left( t\right)$，则在$\bar{Q}$的数据库$\overline{\mathcal{D}}$上执行插入$+ \bar{R}\left( {\left\lbrack  {\tau ,\infty }\right\rbrack  ,t}\right)$；若是删除操作$- R\left( t\right)$，则应用截断$\left( {-\bar{R}\left( {\left\lbrack  {{\tau }^{\prime },\infty }\right\rbrack  ,t}\right) , + \bar{R}\left( {\left\lbrack  {{\tau }^{\prime },\tau }\right\rbrack  ,t}\right) }\right)$。对于紧接在某个更新${\delta }_{\tau }\mathcal{D}$后到达的$Q$枚举请求，我们触发$\bar{Q}$的枚举过程——该过程以恒定延迟枚举${\bar{Q}}_{\tau }\left( {\overline{\mathcal{D}}}^{\left( \tau \right) }\right)$中的元组（参见式(49)）。根据构造，${\bar{Q}}_{\tau }\left( {\overline{\mathcal{D}}}^{\left( \tau \right) }\right)$中的元组在其点变量（即vars(Q)）上的投影必须互不相同。特别地，

$$
\left| {Q\left( {\mathcal{D}}^{\left( \tau \right) }\right) }\right|  = \left| {{\bar{Q}}_{\tau }\left( {\overline{\mathcal{D}}}^{\left( \tau \right) }\right) }\right|  \tag{50}
$$

Hence,by reporting the projections of the tuples in ${\bar{Q}}_{\tau }\left( {\overline{\mathcal{D}}}^{\left( \tau \right) }\right)$ onto $\operatorname{vars}\left( Q\right)$ ,we enumerate the output $Q\left( {\mathcal{D}}^{\left( \tau \right) }\right)$ with constant delay.

因此，通过报告${\bar{Q}}_{\tau }\left( {\overline{\mathcal{D}}}^{\left( \tau \right) }\right)$中元组在$\operatorname{vars}\left( Q\right)$上的投影，我们以恒定延迟枚举输出$Q\left( {\mathcal{D}}^{\left( \tau \right) }\right)$。

The following proposition will be useful later for the proof of Lemma 7.1. It basically says that when we truncate a tuple from $\bar{R}\left( {\left\lbrack  {{\tau }^{\prime },\infty }\right\rbrack  ,t}\right)$ to $\bar{R}\left( {\left\lbrack  {{\tau }^{\prime },\tau }\right\rbrack  ,t}\right)$ ,the truncated tuple is still going to join with all other tuples that it used to join with before the truncation.

下述命题将在后续引理7.1的证明中发挥作用。其核心含义是：当我们将元组从$\bar{R}\left( {\left\lbrack  {{\tau }^{\prime },\infty }\right\rbrack  ,t}\right)$截断至$\bar{R}\left( {\left\lbrack  {{\tau }^{\prime },\tau }\right\rbrack  ,t}\right)$时，被截断的元组仍能与截断前所有与之连接的元组保持连接关系。

Proposition E.2. Consider a truncation update ${\delta }_{\tau }\overline{\mathcal{D}} = \left\{  {-{\bar{R}}_{j}\left( {\left\lbrack  {{\tau }^{\prime },\infty }\right\rbrack  ,{t}_{j}}\right) , + {\bar{R}}_{j}\left( {\left\lbrack  {{\tau }^{\prime },\tau }\right\rbrack  ,{t}_{j}}\right) }\right\}$ . Suppose that the tuple before truncation ${\bar{R}}_{j}\left( {\left\lbrack  {{\tau }^{\prime },\infty }\right\rbrack  ,{t}_{j}}\right)$ used to join with a set of tuples in $\left\{  {{\bar{R}}_{i}\left( {\left\lbrack  {{\alpha }_{i},{\beta }_{i}}\right\rbrack  ,{t}_{i}}\right)  \mid  i \in  S}\right\}$ for some $S \subseteq  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack$ ,i.e.

命题E.2。考虑一个截断更新${\delta }_{\tau }\overline{\mathcal{D}} = \left\{  {-{\bar{R}}_{j}\left( {\left\lbrack  {{\tau }^{\prime },\infty }\right\rbrack  ,{t}_{j}}\right) , + {\bar{R}}_{j}\left( {\left\lbrack  {{\tau }^{\prime },\tau }\right\rbrack  ,{t}_{j}}\right) }\right\}$。假设截断前的元组${\bar{R}}_{j}\left( {\left\lbrack  {{\tau }^{\prime },\infty }\right\rbrack  ,{t}_{j}}\right)$曾与$\left\{  {{\bar{R}}_{i}\left( {\left\lbrack  {{\alpha }_{i},{\beta }_{i}}\right\rbrack  ,{t}_{i}}\right)  \mid  i \in  S}\right\}$中的一组元组在某个$S \subseteq  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack$条件下连接，即

$$
{ \bowtie  }_{i \in  S}{\bar{R}}_{i}\left( {\left\lbrack  {{\alpha }_{i},{\beta }_{i}}\right\rbrack  ,{t}_{i}}\right)  \bowtie  {\bar{R}}_{j}\left( {\left\lbrack  {{\tau }^{\prime },\infty }\right\rbrack  ,{t}_{j}}\right)  = \left( {\left\lbrack  {\alpha ,\beta }\right\rbrack  ,t}\right) 
$$

Then, the following hold:

则下列结论成立：

(1) ${\beta }_{i} = \infty$ for all $i \in  S$ and $\beta  = \infty$

(1) ${\beta }_{i} = \infty$ 对所有$i \in  S$和$\beta  = \infty$成立

(2) The truncated tuple ${\bar{R}}_{j}\left( {\left\lbrack  {{\tau }^{\prime },\tau }\right\rbrack  ,{t}_{j}}\right)$ still joins with the same set of tuples. Namely

(2) 截断后的元组${\bar{R}}_{j}\left( {\left\lbrack  {{\tau }^{\prime },\tau }\right\rbrack  ,{t}_{j}}\right)$仍与同一组元组连接。即

$$
{ \bowtie  }_{i \in  S}{\bar{R}}_{i}\left( {\left\lbrack  {{\alpha }_{i},{\beta }_{i}}\right\rbrack  ,{t}_{i}}\right)  \bowtie  {\bar{R}}_{j}\left( {\left\lbrack  {{\tau }^{\prime },\tau }\right\rbrack  ,{t}_{j}}\right)  = \left( {\left\lbrack  {\alpha ,\tau }\right\rbrack  ,t}\right) 
$$

Proof. Right before time $\tau$ when we are about to truncate,the database ${\overline{\mathcal{D}}}^{\left( \tau  - 1\right) }$ does not yet contain any interval $\left\lbrack  {{\alpha }_{i},{\beta }_{i}}\right\rbrack$ where ${\alpha }_{i} \geq  \tau$ . This is because $\tau$ is the current time,and those intervals $\left\lbrack  {{\alpha }_{i},{\beta }_{i}}\right\rbrack$ where ${\alpha }_{i} \geq  \tau$ start in the future. This proves part 2. Moreover,every interval $\left\lbrack  {{\alpha }_{i},{\beta }_{i}}\right\rbrack$ in the database ${\overline{\mathcal{D}}}^{\left( \tau  - 1\right) }$ where ${\alpha }_{i} < \tau  \leq  {\beta }_{i}$ must satisfy ${\beta }_{i} = \infty$ . This is because such an interval ends in the future,thus we could not have encountered its end yet. This proves part 1.

证明。在即将截断的时刻$\tau$之前，数据库${\overline{\mathcal{D}}}^{\left( \tau  - 1\right) }$中尚未包含任何满足${\alpha }_{i} \geq  \tau$的区间$\left\lbrack  {{\alpha }_{i},{\beta }_{i}}\right\rbrack$。这是因为$\tau$是当前时间，而那些满足${\alpha }_{i} \geq  \tau$的区间$\left\lbrack  {{\alpha }_{i},{\beta }_{i}}\right\rbrack$始于未来。这证明了第二部分。此外，数据库${\overline{\mathcal{D}}}^{\left( \tau  - 1\right) }$中每个满足${\alpha }_{i} < \tau  \leq  {\beta }_{i}$的区间$\left\lbrack  {{\alpha }_{i},{\beta }_{i}}\right\rbrack$必然满足${\beta }_{i} = \infty$。因为此类区间终结于未来，故我们尚未遇到其终点。这证明了第一部分。

E. 1.2 From the Maintenance of $\bar{Q}$ to the Maintenance of $\widehat{Q}$ . Now,we give a reduction from the maintenance of $\bar{Q}$ to the maintenance of $\widehat{Q}$ . Let $\widehat{\mathcal{D}}$ be the database instance for $\widehat{Q}$ . In order to translate an update ${\delta }_{\tau }\overline{\mathcal{D}}$ for $\bar{Q}$ into an update ${\delta }_{\tau }\widehat{\mathcal{D}}$ for $\widehat{Q}$ ,we take the canonical partition of ${\delta }_{\tau }\overline{\mathcal{D}}$ ,as given by Definition D.4. In particular,suppose that $N$ ,the length of the update stream, is a power of 2 and is known in advance. (See Remark E. 1 about removing this assumption.) The corresponding update to $\widehat{Q}$ is

E.1.2 从$\bar{Q}$的维护到$\widehat{Q}$的维护。现在，我们将$\bar{Q}$的维护问题归约到$\widehat{Q}$的维护。设$\widehat{\mathcal{D}}$为$\widehat{Q}$的数据库实例。为了将$\bar{Q}$的更新${\delta }_{\tau }\overline{\mathcal{D}}$转换为$\widehat{Q}$的更新${\delta }_{\tau }\widehat{\mathcal{D}}$，我们采用定义D.4给出的${\delta }_{\tau }\overline{\mathcal{D}}$规范划分。特别地，假设更新流长度$N$是2的幂且预先已知。（关于如何移除该假设，参见备注E.1。）对$\widehat{Q}$的相应更新为

$$
{\delta }_{\tau }\overset{⏜}{\mathcal{D}} \leftarrow  {\mathrm{{CP}}}_{N}\left( {{\delta }_{\tau }\overline{\mathcal{D}}}\right)  \tag{51}
$$

We say that a stream ${\delta }_{1}\widehat{\mathcal{D}},{\delta }_{2}\widehat{\mathcal{D}},\ldots$ is a restricted canonical update stream for $\widehat{Q}$ if there is a restricted update stream ${\delta }_{1}\overline{\mathcal{D}},{\delta }_{2}\overline{\mathcal{D}},\ldots$ for $\bar{Q}$ such that each ${\delta }_{\tau }\widehat{\mathcal{D}}$ is the canonical partition of ${\delta }_{\tau }\overline{\mathcal{D}}$ with respect to $N$ . The restricted result of $\widehat{Q}$ after update ${\delta }_{\tau }\widehat{\mathcal{D}}$ is defined as the set of all output tuples of $\widehat{Q}$ where the bitstring ${Z}_{1} \circ  \cdots  \circ  {Z}_{k}$ (when interpreted as an interval in the segment tree ${\mathcal{T}}_{N}$ ) contains $\tau$ . Formally,

若存在受限更新流${\delta }_{1}\overline{\mathcal{D}},{\delta }_{2}\overline{\mathcal{D}},\ldots$使得每个${\delta }_{\tau }\widehat{\mathcal{D}}$都是${\delta }_{\tau }\overline{\mathcal{D}}$关于$N$的规范划分，则称流${\delta }_{1}\widehat{\mathcal{D}},{\delta }_{2}\widehat{\mathcal{D}},\ldots$是$\widehat{Q}$的受限规范更新流。更新${\delta }_{\tau }\widehat{\mathcal{D}}$后$\widehat{Q}$的受限结果定义为：当位串${Z}_{1} \circ  \cdots  \circ  {Z}_{k}$（解释为线段树${\mathcal{T}}_{N}$中的区间）包含$\tau$时，$\widehat{Q}$所有输出元组的集合。形式化表示为：

$$
{\widehat{Q}}_{\tau }\left( {\widehat{\mathcal{D}}}^{\left( \tau \right) }\right) \overset{\text{ def }}{ = }\mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{\substack{{{z}_{1},\ldots ,{z}_{k + 1} \in  \{ 0,1{\} }^{ * }} \\  {{z}_{1} \circ  \cdots  \circ  {z}_{k + 1} = \operatorname{CP}\left( \left\lbrack  {\tau ,\tau }\right\rbrack  \right) } }}{\sigma }_{{Z}_{1} = {z}_{1},\ldots ,{Z}_{k} = {z}_{k}}\widehat{Q}\left( {\widehat{\mathcal{D}}}^{\left( \tau \right) }\right)  \tag{52}
$$

<!-- Media -->

We are ready to introduce the maintenance problem for $\widehat{Q}$ :

现在可以正式提出$\widehat{Q}$的维护问题：

---

		Problem: $\widehat{\mathrm{{IVM}}}\left\lbrack  \widehat{Q}\right\rbrack$

		问题：$\widehat{\mathrm{{IVM}}}\left\lbrack  \widehat{Q}\right\rbrack$

	Parameter: A multivariate extension query $\widehat{Q}$

		参数：多元扩展查询$\widehat{Q}$

Given: Restricted canonical update stream ${\delta }_{1}\widehat{\mathcal{D}},{\delta }_{2}\widehat{\mathcal{D}},\ldots$

给定：受限规范更新流${\delta }_{1}\widehat{\mathcal{D}},{\delta }_{2}\widehat{\mathcal{D}},\ldots$

Task: Ensure constant-delay enumeration of ${\widehat{Q}}_{\tau }\left( {\widehat{\mathcal{D}}}^{\left( \tau \right) }\right)$ after each update ${\delta }_{\tau }\widehat{\mathcal{D}}$

任务：确保每次更新${\delta }_{\tau }\widehat{\mathcal{D}}$后${\widehat{Q}}_{\tau }\left( {\widehat{\mathcal{D}}}^{\left( \tau \right) }\right)$的常数延迟枚举

---

<!-- Media -->

We formalize the reduction from $\overline{\operatorname{IVM}}\left\lbrack  \bar{Q}\right\rbrack$ to $\widehat{\operatorname{IVM}}\left\lbrack  \widehat{Q}\right\rbrack$ :

我们将$\overline{\operatorname{IVM}}\left\lbrack  \bar{Q}\right\rbrack$到$\widehat{\operatorname{IVM}}\left\lbrack  \widehat{Q}\right\rbrack$的归约形式化如下：

LEMMA E.3. If $\overset{⏜}{IVM}\left\lbrack  \widehat{Q}\right\rbrack$ can be solved with (amortized) $f\left( N\right)$ update time for any update stream of length $N$ and some monotonic function $f$ ,then $\overline{IVM}\left\lbrack  \bar{Q}\right\rbrack$ can be solved with $\widetilde{O}\left( {f\left( N\right) }\right)$ amortized update time.

引理E.3. 若对于长度为$N$的任意更新流和某个单调函数$f$，$\overset{⏜}{IVM}\left\lbrack  \widehat{Q}\right\rbrack$能以（分摊）$f\left( N\right)$更新时间求解，则$\overline{IVM}\left\lbrack  \bar{Q}\right\rbrack$能以$\widetilde{O}\left( {f\left( N\right) }\right)$分摊更新时间求解。

Proof. The reduction expressed in Lemma E. 3 works as follows. Consider an update stream ${\delta }_{1}\overline{\mathcal{D}},{\delta }_{2}\overline{\mathcal{D}}\ldots$ for $\bar{Q}$ . We transform each update ${\delta }_{\tau }\overline{\mathcal{D}}$ into its canonical partition ${\delta }_{\tau }\widehat{\mathcal{D}}$ with respect to $N$ ,and apply ${\delta }_{\tau }\widehat{\mathcal{D}}$ on the database $\widehat{\mathcal{D}}$ for $\widehat{Q}$ . As a result,the algorithm maintains the invariant that the database $\widehat{\mathcal{D}}$ is always the canonical partition of the database $\overline{\mathcal{D}}$ with respect to $N$ . Formally,

证明. 引理E.3所述的归约流程如下：对于$\bar{Q}$的更新流${\delta }_{1}\overline{\mathcal{D}},{\delta }_{2}\overline{\mathcal{D}}\ldots$，将每个更新${\delta }_{\tau }\overline{\mathcal{D}}$转换为关于$N$的规范划分${\delta }_{\tau }\widehat{\mathcal{D}}$，并对$\widehat{Q}$的数据库$\widehat{\mathcal{D}}$应用${\delta }_{\tau }\widehat{\mathcal{D}}$。该算法始终保持不变式：数据库$\widehat{\mathcal{D}}$始终是数据库$\overline{\mathcal{D}}$关于$N$的规范划分。形式化表示为：

$$
{\widehat{\mathcal{D}}}^{\left( \tau \right) } = {\mathrm{{CP}}}_{N}\left( {\overline{\mathcal{D}}}^{\left( \tau \right) }\right) ,\;\text{ for all }\tau  \in  \left\lbrack  N\right\rbrack  .
$$

Moreover,by Definition D.3,the number of single-tuple updates in ${\delta }_{\tau }\widehat{\mathcal{D}}$ is at most polylogarithmic in $N$ (which is $\widetilde{O}\left( 1\right)$ ).

此外根据定义D.3，${\delta }_{\tau }\widehat{\mathcal{D}}$中单元组更新的数量至多为$N$（即$\widetilde{O}\left( 1\right)$）的多对数级。

Theorem D. 5 connects the full outputs of $\bar{Q}\left( {\overline{\mathcal{D}}}^{\left( \tau \right) }\right)$ and $\widehat{Q}\left( {\widehat{\mathcal{D}}}^{\left( \tau \right) }\right)$ using the following relation:

定理D.5通过以下关系连接$\bar{Q}\left( {\overline{\mathcal{D}}}^{\left( \tau \right) }\right)$与$\widehat{Q}\left( {\widehat{\mathcal{D}}}^{\left( \tau \right) }\right)$的完整输出：

$$
{\mathrm{{CP}}}_{N}^{\left( 1\right) }\left( {\bar{Q}\left( {\overline{\mathcal{D}}}^{\left( \tau \right) }\right) }\right)  = {G}_{k}\left( {\widehat{Q}\left( {\widehat{\mathcal{D}}}^{\left( \tau \right) }\right) }\right)  \tag{53}
$$

From the above equation along with (52) and (48), we conclude that:

结合上述方程及(52)和(48)，我们得出结论：

$$
{\pi }_{\operatorname{vars}\left( Q\right) }{\widehat{Q}}_{\tau }\left( {\widehat{\mathcal{D}}}^{\left( \tau \right) }\right)  = {\pi }_{\operatorname{vars}\left( Q\right) }{\bar{Q}}_{\tau }\left( {\bar{\mathcal{D}}}^{\left( \tau \right) }\right)  \tag{54}
$$

$$
\left| {{\widehat{Q}}_{\tau }\left( {\widehat{\mathcal{D}}}^{\left( \tau \right) }\right) }\right|  = \left| {{\bar{Q}}_{\tau }\left( {\overline{\mathcal{D}}}^{\left( \tau \right) }\right) }\right|  \tag{55}
$$

Equation (55) follows from the fact that for any segment $\left\lbrack  Z\right\rbrack$ where $\tau  \in  \left\lbrack  Z\right\rbrack$ ,there is exactly one segment in the canonical partition of $\left\lbrack  Z\right\rbrack$ that contains $\tau$ . Hence,we can use constant-delay enumeration for ${\widehat{Q}}_{\tau }\left( {\widehat{\mathcal{D}}}^{\left( \tau \right) }\right)$ in order to enumerate the output of ${\bar{Q}}_{\tau }\left( {\overline{\mathcal{D}}}^{\left( \tau \right) }\right)$ .

方程(55)的成立基于以下事实：对于任意段$\left\lbrack  Z\right\rbrack$（其中$\tau  \in  \left\lbrack  Z\right\rbrack$），在$\left\lbrack  Z\right\rbrack$的标准划分中恰好存在一个包含$\tau$的段。因此，我们可以对${\widehat{Q}}_{\tau }\left( {\widehat{\mathcal{D}}}^{\left( \tau \right) }\right)$采用恒定延迟枚举来实现${\bar{Q}}_{\tau }\left( {\overline{\mathcal{D}}}^{\left( \tau \right) }\right)$的输出枚举。

Finally,if the length of the update stream $N$ is not known in advance,then we use the doubling trick described in Remark E.1. In particular,we initialize $N$ to 1 . Before we process $\tau$ -th update for $\tau  = N + 1$ ,we replace the segment tree ${\mathcal{T}}_{N}$ by ${\mathcal{T}}_{2N}$ and replace $N$ by ${2N}$ . Replacing the segment tree corresponds to creating a copy of the old tree and adding a new root node whose two children are the old segment tree and the copy. After updating the segment tree, we can redo the previous inserts into the new tree. The total update time would be

最后，若更新流$N$的长度未知，则采用备注E.1所述的倍增策略。具体而言，我们将$N$初始化为1。在处理$\tau  = N + 1$的第$\tau$次更新前，将线段树${\mathcal{T}}_{N}$替换为${\mathcal{T}}_{2N}$，并将$N$更新为${2N}$。替换线段树的操作包括复制旧树结构，并添加以旧树及其副本为子节点的新根节点。更新线段树后，可重新执行先前插入操作。总更新时间为

$$
\widetilde{O}\left( 1\right) \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  \left\lceil  {{\log }_{2}N}\right\rceil  \right\rbrack  }}{2}^{i} \cdot  f\left( {2}^{i}\right)  = \widetilde{O}\left( {N \cdot  f\left( N\right) }\right) 
$$

(Recall that $f$ is monotonic.) Hence,the amortized update time is still $\widetilde{O}\left( {f\left( N\right) }\right)$ .

（注意$f$具有单调性）。因此，摊还更新时间仍为$\widetilde{O}\left( {f\left( N\right) }\right)$。

E. 1.3 Update Time for $\widehat{Q}$ . Note that (49),(50),(54),and (55) imply:

E.1.3 $\widehat{Q}$的更新时间。注意(49)、(50)、(54)及(55)式表明：

$$
Q\left( {\mathcal{D}}^{\left( \tau \right) }\right)  = {\pi }_{\operatorname{vars}\left( Q\right) }{\widehat{Q}}_{\tau }\left( {\widehat{\mathcal{D}}}^{\left( \tau \right) }\right) 
$$

$$
\left| {Q\left( {\mathcal{D}}^{\left( \tau \right) }\right) }\right|  = \left| {{\widehat{Q}}_{\tau }\left( {\widehat{\mathcal{D}}}^{\left( \tau \right) }\right) }\right| 
$$

This basically means that we can do constant-delay enumeration for $Q\left( {\mathcal{D}}^{\left( \tau \right) }\right)$ using constant-delay enumeration for ${\widehat{Q}}_{\tau }\left( {\widehat{\mathcal{D}}}^{\left( \tau \right) }\right)$ . Now,we prove the following statement:

这意味着我们可以通过对${\widehat{Q}}_{\tau }\left( {\widehat{\mathcal{D}}}^{\left( \tau \right) }\right)$实施恒定延迟枚举，实现对$Q\left( {\mathcal{D}}^{\left( \tau \right) }\right)$的恒定延迟枚举。现证明以下命题：

LemMA E.4. For any restricted canonical update stream of length $N$ ,the problem $\mathop{\operatorname{IVM}}\limits^{⏜}\left\lbrack  \widehat{Q}\right\rbrack$ can be solved with $\widetilde{O}\left( {N}^{\mathrm{w}\left( \widehat{Q}\right)  - 1}\right)$ amortized update time,where $\mathrm{w}\left( \widehat{Q}\right)$ is the fractional hypertree width of $\widehat{Q}$ .

引理E.4。对于任何长度为$N$的限制标准更新流，问题$\mathop{\operatorname{IVM}}\limits^{⏜}\left\lbrack  \widehat{Q}\right\rbrack$可在$\widetilde{O}\left( {N}^{\mathrm{w}\left( \widehat{Q}\right)  - 1}\right)$的摊余更新时间求解，其中$\mathrm{w}\left( \widehat{Q}\right)$表示$\widehat{Q}$的分数超树宽度。

Before we prove the above lemma, we need some preliminaries:

在证明该引理前，需作如下准备：

Definition E.5. A subset $Y \subseteq  \operatorname{vars}\left( \widehat{Q}\right)$ is called $Z$ -prefix-closed if it satisfies the following property: For every ${Z}_{i} \in  Y$ , we must have $\left\{  {{Z}_{1},\ldots ,{Z}_{i - 1}}\right\}   \subseteq  Y$ . An atom $R\left( Y\right)$ is $Z$ -prefix-closed if its schema $Y$ is $Z$ -prefix-closed.

定义E.5。子集$Y \subseteq  \operatorname{vars}\left( \widehat{Q}\right)$若满足以下性质则称为$Z$-前缀封闭：对于每个${Z}_{i} \in  Y$，必有$\left\{  {{Z}_{1},\ldots ,{Z}_{i - 1}}\right\}   \subseteq  Y$。当原子$R\left( Y\right)$的模式$Y$满足$Z$-前缀封闭时，该原子称为$Z$-前缀封闭。

Recall from Appendix B that a tree decomposition $\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)$ of a query $Q$ is called optimal if it minimizes the fractional hypertree width of $Q$ .

根据附录B，查询$Q$的树分解$\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)$若能使$Q$的分数超树宽度最小化，则称为最优树分解。

Proposition E.6. Let ${\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }$ be a component of $\widehat{Q}$ . There must exist an optimal tree decomposition $\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)$ for ${\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }$ where every bag of $\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)$ is $Z$ -prefix-closed.

命题E.6。设${\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }$为$\widehat{Q}$的组件。必存在${\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }$的最优树分解$\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)$，其中每个包都是$Z$-前缀封闭的。

Proof or Proposition E.6. The proof is based on the equivalence of tree decompositions and variable elimination orders. See Appendix B for some background. In particular, the proof works as follows. Pick some optimal variable elimination order $\mathbf{\mu }$ . We inductively prove the following:

证明或命题E.6。该证明基于树分解与变量消元顺序的等价性。相关背景知识参见附录B。具体而言，证明过程如下：选取某个最优变量消元顺序$\mathbf{\mu }$，我们通过归纳法证明以下内容：

CLAIM E.2. Throughout variable elimination,every atom is $Z$ -prefix-closed.

声明E.2。在变量消元过程中，每个原子都是$Z$-前缀封闭的。

CLAIM E.3. For every variable $X \in  \mu$ ,the set ${U}_{X}^{\mu }$ is prefix-closed.

声明E.3。对于每个变量$X \in  \mu$，集合${U}_{X}^{\mu }$都是前缀封闭的。

Initially,both claims above hold. Now,suppose we pick a variable $X$ to eliminate. By induction,all atoms that contain $X$ are $Z$ -prefix-closed,hence ${U}_{X}^{\mu }$ is $Z$ -prefix-closed. Now in order to show that the newly added atom ${R}_{X}^{\mu }\left( {{U}_{X}^{\mu }-\{ X\} }\right)$ can be assumed to be $Z$ -prefix closed,we recognize two cases. If $X$ is not in $\left\{  {{Z}_{1},\ldots ,{Z}_{k}}\right\}$ ,then ${U}_{X}^{\mu } - \{ X\}$ is obviously $Z$ -prefix-closed. On the other hand,suppose $X = {Z}_{i}$ for some $i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack$ . Let $j$ be the largest index where ${Z}_{j} \in  {U}_{X}^{\mu }$ . If ${Z}_{j} = {Z}_{i}$ ,then ${U}_{X}^{\mu } - \{ X\}$ is $Z$ -prefix closed. Otherwise, ${U}_{X}^{\mu }$ must contain ${Z}_{i},{Z}_{i + 1},\ldots ,{Z}_{j}$ . Moreover,none of these variables can occur in any atom other than ${R}_{X}^{\mu }\left( {{U}_{X}^{\mu }-\{ X\} }\right)$ . Hence,there is an optimal variable elimination order ${\mu }^{\prime }$ that eliminates them in any order. We choose the order ${Z}_{j},{Z}_{j - 1},\ldots ,{Z}_{i}$ .

初始状态下，上述两项声明均成立。现假设选取变量$X$进行消元。根据归纳假设，所有包含$X$的原子都是$Z$-前缀封闭的，因此${U}_{X}^{\mu }$具有$Z$-前缀封闭性。为证明新增原子${R}_{X}^{\mu }\left( {{U}_{X}^{\mu }-\{ X\} }\right)$可被视为$Z$-前缀封闭，我们考虑两种情况：若$X$不在$\left\{  {{Z}_{1},\ldots ,{Z}_{k}}\right\}$中，则${U}_{X}^{\mu } - \{ X\}$显然满足$Z$-前缀封闭性；反之，假设存在$i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack$使得$X = {Z}_{i}$成立。设$j$为满足${Z}_{j} \in  {U}_{X}^{\mu }$的最大索引，若${Z}_{j} = {Z}_{i}$，则${U}_{X}^{\mu } - \{ X\}$具有$Z$-前缀封闭性。否则，${U}_{X}^{\mu }$必须包含${Z}_{i},{Z}_{i + 1},\ldots ,{Z}_{j}$，且这些变量仅出现在原子${R}_{X}^{\mu }\left( {{U}_{X}^{\mu }-\{ X\} }\right)$中。因此存在最优消元顺序${\mu }^{\prime }$可按任意顺序消去它们，我们选择${Z}_{j},{Z}_{j - 1},\ldots ,{Z}_{i}$顺序。

Proof of Lemma E.4. The proof of Lemma E.4 relies on the proof of Theorem 4.1 that is given in Section C.3. Consider an optimal tree decomposition $\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)$ for $\widehat{Q}$ ,i.e. where $\mathrm{w}\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)  = \mathrm{w}\left( \widehat{Q}\right)$ . Let ${\delta }_{1}\widehat{\mathcal{D}},{\delta }_{2}\widehat{\mathcal{D}},\ldots$ be a restricted canonical update stream for $\widehat{\mathcal{Q}}$ . Recall that each ${\delta }_{\tau }\widehat{\mathcal{D}}$ is the canonical partition of a corresponding update ${\delta }_{\tau }\overline{\mathcal{D}}$ to $\overline{\mathcal{D}}$ . Consider an update ${\delta }_{\tau }\widehat{\mathcal{D}} = {\mathrm{{CP}}}_{N}\left( {{\delta }_{\tau }\overline{\mathcal{D}}}\right)$ in this stream. First,assume that ${\delta }_{\tau }\overline{\mathcal{D}}$ is an insert,i.e. ${\delta }_{\tau }\overline{\mathcal{D}} = \left\{  {+{\bar{R}}_{j}\left( {\left\lbrack  {\tau ,\infty }\right\rbrack  ,{t}_{j}}\right) }\right\}$ for some $j \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack$ and tuple ${t}_{j}$ with schema ${X}_{j}$ . In this case,the corresponding ${\delta }_{\tau }\widehat{\mathcal{D}} = {\mathrm{{CP}}}_{N}\left( {{\delta }_{\tau }\overline{\mathcal{D}}}\right)$ is a set of single-tuple inserts,and we can apply these inserts to the materialized bags of the tree decomposition $\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)$ ,just as explained in the proof of Theorem 4.1. Truncation is more tricky. In particular,suppose that ${\delta }_{\tau }\overline{\mathcal{D}}$ is a truncation,i.e. ${\delta }_{\tau }\overline{\mathcal{D}} =$ $\left\{  {-{\bar{R}}_{j}\left( {\left\lbrack  {{\tau }^{\prime },\infty }\right\rbrack  ,{t}_{j}}\right) , + {\bar{R}}_{j}\left( {\left\lbrack  {{\tau }^{\prime },\tau }\right\rbrack  ,{t}_{j}}\right) }\right\}$ for some ${\tau }^{\prime } < \tau ,j \in  \left\lbrack  m\right\rbrack$ and tuple ${t}_{j}$ over schema ${X}_{j}$ . In this case,we update the materialized bags of $\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)$ using the updates given by ${\mathrm{{CP}}}_{N}\left( {{\delta }_{\tau }\overline{\mathcal{D}}}\right)$ . Some of these updates are inserts,namely ${\mathrm{{CP}}}_{N}\left( \left\{  {+{\bar{R}}_{j}\left( {\left\lbrack  {{\tau }^{\prime },\tau }\right\rbrack  ,{t}_{j}}\right) }\right\}  \right)$ ,and these are accounted for by the analysis from the proof of Theorem 4.1. In particular,they are covered by the $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}\left( {N}^{\mathrm{w}\left( \widetilde{Q}\right) }\right)$ total update time over all inserts combined. However,some of the updates in ${\mathrm{{CP}}}_{N}\left( {{\delta }_{\tau }\overline{\mathcal{D}}}\right)$ are deletes,namely ${\mathrm{{CP}}}_{N}\left( \left\{  {-{\bar{R}}_{j}\left( {\left\lbrack  {{\tau }^{\prime },\infty }\right\rbrack  ,{t}_{j}}\right) }\right\}  \right)$ ,and in order to account for those,we will use amortized analysis. In particular, we will deposit extra credits with every insert so that we can use them later to pay for the deletes during truncation. We explain this argument in detail below.

引理E.4的证明。该引理证明依赖于附录C.3给出的定理4.1证明。考虑为$\widehat{Q}$构造的最优树分解$\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)$，即满足$\mathrm{w}\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)  = \mathrm{w}\left( \widehat{Q}\right)$的情形。设${\delta }_{1}\widehat{\mathcal{D}},{\delta }_{2}\widehat{\mathcal{D}},\ldots$是$\widehat{\mathcal{Q}}$的受限规范更新流，注意每个${\delta }_{\tau }\widehat{\mathcal{D}}$都是对$\overline{\mathcal{D}}$执行对应更新操作${\delta }_{\tau }\overline{\mathcal{D}}$的规范划分。分析该流中的某个更新操作${\delta }_{\tau }\widehat{\mathcal{D}} = {\mathrm{{CP}}}_{N}\left( {{\delta }_{\tau }\overline{\mathcal{D}}}\right)$：首先假设${\delta }_{\tau }\overline{\mathcal{D}}$是插入操作，即对具有模式${X}_{j}$的元组${t}_{j}$和某个$j \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack$执行${\delta }_{\tau }\overline{\mathcal{D}} = \left\{  {+{\bar{R}}_{j}\left( {\left\lbrack  {\tau ,\infty }\right\rbrack  ,{t}_{j}}\right) }\right\}$操作。此时对应的${\delta }_{\tau }\widehat{\mathcal{D}} = {\mathrm{{CP}}}_{N}\left( {{\delta }_{\tau }\overline{\mathcal{D}}}\right)$是一组单元组插入操作，可按照定理4.1证明所述方法将这些插入应用到树分解$\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)$的具体化包上。截断操作更为复杂，假设${\delta }_{\tau }\overline{\mathcal{D}}$是截断操作，即对模式${X}_{j}$上的元组${t}_{j}$和某个${\tau }^{\prime } < \tau ,j \in  \left\lbrack  m\right\rbrack$执行${\delta }_{\tau }\overline{\mathcal{D}} =$$\left\{  {-{\bar{R}}_{j}\left( {\left\lbrack  {{\tau }^{\prime },\infty }\right\rbrack  ,{t}_{j}}\right) , + {\bar{R}}_{j}\left( {\left\lbrack  {{\tau }^{\prime },\tau }\right\rbrack  ,{t}_{j}}\right) }\right\}$操作。此时我们通过${\mathrm{{CP}}}_{N}\left( {{\delta }_{\tau }\overline{\mathcal{D}}}\right)$提供的更新来修改$\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)$的具体化包。其中部分更新是插入操作（即${\mathrm{{CP}}}_{N}\left( \left\{  {+{\bar{R}}_{j}\left( {\left\lbrack  {{\tau }^{\prime },\tau }\right\rbrack  ,{t}_{j}}\right) }\right\}  \right)$），这些可由定理4.1证明中的分析处理——它们被包含在所有插入操作共有的$\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}\left( {N}^{\mathrm{w}\left( \widetilde{Q}\right) }\right)$总更新时间内。但${\mathrm{{CP}}}_{N}\left( {{\delta }_{\tau }\overline{\mathcal{D}}}\right)$中还包含删除操作（即${\mathrm{{CP}}}_{N}\left( \left\{  {-{\bar{R}}_{j}\left( {\left\lbrack  {{\tau }^{\prime },\infty }\right\rbrack  ,{t}_{j}}\right) }\right\}  \right)$），为处理这些操作我们将采用摊还分析：在每次插入时预存额外信用值，后续截断时可用这些信用值支付删除操作成本。下文将详细阐述该论证过程。

We start with the following claim, which is very similar in spirit to Proposition E.2:

我们首先提出以下主张，其核心思想与命题E.2高度相似：

CLAIM E.4. Let ${\delta }_{\tau }\overline{\mathcal{D}} = \left\{  {+{\bar{R}}_{j}\left( {\left\lbrack  {\tau ,\infty }\right\rbrack  ,{t}_{j}}\right) }\right\}$ be an insert into $\overline{\mathcal{D}}$ ,for some $j \in  \left\lbrack  m\right\rbrack$ and tuple ${t}_{j}$ over the schema ${X}_{j}$ . For some $i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack   - \{ j\}$ ,let $\left( {\left\lbrack  {\alpha ,\beta }\right\rbrack  ,{t}_{i}}\right)$ be a tuple of another relation ${\bar{R}}_{i}$ that joins with the inserted tuple $\left( {\left\lbrack  {\tau ,\infty }\right\rbrack  ,{t}_{j}}\right)$ of ${\bar{R}}_{j}$ . Then, we must have $\alpha  < \tau$ and $\beta  = \infty$ . Namely,the interval $\left\lbrack  {\alpha ,\beta }\right\rbrack$ strictly contains $\left\lbrack  {\tau ,\infty }\right\rbrack$ .

主张E.4。设${\delta }_{\tau }\overline{\mathcal{D}} = \left\{  {+{\bar{R}}_{j}\left( {\left\lbrack  {\tau ,\infty }\right\rbrack  ,{t}_{j}}\right) }\right\}$为对$\overline{\mathcal{D}}$的插入操作，其中$j \in  \left\lbrack  m\right\rbrack$为模式${X}_{j}$上的某个元组${t}_{j}$。对于某个$i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack   - \{ j\}$，令$\left( {\left\lbrack  {\alpha ,\beta }\right\rbrack  ,{t}_{i}}\right)$为另一关系${\bar{R}}_{i}$中与被插入元组$\left( {\left\lbrack  {\tau ,\infty }\right\rbrack  ,{t}_{j}}\right)$（来自${\bar{R}}_{j}$）连接的元组。则必有$\alpha  < \tau$且$\beta  = \infty$。换言之，区间$\left\lbrack  {\alpha ,\beta }\right\rbrack$严格包含$\left\lbrack  {\tau ,\infty }\right\rbrack$。

Proof of CLAIM E.4. The above claim follows from the fact that $\tau$ is the current time,and any interval $\left\lbrack  {\alpha ,\beta }\right\rbrack$ where $\alpha  \geq  \tau$ would start in the future,hence it cannot exist yet in $\overline{\mathcal{D}}$ . Moreover,in order for $\left\lbrack  {\alpha ,\beta }\right\rbrack$ to overlap with $\left\lbrack  {\tau ,\infty }\right\rbrack$ ,it must be the case that $\beta  \geq  \tau$ ,which implies that $\beta  = \infty$ because it is in the future. This proves the claim.

主张E.4的证明。上述主张基于以下事实：$\tau$为当前时间，而任何满足$\alpha  \geq  \tau$的区间$\left\lbrack  {\alpha ,\beta }\right\rbrack$都起始于未来时刻，因此不可能已存在于$\overline{\mathcal{D}}$中。此外，要使$\left\lbrack  {\alpha ,\beta }\right\rbrack$与$\left\lbrack  {\tau ,\infty }\right\rbrack$重叠，必须满足$\beta  \geq  \tau$，这意味着$\beta  = \infty$（因其处于未来时段）。由此得证。

For the purpose of analyzing the algorithm,we distinguish between two types of tuples in $\overline{\mathcal{D}}$ : finalized tuples and provisional tuples. Finalized tuples cannot be deleted once they are inserted, while provisional tuples can be deleted later. Also for the purpose of analyzing the algorithm, we slightly modify the way the algorithm handles inserts. In particular,whenever we have an insert $+ {\bar{R}}_{j}\left( {\left\lbrack  {\tau ,\infty }\right\rbrack  ,{t}_{j}}\right)$ into $\overline{\mathcal{D}}$ ,we insert this tuple as provisional (since it might be deleted later due to truncation) and we insert an additional tuple $\left( {\left\lbrack  {\tau ,\tau }\right\rbrack  ,{t}_{j}}\right)$ into ${\bar{R}}_{j}$ that is finalized. In particular,we know for sure that the tuple $\left( {\left\lbrack  {\tau ,\tau }\right\rbrack  ,{t}_{j}}\right)$ will never be deleted later because the lifespan of the tuple ${t}_{j}$ extends at least until the current time $\tau$ . We also update $\widehat{\mathcal{D}}$ by applying the two corresponding inserts,namely ${\mathrm{{CP}}}_{N}\left( \left\{  {+{\bar{R}}_{j}\left( {\left\lbrack  {\tau ,\infty }\right\rbrack  ,{t}_{j}}\right) }\right\}  \right)$ and ${\mathrm{{CP}}}_{N}\left( \left\{  {+{\bar{R}}_{j}\left( {\left\lbrack  {\tau ,\tau }\right\rbrack  ,{t}_{j}}\right) }\right\}  \right)$ . Tuples in $\widehat{\mathcal{D}}$ inherit the provisional/finalized property from the underlying tuples in $\overline{\mathcal{D}}$ . We make the following claim:

为分析算法之目的，我们将$\overline{\mathcal{D}}$中的元组区分为两类：定型元组(finalized tuples)与暂存元组(provisional tuples)。定型元组一经插入即不可删除，而暂存元组后续可被删除。出于算法分析需要，我们微调了算法处理插入操作的方式。具体而言，每当执行插入操作$+ {\bar{R}}_{j}\left( {\left\lbrack  {\tau ,\infty }\right\rbrack  ,{t}_{j}}\right)$至$\overline{\mathcal{D}}$时，该元组将作为暂存元组插入（因其可能因截断操作被后续删除），同时我们会在${\bar{R}}_{j}$中插入一个定型元组$\left( {\left\lbrack  {\tau ,\tau }\right\rbrack  ,{t}_{j}}\right)$。特别地，可确定元组$\left( {\left\lbrack  {\tau ,\tau }\right\rbrack  ,{t}_{j}}\right)$永不会被删除，因为元组${t}_{j}$的生命周期至少延续至当前时间$\tau$。我们还会通过执行对应的两个插入操作（即${\mathrm{{CP}}}_{N}\left( \left\{  {+{\bar{R}}_{j}\left( {\left\lbrack  {\tau ,\infty }\right\rbrack  ,{t}_{j}}\right) }\right\}  \right)$与${\mathrm{{CP}}}_{N}\left( \left\{  {+{\bar{R}}_{j}\left( {\left\lbrack  {\tau ,\tau }\right\rbrack  ,{t}_{j}}\right) }\right\}  \right)$）来更新$\widehat{\mathcal{D}}$。$\widehat{\mathcal{D}}$中的元组继承$\overline{\mathcal{D}}$底层元组的暂存/定型属性。现提出如下主张：

CLAIM E.5. The total time needed to apply the insert ${\mathrm{{CP}}}_{N}\left( \left\{  {+{\bar{R}}_{j}\left( {\left\lbrack  {\tau ,\infty }\right\rbrack  ,{t}_{j}}\right) }\right\}  \right)$ into $\widehat{\mathcal{D}}$ is within a $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}\left( 1\right)$ factor of the total time needed to apply the insert ${\mathrm{{CP}}}_{N}\left( \left\{  {+{\bar{R}}_{j}\left( {\left\lbrack  {\tau ,\tau }\right\rbrack  ,{t}_{j}}\right) }\right\}  \right)$ .

主张E.5。执行插入操作${\mathrm{{CP}}}_{N}\left( \left\{  {+{\bar{R}}_{j}\left( {\left\lbrack  {\tau ,\infty }\right\rbrack  ,{t}_{j}}\right) }\right\}  \right)$至$\widehat{\mathcal{D}}$所需总时间，与执行插入操作${\mathrm{{CP}}}_{N}\left( \left\{  {+{\bar{R}}_{j}\left( {\left\lbrack  {\tau ,\tau }\right\rbrack  ,{t}_{j}}\right) }\right\}  \right)$所需总时间存在$\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}\left( 1\right)$倍率关系。

The above claim follows from Claim E. 4 along with the fact that every bag in the tree decomposition $\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)$ is $Z$ -prefix closed (Proposition E.6). In particular,Claim E.4 basically says that the two intervals $\left\lbrack  {\tau ,\infty }\right\rbrack$ and $\left\lbrack  {\tau ,\tau }\right\rbrack$ are "indistinguishable" as far as joins are concerned. Proposition E. 6 ensures that whenever we take a projection in the query plan corresponding to the tree decomposition $\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)$ ,we are basically replacing these two intervals with two new intervals that contain them. Hence, the new intervals remain indistinguishable.

上述主张源自主张E.4，结合树分解$\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)$中每个包均满足$Z$-前缀封闭性这一事实（命题E.6）。特别地，主张E.4本质上表明就连接操作而言，两个区间$\left\lbrack  {\tau ,\infty }\right\rbrack$与$\left\lbrack  {\tau ,\tau }\right\rbrack$具有"不可区分性"。命题E.6确保：当我们在对应树分解$\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)$的查询计划中执行投影操作时，本质上是用包含这两个区间的新区间进行替换。因此新区间仍保持不可区分性。

Now, we can analyze the total update time as follows. Claim E. 5 implies that the total time needed to apply provisional inserts is upper bounded by the total time needed to apply finalized inserts. Hence, the overall runtime is dominated by finalized inserts. Moreover, every time we apply a provisional insert, we can deposit one credit with every tuple that we insert into any bag of $\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)$ ,in order to pay for the potential delete of this tuple in the future. These credits cover the cost of all deletes of provisional tuples. (Recall that finalized tuples cannot be deleted.) Finally, the total time needed to apply all finalized inserts is bounded by $\widetilde{O}\left( {N}^{\mathrm{w}\left( \widehat{Q}\right) }\right)$ ,by Theorem 4.1.

现在，我们可以按如下方式分析总更新时间。声明E.5表明，应用临时插入所需的总时间上界不超过应用最终插入所需的总时间。因此，整体运行时间主要由最终插入决定。此外，每次应用临时插入时，我们可以为插入$\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)$任何包中的每个元组存入一个信用点，用于支付未来可能删除该元组的成本。这些信用点覆盖了所有临时元组删除的开销。（需注意最终元组不可删除。）最终，应用所有最终插入所需的总时间受$\widetilde{O}\left( {N}^{\mathrm{w}\left( \widehat{Q}\right) }\right)$限制，根据定理4.1。

We are left with explaining how to support constant-delay enumeration of ${\widehat{Q}}_{\tau }\left( {\widehat{\mathcal{D}}}^{\left( \tau \right) }\right)$ . To that end,we enumerate selections ${\sigma }_{{Z}_{1} = {z}_{1},\ldots ,{Z}_{k} = {z}_{k}}\widehat{Q}\left( {\widehat{\mathcal{D}}}^{\left( \tau \right) }\right)$ for various assignments ${Z}_{1} = {z}_{1},\ldots ,{Z}_{k} = {z}_{k}$ ,as defined by (52). Luckily,for every component ${\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }$ of $\widehat{Q}$ ,there is one input relation ${\widehat{R}}_{{\sigma }_{k}}$ that contains the variables $\left\{  {{Z}_{1},\ldots ,{Z}_{k}}\right\}$ ; see (6). Hence,every tree decomposition of ${\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }$ must contain a bag $B$ that contains all the variables ${Z}_{1},\ldots ,{Z}_{k}$ . In order to enumerate a selection ${\sigma }_{{Z}_{1} = {z}_{1},\ldots ,{Z}_{k} = {z}_{k}}\widehat{Q}\left( {\widehat{\mathcal{D}}}^{\left( \tau \right) }\right)$ with constant delay,we can use the bag $B$ as the root of the tree decomposition.

接下来需要解释如何支持${\widehat{Q}}_{\tau }\left( {\widehat{\mathcal{D}}}^{\left( \tau \right) }\right)$的常数延迟枚举。为此，我们按照(52)定义枚举各种赋值${Z}_{1} = {z}_{1},\ldots ,{Z}_{k} = {z}_{k}$对应的选择${\sigma }_{{Z}_{1} = {z}_{1},\ldots ,{Z}_{k} = {z}_{k}}\widehat{Q}\left( {\widehat{\mathcal{D}}}^{\left( \tau \right) }\right)$。幸运的是，对于$\widehat{Q}$的每个组件${\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }$，都存在一个包含变量$\left\{  {{Z}_{1},\ldots ,{Z}_{k}}\right\}$的输入关系${\widehat{R}}_{{\sigma }_{k}}$；参见(6)。因此，${\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }$的每个树分解必须包含一个囊括所有变量${Z}_{1},\ldots ,{Z}_{k}$的包$B$。为了实现常数延迟枚举选择${\sigma }_{{Z}_{1} = {z}_{1},\ldots ,{Z}_{k} = {z}_{k}}\widehat{Q}\left( {\widehat{\mathcal{D}}}^{\left( \tau \right) }\right)$，我们可以将包$B$作为树分解的根节点。

### E.2 Upper Bound for ${\mathrm{{IVM}}}_{\delta }^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$

### E.2 ${\mathrm{{IVM}}}_{\delta }^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$的上界

In the previous section,we proved Lemma 7.1 for the IVM ${}^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$ problem for a given query $Q$ . In this section,we extend the proof and the corresponding algorithm from the previous section in order to prove Lemma 7.1 for the ${\mathrm{{IVM}}}_{\delta }^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$ problem.

在前一节中，我们针对给定查询$Q$的IVM${}^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$问题证明了引理7.1。本节我们将扩展前一节的证明及相应算法，为${\mathrm{{IVM}}}_{\delta }^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$问题证明引理7.1。

Here are the main changes to Algorithm 2 in order to support ${\mathrm{{IVM}}}_{\delta }^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$ ,i.e. support constant-delay enumeration of ${\delta }_{\tau }Q\left( \mathcal{D}\right)$ after every update ${\delta }_{\tau }\mathcal{D}$ :

以下是算法2为支持${\mathrm{{IVM}}}_{\delta }^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$（即在每次更新${\delta }_{\tau }\mathcal{D}$后支持${\delta }_{\tau }Q\left( \mathcal{D}\right)$的常数延迟枚举）所作的主要修改：

- Insertion ${\delta }_{\tau }\mathcal{D} = \left\{  {+{R}_{j}\left( t\right) }\right\}$ : In addition to applying the insert $\left\{  {+{\bar{R}}_{j}\left( {\left\lbrack  {\tau ,\infty }\right\rbrack  ,t}\right) }\right\}$ into $\overline{\mathcal{D}}$ ,we now also insert $+ {\bar{R}}_{j}\left( {\left\lbrack  {\tau ,\tau }\right\rbrack  ,t}\right)$ .

- 插入${\delta }_{\tau }\mathcal{D} = \left\{  {+{R}_{j}\left( t\right) }\right\}$：除了将插入$\left\{  {+{\bar{R}}_{j}\left( {\left\lbrack  {\tau ,\infty }\right\rbrack  ,t}\right) }\right\}$应用到$\overline{\mathcal{D}}$外，我们现在还需插入$+ {\bar{R}}_{j}\left( {\left\lbrack  {\tau ,\tau }\right\rbrack  ,t}\right)$。

- Deletion ${\delta }_{\tau }\mathcal{D} = \left\{  {-{R}_{j}\left( t\right) }\right\}$ : In addition to applying the update $\left\{  {-{\bar{R}}_{j}\left( {\left\lbrack  {{\tau }^{\prime },\infty }\right\rbrack  ,t}\right) , + {\bar{R}}_{j}\left( {\left\lbrack  {{\tau }^{\prime },\tau }\right\rbrack  ,t}\right) }\right\}$ to $\overline{\mathcal{D}}$ ,we now also apply the insert $+ {\bar{R}}_{j}\left( {\left\lbrack  {\tau ,\tau }\right\rbrack  ,\mathbf{t}}\right)$ .

- 删除操作 ${\delta }_{\tau }\mathcal{D} = \left\{  {-{R}_{j}\left( t\right) }\right\}$：除了将更新$\left\{  {-{\bar{R}}_{j}\left( {\left\lbrack  {{\tau }^{\prime },\infty }\right\rbrack  ,t}\right) , + {\bar{R}}_{j}\left( {\left\lbrack  {{\tau }^{\prime },\tau }\right\rbrack  ,t}\right) }\right\}$应用于$\overline{\mathcal{D}}$外，我们现在还应用插入操作$+ {\bar{R}}_{j}\left( {\left\lbrack  {\tau ,\tau }\right\rbrack  ,\mathbf{t}}\right)$。

- Enumeration Suppose we want to enumerate ${\delta }_{\tau }Q\left( \mathcal{D}\right)$ after an update ${\delta }_{\tau }\mathcal{D} = s{R}_{j}\left( t\right)$ where $s \in  \{  + , - \}$ . Then, we enumerate the following:

- 枚举操作 假设我们需要在更新${\delta }_{\tau }\mathcal{D} = s{R}_{j}\left( t\right)$后枚举${\delta }_{\tau }Q\left( \mathcal{D}\right)$，其中$s \in  \{  + , - \}$。那么，我们将枚举以下内容：

$$
{\delta }_{\tau }Q\left( \mathcal{D}\right)  = s\left( {{\pi }_{\operatorname{vars}\left( Q\right) }\mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{\substack{{{z}_{1},\ldots ,{z}_{k} \in  \{ 0,1{\} }^{ * }} \\  {{z}_{1} \circ  \cdots  \circ  {z}_{k} = \operatorname{CP}\left( \left\lbrack  {\tau ,\tau }\right\rbrack  \right) } }}{\sigma }_{{Z}_{1} = {z}_{1},\ldots ,{Z}_{k} = {z}_{k}}\widehat{Q}\left( \widehat{\mathcal{D}}\right) }\right)  \tag{56}
$$

We call the above modified version of Algorithm 2 the delta version of Algorithm 2. Before we prove that the delta version of Algorithm 2 proves Lemma 7.1 for ${\mathrm{{IVM}}}_{\delta }^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$ ,we need some preliminaries.

我们将上述修改版的算法2称为算法2的增量版本。在证明该增量版本能为${\mathrm{{IVM}}}_{\delta }^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$满足引理7.1之前，需要先进行若干准备工作。

Proposition E.7. Given a discrete interval $\left\lbrack  {\alpha ,\beta }\right\rbrack$ for some $\alpha ,\beta  \in  \mathbb{N}$ ,let $N$ be a power of 2 such that $N \geq  \beta$ . If ${\mathrm{{CP}}}_{N}\left( \left\lbrack  {\alpha ,\beta }\right\rbrack  \right)$ contains a singleton interval $\left\lbrack  {x,x}\right\rbrack$ for some $x \in  \mathbb{N}$ ,then $x$ is an endpoint of $\left\lbrack  {\alpha ,\beta }\right\rbrack$ . In particular,either $x = \alpha$ or $x = \beta$ .

命题E.7。给定离散区间$\left\lbrack  {\alpha ,\beta }\right\rbrack$（其中$\alpha ,\beta  \in  \mathbb{N}$），令$N$为2的幂次方且满足$N \geq  \beta$。若${\mathrm{{CP}}}_{N}\left( \left\lbrack  {\alpha ,\beta }\right\rbrack  \right)$包含单例区间$\left\lbrack  {x,x}\right\rbrack$（其中$x \in  \mathbb{N}$），则$x$必为$\left\lbrack  {\alpha ,\beta }\right\rbrack$的端点。具体而言，要么$x = \alpha$，要么$x = \beta$。

Proposition E. 7 follows from the definition of a segment tree ${\mathcal{T}}_{N}$ and canonical partition ${\mathrm{{CP}}}_{N}$ . For example,consider the segment tree ${\mathcal{T}}_{8}$ depicted in Figure 2. Let’s take the segment $\left\lbrack  {2,5}\right\rbrack$ . Its canonical partition consists of $\{ \left\lbrack  {2,2}\right\rbrack  ,\left\lbrack  {3,4}\right\rbrack  ,\left\lbrack  {5,5}\right\rbrack  \}$ . Note that only $\left\lbrack  {2,2}\right\rbrack$ and $\left\lbrack  {5,5}\right\rbrack$ are singleton intervals. But 2 and 5 are the endpoints of the interval $\left\lbrack  {2,5}\right\rbrack$ . The above proposition says that we cannot find in the canonical partition of any segment $\left\lbrack  {\alpha ,\beta }\right\rbrack$ any singleton intervals that are not end points of $\left\lbrack  {\alpha ,\beta }\right\rbrack$ .

命题E.7的结论源自线段树${\mathcal{T}}_{N}$与规范划分${\mathrm{{CP}}}_{N}$的定义。例如观察图2所示的线段树${\mathcal{T}}_{8}$。取线段$\left\lbrack  {2,5}\right\rbrack$，其规范划分由$\{ \left\lbrack  {2,2}\right\rbrack  ,\left\lbrack  {3,4}\right\rbrack  ,\left\lbrack  {5,5}\right\rbrack  \}$构成。注意其中仅$\left\lbrack  {2,2}\right\rbrack$和$\left\lbrack  {5,5}\right\rbrack$是单例区间，而2和5正是区间$\left\lbrack  {2,5}\right\rbrack$的端点。该命题表明：在任何线段$\left\lbrack  {\alpha ,\beta }\right\rbrack$的规范划分中，都不可能找到非$\left\lbrack  {\alpha ,\beta }\right\rbrack$端点的单例区间。

The following theorem implies Lemma 7.1 for ${\mathrm{{IVM}}}_{\delta }^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$ :

以下定理为${\mathrm{{IVM}}}_{\delta }^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$证明了引理7.1：

THEOREM E.8. The delta version of Algorithm 2 solves the IVM ${}_{\delta }^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$ with amortized update time $\widetilde{O}\left( {N}^{\mathrm{w}\left( \widehat{Q}\right)  - 1}\right)$ where $\mathrm{w}\left( \widehat{Q}\right)$ is the fractional hypertree width of $\widehat{Q}$ .

定理E.8。算法2的增量版本能以$\widetilde{O}\left( {N}^{\mathrm{w}\left( \widehat{Q}\right)  - 1}\right)$的摊还更新时间解决IVM${}_{\delta }^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$问题，其中$\mathrm{w}\left( \widehat{Q}\right)$表示$\widehat{Q}$的分数超树宽度。

Proof. The proof is very similar to the proof of Lemma 7.1 from Section E.1 for IVM ${}^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$ . For every insert or delete ${\delta }_{\tau }\mathcal{D} = \left\{  {s{R}_{j}\left( t\right) }\right\}$ where $s \in  \{  + , - \}$ ,we now have to insert an extra tuple $+ {\bar{R}}_{j}\left( {\left\lbrack  {\tau ,\tau }\right\rbrack  ,t}\right)$ into $\overline{\mathcal{D}}$ . This only increases the size of $\overline{\mathcal{D}}$ by a constant factor. Hence,it doesn’t affect the overall runtime.

证明。该证明与附录E.1节中引理7.1的IVM${}^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$证明高度相似。对于每次插入或删除${\delta }_{\tau }\mathcal{D} = \left\{  {s{R}_{j}\left( t\right) }\right\}$操作（当$s \in  \{  + , - \}$时），我们现在需要向$\overline{\mathcal{D}}$中额外插入一个元组$+ {\bar{R}}_{j}\left( {\left\lbrack  {\tau ,\tau }\right\rbrack  ,t}\right)$。这仅会使$\overline{\mathcal{D}}$的大小增加一个常数因子，因此不会影响整体运行时间。

Next,we prove correctness. In particular,our target is to prove that (56) does indeed compute ${\delta }_{\tau }Q\left( \mathcal{D}\right)$ ,as defined by (3). To that end, we define the following:

接下来我们证明正确性。具体而言，我们的目标是证明公式(56)确实能计算出公式(3)定义的${\delta }_{\tau }Q\left( \mathcal{D}\right)$。为此，我们定义如下内容：

$$
{\bar{Q}}_{{\delta }_{\tau }}\left( {\overline{\mathcal{D}}}^{\left( \tau \right) }\right) \overset{\text{ def }}{ = }{\sigma }_{\left\lbrack  Z\right\rbrack   = \left\lbrack  {\tau ,\tau }\right\rbrack  }\bar{Q}\left( {\overline{\mathcal{D}}}^{\left( \tau \right) }\right)  \tag{57}
$$

$$
{\widehat{Q}}_{{\delta }_{\tau }}\left( {\widehat{\mathcal{D}}}^{\left( \tau \right) }\right) \overset{\text{ def }}{ = }\mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{\substack{{{z}_{1},\ldots ,{z}_{k} \in  \{ 0,1{\} }^{ * }} \\  {{z}_{1} \circ  \cdots {z}_{k} = \operatorname{CP}\left( \left\lbrack  {\tau ,\tau }\right\rbrack  \right) } }}{\sigma }_{{Z}_{1} = {z}_{1},\ldots ,{Z}_{k} = {z}_{k}}\widehat{Q}\left( {\widehat{\mathcal{D}}}^{\left( \tau \right) }\right)  \tag{58}
$$

Compare the above to (48) and (52) respectively. The correctness proof goes through two steps. First, we prove that

请分别对照公式(48)和(52)进行理解。正确性证明分为两步：首先我们证明

$$
{\delta }_{\tau }Q\left( \mathcal{D}\right)  = s\left( {{\pi }_{\operatorname{vars}\left( Q\right) }{\bar{Q}}_{{\delta }_{\tau }}\left( {\overline{\mathcal{D}}}^{\left( \tau \right) }\right) }\right) 
$$

where recall that $s \in  \{  + , - \}$ depending on the update ${\delta }_{\tau }\mathcal{D} = \left\{  {s{R}_{j}\left( t\right) }\right\}$ . This follows from the fact that $\tau$ is the unique timestamp for the update ${\delta }_{\tau }\mathcal{D}$ . In particular,all corresponding updates $\bar{s}{\bar{R}}_{j}\left( {\left\lbrack  {\alpha ,\beta }\right\rbrack  ,t}\right)$ in ${\delta }_{\tau }\overline{\mathcal{D}}$ (where $\bar{s} \in  \{  + , - \}$ ) must have $\tau  \in  \left\lbrack  {\alpha ,\beta }\right\rbrack$ . Moreover,no other tuple $\left( {\left\lbrack  {{\alpha }^{\prime },{\beta }^{\prime }}\right\rbrack  ,{t}^{\prime }}\right)$ in ${\bar{R}}_{j}$ can have $\tau  \in  \{ \alpha ,\beta \}$ (because the timestamp $\tau$ is unique to this update).

此处需注意$s \in  \{  + , - \}$的取值取决于更新操作${\delta }_{\tau }\mathcal{D} = \left\{  {s{R}_{j}\left( t\right) }\right\}$。这是因为$\tau$是该次更新${\delta }_{\tau }\mathcal{D}$的唯一时间戳。特别地，${\delta }_{\tau }\overline{\mathcal{D}}$中所有对应更新$\bar{s}{\bar{R}}_{j}\left( {\left\lbrack  {\alpha ,\beta }\right\rbrack  ,t}\right)$（当$\bar{s} \in  \{  + , - \}$时）都必须满足$\tau  \in  \left\lbrack  {\alpha ,\beta }\right\rbrack$。此外，${\bar{R}}_{j}$中不存在其他元组$\left( {\left\lbrack  {{\alpha }^{\prime },{\beta }^{\prime }}\right\rbrack  ,{t}^{\prime }}\right)$能具有$\tau  \in  \{ \alpha ,\beta \}$（因为时间戳$\tau$是该次更新独有的）。

The second step of the correctness proof is to prove that

正确性证明的第二步是证明

$$
{\pi }_{\operatorname{vars}\left( Q\right) }{\bar{Q}}_{{\delta }_{\tau }}\left( {\overline{\mathcal{D}}}^{\left( \tau \right) }\right)  = {\pi }_{\operatorname{vars}\left( Q\right) }{\widehat{Q}}_{{\delta }_{\tau }}\left( {\widehat{\mathcal{D}}}^{\left( \tau \right) }\right)  \tag{59}
$$

The containment $\subseteq$ in (59) holds thanks to the extra intervals $+ {\bar{R}}_{j}\left( {\left\lbrack  {\tau ,\tau }\right\rbrack  ,t}\right)$ that we are inserting to $\overline{\mathcal{D}}$ in the delta version of Algorithm 2. The other containment 2 in (59) follows from Proposition E.7. In particular, (58) only selects the leaf corresponding to $\left\lbrack  {\tau ,\tau }\right\rbrack$ in the segment tree,but Proposition E. 7 tells us that this leaf could not belong to the canonical partition of any interval $\left\lbrack  {\alpha ,\beta }\right\rbrack$ unless $\tau  \in  \{ \alpha ,\beta \}$ . Such intervals could only have been added to $\overline{\mathcal{D}}$ during the update ${\delta }_{\tau }\mathcal{D}$ because the timestamp $\tau$ is unique to this update.

公式(59)中的包含关系$\subseteq$成立，得益于我们在算法2的增量版本中向$\overline{\mathcal{D}}$插入的额外区间$+ {\bar{R}}_{j}\left( {\left\lbrack  {\tau ,\tau }\right\rbrack  ,t}\right)$。而公式(59)中的另一个包含关系2则源自命题E.7。具体来说，虽然公式(58)仅选择线段树中对应$\left\lbrack  {\tau ,\tau }\right\rbrack$的叶节点，但命题E.7指出：除非满足$\tau  \in  \{ \alpha ,\beta \}$，否则该叶节点不可能属于任何区间$\left\lbrack  {\alpha ,\beta }\right\rbrack$的规范划分。这类区间仅可能在更新${\delta }_{\tau }\mathcal{D}$时被加入$\overline{\mathcal{D}}$，因为时间戳$\tau$是该次更新独有的。

Finally,we show that constant-delay enumeration for ${\widehat{\mathcal{Q}}}_{{\delta }_{\tau }}\left( {\widehat{\mathcal{D}}}^{\left( \tau \right) }\right)$ implies constant-delay enumeration for ${\delta }_{\tau }Q\left( \mathcal{D}\right)$ . To that end, we prove:

最后我们证明，对${\widehat{\mathcal{Q}}}_{{\delta }_{\tau }}\left( {\widehat{\mathcal{D}}}^{\left( \tau \right) }\right)$的常数延迟枚举意味着对${\delta }_{\tau }Q\left( \mathcal{D}\right)$的常数延迟枚举。为此需要证明：

$$
\left| {{\widehat{Q}}_{{\delta }_{\tau }}\left( {\widehat{\mathcal{D}}}^{\left( \tau \right) }\right) }\right|  = \left| {{\delta }_{\tau }Q\left( \mathcal{D}\right) }\right| 
$$

We also prove that in two steps, namely

我们同样分两步进行证明：

$$
\left| {{\bar{Q}}_{{\delta }_{\tau }}\left( {\overline{\mathcal{D}}}^{\left( \tau \right) }\right) }\right|  = \left| {{\delta }_{\tau }Q\left( \mathcal{D}\right) }\right|  \tag{60}
$$

$$
\left| {{\widehat{Q}}_{{\delta }_{\tau }}\left( {\widehat{\mathcal{D}}}^{\left( \tau \right) }\right) }\right|  = \left| {{\bar{Q}}_{{\delta }_{\tau }}\left( {\overline{\mathcal{D}}}^{\left( \tau \right) }\right) }\right|  \tag{61}
$$

Equation (60) follows from (57) along with the definition of ${\delta }_{\tau }Q\left( \mathcal{D}\right)$ . Equation (61) follows from the fact that the delta version of Algorithm 2 ensures the following: for every timestamp $\tau$ ,there can be at most two tuples in $\overline{\mathcal{D}}$ where $\tau$ appears as an end point of an interval. The two tuples must have the form $\left( {\left\lbrack  {\tau ,\tau }\right\rbrack  ,t}\right)$ and $\left( {\left\lbrack  {\alpha ,\beta }\right\rbrack  ,t}\right)$ for the same $t$ where either $\alpha  = \tau$ or $\beta  = \tau$ . When we take the canonical partition,both tuples are going to produce the same tuple in ${\widehat{Q}}_{{\delta }_{\tau }}\left( {\widehat{\mathcal{D}}}^{\left( \tau \right) }\right)$ .

式(60)由式(57)及${\delta }_{\tau }Q\left( \mathcal{D}\right)$的定义推导得出。式(61)的成立基于算法2的delta版本确保以下特性：对于每个时间戳$\tau$，在$\overline{\mathcal{D}}$中最多存在两个元组，其中$\tau$作为区间端点出现。这两个元组必须具有相同$t$下的$\left( {\left\lbrack  {\tau ,\tau }\right\rbrack  ,t}\right)$和$\left( {\left\lbrack  {\alpha ,\beta }\right\rbrack  ,t}\right)$形式，且满足$\alpha  = \tau$或$\beta  = \tau$条件。当进行规范分割时，两个元组将在${\widehat{Q}}_{{\delta }_{\tau }}\left( {\widehat{\mathcal{D}}}^{\left( \tau \right) }\right)$中生成相同的元组。

### E.3 Upper bound in terms of $\left| \mathcal{D}\right|$ instead of $N$

### E.3 基于$\left| \mathcal{D}\right|$而非$N$的上界

The upper bound in Lemma 7.1 is written in terms of the length $N$ of the update stream. In this section,we show how to use Lemma 7.1 as a black box in order to prove Theorem 4.4,which gives a stronger bound where $N$ is replaced by the current database size $\left| {\mathcal{D}}^{\left( \tau \right) }\right|$ at the $\tau$ -th update. Note that $\left| {\mathcal{D}}^{\left( \tau \right) }\right|  \leq  N$ and $\left| {\mathcal{D}}^{\left( \tau \right) }\right|$ could be unboundedly smaller than $N$ (specifically when the stream contains many deletes before the timestamp $\tau$ ).

引理7.1中的上界是以更新流长度$N$表述的。本节将演示如何将引理7.1作为黑盒来证明定理4.4，该定理给出了更强约束——用第$\tau$次更新时的当前数据库大小$\left| {\mathcal{D}}^{\left( \tau \right) }\right|$替代$N$。注意$\left| {\mathcal{D}}^{\left( \tau \right) }\right|  \leq  N$和$\left| {\mathcal{D}}^{\left( \tau \right) }\right|$可能无限小于$N$（特别是当流在时间戳$\tau$前包含大量删除操作时）。

Theorem 4.4. For any query $Q$ ,both ${\operatorname{IVM}}^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$ and ${\operatorname{IVM}}_{\delta }^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$ can be solved with $\widetilde{O}\left( {\left| {\mathcal{D}}^{\left( \tau \right) }\right| }^{\mathrm{w}\left( \widehat{Q}\right)  - 1}\right)$ amortized update time and non-amortized constant enumeration delay,where $\widehat{Q}$ is the multivariate extension of $Q$ ,and $\left| {\mathcal{D}}^{\left( \tau \right) }\right|$ is the current database size at update time $\tau$ .

定理4.4. 对于任意查询$Q$，${\operatorname{IVM}}^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$和${\operatorname{IVM}}_{\delta }^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$均可通过$\widetilde{O}\left( {\left| {\mathcal{D}}^{\left( \tau \right) }\right| }^{\mathrm{w}\left( \widehat{Q}\right)  - 1}\right)$摊还更新时间和非摊还常数枚举延迟求解，其中$\widehat{Q}$是$Q$的多变量扩展，$\left| {\mathcal{D}}^{\left( \tau \right) }\right|$表示更新时刻$\tau$的当前数据库规模。

Having an amortized update time of $\widetilde{O}\left( {\left| {\mathcal{D}}^{\left( \tau \right) }\right| }^{\mathrm{w}\left( \widehat{Q}\right)  - 1}\right)$ means that all $N$ updates combined take time $\widetilde{O}\left( {\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{\tau  \in  \left\lbrack  N\right\rbrack  }}{\left| {\mathcal{D}}^{\left( \tau \right) }\right| }^{\mathrm{w}\left( \widehat{Q}\right)  - 1}}\right) .$

具有$\widetilde{O}\left( {\left| {\mathcal{D}}^{\left( \tau \right) }\right| }^{\mathrm{w}\left( \widehat{Q}\right)  - 1}\right)$的摊还更新时间意味着所有$N$次更新总共耗时$\widetilde{O}\left( {\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{\tau  \in  \left\lbrack  N\right\rbrack  }}{\left| {\mathcal{D}}^{\left( \tau \right) }\right| }^{\mathrm{w}\left( \widehat{Q}\right)  - 1}}\right) .$

Proof. The proof uses Lemma 7.1 as a black box. In particular, we will use the same maintenance algorithm from the proof of Lemma 7.1. However, every now and then, we will "reset" the algorithm by taking the current database ${\mathcal{D}}^{\left( \tau \right) }$ ,creating a new empty database from scratch,and inserting every tuple in ${\mathcal{D}}^{\left( \tau \right) }$ into the new database as a single-tuple update. After that,we process the next $\frac{1}{2}\left| {\mathcal{D}}^{\left( \tau \right) }\right|$ updates ${}^{8}$ from the original update sequence,and then we apply another reset. The total number of updates between the last two resets is thus $\frac{3}{2}\left| {\mathcal{D}}^{\left( \tau \right) }\right|$ . By Lemma 7.1,these $\frac{3}{2}\left| {\mathcal{D}}^{\left( \tau \right) }\right|$ updates together take time $\widetilde{O}\left( {\left| {\mathcal{D}}^{\left( \tau \right) }\right| }^{\mathrm{w}\left( \widehat{Q}\right) }\right)$ . Moreover,note that between the two resets,the database size cannot change by more than a factor of 2 . This is because we only have $\frac{1}{2}\left| {\mathcal{D}}^{\left( \tau \right) }\right|$ updates from the original stream that are applied to ${\mathcal{D}}^{\left( \tau \right) }$ . In the two extreme cases,all of these updates are inserts or all of them are deletes.

证明。该证明将引理7.1作为黑盒使用。特别地，我们将沿用引理7.1证明中的相同维护算法。但每隔一段时间，我们会通过获取当前数据库${\mathcal{D}}^{\left( \tau \right) }$进行"重置"：新建一个空数据库，并将${\mathcal{D}}^{\left( \tau \right) }$中的每个元组作为单次更新插入新库。之后处理原始更新序列中的后续$\frac{1}{2}\left| {\mathcal{D}}^{\left( \tau \right) }\right|$次更新${}^{8}$，再进行下一次重置。因此两次重置间的总更新次数为$\frac{3}{2}\left| {\mathcal{D}}^{\left( \tau \right) }\right|$。根据引理7.1，这些$\frac{3}{2}\left| {\mathcal{D}}^{\left( \tau \right) }\right|$次更新共耗时$\widetilde{O}\left( {\left| {\mathcal{D}}^{\left( \tau \right) }\right| }^{\mathrm{w}\left( \widehat{Q}\right) }\right)$。需注意两次重置间数据库规模变化不超过2倍，因为仅对${\mathcal{D}}^{\left( \tau \right) }$施加了原始流中的$\frac{1}{2}\left| {\mathcal{D}}^{\left( \tau \right) }\right|$次更新。在两种极端情况下，这些更新要么全是插入操作，要么全是删除操作。

Consider the original update stream of length $N$ . Initially, $\left| {\mathcal{D}}^{\left( 0\right) }\right|  = 0$ . While using the above scheme to process the stream,suppose that we end up performing $k$ resets in total at time stamps ${\tau }_{1}\overset{\text{ def }}{ = }0 < {\tau }_{2} < \cdots  < {\tau }_{k} \leq  N$ . WLOG assume that ${\tau }_{k} = N$ . From the above construction,for any $i \in  \left\lbrack  {k - 1}\right\rbrack$ ,we have the following:

考虑长度为$N$的原始更新流。初始时$\left| {\mathcal{D}}^{\left( 0\right) }\right|  = 0$。采用上述方案处理流时，假设最终在时间戳${\tau }_{1}\overset{\text{ def }}{ = }0 < {\tau }_{2} < \cdots  < {\tau }_{k} \leq  N$处共执行$k$次重置。不失一般性，设${\tau }_{k} = N$。根据上述构造，对任意$i \in  \left\lbrack  {k - 1}\right\rbrack$有以下关系：

$$
{\tau }_{i + 1} - {\tau }_{i} = \frac{1}{2}\left| {\mathcal{D}}^{\left( {\tau }_{i}\right) }\right|  \tag{62}
$$

$$
\frac{1}{2}\left| {\mathcal{D}}^{\left( {\tau }_{i}\right) }\right|  \leq  \left| {\mathcal{D}}^{\left( \tau \right) }\right|  \leq  \frac{3}{2}\left| {\mathcal{D}}^{\left( {\tau }_{i}\right) }\right| ,\;\forall \tau  \in  \left\lbrack  {{\tau }_{i},{\tau }_{i + 1}}\right)  \tag{63}
$$

Eq. (63) holds because between ${\tau }_{i}$ and ${\tau }_{i + 1}$ ,we are making $\frac{1}{2}\left| {\mathcal{D}}^{\left( {\tau }_{i}\right) }\right|$ updates to ${\mathcal{D}}^{\left( {\tau }_{i}\right) }$ ,which in the two extreme cases are either all inserts or all deletes. As noted before,from the beginning of the ${\tau }_{i}$ -reset until the beginning of the ${\tau }_{i + 1}$ - reset,we process $\frac{3}{2}\left| {\mathcal{D}}^{\left( {\tau }_{i}\right) }\right|$ updates,which together take time $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}\left( {\left| {\mathcal{D}}^{\left( {\tau }_{i}\right) }\right| }^{\mathrm{w}\left( \widehat{Q}\right) }\right)$ ,thanks to Lemma 7.1. To bound the total runtime,we sum up over $i \in  \left\lbrack  {k - 1}\right\rbrack$ : (We don’t actually need to perform the last reset at time ${\tau }_{k} = N$ since there are no subsequent updates anyhow.)

式(63)成立是因为在${\tau }_{i}$与${\tau }_{i + 1}$之间，我们对${\mathcal{D}}^{\left( {\tau }_{i}\right) }$执行了$\frac{1}{2}\left| {\mathcal{D}}^{\left( {\tau }_{i}\right) }\right|$次更新，在两种极端情况下这些更新要么全为插入要么全为删除。如前所述，从第${\tau }_{i}$次重置开始到第${\tau }_{i + 1}$次重置开始期间，处理了$\frac{3}{2}\left| {\mathcal{D}}^{\left( {\tau }_{i}\right) }\right|$次更新，根据引理7.1共耗时$\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}\left( {\left| {\mathcal{D}}^{\left( {\tau }_{i}\right) }\right| }^{\mathrm{w}\left( \widehat{Q}\right) }\right)$。为求总运行时间上界，我们对$i \in  \left\lbrack  {k - 1}\right\rbrack$求和：（实际上无需在${\tau }_{k} = N$时刻执行最后一次重置，因为后续已无更新。）

$$
\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  {k - 1}\right\rbrack  }}{\left| {\mathcal{D}}^{\left( {\tau }_{i}\right) }\right| }^{\mathrm{w}\left( \widehat{Q}\right) } = \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  {k - 1}\right\rbrack  }}\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{\tau  \in  \left\lbrack  {{\tau }_{i},{\tau }_{i + 1}}\right) }}\frac{1}{{\tau }_{i + 1} - {\tau }_{i}} \cdot  {\left| {\mathcal{D}}^{\left( {\tau }_{i}\right) }\right| }^{\mathrm{w}\left( \widehat{Q}\right) } \tag{64}
$$

$$
 = 2 \cdot  \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  {k - 1}\right\rbrack  }}\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{\tau  \in  \left\lbrack  {{\tau }_{i},{\tau }_{i + 1}}\right) }}{\left| {\mathcal{D}}^{\left( {\tau }_{i}\right) }\right| }^{w\left( \widehat{Q}\right)  - 1} \tag{65}
$$

$$
 \leq  {2}^{\mathrm{w}\left( \widehat{Q}\right) } \cdot  \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  {k - 1}\right\rbrack  }}\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{\tau  \in  \left\lbrack  {{\tau }_{i},{\tau }_{i + 1}}\right) }}{\left| {\mathcal{D}}^{\left( \tau \right) }\right| }^{\mathrm{w}\left( \widehat{Q}\right)  - 1} \tag{66}
$$

Insert-Only versus Insert-Delete in Dynamic Query Evaluation

动态查询评估中的纯插入与插入-删除对比

$$
 = O\left( 1\right)  \cdot  \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{\tau  \in  \left\lbrack  N\right\rbrack  }}{\left| {\mathcal{D}}^{\left( \tau \right) }\right| }^{\mathrm{w}\left( \widehat{Q}\right)  - 1} \tag{67}
$$

${}^{8}$ For simplicity,we assume that $\left| {\mathcal{D}}^{\left( \tau \right) }\right|$ is a positive even integer.

${}^{8}$ 为简化分析，假定$\left| {\mathcal{D}}^{\left( \tau \right) }\right|$为正偶数。

Equality (65) follows from Eq. (62), while inequality (66) follows from Eq. (63). In particular, the total time needed to process all $N$ updates in the stream is $\widetilde{O}\left( {\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{\tau  \in  \left\lbrack  N\right\rbrack  }}{\left| {\mathcal{D}}^{\left( \tau \right) }\right| }^{\mathrm{w}\left( \widetilde{Q}\right)  - 1}}\right)$ . This implies that the amortized time per update is $\widetilde{O}\left( {\left| {\mathcal{D}}^{\left( \tau \right) }\right| }^{\mathrm{w}\left( \widehat{Q}\right)  - 1}\right)$ ,as desired.

等式(65)由式(62)推导得出，不等式(66)则源于式(63)。特别地，处理流中所有$N$更新所需的总时间为$\widetilde{O}\left( {\mathop{\sum }\limits_{{\tau  \in  \left\lbrack  N\right\rbrack  }}{\left| {\mathcal{D}}^{\left( \tau \right) }\right| }^{\mathrm{w}\left( \widetilde{Q}\right)  - 1}}\right)$。这意味着每次更新的摊分时间为$\widetilde{O}\left( {\left| {\mathcal{D}}^{\left( \tau \right) }\right| }^{\mathrm{w}\left( \widehat{Q}\right)  - 1}\right)$，符合预期。

### E.4 Lower Bound for ${\mathrm{{IVM}}}_{\delta }^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$

### E.4 ${\mathrm{{IVM}}}_{\delta }^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$的下界证明

The goal of this section is to prove Theorem 4.5,which gives a lower bound on the ${\mathrm{{IVM}}}_{\delta }^{ \pm  }$ update time of a query $Q$ in terms of a lower bound on the static evaluation time for any component ${\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }$ in its multivariate extension. Specifically, we will prove the following stronger lower bound:

本节旨在证明定理4.5，该定理通过多元扩展中任意组件${\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }$静态评估时间的下界，给出了查询$Q$的${\mathrm{{IVM}}}_{\delta }^{ \pm  }$更新时间下界。具体而言，我们将证明以下更强下界：

Lemma E.9. Let $Q$ be a query and ${\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }$ any component of its multivariate extension. For any constant $\gamma  > 0,{IV}{M}_{\delta }^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$ cannot be solved with amortized update time $\widetilde{O}\left( {N}^{\omega \left( {\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }\right)  - 1 - \gamma }\right)$ .

引理E.9。设$Q$为查询，${\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }$为其多元扩展中的任意组件。对于任何常数$\gamma  > 0,{IV}{M}_{\delta }^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$，该问题无法在$\widetilde{O}\left( {N}^{\omega \left( {\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }\right)  - 1 - \gamma }\right)$的摊分更新时间下解决。

The above lemma immediately implies Theorem 4.5 since the number of single-tuple updates $N$ is larger than the size of the database $\left| \mathcal{D}\right|$ ,and could be unboundedly larger. In order to prove Lemma E.9,we give a reduction from the static evaluation of ${\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }$ to the ${\mathrm{{IVM}}}_{\delta }^{ \pm  }$ problem for $Q$ . Example 7.3 introduces the main idea behind this reduction.

由于单次元组更新次数$N$大于数据库$\left| \mathcal{D}\right|$的规模，且可能无限更大，该引理直接推导出定理4.5。为证明引理E.9，我们将${\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }$的静态评估问题归约到$Q$的${\mathrm{{IVM}}}_{\delta }^{ \pm  }$问题。示例7.3阐述了该归约的核心思想。

For the rest of this section,we fix a query $Q$ ,its time extension $\bar{Q}$ (Definition 6.2),and one component ${\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }$ in its multivariate extension $\widehat{Q}$ (Definition 3.3). Moreover,we fix an arbitrary database instance ${\widehat{\mathcal{D}}}_{\sigma }$ for ${\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }$ . Algorithm 3 describes our reduction from $\operatorname{Eval}\left\lbrack  {\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }\right\rbrack$ to ${\mathrm{{IVM}}}_{\delta }^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$ . Theorem D. 8 already gives a reduction from $\operatorname{Eval}\left\lbrack  {\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }\right\rbrack$ to the (static) evaluation of $\bar{Q}$ over some database instance $\overline{\mathcal{D}}\overset{\text{ def }}{ = }\mathrm{{IV}}\left( {\widehat{\mathcal{D}}}_{\sigma }\right)$ that is given by Definition D. 6 and satisfies the following properties:

在本节后续内容中，我们固定查询$Q$、其时态扩展$\bar{Q}$（定义6.2）及其多元扩展$\widehat{Q}$（定义3.3）中的组件${\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }$。此外，我们为${\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }$指定任意数据库实例${\widehat{\mathcal{D}}}_{\sigma }$。算法3描述了从$\operatorname{Eval}\left\lbrack  {\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }\right\rbrack$到${\mathrm{{IVM}}}_{\delta }^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$的归约过程。定理D.8已给出从$\operatorname{Eval}\left\lbrack  {\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }\right\rbrack$到特定数据库实例$\overline{\mathcal{D}}\overset{\text{ def }}{ = }\mathrm{{IV}}\left( {\widehat{\mathcal{D}}}_{\sigma }\right)$上$\bar{Q}$（静态）评估的归约，该实例由定义D.6给出并满足以下特性：

$$
\left| \overline{\mathcal{D}}\right|  = \left| {\widehat{\mathcal{D}}}_{\sigma }\right| 
$$

$$
{H}_{k}\left( {\bar{Q}\left( \overline{\mathcal{D}}\right) }\right)  = {\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }\left( {\widehat{\mathcal{D}}}_{\sigma }\right) 
$$

$$
\left| {\bar{Q}\left( \overline{\mathcal{D}}\right) }\right|  = \left| {{\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }\left( {\widehat{\mathcal{D}}}_{\sigma }\right) }\right| 
$$

(Recall the definition of ${H}_{k}$ from Theorem D.8.) The above properties follow from Proposition D.7 and Theorem D.8, which also imply that $\overline{\mathcal{D}}$ can be constructed in linear time in $\left| {\widehat{\mathcal{D}}}_{\sigma }\right|$ ,and ${H}_{k}\left( {\bar{Q}\left( \overline{\mathcal{D}}\right) }\right)$ can be computed in linear time in $\left| {\bar{Q}\left( \overline{\mathcal{D}}\right) }\right|  = \left| {{\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }\left( {\overline{\mathcal{D}}}_{\sigma }\right) }\right|$ . It remains to show how to reduce the (static) evaluation of $\bar{Q}$ over $\overline{\mathcal{D}}$ into ${\operatorname{IVM}}_{\delta }^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$ . This is shown by the following Lemma.

（回顾定理D.8中${H}_{k}$的定义。）上述性质源自命题D.7和定理D.8，这两个结论同时表明：$\overline{\mathcal{D}}$可在$\left| {\widehat{\mathcal{D}}}_{\sigma }\right|$的线性时间内构建，且${H}_{k}\left( {\bar{Q}\left( \overline{\mathcal{D}}\right) }\right)$可在$\left| {\bar{Q}\left( \overline{\mathcal{D}}\right) }\right|  = \left| {{\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }\left( {\overline{\mathcal{D}}}_{\sigma }\right) }\right|$的线性时间内计算得出。仍需说明如何将$\bar{Q}$在$\overline{\mathcal{D}}$上的（静态）求值问题归约为${\operatorname{IVM}}_{\delta }^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$。下述引理将给出证明。

LEMMA E.10. If ${IV}{M}_{\delta }^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$ can be solved with $f\left( N\right)$ amortized update time for any update stream of length $N$ and some function $f$ ,then Eval $\left\lbrack  \bar{Q}\right\rbrack$ can be solved in $\widetilde{O}\left( {\left| \overline{\mathcal{D}}\right|  \cdot  f\left( {2\left| \overline{\mathcal{D}}\right| }\right)  + \left| {\bar{Q}\left( \overline{\mathcal{D}}\right) }\right| }\right)$ time for any database $\overline{\mathcal{D}}$ .

引理E.10。若对于任意长度为$N$的更新流及某个函数$f$，${IV}{M}_{\delta }^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$能以$f\left( N\right)$的摊还更新时间求解，则对于任意数据库$\overline{\mathcal{D}}$，求值问题$\left\lbrack  \bar{Q}\right\rbrack$可在$\widetilde{O}\left( {\left| \overline{\mathcal{D}}\right|  \cdot  f\left( {2\left| \overline{\mathcal{D}}\right| }\right)  + \left| {\bar{Q}\left( \overline{\mathcal{D}}\right) }\right| }\right)$时间内求解。

Assuming Lemma E. 10 holds,Theorem 4.5 follows by taking $f\left( N\right)  = {N}^{\omega \left( {\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }\right)  - 1 - \gamma }$ for some constant $\gamma  > 0$ . (Recall Definition 3.2.) By Lemma E.10,this would lead into an algorithm for Eval $\left\lbrack  \bar{Q}\right\rbrack$ with runtime $\widetilde{O}\left( {{\left| \overline{\mathcal{D}}\right| }^{\omega \left( {\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }\right)  - \gamma } + \left| {\bar{Q}\left( \overline{\mathcal{D}}\right) }\right| }\right)$ , and by extension,an algorithm for $\operatorname{Eval}\left\lbrack  {\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }\right\rbrack$ with runtime $\widetilde{O}\left( {{\left| {\widehat{\mathcal{D}}}_{\sigma }\right| }^{\omega \left( {\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }\right)  - \gamma } + \left| {{\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }\left( {\widehat{\mathcal{D}}}_{\sigma }\right) }\right| }\right)$ . This would contradict the definition of $\omega \left( {\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }\right)$ ,thus proving that our choice of $f\left( N\right)$ is not possible. Next,we prove Lemma E.10.

假设引理E.10成立，取某个常数$\gamma  > 0$对应的$f\left( N\right)  = {N}^{\omega \left( {\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }\right)  - 1 - \gamma }$即可推导出定理4.5。（参见定义3.2。）根据引理E.10，这将产生一个运行时为$\widetilde{O}\left( {{\left| \overline{\mathcal{D}}\right| }^{\omega \left( {\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }\right)  - \gamma } + \left| {\bar{Q}\left( \overline{\mathcal{D}}\right) }\right| }\right)$的求值算法$\left\lbrack  \bar{Q}\right\rbrack$，进而推演出运行时为$\widetilde{O}\left( {{\left| {\widehat{\mathcal{D}}}_{\sigma }\right| }^{\omega \left( {\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }\right)  - \gamma } + \left| {{\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }\left( {\widehat{\mathcal{D}}}_{\sigma }\right) }\right| }\right)$的$\operatorname{Eval}\left\lbrack  {\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }\right\rbrack$算法。该结果与$\omega \left( {\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }\right)$的定义相矛盾，从而证明我们选择的$f\left( N\right)$不可行。接下来我们将证明引理E.10。

Proof of Lemma E.10. Following Algorithm 3,the reduction works as follows. We collect a list $L$ of all pairs $\left( {\alpha ,\left( {\left\lbrack  {\alpha ,\beta }\right\rbrack  ,{\mathbf{t}}_{j}}\right) }\right)$ and $\left( {\beta ,\left( {\left\lbrack  {\alpha ,\beta }\right\rbrack  ,{\mathbf{t}}_{j}}\right) }\right)$ for every tuple $\left( {\left\lbrack  {\alpha ,\beta }\right\rbrack  ,{\mathbf{t}}_{j}}\right)$ that occurs in any relation ${\bar{R}}_{j}$ in $\overline{\mathcal{D}}$ . We go through pairs $\left( {\tau ,\left( {\left\lbrack  {\alpha ,\beta }\right\rbrack  ,{\mathbf{t}}_{j}}\right) }\right)$ in the list $L$ in order of $\tau$ . If $\tau  = \alpha$ ,then we insert ${\mathbf{t}}_{j}$ into ${R}_{j}$ . Otherwise,we delete ${\mathbf{t}}_{j}$ from ${R}_{j}$ . In total,we have $2\left| \overline{\mathcal{D}}\right|$ updates and the amortized time per update is $f\left( {2\left| \overline{\mathcal{D}}\right| }\right)$ .

引理E.10的证明。遵循算法3，归约过程如下：我们收集所有出现在$\overline{\mathcal{D}}$中任意关系${\bar{R}}_{j}$内的元组$\left( {\left\lbrack  {\alpha ,\beta }\right\rbrack  ,{\mathbf{t}}_{j}}\right)$对应的$\left( {\alpha ,\left( {\left\lbrack  {\alpha ,\beta }\right\rbrack  ,{\mathbf{t}}_{j}}\right) }\right)$与$\left( {\beta ,\left( {\left\lbrack  {\alpha ,\beta }\right\rbrack  ,{\mathbf{t}}_{j}}\right) }\right)$对列表$L$。按$\tau$顺序遍历列表$L$中的$\left( {\tau ,\left( {\left\lbrack  {\alpha ,\beta }\right\rbrack  ,{\mathbf{t}}_{j}}\right) }\right)$对。若$\tau  = \alpha$，则将${\mathbf{t}}_{j}$插入${R}_{j}$；否则从${R}_{j}$中删除${\mathbf{t}}_{j}$。总共进行$2\left| \overline{\mathcal{D}}\right|$次更新，每次更新的摊还时间为$f\left( {2\left| \overline{\mathcal{D}}\right| }\right)$。

Algorithm 3 Reduction from $\operatorname{Eval}\left\lbrack  {\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }\right\rbrack$ to ${\mathrm{{IVM}}}_{\delta }^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$

算法3 从$\operatorname{Eval}\left\lbrack  {\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }\right\rbrack$到${\mathrm{{IVM}}}_{\delta }^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$的归约

---

Inputs

输入

	- A query ${\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }$ - Equation (6)

	- 查询${\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }$ - 公式(6)

	- An arbitrary database instance ${\widehat{\mathcal{D}}}_{\sigma }$ for ${\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }$

	- 为${\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }$构造的任意数据库实例${\widehat{\mathcal{D}}}_{\sigma }$

$\mathbf{{Algorithm}}$

	- Construct $\bar{Q}$ - Equation (13)

	- 构造$\bar{Q}$ - 公式(13)

	- $\overline{\mathcal{D}} \leftarrow  \operatorname{IV}\left( {\widehat{\mathcal{D}}}_{\sigma }\right)$ - Definition D.6

	- $\overline{\mathcal{D}} \leftarrow  \operatorname{IV}\left( {\widehat{\mathcal{D}}}_{\sigma }\right)$ - 定义D.6

	- Initialize $\mathcal{D}\overset{\text{ def }}{ = }{\left( {R}_{j}\right) }_{j \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack  }$ to be empty $\vartriangleright$ Database for $Q$

	- 初始化$\mathcal{D}\overset{\text{ def }}{ = }{\left( {R}_{j}\right) }_{j \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack  }$为空$\vartriangleright$ 用于$Q$的数据库

	- $\vartriangleright$ Sort tuples in $\overline{\mathcal{D}}$ by their end points

	- $\vartriangleright$ 按端点对$\overline{\mathcal{D}}$中的元组排序

											$L \leftarrow  \left\{  {\left( {\alpha ,\left( {\left\lbrack  {\alpha ,\beta }\right\rbrack  ,{t}_{j}}\right) }\right)  \mid  \left( {\left\lbrack  {\alpha ,\beta }\right\rbrack  ,{t}_{j}}\right)  \in  {\bar{R}}_{j}\text{ for some }j \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack  }\right\}$

													$\cup  \left\{  {\left( {\beta ,\left( {\left\lbrack  {\alpha ,\beta }\right\rbrack  ,{t}_{j}}\right) }\right)  \mid  \left( {\left\lbrack  {\alpha ,\beta }\right\rbrack  ,{t}_{j}}\right)  \in  {\bar{R}}_{j}\text{ for some }j \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack  }\right\}$

	- $\Delta  \leftarrow  \varnothing$

	- For each $\left( {\tau ,\left( {\left\lbrack  {\alpha ,\beta }\right\rbrack  ,{t}_{j}}\right) }\right)  \in  L$ ordered by $\tau$ :

	- 对于每个按$\tau$排序的$\left( {\tau ,\left( {\left\lbrack  {\alpha ,\beta }\right\rbrack  ,{t}_{j}}\right) }\right)  \in  L$：

			- if $\tau  = \alpha$ ,

			- 若$\tau  = \alpha$，

						insert ${t}_{j}$ into ${R}_{j}$

						将${t}_{j}$插入${R}_{j}$

			- Otherwise,

			- 否则，

						delete ${\mathbf{t}}_{j}$ from ${R}_{j}$

						从${R}_{j}$中删除${\mathbf{t}}_{j}$

			$- \Delta  \leftarrow  \Delta  \cup  \left( {\{ \tau \}  \times  {\delta }_{\tau }Q\left( \mathcal{D}\right) }\right) \; \vartriangleright$ Invoke constant-delay enumeration from ${\operatorname{IVM}}_{\delta }^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$ to get ${\delta }_{\tau }Q\left( \mathcal{D}\right)$

			$- \Delta  \leftarrow  \Delta  \cup  \left( {\{ \tau \}  \times  {\delta }_{\tau }Q\left( \mathcal{D}\right) }\right) \; \vartriangleright$ 调用${\operatorname{IVM}}_{\delta }^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$的恒定延迟枚举获取${\delta }_{\tau }Q\left( \mathcal{D}\right)$

	- $\bar{Q}\left( \overline{\mathcal{D}}\right)  \leftarrow  \left\{  {\left( {\left\lbrack  {\tau ,{\tau }^{\prime }}\right\rbrack  ,\mathbf{t}}\right)  \mid  \left( {\tau , + Q\left( \mathbf{t}\right) }\right)  \in  \Delta  \land  \left( {{\tau }^{\prime }, - Q\left( \mathbf{t}\right) }\right)  \in  \Delta }\right\}$

	- return ${\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }\left( {\widehat{\mathcal{D}}}_{\sigma }\right)  = {H}_{k}\left( {\bar{Q}\left( \overline{\mathcal{D}}\right) }\right)$ - Theorem D. 8

	- 返回${\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }\left( {\widehat{\mathcal{D}}}_{\sigma }\right)  = {H}_{k}\left( {\bar{Q}\left( \overline{\mathcal{D}}\right) }\right)$ - 定理D.8

After every insert or delete,we invoke the constant-delay enumeration procedure for ${\mathrm{{IVM}}}_{\delta }^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$ in order to retrieve ${\delta }_{\tau }Q\left( \mathcal{D}\right)$ ,i.e. the output tuples of $Q$ that are inserted or deleted due to the insert or delete in ${R}_{j}$ . Whenever constant-delay enumeration reports a delete $- Q\left( t\right)$ at time ${\tau }^{\prime }$ where the the same tuple $t$ was reportedly inserted $+ Q\left( t\right)$ at a previous time $\tau$ ,we report the tuple $\left( {\left\lbrack  {\tau ,{\tau }^{\prime }}\right\rbrack  ,t}\right)$ as an output tuple of $\bar{Q}$ . The overall time we spend on enumeration is $\widetilde{O}\left( \left| {\bar{Q}\left( \overline{\mathcal{D}}\right) }\right| \right)$ .

在每次插入或删除操作后，我们调用${\mathrm{{IVM}}}_{\delta }^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$的恒定延迟枚举程序以获取${\delta }_{\tau }Q\left( \mathcal{D}\right)$，即由于${R}_{j}$中的插入或删除操作导致$Q$中被插入或删除的输出元组。当恒定延迟枚举报告在时间${\tau }^{\prime }$删除$- Q\left( t\right)$，而同一元组$t$曾在先前时间$\tau$被报告为插入$+ Q\left( t\right)$时，我们将该元组$\left( {\left\lbrack  {\tau ,{\tau }^{\prime }}\right\rbrack  ,t}\right)$作为$\bar{Q}$的输出元组上报。我们在枚举阶段消耗的总时间为$\widetilde{O}\left( \left| {\bar{Q}\left( \overline{\mathcal{D}}\right) }\right| \right)$。

We conclude this section with the following remark about the prospects of extending Theorem 4.5 from ${\mathrm{{IVM}}}_{\delta }^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$ to ${\operatorname{IVM}}^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$ .

本节最后，我们就将定理4.5从${\mathrm{{IVM}}}_{\delta }^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$推广到${\operatorname{IVM}}^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$的前景作如下说明。

Remark E.6. It is not clear how to extend the proof of Theorem 4.5,which holds for the ${\mathrm{{IVM}}}_{\delta }^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$ problem,so that it also works for the ${\mathrm{{IVM}}}^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$ problem. The main issue lies in the enumeration step. In particular,suppose that the IVM oracle that we have in the proof is for ${\operatorname{IVM}}^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$ . Per the above reduction,we invoke constant-delay enumeration after every update in $Q$ in order to extract the output of $\bar{Q}$ . However,now the oracle enumerates the full output of $Q$ after every update. As a result,the same output tuple of $Q$ might be reported many times throughout its lifetime. This in turn would prevent us from bounding the total enumeration time with $\widetilde{O}\left( \left| {\widetilde{Q}\left( \overline{\mathcal{D}}\right) }\right| \right)$ ,as we did in the original proof. Proving an analog of Theorem 4.5 for ${\operatorname{IVM}}^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$ remains an open problem.

备注E.6。目前尚不清楚如何将针对${\mathrm{{IVM}}}_{\delta }^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$问题成立的定理4.5证明进行扩展，使其同样适用于${\mathrm{{IVM}}}^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$问题。核心难点在于枚举步骤。具体而言，假设证明中使用的IVM预言机针对的是${\operatorname{IVM}}^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$。根据上述归约方法，我们在$Q$每次更新后调用恒定延迟枚举以提取$\bar{Q}$的输出。然而现在该预言机会在每次更新后枚举$Q$的完整输出，这可能导致$Q$的同一输出元组在其生命周期内被多次上报，从而无法像原证明那样用$\widetilde{O}\left( \left| {\widetilde{Q}\left( \overline{\mathcal{D}}\right) }\right| \right)$来界定总枚举时间。针对${\operatorname{IVM}}^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$证明类似定理4.5的命题仍是一个未解难题。
