### F.1 Proof of Proposition F. 1

### F.1 命题F.1的证明

Proposition F.1. A query is hierarchical if and only if its multivariate extension is acyclic.

命题F.1：当且仅当查询的多变量扩展是无环的，该查询才是层次化的。

It follows from Proposition F. 2 that the multivariate extension $\widehat{Q}$ of any non-hierarchical query $Q$ is not acyclic. This is because acyclic queries have a fractional hypertree width of 1 . It remains to prove:

根据命题F.2可推知，任何非层次化查询$Q$的多变量扩展$\widehat{Q}$必然有环。这是因为无环查询的分式超树宽度为1。尚待证明：

LEMMA F.6. The multivariate extension of any hierarchical query is acyclic.

引理F.6：任何层次化查询的多变量扩展都是无环的。

First, we introduce some simple notions. Consider a query

首先引入若干基本概念。考虑一个查询

$$
Q = {R}_{1}\left( {\mathbf{X}}_{1}\right)  \land  \cdots  \land  {R}_{k}\left( {\mathbf{X}}_{k}\right) .
$$

A variable $X$ is called root variable if it is contained in every atom,i.e., $X \in  \mathop{\bigcap }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack  }}{X}_{i}$ . It is a leaf variable if it is contained in exactly one atom,i.e, $X \in  {X}_{i}$ for some $i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack$ and $X \notin  {X}_{j}$ for all $j \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack   \smallsetminus  \{ i\}$ . We say that an atom ${R}_{i}\left( {\mathbf{X}}_{i}\right)$ is subsumed by another atom ${R}_{j}\left( {\mathbf{X}}_{j}\right)$ if ${\mathbf{X}}_{i} \subseteq  {\mathbf{X}}_{j}$ . A query ${Q}^{\prime }$ results from $Q$ by the elimination of variable $X$ if it is of the form

若变量$X$存在于每个原子中（即$X \in  \mathop{\bigcap }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack  }}{X}_{i}$），则称其根变量；若仅存在于单个原子中（即存在$i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack$使$X \in  {X}_{i}$成立，且对所有$j \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack   \smallsetminus  \{ i\}$有$X \notin  {X}_{j}$），则称其叶变量。当${\mathbf{X}}_{i} \subseteq  {\mathbf{X}}_{j}$时，称原子${R}_{i}\left( {\mathbf{X}}_{i}\right)$被原子${R}_{j}\left( {\mathbf{X}}_{j}\right)$包含。若查询$Q$经消除变量$X$后形如以下形式，则称其派生查询${Q}^{\prime }$：

$$
{Q}^{\prime } = {R}_{1}\left( {{X}_{1}\smallsetminus \{ X\} }\right)  \land  \cdots  \land  {R}_{k}\left( {{X}_{k}\smallsetminus \{ X\} }\right) .
$$

It results by the elimination of an atom ${R}_{i}\left( {X}_{i}\right)$ if it has the form

若查询经消除原子${R}_{i}\left( {X}_{i}\right)$后形如以下形式，则称其派生查询：

$$
{Q}^{\prime } = {R}_{1}\left( {\mathbf{X}}_{1}\right)  \land  \cdots  \land  {R}_{i - 1}\left( {\mathbf{X}}_{i - 1}\right)  \land  {R}_{i + 1}\left( {\mathbf{X}}_{i + 1}\right)  \land  \cdots  \land  {R}_{k}\left( {\mathbf{X}}_{k}\right) .
$$

Observe that the elimination of a leaf variable and the elimination of a subsumed atom are exactly the two steps of the GYO-reduction [46]. The following claim follows directly from the definition of acyclic queries and the correctness of the GYO-reduction:

注意：叶变量消除与被包含原子消除正是GYO归约[46]的两个步骤。根据无环查询的定义及GYO归约的正确性，可直接得出：

CLAIM F.1. Let $Q$ and ${Q}^{\prime }$ be join queries such that ${Q}^{\prime }$ results from $Q$ by the elimination of a root variable,a leaf variable, or a subsumed atom. The query $Q$ is acyclic if and only if the query ${Q}^{\prime }$ is acyclic.

断言F.1：设$Q$和${Q}^{\prime }$为连接查询，若${Q}^{\prime }$是通过消除根变量、叶变量或被包含原子从$Q$派生而来，则$Q$是无环的当且仅当${Q}^{\prime }$是无环的。

Proof of CLAIM F.1. We distinguish on how ${Q}^{\prime }$ is obtained from $Q$ .

断言F.1的证明：根据${Q}^{\prime }$从$Q$派生的方式分类讨论。

${Q}^{\prime }$ is obtained by the elimination of a leaf variable or a subsumed atom. In this case,the claim follows directly from the correctness of the GYO-reduction [46], which says that a query is acyclic if and only if we obtain the trivial query consisting of a single atom with empty schema by repeatedly applying leaf variable and subsumed atom elimination. Indeed, $Q$ is GYO-reducible if and only if ${Q}^{\prime }$ is GYO-reducible.

${Q}^{\prime }$通过消除叶变量或被包含原子获得。此时根据GYO归约[46]的正确性可直接得证——该归约指出，当且仅当通过反复应用叶变量消除和被包含原子消除能得到含空模式的单原子平凡查询时，原查询才是无环的。事实上，$Q$可GYO归约当且仅当${Q}^{\prime }$可GYO归约。

${Q}^{\prime }$ is obtained by root variable elimination. Assume that ${Q}^{\prime }$ is obtained from $Q$ by removing a root variable $X$ . Consider a join tree $\mathcal{T}$ for $Q$ ,and let ${\mathcal{T}}^{\prime }$ be the tree obtained by removing the variable $X$ from every node in $\mathcal{T}$ . There is a one-to-one mapping between the nodes in ${\mathcal{T}}^{\prime }$ and the atoms in ${Q}^{\prime }$ . Moreover,removing $X$ from all nodes in $\mathcal{T}$ does not affect the connectivity property in the join tree. Hence, ${\mathcal{T}}^{\prime }$ must be a join tree for ${Q}^{\prime }$ .

${Q}^{\prime }$通过根变量消除获得。假设${Q}^{\prime }$是通过从$Q$中移除根变量$X$得到的。考虑$Q$的连接树$\mathcal{T}$，设${\mathcal{T}}^{\prime }$为从$\mathcal{T}$每个节点中移除变量$X$后得到的树。${\mathcal{T}}^{\prime }$中的节点与${Q}^{\prime }$中的原子存在一一对应关系。此外，从$\mathcal{T}$所有节点中移除$X$不会影响连接树的连通性。因此，${\mathcal{T}}^{\prime }$必然是${Q}^{\prime }$的连接树。

Analogously,any join tree for ${Q}^{\prime }$ turns into a join tree for $Q$ by adding the same variable $X$ to all nodes. We conclude that $Q$ is acyclic if and only if ${Q}^{\prime }$ is acyclic.

同理，任何${Q}^{\prime }$的连接树通过向所有节点添加相同变量$X$都可转换为$Q$的连接树。由此可得：$Q$是无环的当且仅当${Q}^{\prime }$是无环的。

We are ready to prove Lemma F.6. Consider a hierarchical query $Q = {R}_{1}\left( {X}_{1}\right)  \land  \cdots  \land  {R}_{k}\left( {X}_{k}\right)$ and its multivariate extension $\widehat{Q} = \mathop{\bigvee }\limits_{{\sigma  \in  {\sum }_{k}}}{\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }$ (Eq (7)). We show that each component ${\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }$ of $\widehat{Q}$ is acyclic. WLOG we only consider the component for $\mathbf{\sigma } = \left( {1,\ldots ,k}\right)$ :

现在证明引理F.6。考虑分层查询$Q = {R}_{1}\left( {X}_{1}\right)  \land  \cdots  \land  {R}_{k}\left( {X}_{k}\right)$及其多元扩展$\widehat{Q} = \mathop{\bigvee }\limits_{{\sigma  \in  {\sum }_{k}}}{\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }$（公式7）。我们将证明$\widehat{Q}$的每个分量${\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }$都是无环的。不失一般性，仅考察$\mathbf{\sigma } = \left( {1,\ldots ,k}\right)$对应的分量：

$$
{\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma } = {\widehat{R}}_{1}\left( {{Z}_{1}{X}_{1}}\right)  \land  {\widehat{R}}_{2}\left( {{Z}_{1},{Z}_{2},{X}_{2}}\right)  \land  \cdots  \land  {\widehat{R}}_{k}\left( {{Z}_{1},\ldots ,{Z}_{k},{X}_{k}}\right) .
$$

The other components are symmetric.

其余分量具有对称性。

The proof is by induction on the number $k$ of atoms in ${\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }$ .

采用归纳法对${\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }$中原子数量$k$进行证明。

Base Case. Assume that $k = 1$ ,that is, ${\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }$ consists of a single atom. In this case,the query is obviously acyclic.

基础情形。设$k = 1$，即${\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }$仅含单个原子。此时查询显然是无环的。

Induction Step. Assume now that ${\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }$ has $k > 1$ atoms. The variable ${Z}_{1}$ is a root variable. We eliminate it and obtain

归纳步骤。设${\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }$含$k > 1$个原子。变量${Z}_{1}$是根变量。消除该变量后得到

$$
{\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }^{\prime } = {\widehat{R}}_{1}^{\prime }\left( {\mathbf{X}}_{1}\right)  \land  {\widehat{R}}_{2}^{\prime }\left( {{Z}_{2},{\mathbf{X}}_{2}}\right)  \land  \cdots  \land  {\widehat{R}}_{k}^{\prime }\left( {{Z}_{2},\ldots ,{Z}_{k},{\mathbf{X}}_{k}}\right) .
$$

The atom ${\widehat{R}}_{1}^{\prime }\left( {X}_{1}\right)$ has now the same schema as the atom ${R}_{1}\left( {X}_{1}\right)$ in the original hierarchical query $Q$ . We remove from ${\widehat{R}}_{1}^{\prime }\left( {X}_{1}\right)$ all variables that are leaves in ${\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }^{\prime }$ and obtain the atom ${\widehat{R}}_{1}^{\prime \prime }\left( {X}_{1}^{\prime }\right)$ . It follows from the definition of hierarchical queries that the schema ${X}_{1}^{\prime }$ must be subsumed by the schema of one of the atoms ${\widehat{R}}_{2}^{\prime }\left( {{Z}_{2},{X}_{2}}\right) ,\ldots ,{\widehat{R}}_{k}^{\prime }\left( {{Z}_{2},\ldots ,{Z}_{k},{X}_{k}}\right)$ . We remove the atom ${\widehat{R}}_{1}^{\prime \prime }\left( {\mathbf{X}}_{1}^{\prime }\right)$ from ${\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }^{\prime }$ and obtain the query

原子${\widehat{R}}_{1}^{\prime }\left( {X}_{1}\right)$现在与原始分层查询$Q$中的原子${R}_{1}\left( {X}_{1}\right)$具有相同模式。从${\widehat{R}}_{1}^{\prime }\left( {X}_{1}\right)$中移除${\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }^{\prime }$中的所有叶变量，得到原子${\widehat{R}}_{1}^{\prime \prime }\left( {X}_{1}^{\prime }\right)$。根据分层查询的定义，模式${X}_{1}^{\prime }$必须被某个原子${\widehat{R}}_{2}^{\prime }\left( {{Z}_{2},{X}_{2}}\right) ,\ldots ,{\widehat{R}}_{k}^{\prime }\left( {{Z}_{2},\ldots ,{Z}_{k},{X}_{k}}\right)$的模式包含。从${\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }^{\prime }$中移除原子${\widehat{R}}_{1}^{\prime \prime }\left( {\mathbf{X}}_{1}^{\prime }\right)$后得到查询

$$
{\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }^{\prime \prime } = {\widehat{R}}_{2}^{\prime }\left( {{Z}_{2},{X}_{2}}\right)  \land  \cdots  \land  {\widehat{R}}_{k}^{\prime }\left( {{Z}_{2},\ldots ,{Z}_{k},{X}_{k}}\right) .
$$

This query has $k - 1$ atoms and contains the variables ${Z}_{2},\ldots ,{Z}_{k}$ but not ${Z}_{1}$ . Observe that ${\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }^{\prime \prime }$ is the multivariate extension of a query ${Q}^{\prime }$ that results from $Q$ by skipping the atom ${R}_{1}\left( {X}_{1}\right)$ . Hierarchical queries remain hierarchical under atom elimination. Hence, ${Q}^{\prime }$ is hierarchical. By induction hypothesis, ${\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }^{\prime \prime }$ is acyclic. Since we obtained ${\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }^{\prime \prime }$ from ${\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }$ by eliminating root variables,leaf variables,or subsumed atoms,it follows from Claim F. 1 that ${\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }$ must be acyclic. This completes the induction step.

该查询包含$k - 1$个原子，含有变量${Z}_{2},\ldots ,{Z}_{k}$但不含${Z}_{1}$。注意到${\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }^{\prime \prime }$是查询${Q}^{\prime }$的多变量扩展，后者通过跳过原子${R}_{1}\left( {X}_{1}\right)$从$Q$得到。层次化查询在原子消除下仍保持层次性，因此${Q}^{\prime }$是层次化的。根据归纳假设，${\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }^{\prime \prime }$是非循环的。由于我们通过消除根变量、叶变量或被包含原子从${\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }$得到${\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }^{\prime \prime }$，根据主张F.1可知${\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }$必为非循环。至此完成归纳步骤。

$$
{\widehat{R}}_{1}\left( {{Z}_{1},\ldots ,{Z}_{k - 1},{X}_{1}}\right) ,{\widehat{R}}_{2}\left( {{Z}_{1},\ldots ,{Z}_{k - 2},{X}_{2}}\right) ,{\widehat{R}}_{3}\left( {{Z}_{1},\ldots ,{Z}_{k},{X}_{3}}\right)

where $\left\{  {{Z}_{1},\ldots ,{Z}_{k}}\right\}$ are fresh variables. When restricted to the variables in $Y$ ,the resulting query,which we call

其中$\left\{  {{Z}_{1},\ldots ,{Z}_{k}}\right\}$为新变量。此外，${\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }$中其他原子不包含这三个变量$\left\{  {{Z}_{k - 2},{Z}_{k - 1},{Z}_{k}}\right\}$。设$Y = \left\{  {X,Y,{Z}_{k - 1}}\right\}$。当限制于$Y$中的变量时，所得查询（记为

${\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }^{\prime }$ ,is a triangle query. In particular,let ${\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }^{\prime }\overset{\text{ def }}{ = }{\left( {\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }\right) }_{Y}$ ,where the notation ${\left( {\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }\right) }_{Y}$ was defined in (4). Then,

${\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }^{\prime }$）是一个三角查询。具体而言，设${\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }^{\prime }\overset{\text{ def }}{ = }{\left( {\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }\right) }_{Y}$（其中记号${\left( {\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }\right) }_{Y}$定义见式(4)），则有

$$
{\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }^{\prime } = {\widehat{R}}_{1}\left( {{Z}_{k - 1},X}\right) ,{\widehat{R}}_{2}\left( {X,Y}\right) ,{\widehat{R}}_{3}\left( {{Z}_{k - 1},Y}\right) 
$$

Therefore, ${\rho }^{ * }\left( {\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }^{\prime }\right)  = 3/2$ . We use this fact to to show that $\mathrm{w}\left( {\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }\right)$ must be at least $3/2$ . To that end,we use variable elimination orders (see Appendix B). In particular, we use the definition of fractional hypertree width in terms of variable elimination orders,as given by Eq. (26). Let $\mathbf{\mu }$ be an optimal variable elimination order for ${\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }$ ,i.e., $\mathrm{w}\left( \mathbf{\mu }\right)  = \mathrm{w}\left( {\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }\right)$ . We make the following claim:

因此${\rho }^{ * }\left( {\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }^{\prime }\right)  = 3/2$。我们利用这一事实证明$\mathrm{w}\left( {\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }\right)$至少为$3/2$。为此采用变量消除序（见附录B），具体通过式(26)给出的基于变量消除序的分式超树宽定义。设$\mathbf{\mu }$为${\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }$的最优变量消除序，即$\mathrm{w}\left( \mathbf{\mu }\right)  = \mathrm{w}\left( {\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }\right)$。我们提出如下主张：

CLAIM F.2. There must be a variable $W \in  \mu$ where ${U}_{W}^{\mu } \supseteq  Y$ .

主张F.2 必存在变量$W \in  \mu$满足${U}_{W}^{\mu } \supseteq  Y$

We prove the above claim as follows. Initially,the restricted query ${\left( {\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }\right) }_{Y}$ is a triangle query. Every time we eliminate a variable $W$ that does not satisfy ${U}_{W}^{\mu } \supseteq  Y$ ,the restricted query ${\left( {\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }\right) }_{Y}$ remains a triangle query. Hence,elimination could not have terminated because we haven’t yet eliminated any of the variables in $Y$ . Therefore,there must be a variable $W \in  \mathbf{\mu }$ where ${\mathbf{U}}_{W}^{\mathbf{\mu }} \supseteq  \mathbf{Y}$ . Now,we have:

我们通过以下方式证明上述主张。最初，受限查询${\left( {\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }\right) }_{Y}$是一个三角查询。每次消除不满足${U}_{W}^{\mu } \supseteq  Y$的变量$W$时，受限查询${\left( {\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }\right) }_{Y}$仍保持为三角查询。因此，消除过程不可能终止，因为我们尚未消除$Y$中的任何变量。故必然存在变量$W \in  \mathbf{\mu }$满足${\mathbf{U}}_{W}^{\mathbf{\mu }} \supseteq  \mathbf{Y}$。此时可得：

$$
\mathrm{w}\left( {\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }\right)  = \mathrm{w}\left( \mathbf{\mu }\right)  \geq  {\rho }^{ * }\left( {U}_{W}^{\mathbf{\mu }}\right)  \geq  {\rho }^{ * }\left( Y\right)  = 3/2.
$$

The first equality above follows from our assumption that $\mathbf{\mu }$ is an optimal variable elimination order for ${\widehat{Q}}_{\mathbf{\sigma }}$ . The second inequality above follows from the monotonicity of the fractional edge cover number ${\rho }^{ * }$ (see Appendix B).

第一个等式源于我们假设$\mathbf{\mu }$是${\widehat{Q}}_{\mathbf{\sigma }}$的最优变量消除顺序。第二个不等式源于分数边覆盖数${\rho }^{ * }$的单调性（参见附录B）。

### F.3 Proof of Proposition F. 3

### F.3 命题F.3的证明

Proposition F.3. For any Loomis-Whitney query $Q,\mathrm{w}\left( \widehat{Q}\right)  = \frac{3}{2}$ .

命题F.3。对于任意Loomis-Whitney查询$Q,\mathrm{w}\left( \widehat{Q}\right)  = \frac{3}{2}$。

Consider an arbitrary Loomis-Whitney query $Q$ of some degree $k \geq  3$ and its multivariate extension $\widehat{Q}$ with fresh variables ${Z}_{1},\ldots ,{Z}_{k}$ . Proposition F. 2 implies that the fractional hypertree width of $\widehat{Q}$ is at least $\frac{3}{2}$ . Let ${\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }$ be an arbitrary component of $\widehat{Q}$ . Next,we show that the fractional hypertree width of ${\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }$ is at most $\frac{3}{2}$ ,which concludes the proof.

考虑任意度数为$k \geq  3$的Loomis-Whitney查询$Q$及其带新变量${Z}_{1},\ldots ,{Z}_{k}$的多元扩展$\widehat{Q}$。命题F.2表明$\widehat{Q}$的分分数超树宽度至少为$\frac{3}{2}$。设${\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }$为$\widehat{Q}$的任意分量。接下来我们将证明${\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }$的分分数超树宽度至多为$\frac{3}{2}$，由此完成证明。

Let ${\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }^{\prime }$ be the query that we obtain from ${\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }$ by eliminating the variable ${Z}_{k}$ . Since this variable occurs in only a single atom in ${\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }$ ,the fractional hypertree width of ${\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }$ and ${\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }^{\prime }$ must be the same. This follows from the equivalence between variable elimination orders and tree decompositions,as explained in Section B. It remains to show that ${\widehat{\mathcal{Q}}}_{\sigma }^{\prime }$ has a fractional hypertree width of $\frac{3}{2}$ . By definition of a multivariate extension, ${\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }^{\prime }$ must contain three atoms of the form:

设${\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }^{\prime }$是通过消除变量${Z}_{k}$从${\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }$得到的查询。由于该变量仅在${\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }$的单个原子中出现，${\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }$与${\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }^{\prime }$的分分数超树宽度必然相同。这源于变量消除顺序与树分解的等价性，如B节所述。仍需证明${\widehat{\mathcal{Q}}}_{\sigma }^{\prime }$具有$\frac{3}{2}$的分分数超树宽度。根据多元扩展的定义，${\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }^{\prime }$必须包含以下形式的三个原子：

$$
{\widehat{R}}_{1}\left( {{Z}_{1},\ldots ,{Z}_{k - 2},{\mathbf{X}}_{1}}\right) ,{\widehat{R}}_{2}\left( {{Z}_{1},\ldots ,{Z}_{k - 1},{\mathbf{X}}_{2}}\right) ,{\widehat{R}}_{3}\left( {{Z}_{1},\ldots ,{Z}_{k - 1},{\mathbf{X}}_{3}}\right) 
$$

Note that each of the variables $\left\{  {{Z}_{1},\ldots ,{Z}_{k - 1}}\right\}$ is contained in at least two of the atoms ${\widehat{R}}_{1},{\widehat{R}}_{2},{\widehat{R}}_{3}$ above. Moreover, because ${X}_{1},{X}_{2},{X}_{3}$ are the schemas of three atoms in a Loomis-Whitney query $Q$ ,each variable $X \in  \operatorname{vars}\left( Q\right)$ must occur in at least two of the three atoms ${\widehat{R}}_{1},{\widehat{R}}_{2},{\widehat{R}}_{3}$ above. Consider now a fractional edge cover ${\lambda }_{\widehat{R}} = {\lambda }_{\widehat{S}} = {\lambda }_{\widehat{T}} = \frac{1}{2}$ . This is indeed a valid fractional edge cover for ${\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }^{\prime }$ ,and it proves that ${\rho }^{ * }\left( {\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }^{\prime }\right)  \leq  \frac{3}{2}$ . This implies that $\mathrm{w}\left( {\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }\right)  \leq  \frac{3}{2}$ .

请注意，每个变量$\left\{  {{Z}_{1},\ldots ,{Z}_{k - 1}}\right\}$都至少包含在上述原子${\widehat{R}}_{1},{\widehat{R}}_{2},{\widehat{R}}_{3}$中的两个里。此外，由于${X}_{1},{X}_{2},{X}_{3}$是Loomis-Whitney查询$Q$中三个原子的模式，每个变量$X \in  \operatorname{vars}\left( Q\right)$必须出现在上述三个原子${\widehat{R}}_{1},{\widehat{R}}_{2},{\widehat{R}}_{3}$中的至少两个里。现在考虑一个分数边覆盖${\lambda }_{\widehat{R}} = {\lambda }_{\widehat{S}} = {\lambda }_{\widehat{T}} = \frac{1}{2}$，这确实是一个有效的分数边覆盖，适用于${\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }^{\prime }$，并且它证明了${\rho }^{ * }\left( {\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }^{\prime }\right)  \leq  \frac{3}{2}$。这意味着$\mathrm{w}\left( {\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }\right)  \leq  \frac{3}{2}$。

### F.4 Proof of Proposition F. 4

### F.4 命题F.4的证明

Proposition F.4. For any query $Q,\mathrm{w}\left( Q\right)  \leq  \mathrm{w}\left( \widehat{Q}\right)  \leq  \mathrm{w}\left( Q\right)  + 1$ .

命题F.4。对于任何查询$Q,\mathrm{w}\left( Q\right)  \leq  \mathrm{w}\left( \widehat{Q}\right)  \leq  \mathrm{w}\left( Q\right)  + 1$。

We consider a query $Q = {R}_{1}\left( {\mathbf{X}}_{1}\right)  \land  \ldots  \land  {R}_{k}\left( {\mathbf{X}}_{k}\right)$ and its multivariate extension $\widehat{Q} = \mathop{\bigvee }\limits_{{\forall \sigma  \in  {\sum }_{k}}}{\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }$ with fresh variables ${Z}_{1},\ldots ,{Z}_{k}$ (Eq. (7)).

我们考虑一个查询$Q = {R}_{1}\left( {\mathbf{X}}_{1}\right)  \land  \ldots  \land  {R}_{k}\left( {\mathbf{X}}_{k}\right)$及其带有新变量${Z}_{1},\ldots ,{Z}_{k}$的多变量扩展$\widehat{Q} = \mathop{\bigvee }\limits_{{\forall \sigma  \in  {\sum }_{k}}}{\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }$（方程（7））。

Proof of $\mathrm{w}\left( Q\right)  \leq  \mathrm{w}\left( \widehat{Q}\right)$ . For the sake of contradiction,assume that $\mathrm{w}\left( Q\right)  > \mathrm{w}\left( \widehat{Q}\right)$ . Consider an arbitrary component ${\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }$ of $\widehat{Q}$ . It follows from the definition of the fractional hypertree width that $\mathrm{w}\left( {\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }\right)  \leq  \mathrm{w}\left( \widehat{Q}\right)$ . Hence, $\mathrm{w}\left( Q\right)  > \mathrm{w}\left( {\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }\right)$ . Let $\left( {{\mathcal{T}}_{\sigma },{\chi }_{\sigma }}\right)$ be a tree decomposition of ${\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }$ with $\mathrm{w}\left( {{\mathcal{T}}_{\sigma },{\chi }_{\sigma }}\right)  = \mathrm{w}\left( {\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }\right)$ . We will construct from $\left( {{\mathcal{T}}_{\sigma },{\chi }_{\sigma }}\right)$ a tree decomposition $\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)$ for $Q$ with $\mathrm{w}\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)  \leq  \mathrm{w}\left( {{\mathcal{T}}_{\sigma },{\chi }_{\sigma }}\right)$ . This implies that $\mathrm{w}\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)  \leq  \mathrm{w}\left( \widehat{Q}\right)$ ,hence $\mathrm{w}\left( Q\right)  \leq  \mathrm{w}\left( \widehat{Q}\right)$ ,which is a contradiction.

反证法证明 $\mathrm{w}\left( Q\right)  \leq  \mathrm{w}\left( \widehat{Q}\right)$。假设相反情况成立，即 $\mathrm{w}\left( Q\right)  > \mathrm{w}\left( \widehat{Q}\right)$。考虑 $\widehat{Q}$ 的任意分量 ${\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }$，根据分数超树宽度的定义可得 $\mathrm{w}\left( {\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }\right)  \leq  \mathrm{w}\left( \widehat{Q}\right)$，因此 $\mathrm{w}\left( Q\right)  > \mathrm{w}\left( {\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }\right)$。设 $\left( {{\mathcal{T}}_{\sigma },{\chi }_{\sigma }}\right)$ 是 ${\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }$ 的树分解且满足 $\mathrm{w}\left( {{\mathcal{T}}_{\sigma },{\chi }_{\sigma }}\right)  = \mathrm{w}\left( {\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }\right)$，我们将基于此构造 $Q$ 的树分解 $\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)$ 并满足 $\mathrm{w}\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)  \leq  \mathrm{w}\left( {{\mathcal{T}}_{\sigma },{\chi }_{\sigma }}\right)$。这意味着 $\mathrm{w}\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)  \leq  \mathrm{w}\left( \widehat{Q}\right)$，从而得出 $\mathrm{w}\left( Q\right)  \leq  \mathrm{w}\left( \widehat{Q}\right)$，形成矛盾。

Without loss of generality,assume that ${\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }$ is of the form ${\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma } = {\widehat{R}}_{1}\left( {{Z}_{1},{X}_{1}}\right) ,\ldots ,{\widehat{R}}_{k}\left( {{Z}_{1},\ldots ,{Z}_{k},{X}_{k}}\right)$ . For all other components of $\widehat{Q}$ ,the argument is completely analogous. Let ${\widehat{B}}_{1},\ldots ,{\widehat{B}}_{n}$ be the bags of $\left( {{\mathcal{T}}_{\sigma },{\chi }_{\sigma }}\right)$ . The pair $\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)$ results from $\left( {{\mathcal{T}}_{\sigma },{\chi }_{\sigma }}\right)$ by eliminating the variables ${Z}_{1},\ldots ,{Z}_{k}$ from all bags. Let ${B}_{1},\ldots ,{B}_{n}$ denote the bags of $\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)$ . The pair $\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)$ constitutes a tree decomposition for $Q$ ,since (a) for each atom ${R}_{i}\left( {X}_{i}\right)$ of $Q$ there must still be one bag that contains ${X}_{i}$ and (b) for each variable $X \in  \mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack  }}{X}_{i}$ the bags in $\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)$ containing $X$ build a connected subtree. Given $i \in  \left\lbrack  n\right\rbrack$ ,let ${\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }^{i}$ be the query that we obtain from ${\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }$ by restricting the schema of each atom to the variables in ${\widehat{B}}_{i}$ . Similarly,let ${Q}^{i}$ be the query obtained from $Q$ by restricting the schema of each atom to the variables in the bag ${B}_{i}$ . Consider now a fractional edge cover ${\left( {\lambda }_{{\widehat{R}}_{i}\left( {X}_{i}\right) }\right) }_{i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack  }$ for ${\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }^{i}$ . This tuple is also a fractional edge cover for ${Q}^{i}$ ,since ${B}_{i} \subseteq  {\widehat{B}}_{i}$ . This means that ${\rho }^{ * }\left( {Q}^{i}\right)  \leq  {\rho }^{ * }\left( {\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }^{i}\right)$ . Hence, $\mathrm{w}\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)  \leq  \mathrm{w}\left( {{\mathcal{T}}_{\sigma },{\chi }_{\sigma }}\right)$ .

不失一般性，假设${\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }$具有${\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma } = {\widehat{R}}_{1}\left( {{Z}_{1},{X}_{1}}\right) ,\ldots ,{\widehat{R}}_{k}\left( {{Z}_{1},\ldots ,{Z}_{k},{X}_{k}}\right)$的形式。对于$\widehat{Q}$的所有其他分量，论证过程完全类似。令${\widehat{B}}_{1},\ldots ,{\widehat{B}}_{n}$表示$\left( {{\mathcal{T}}_{\sigma },{\chi }_{\sigma }}\right)$的包集合。通过从所有包中消除变量${Z}_{1},\ldots ,{Z}_{k}$，可从$\left( {{\mathcal{T}}_{\sigma },{\chi }_{\sigma }}\right)$得到序对$\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)$。令${B}_{1},\ldots ,{B}_{n}$表示$\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)$的包集合。该序对$\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)$构成了$Q$的树分解，因为：(a)对于$Q$的每个原子${R}_{i}\left( {X}_{i}\right)$，必须仍存在一个包含${X}_{i}$的包；(b)对于每个变量$X \in  \mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack  }}{X}_{i}$，$\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)$中包含$X$的包形成连通子树。给定$i \in  \left\lbrack  n\right\rbrack$，令${\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }^{i}$为通过将每个原子的模式限制在${\widehat{B}}_{i}$变量内而从${\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }$获得的查询。类似地，令${Q}^{i}$为通过将每个原子的模式限制在包${B}_{i}$变量内而从$Q$获得的查询。现考虑${\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }^{i}$的分数边覆盖${\left( {\lambda }_{{\widehat{R}}_{i}\left( {X}_{i}\right) }\right) }_{i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack  }$。由于${B}_{i} \subseteq  {\widehat{B}}_{i}$，该元组也是${Q}^{i}$的分数边覆盖。这意味着${\rho }^{ * }\left( {Q}^{i}\right)  \leq  {\rho }^{ * }\left( {\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }^{i}\right)$。因此，$\mathrm{w}\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)  \leq  \mathrm{w}\left( {{\mathcal{T}}_{\sigma },{\chi }_{\sigma }}\right)$。

Proof of $\widehat{\mathrm{w}}\left( Q\right)  \leq  \mathrm{w}\left( Q\right)  + 1$ . Let ${\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }$ be an arbitrary component of $\widehat{Q}$ . Consider a tree decomposition $\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)$ of $Q$ with fractional hypertree width w. We will transform $\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)$ into a tree decomposition $\left( {{\mathcal{T}}_{\sigma },{\chi }_{\sigma }}\right)$ for ${\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }$ with fractional hypertree width of at most $\mathrm{w}\left( Q\right)  + 1$ . This implies that $\mathrm{w}\left( \widehat{Q}\right)$ is at most $\mathrm{w}\left( Q\right)  + 1$ .

$\widehat{\mathrm{w}}\left( Q\right)  \leq  \mathrm{w}\left( Q\right)  + 1$的证明。令${\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }$为$\widehat{Q}$的任意分量。考虑具有分数超树宽度w的$Q$的树分解$\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)$。我们将把$\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)$转化为分数超树宽度至多为$\mathrm{w}\left( Q\right)  + 1$的${\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }$的树分解$\left( {{\mathcal{T}}_{\sigma },{\chi }_{\sigma }}\right)$。这表明$\mathrm{w}\left( \widehat{Q}\right)$至多为$\mathrm{w}\left( Q\right)  + 1$。

Without loss of generality,assume that ${\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }$ is of the form ${\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma } = {\widehat{R}}_{1}\left( {{Z}_{1},{X}_{1}}\right) ,\ldots ,{\widehat{R}}_{k}\left( {{Z}_{1},\ldots ,{Z}_{k},{X}_{k}}\right)$ . For other components of $\widehat{Q}$ ,the argument is analogous. Let ${B}_{1},\ldots ,{B}_{n}$ be the bags of $\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)$ . The pair $\left( {{\mathcal{T}}_{\sigma },{\chi }_{\sigma }}\right)$ results from $\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)$ by adding to each bag the variables ${Z}_{1},\ldots ,{Z}_{k}$ . Let ${\widehat{B}}_{1} = {B}_{1} \cup  \left\{  {{Z}_{1},\ldots ,{Z}_{k}}\right\}  ,\ldots ,{\widehat{B}}_{n} \cup  \left\{  {{Z}_{1},\ldots ,{Z}_{k}}\right\}$ be the bags of $\left( {{\mathcal{T}}_{\sigma },{\chi }_{\sigma }}\right)$ . By construction, $\left( {{\mathcal{T}}_{\sigma },{\chi }_{\sigma }}\right)$ constitutes a tree decomposition for ${\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }$ ,since (a) for each $i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack$ ,the variables $\left\{  {{Z}_{1},\ldots ,{Z}_{i}}\right\}   \cup  {X}_{i}$ must be included in one bag and (b) for each variable $X \in  \left\{  {{Z}_{1},\ldots ,{Z}_{k}}\right\}   \cup  \mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack  }}{X}_{i}$ ,the bags that include $X$ still build a connected subtree. It remains to show that $\mathrm{w}\left( {{\mathcal{T}}_{\sigma },{\chi }_{\sigma }}\right)  \leq  \mathrm{w}\left( Q\right)  + 1$ . For some $i \in  \left\lbrack  n\right\rbrack$ ,let ${Q}^{i}$ be the query obtained from $Q$ by restricting each atom to the variables in ${B}_{i}$ and let ${\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }^{i}$ be the query obtained from ${\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }$ by restricting each atom to the variables in ${\widehat{B}}_{i}$ . Consider a fractional edge cover ${\left( {\lambda }_{{R}_{i}\left( {X}_{i}\right) }\right) }_{i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack  }$ for ${Q}^{i}$ . We can turn it into a fractional edge cover for ${\widehat{Q}}^{i}$ by setting ${\lambda }_{{R}_{k}\left( {X}_{k}\right) }$ to 1 . This means that ${\rho }^{ * }\left( {\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }^{i}\right)  \leq  {\rho }^{ * }\left( {Q}^{i}\right)  + 1$ . This implies $\mathrm{w}\left( {{\mathcal{T}}_{\sigma },{\chi }_{\sigma }}\right)  \leq  \mathrm{w}\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)  + 1$ .

不失一般性，假设${\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }$具有${\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma } = {\widehat{R}}_{1}\left( {{Z}_{1},{X}_{1}}\right) ,\ldots ,{\widehat{R}}_{k}\left( {{Z}_{1},\ldots ,{Z}_{k},{X}_{k}}\right)$的形式。对于$\widehat{Q}$的其他组成部分，论证过程类似。令${B}_{1},\ldots ,{B}_{n}$表示$\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)$的包集合。通过向每个包中添加变量${Z}_{1},\ldots ,{Z}_{k}$，从$\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)$得到序对$\left( {{\mathcal{T}}_{\sigma },{\chi }_{\sigma }}\right)$。令${\widehat{B}}_{1} = {B}_{1} \cup  \left\{  {{Z}_{1},\ldots ,{Z}_{k}}\right\}  ,\ldots ,{\widehat{B}}_{n} \cup  \left\{  {{Z}_{1},\ldots ,{Z}_{k}}\right\}$为$\left( {{\mathcal{T}}_{\sigma },{\chi }_{\sigma }}\right)$的包集合。根据构造，$\left( {{\mathcal{T}}_{\sigma },{\chi }_{\sigma }}\right)$构成了${\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }$的树分解，因为：(a) 对于每个$i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack$，变量$\left\{  {{Z}_{1},\ldots ,{Z}_{i}}\right\}   \cup  {X}_{i}$必须包含在一个包中；(b) 对于每个变量$X \in  \left\{  {{Z}_{1},\ldots ,{Z}_{k}}\right\}   \cup  \mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack  }}{X}_{i}$，包含$X$的包仍形成连通子树。还需证明$\mathrm{w}\left( {{\mathcal{T}}_{\sigma },{\chi }_{\sigma }}\right)  \leq  \mathrm{w}\left( Q\right)  + 1$。对于某些$i \in  \left\lbrack  n\right\rbrack$，令${Q}^{i}$是通过将每个原子限制在${B}_{i}$变量内从$Q$获得的查询，令${\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }^{i}$是通过将每个原子限制在${\widehat{B}}_{i}$变量内从${\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }$获得的查询。考虑${Q}^{i}$的分数边覆盖${\left( {\lambda }_{{R}_{i}\left( {X}_{i}\right) }\right) }_{i \in  \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack  }$。通过将${\lambda }_{{R}_{k}\left( {X}_{k}\right) }$设为1，可将其转化为${\widehat{Q}}^{i}$的分数边覆盖。这意味着${\rho }^{ * }\left( {\widehat{Q}}_{\sigma }^{i}\right)  \leq  {\rho }^{ * }\left( {Q}^{i}\right)  + 1$。由此可得$\mathrm{w}\left( {{\mathcal{T}}_{\sigma },{\chi }_{\sigma }}\right)  \leq  \mathrm{w}\left( {\mathcal{T},\chi }\right)  + 1$。

### F.5 Proof of Proposition F. 5

### F.5 命题F.5的证明

Proposition F.5. For any non-hierarchical query $Q$ without self-joins,update stream of length $N$ ,and $\gamma  > 0$ ,there is no algorithm that solves ${\mathrm{{IVM}}}^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$ or ${\mathrm{{IVM}}}_{\delta }^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$ with $\widetilde{O}\left( {N}^{1/2 - \gamma }\right)$ amortized update time,unless the $\mathrm{{OMv}}$ -Conjecture fails.

命题F.5。对于任何无自连接的非层次化查询$Q$、长度为$N$的更新流以及$\gamma  > 0$，不存在能以$\widetilde{O}\left( {N}^{1/2 - \gamma }\right)$摊还更新时间解决${\mathrm{{IVM}}}^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$或${\mathrm{{IVM}}}_{\delta }^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$的算法，除非$\mathrm{{OMv}}$猜想不成立。

The proof is based on the Online Vector-Matrix-Vector Multiplication (OuMv) Conjecture, which is implied by the Online Matrix-Vector Multiplication (OMv) Conjecture [16]. See Appendix B.

该证明基于在线向量-矩阵-向量乘法(OuMv)猜想，该猜想由在线矩阵-向量乘法(OMv)猜想[16]所蕴含。详见附录B。

Consider the simplest non-hierarchical query ${Q}_{nh} = R\left( A\right)  \land  S\left( {A,B}\right)  \land  T\left( B\right)$ . The proof of Proposition F. 5 consists of three parts. First,we show hardness for the problem ${\mathrm{{IVM}}}^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  {Q}_{nh}\right\rbrack$ (Section F.5.1). Afterwards,we show how the hardness proof for ${\mathrm{{IVM}}}^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  {Q}_{nh}\right\rbrack$ extends to a hardness proof for ${\mathrm{{IVM}}}_{\delta }^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  {Q}_{nh}\right\rbrack$ (Section F.5.2). Finally,we explain how these two results generalize to arbitrary non-hierarchical queries without self-joins (Section F.5.3). The technique used in the latter part is standard in the literature (see, e.g. [8]), so we only give the high-level idea.

考虑最简单的非层次化查询${Q}_{nh} = R\left( A\right)  \land  S\left( {A,B}\right)  \land  T\left( B\right)$。命题F.5的证明包含三部分：首先我们证明问题${\mathrm{{IVM}}}^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  {Q}_{nh}\right\rbrack$的困难性(章节F.5.1)；随后展示如何将${\mathrm{{IVM}}}^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  {Q}_{nh}\right\rbrack$的困难性证明扩展到${\mathrm{{IVM}}}_{\delta }^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  {Q}_{nh}\right\rbrack$(章节F.5.2)；最后说明这两个结果如何推广到任意无自连接的非层次化查询(章节F.5.3)。后部分采用的技术在文献中属标准方法(例如参见[8])，故仅给出高层思路。

F.5.1 Hardness of ${IV}{M}^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  {Q}_{nh}\right\rbrack$ . We reduce the OuMv problem to ${\mathrm{{IVM}}}^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  {Q}_{\mathrm{{nh}}}\right\rbrack$ . Assume that there is some $\gamma  > 0$ and an algorithm $\mathcal{A}$ that solves ${\operatorname{IVM}}^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  {Q}_{\mathrm{{nh}}}\right\rbrack$ with $\widetilde{O}\left( {N}^{1/2 - \gamma }\right)$ amortized update time for update streams of length $N$ . We show how to use $\mathcal{A}$ to solve the OuMv problem in sub-cubic time,which is a contradiction to the OuMv Conjecture. Let the $n \times  n$ matrix $M$ and the $n \times  1$ column vectors $\left( {{u}_{1},{v}_{1}}\right) ,\ldots ,\left( {{u}_{n},{v}_{n}}\right)$ be the input to the OuMv problem. For $i,j \in  \left\lbrack  n\right\rbrack$ ,we denote by $\mathbf{M}\left( {i,j}\right)$ the Boolean value in $\mathbf{M}$ at position(i,j)and by $\mathbf{u}\left( i\right)$ (and $\mathbf{v}\left( i\right)$ ) the value in $\mathbf{u}\left( i\right)$ (and $v\left( i\right) )$ at position $i$ . The idea is to use relation $S$ to encode the matrix $\mathbf{M}$ and the relations $R$ and $T$ to encode the vectors ${u}_{r}$ and ${v}_{r}$ for $r \in  \left\lbrack  n\right\rbrack$ . The encoding closely follows prior work [8]. Starting from the empty database,we execute at most ${n}^{2}$ inserts to $S$ such that $\left( {i,j}\right)  \in  S \Leftrightarrow  \mathbf{M}\left( {i,j}\right)  = 1$ . In each round $r \in  \left\lbrack  n\right\rbrack$ ,we do the following. We use at most ${4n}$ updates to delete the tuples in $R$ and $T$ and to insert new tuples such that $i \in  \mathbf{R} \Leftrightarrow  {\mathbf{u}}_{r}\left( i\right)  = 1$ and $j \in  T \Leftrightarrow  {\mathbf{v}}_{r}\left( j\right)  = 1$ . At the end of round $r$ we request the enumeration of the result of ${Q}_{\text{nh }}$ . It holds ${\mathbf{u}}_{r}^{\top }\mathbf{M}{\mathbf{v}}_{r} = 1$ if and only if the result of ${Q}_{\mathrm{{nh}}}$ contains at least one tuple. So,we can stop the enumeration process after constant time. Summed up over $n$ rounds,we execute ${n}^{2} + 4{n}^{2} = O\left( {n}^{2}\right)$ updates,some of which are followed by enumeration requests that do not require more than constant time. Hence,using the update mechanism of algorithm $\mathcal{A}$ ,the overall time to solve the OuMv problem takes $O\left( {\operatorname{polylog}\left( {n}^{2}\right)  \cdot  {n}^{2} \cdot  {\left( {n}^{2}\right) }^{1/2 - \gamma }}\right)  = O\left( {\operatorname{polylog}\left( {n}^{2}\right)  \cdot  {n}^{3 - {2\gamma }}}\right)$ . As the polylogarithmic factor polylog $\left( {n}^{2}\right)$ can be asymptotically upper-bounded by ${n}^{{\gamma }^{\prime }}$ for arbitrarily small ${\gamma }^{\prime }$ ,we conclude that the OuMv problem can be solved in time $O\left( {n}^{3 - {\gamma }^{\prime \prime }}\right)$ for some ${\gamma }^{\prime \prime } > 0$ . This contradicts the OuMv Conjecture.

F.5.1 ${IV}{M}^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  {Q}_{nh}\right\rbrack$的硬度证明。我们将OuMv问题归约至${\mathrm{{IVM}}}^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  {Q}_{\mathrm{{nh}}}\right\rbrack$。假设存在某个$\gamma  > 0$及算法$\mathcal{A}$，能在长度为$N$的更新流中以$\widetilde{O}\left( {N}^{1/2 - \gamma }\right)$的摊还更新时间求解${\operatorname{IVM}}^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  {Q}_{\mathrm{{nh}}}\right\rbrack$。我们演示如何用$\mathcal{A}$在次立方时间内解决OuMv问题，这与OuMv猜想矛盾。设$n \times  n$矩阵$M$和$n \times  1$列向量$\left( {{u}_{1},{v}_{1}}\right) ,\ldots ,\left( {{u}_{n},{v}_{n}}\right)$为OuMv问题的输入。对于$i,j \in  \left\lbrack  n\right\rbrack$，用$\mathbf{M}\left( {i,j}\right)$表示$\mathbf{M}$中位置(i,j)的布尔值，$\mathbf{u}\left( i\right)$（及$\mathbf{v}\left( i\right)$）表示$\mathbf{u}\left( i\right)$（及$v\left( i\right) )$）在位置$i$的值。核心思路是利用关系$S$编码矩阵$\mathbf{M}$，用关系$R$和$T$编码$r \in  \left\lbrack  n\right\rbrack$轮的向量${u}_{r}$与${v}_{r}$。该编码方式严格遵循先前研究[8]。从空数据库出发，我们执行最多${n}^{2}$次插入至$S$使得$\left( {i,j}\right)  \in  S \Leftrightarrow  \mathbf{M}\left( {i,j}\right)  = 1$。每轮$r \in  \left\lbrack  n\right\rbrack$执行以下操作：用最多${4n}$次更新删除$R$和$T$中的元组，并插入新元组以满足$i \in  \mathbf{R} \Leftrightarrow  {\mathbf{u}}_{r}\left( i\right)  = 1$和$j \in  T \Leftrightarrow  {\mathbf{v}}_{r}\left( j\right)  = 1$。在第$r$轮结束时，请求枚举${Q}_{\text{nh }}$的结果。当且仅当${Q}_{\mathrm{{nh}}}$结果包含至少一个元组时，${\mathbf{u}}_{r}^{\top }\mathbf{M}{\mathbf{v}}_{r} = 1$成立。因此我们可在常数时间后终止枚举。累计$n$轮共执行${n}^{2} + 4{n}^{2} = O\left( {n}^{2}\right)$次更新，其中部分更新后接耗时不超过常数时间的枚举请求。因此，利用算法$\mathcal{A}$的更新机制，解决OuMv问题的总时间为$O\left( {\operatorname{polylog}\left( {n}^{2}\right)  \cdot  {n}^{2} \cdot  {\left( {n}^{2}\right) }^{1/2 - \gamma }}\right)  = O\left( {\operatorname{polylog}\left( {n}^{2}\right)  \cdot  {n}^{3 - {2\gamma }}}\right)$。由于多对数因子polylog$\left( {n}^{2}\right)$对于任意小${\gamma }^{\prime }$均可渐近上界为${n}^{{\gamma }^{\prime }}$，我们得出OuMv问题可在$O\left( {n}^{3 - {\gamma }^{\prime \prime }}\right)$时间内解决（其中${\gamma }^{\prime \prime } > 0$为某常数），这与OuMv猜想相矛盾。

F.5.2 Hardness of ${IV}{M}_{\delta }^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  {Q}_{nh}\right\rbrack$ . We reduce the OuMv problem to ${\mathrm{{IVM}}}_{\delta }^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  {Q}_{\mathrm{{nh}}}\right\rbrack$ . The encoding of the matrix and the vectors is exactly as in Section F.5.1. The only difference is how we obtain the result of ${\mathbf{u}}_{r}^{\top }\mathbf{M}{\mathbf{v}}_{r}$ in each round $r \in  \left\lbrack  n\right\rbrack$ . We first execute the necessary updates to encode ${\mathbf{u}}_{r}$ in $R$ . Then,we delete the contents of $T$ . Afterwards,we insert,one by one,each value $j$ with ${v}_{r}\left( j\right)  = 1$ to $T$ . After each insert,we request the enumeration of the delta in the result of ${Q}_{\text{nh }}$ and check whether the delta contains at lease one tuple. The main observation is that ${\mathbf{u}}^{\top }\mathbf{M}\mathbf{v} = 1$ if and only if one of the enumeration requests in round $r$ reports at least one tuple. The overall number of updates is as in Section F.5.1. The additional cost caused by each enumeration request is constant.

F.5.2 ${IV}{M}_{\delta }^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  {Q}_{nh}\right\rbrack$的硬度证明。我们将OuMv问题归约至${\mathrm{{IVM}}}_{\delta }^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  {Q}_{\mathrm{{nh}}}\right\rbrack$。矩阵与向量的编码方式与F.5.1节完全相同，唯一区别在于如何获取每轮$r \in  \left\lbrack  n\right\rbrack$中${\mathbf{u}}_{r}^{\top }\mathbf{M}{\mathbf{v}}_{r}$的结果。我们首先执行必要更新以在$R$中编码${\mathbf{u}}_{r}$，随后清空$T$内容。接着逐个插入每个值$j$（附带${v}_{r}\left( j\right)  = 1$）至$T$。每次插入后，我们请求枚举${Q}_{\text{nh }}$结果中的增量，并检查该增量是否包含至少一个元组。核心观察结论是：当且仅当轮次$r$中的枚举请求报告至少一个元组时，${\mathbf{u}}^{\top }\mathbf{M}\mathbf{v} = 1$成立。总更新次数与F.5.1节相同，每次枚举请求产生的额外开销为常量。

F.5.3 Hardness for Arbitrary Non-Hierarchical Queries. Given any non-hierarchical query $Q$ without self-joins,we can reduce the problems ${\mathrm{{IVM}}}^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  {Q}_{\mathrm{{nh}}}\right\rbrack$ and ${\mathrm{{IVM}}}_{\delta }^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  {Q}_{\mathrm{{nh}}}\right\rbrack$ to ${\mathrm{{IVM}}}^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$ and respectively ${\mathrm{{IVM}}}_{\delta }^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$ . We give the high-level idea of the reduction. Since $Q$ is non-hierarchical,it must contain two variables ${A}^{\prime }$ and ${B}^{\prime }$ and three atoms ${R}^{\prime }\left( {X}_{R}\right) ,{S}^{\prime }\left( {X}_{S}\right)$ , and ${T}^{\prime }\left( {X}_{T}\right)$ such that ${A}^{\prime } \in  {X}_{R},{B}^{\prime } \notin  {X}_{R},{A}^{\prime },{B}^{\prime } \in  {X}_{S},{B}^{\prime } \in  {X}_{T}$ ,and ${A}^{\prime } \notin  {X}_{T}$ . We use ${R}^{\prime }\left( {X}_{R}\right) ,{S}^{\prime }\left( {X}_{S}\right)$ ,and ${T}^{\prime }\left( {X}_{T}\right)$ to encode $R\left( A\right) ,S\left( {A,B}\right)$ ,and respectively $T\left( B\right)$ . While the variables ${A}^{\prime }$ and ${B}^{\prime }$ take on the $A$ - and respectively $B$ -values from the database for ${Q}_{\mathrm{{nh}}}$ ,all other variables in $Q$ take on a fixed dummy value. Hence,we can easily translate updates and enumeration requests to ${Q}_{\mathrm{{nh}}}$ to updates and enumeration requests to $Q$ .

F.5.3 任意非层次化查询的硬度证明。给定不含自连接的非层次化查询$Q$，我们可分别将问题${\mathrm{{IVM}}}^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  {Q}_{\mathrm{{nh}}}\right\rbrack$和${\mathrm{{IVM}}}_{\delta }^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  {Q}_{\mathrm{{nh}}}\right\rbrack$归约至${\mathrm{{IVM}}}^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$与${\mathrm{{IVM}}}_{\delta }^{ \pm  }\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$。以下简述归约思路：由于$Q$非层次化，必含变量${A}^{\prime }$、${B}^{\prime }$及三个原子${R}^{\prime }\left( {X}_{R}\right) ,{S}^{\prime }\left( {X}_{S}\right)$、${T}^{\prime }\left( {X}_{T}\right)$，使得${A}^{\prime } \in  {X}_{R},{B}^{\prime } \notin  {X}_{R},{A}^{\prime },{B}^{\prime } \in  {X}_{S},{B}^{\prime } \in  {X}_{T}$且${A}^{\prime } \notin  {X}_{T}$。我们使用${R}^{\prime }\left( {X}_{R}\right) ,{S}^{\prime }\left( {X}_{S}\right)$和${T}^{\prime }\left( {X}_{T}\right)$分别编码$R\left( A\right) ,S\left( {A,B}\right)$与$T\left( B\right)$。当变量${A}^{\prime }$和${B}^{\prime }$从${Q}_{\mathrm{{nh}}}$的数据库中获取$A$值和$B$值时，$Q$中其余变量均取固定虚拟值。因此，我们可轻松将对${Q}_{\mathrm{{nh}}}$的更新和枚举请求转换为对$Q$的相应操作。
